Explanatory Remarks:

1. Editorial notes or additions are enclosed in ().

Text:

tattvasāraḥ ratnasāriṇīvyākhyāsahitaḥ
śrīviṣṇucittapadapaṅkajasaṃgamāya
ceto mama spṛhayate kimataḥ pareṇa |
no cenmamāpi yatiśekharabhāratīnāṃ
bhāvaḥ kathaṃ bhavitumarhati vāgvidheyaḥ || 1 ||
oṃ namo bhaktisārāya vyavasāyātmane hareḥ |
daityān pracchannadaityān vā śastraiḥ śāstrairvidhūnvate ||
bhagavān varadaguruḥ, cikīrṣitasya granthasyāvighnena parisamāptyarthaṃ smṛtyācārasiddhaṃ maṅgalamācarati - śrīviṣṇucittetyādi
(mama cetaḥ) śrīviṣṇucittasya - viṣṇuḥ citte yasya sa tathoktastasyā smadācāryasya; bhaṭṭanāthasūrivatpratipakṣavijayapurassaraṃ paratattvanirdhāraṇādinā, taddāsyena, tadabhijanena ca tannāmavāhinaḥ; padapaṅkajayossaṃgamāya - pāṇimūrdhopasaṃgraharūpasamīcīnasaṃbandhanāyaspṛhayate - abhilaṣati | spṛherīpsitaḥ iti caturthī | yadyapi dvitīyāvirahātprakarṣānavagamaḥ; tathāpi vartamānena tadavagamaḥ | atra - yasya deve parā bhaktiryathā deve tathā gurau iti śrutirabhipretā ataḥ pareṇa kiṃ - śrīviṣṇucittapadasaṃgamādanyena mama na kimapi prayojanamityarthaḥ | bhagavadvandanāderācāryavandanasāpekṣatvādasya ca tannirapekṣatvāt | ata eva phalasiddhau na tadapekṣāpītyarthaḥ | no cedityādi - śrīviṣṇucittapadapaṅkajasaṃgamo me na bhavati cet | yatiśekharabhāratīnāṃ - śrībhāṣyādīnām | bhāvaḥ - abhiprāyaḥ | pūrvāparāvirodhavivakṣayaikavacanaṃ; nāto’rthatogambhīraṃ bhāṣyam ityanena virodhaḥ | mamāpi - grahaṇadhāraṇākṣamasyāpi mama | vāgvidheyaḥ - cikīrṣitatattvasārādigranthanirmāṇena supratipādanaḥ | bhavituṃ kathaṃ - kena prakāreṇa arhati - yogyo bhavati | na kenāpyevaṃ mama vāgvidheyo bhavatītyarthaḥ | vāgvidheyaḥ - vāgadhīnapravṛttinivṛttiriti vā | anena, yasya kasyacinmandadhiyo’pi (varadagurubhiḥ) śrībhāṣyabhāvābhyanujñāne tasyāpyativiśado bhavati, śirasi karamasau yasya kasyāpi dhatte so’pi ślokānakāṇḍe racayatīti nyāyāditi sūcyate | atra tasyaite kathitāhyarthāḥ prakāśante iti śrutirabhipretā | atra ca śrīviṣṇviti vastunirdeśaḥ, saṃgameti namaskriyā, spṛhayata ityāśīśca kṛtāḥ | vāgvidheya
iti prayojanaṃ darśitam | yatiśekharabhāratīnāmityādinā cikīrṣitagranthasya bhāṣyaprameya eva prameya iti darśitam | śrīviṣṇucitteti devatāpāramārthyamapi tatheti vyañjitam | (ata evātra viṣayādikamapi bhāṣyīyameveti dhvanitam |) bhāṣye bhagavadguruviṣayamaṅgalayoḥ paurvāparyavadatrāpi darśitadiśā tayostaditi granthaśailyapi saiveti vyañjitam | ata eva mātrayāpyatra svakapolakalpitatvaṃ neti sāṃpradāyikatvamāviṣkṛtam | atra siṃhoddhataṃ vṛttam | asmiṃśca granthe vṛttavaicitryamalasapuruṣarañjanārtham ||
mamāpītyapiśabdoktāmaśakiṃta vivṛṇvan, svasya yativarabhāratībhāvavāgvidheyatayopakārātiśayamāha - 
kva pathi viduṣāmeṣā prauḍhī śriyaḥ prabhuṇā śape maśakaśakanaṃ manye mādṛkprabhāvanidarśanam |
yativaravacastejaḥ pratyarthivarganirargalakṣapaṇanipuṇaṃ nityaṃ jāgarti kīrtikaraṃ mayi || 2 ||
eṣā prauḍhī - madīyaṃ prāgalbhyam | viduṣāṃ - nirantaraśāstraśīlanaśālinām | vido laṭo vā iti vartamāne kvasuḥ | pathimārge | kva - na kutrāpīryarthaḥ | viduṣāṃ mārga idamasatkalpamiti hṛdayam | eṣā prauḍhīti kṣepagarbhaṃ vacanam | yadvā eṣā prauḍhī - pūrvaprastutayatiśekharīyā prāgalbhī | viduṣāṃ - jagadvartinām | pathi kva - na kutrāpi dṛśyata ityarthaḥ | śaṃkarādiṣu na kvāpīdṛśī prauḍhī dṛśyata iti bhāvaḥ | idameva satyāpanena dṛḍhayati - śriyaḥ prabhuṇā śapa iti | śriyaḥ prabhuṇeti viśeṣaṇāt śriyā śape tatpatināpi śapa iti labhyato mayā ca sītayā caiva śapto’si raghunandana itivat | nanu
tvacchaktistattulyetyāśaṅkya na tatprauḍhī macchakternidarśanam; kiṃ tu maśakaśaktirityāha - maśaketi | mādṛśāṃ prabhāvasya, nidarśanaṃ - dṛṣṭāntaḥ | maśakaśakanaṃ manye - macchaktidṛṣṭānto maśakaśaktiriti mama matamityarthaḥ | madhyamaṇinyāyenātrāpi satyāpanamanveti | anena, svapragalbhyāvalokino lokasya svoktāvaśraddhāsaṃbhāvanāṃ (varadaguroriti) sūcyate | kathaṃ tarhi bhavādṛśā svapakṣasthāpanaparapakṣanirasanaṃ ca kriyamāṇamupapadyata ityatrāha - yativareti | pratyarthināṃ - paravādināṃ ye vargāḥ - bāhyakudṛṣṭirūpeṇa, kṛdṛṣṭiṣveva pūrvottarabhāgakudṛṣṭirūpeṇa ca sthitāsmaṅghāḥ, teṣāṃ nirargalakṣapaṇe - niśśeṣavināśane, nipuṇaṃ kuśalam | mayi - klībaprāye mayi | kīrtikaraṃ - svayaṃ jitvā tatphalabhūtāyāḥ prathāyā macchirasyāropakam | pāṇḍaveṣu kṛṣṇavanmamaivaṃvidhopakārakaramiti bhāvaḥ | yativaravaca eva tejaḥ - prakāśaḥ | ajñānāndhakāraharaṇādinā rūpaṇam | vacasastejaḥ - parābhibhavanasāmarthyaṃ vā | mayi nityaṃ jāgarti - mayi supte’pi svayamapramattaṃ sthitvā rakṣatītyarthaḥ | ya eṣu suṣaṣu jāgartī tikat | mayi jāgarti - mayyantarhitaṃ sthitvā madarthe jāgarūkaṃ bhavati | sāpahnavaṃ pāṇḍaveṣviti sudarśanaśailī pratyabhijñāpyate | mayi kīrtikaraṃ, pratyarthikṣapaṇanipuṇaṃ teja ityatra nityānukūlamanukūlanṛṇāṃ pareṣāmudvejanaṃ tu tava teja udāharantīti bhagavattajassādharmye vyajyate | vacasteja iti, mānaṃ pradīpam itivat | pratyarthivargetyādi ca, tatraiva ke’pi capalāśśalabhī bhavantī tivat | ato yativaravacasāṃ vedasāmyaṃ vedavedyasāmyaṃ ca vyajyate | atra śriyaḥ prabhuṇā śapa iti śapa upālambhe ityātmanepadam | asmin śloke uttaraśloke ca hariṇīplutaṃ vṛttam ||
nanu kīrtikaramityuktam | kīrtirhi vijayakhyātiḥ | tadāhuḥ vyāsapādāḥ vitaraṇavikramādihetubhedātkīrtiyaśasorbhedaḥ iti | ataḥ kīrtikaratve vijayapradatvamapi vaktavyamityāśaṅkya tadapyastīti vadan, maśakaśakanaṃ manya ityuktaṃ vivṛṇoti -
na khalu kavitāmānaḥ kā naḥ kṣatiḥ pathi gautame
na ca paricayaḥ kāṇāde vā kumāriladarśane |
api gurumate śāstreṣvanyeṣvapīha tathāpi naḥ
phalati vijayaṃ sarvatra śrīyatīśvaragīśśramaḥ || 3 ||
iha - asmin jagati | naḥ - asmākam | kavitayā - kavanena kavanaṃ kavitā kaviḥ iti naighaṇṭukāḥ mānaḥ - parāvadhikotkarṣābhimānaḥ | na khalu - na na | khalu śabdaḥ pratiṣedhe, dārḍhye dviruktiḥ | prasiddhau vā | nātra śriyaḥ prabhuṇā śape iti vaktavyamiti bhāvaḥ | prasiddho hyanyatra kavitāmānaḥ viriñciprapañce madanyaḥ kaviḥ kaḥ iti sāraṃ tu sārasvataṃ jānīte iti ca | naḥ - śrībhāṣyapariśramaśālināmasmākam | kṣatiḥ kā - na kāpi hānirityarthaḥ | kavitāyā hi dvayaṃ prayojanaṃ, sukavitā yadyasti rājyena kim ityuktaṃ rājyalābhasukhaṃ, vādāhaveṣvavācakāvijñātārthārthāntararūpanigrahasthānaprabhedaparihāreṇa samīcīnavākyaprayogeṇa paravādivijayaśceti | tayordvayorapyanena lābhānna ko’pi cheda iti bhāvaḥ| gautame pathi ca - nyāyaśāstre’pi paricayo na | kāṇāde vā na paricayaḥ | gautamaṃ
kāṇādaṃ cānvīkṣakīprabhedau | kumāriladarśane - jaiminisūtravyākhyātuḥ kumārilasya śāstre | gurumate - jaiminisūtrāṇāmeva prasthānāntareṇa (vyākhyānābhiyukto) gururiti prasiddha | tanmate’pi - na paricayaḥ | kiṃ bahunā anyeṣu śāstreṣu - sāṅkhyayogādiṣu paricayo’pi na | tathāpi - tantrāntarapariśramābhāve’pi | naḥ - asmākam | śrīyatīśvaragīśśramaḥ - śrībhāṣyādiṣu pariśramaḥ | sarvatra vijayaṃ - sarvatantreṣu paravādivijayaṃ, sarvāsu sabhāsu vijayaṃ vā | phalati - niṣpādayati | ato vijayapradatvātkīrtikaratvamupapannamiti bhāvaḥ śrīśabdaḥ - pūjyatvārthaḥ | tena adhītya gautamīṃ vidyām ityuktāniṣṭaviraho’pyabhipretaḥ ||
sarvamidamabhyupetyavādarūpam | paramārthatastu śrībhāṣyapariśramaśālino’gre paravādisaṃbhavasyaivābhāvātkeyaṃ kathetyāha - 
yaścakāra yatirājabhāratīcittavṛttiṣu paraṃ pariśramam |
tasya vaidikasadaśśikhāmaṇeragrataḥ kva paravādisaṃbhavaḥ || 4 ||
yaḥ, yatirājabhāratyāḥ śrībhāṣyādirūpāyāḥ cittavṛttiṣu tātparyeṣu | paraṃ pariśramaṃ, cakāra - kṛtavānabhūvam, akarodvā śraddhātiśayena bhogyatātiśayena vā, śramānavabhāsātpārokṣyam | atra bhāratyāṃ śramaḥ, taccittavṛttau pariśramaḥ, cittavṛtiṣu paraḥ pariśrama iti viveko’nusandheyaḥ paricitagahanānaḥ prasnuvīta prasādam ityukteḥ | vaidikānāṃ - bhāgadvayātmakavedasvārasyāvalambināṃ, sadaso - goṣṭhyāḥ, śikhāmaṇeḥ - abhyarhitasya | tasya agrataḥ - śrībhāṣyapariśramaśālino’gre paravādināṃ saṃbhavaḥ kva - teṣāmasaṃbhava evetyarthaḥ | tathā ca vijayapradatvādivacanamabhyupetyavāda iti bhāvaḥ | atrākṣepālaṃkāradhvaniḥ | ākṣepassvayamuktasya pratiṣedho vicāraṇādi tyukteḥ | vedābhimānisarasvatīśirobhūṣaṇabhūtaśrībhāṣyavidāṃ vedādhyetṛjanaśirobhūṣaṇatvaṃ yuktamiti kāvyaliṅgaṃ dhvanyate |
samarthanīyasyārthasya kāvyaliṅgaṃ samarthanami tyukteḥ | atra rathoddhataṃ vṛtam ||
evaṃ svagranthasya balananmūlakatvaṃ paravādibhiraprakampyatvaṃ coktamityupoddhāto’yaṃ vṛttaḥ |
atha granthamārabhate - prathamādhikaraṇa iti | - śiṣyāvadhānārthamidam | atra parvottarapakṣāvucyete iti śeṣaḥ |
sāṅgasaśiraskavedādhyayanavataḥ kṛtsnavedārthaviṣaya āpātapratyayaḥ kṛtsnavedārtha vicārādhikāraḥ | tatrādhyayanakramātkarmavicāre pūrvaṃ kṛte, tadadhīnakarmāsthirālpaphalakatvanirṇayasahitabrahmānantasthiraphalāpātapratyaya uttarabhāgārthavicārādhikāraḥ | āpātapratyayaśca saṃbhāvitaprāmāṇyo vivakṣita iti siddhāntasthitiḥ | tatra pūrvapakṣī prābhākaraḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate | prathamavyutpattiḥ vṛddhavyavahārādanyatra na saṃbhavati | na hi ghaṭo’stītyukte pravṛttyabhāve sati tasya bodhakatvaṃ jñāyate | gāmānayetyādiśabda prayogānantaraṃ prayojyapravṛtiṃ dṛṣṭvā pravṛtteḥ kāryabuddhipūrvakatvaṃ jānan vyutpitsuḥ ayaṃ śabdaḥ kāryarūpārthabodhakaḥ iti niścinoti | tatra bahuṣu prayogeṣvanu vṛttaśabdasyānuvṛttārthena saṃbandhaṃ vyāvṛttaśabdasya vyāvṛttārthena saṃbandhaṃ gṛhītvā āvāpodvāpamukhenārthaviśeṣaiśśabdaviśeṣasaṃbandhaṃ niścinotīti prathamavyutpattiḥ kāryārtha eva | ataḥ ya eva lokikāsta eva vaidikāḥ iti nyāyādvedaḥ kāryapara iti vedāntānāṃ pariniṣpannabrahmabodhakatvābhāvādāpātapratītyasiddhirityadhikārivirahānnedaṃ śāstramārambhaṇīyamiti; tadetadāha - anyatra vyavahārata ityādinā prathamaślokena |
anyatra vyavahārato na bhavati vyutpattiranyatra vā
kaścitkāryadhiyaḥ kathaṃ vyavahareditthaṃ ca niścīyate |
śabdāḥ kāryaparā iti śrutiratastatraiva mānaṃ punaḥ
pramāṇyaṃ kathamaśnuvīta pariniṣpanne pare brahmaṇi || 5 ||
vyavahārataḥ - prayojyaprayojakavṛddhakartṛkoktipravṛttirūpavyavahārāt | anyatra
vyutpattiḥ ādyavyutpattiḥ, śaktigraho na bhavati (anyato) na jāyate | dvitīyādivyutpattistu kāryārthaprathamavyutpattyadhīneti bhāvaḥ | kaścidvā - ko’pi vā | kāryadhiyo’nyatra - kāryabuddhyaviṣaye | kathaṃ vyavaharet - kathaṃ śabda prayogaṃ kuryāt, kathaṃ pravarteta ca, na kathamapītyarthaḥ | vā śabdastvaniyativācakaḥ; prayojyaṃ prayojakaṃ ca
kaścitpadavivakṣitaṃ sūcayati | itthaṃ - evaṃ sati | cakāraḥ, putraste jātaḥ ityādīnāmanyathāsidiṃdha(abhipraiti) | śabdāḥ - sarve śabdāḥ, kāryaparā iti niścīyate | ataḥ - ya eva laukikāḥ iti nyāyena | tatraiva - kārya eva | mānaṃ - pramāṇabhūtā | śrutiḥ - nityavaccha yamāṇaśabdarāśiḥ | pariniṣpanne kāryānātmake | pare - cetanācetanavilakṣaṇe | brahmaṇi punaḥ - brahmaṇi tu | pramāṇyaṃ - bodhakatvam | kathamaśnuvīta kathaṃ prāpnuyāt | pariniṣpanna ityanena, kāryarūpatvābhāvāt tatra pramāṇyaṃ nāstītyuktam | para iti pratyagātmavat kartṛtvena karmavidhiśeṣatvavirahānna tatra pramāṇyamityuktam | (tu śabdena) (tvarthakapunaśśabdena) dhyeyasatyatāniścāyakābhāvānna dhyānavidhiśeṣatvena prāmāṇyamityuktam | ato na kenāpi prakāreṇa tatra prāmāṇyamityarthaḥ ||
atrottaram |
siddhāntaṃ vaktumupakramate - atrottaramiti |
( vadāmītiśeṣaḥ) asmin pūrvapakṣe uttarapakṣaṃ vadāmītyarthaḥ |
yādṛcchikavyutpattiḥ buddhipūrvakavyutpattiriti vyutpattidvayamasti | tatrādyā prayojyaprayojakavyāpāradarśanādhīnā | dvitīyā tu pitrādiśikṣādhīnā | tatra yādṛcchikyeva vyutpattirna buddhipūrvikāpyastīti pūrvapakṣiṇo’bhimānaḥ | tadabhyupetya (yādṛcchikavyutpattissiddhārthe’pi) (yādṛcchikavyutpattāvapi siddhārthe vyutpattiḥ) astītyāha - ya ityādinā |
yaḥ prābodhi parehayāpavarake daṇḍaḥ sthito bodhyatāṃ
caitrāyeti sa tasya bodhanasamudyukto yadūce vacaḥ |
etacceṣṭitabhedavittaditaro mūko yathākarṇayan
bālaḥ khalvidamasya bodhakamiti vyutpattimabhyasyati || 6 ||
parasya - puruṣāntarasya, īhayā - hastādiceṣṭayā, katrryā | yaḥ - puruṣaḥ, karma | apavarake sthito daṇḍaścaitrāya bodhyatāmiti prabodhitaḥ | apavarake - gṛhaikadeśe | jñāpanīyasya jñānaśeṣitvavivakṣayā caitrāya iti caturthī | saḥ - prabodhitaḥ puruṣaḥ | tasya - caitrasya | bodhane - apavarakasthitadaṇḍabodhane, samudyuktassan - caitrasamīpaṃ gatassan
| yadvacaḥ - apavarake daṇḍo’stītyākārikāṃ yāṃ giram | ūce - 
abravīt | (bruvo vaciḥ iti vacestritvādātmanepadam) | mūko yathā - mūka iva etacceṣṭitabhedavit pūrvoktahastādiceṣṭādarśī | itaro bālaḥ - bodhyabodhaka (bodhayitṛ) puruṣatrayavyatirikto vyutpitsuḥ | tadākarṇayan - tāṃ giraṃ śrṛṇvan san | idam - apavarake daṇḍo’stīti vākyam | asya apavarakasthiṃtadaṇḍasya | bodhakamiti vyutpatiṃta - (śaktyadhīna) śābdabodhajanakatvagraham | abhyasyati - ( ādyāvagativiṣayīkaroti), āvartayatīti (vā) | khalu prasiddhau | atra (trikavadityanena) śabdanirapekṣaṃ tattadarthajñānaṃ ceṣṭādinā saṃbhavatīti darśitam | uktarītyā yādṛcchikavyutpattāvapi siddhavastuni śaktigrahasaṃbhavāt kāryārtha eva vyutpattiriti nirbandho nirnibandhana iti bhāvaḥ | pūrvatrātra ca śārdūlavikrīḍitaṃ vṛttam || 6 ||
vastutastu buddhipūrvakavyutpattirapyastyeveti siddhavastuni śabdaśaktigraho nirbādha evetyāha - ( aṅgulyetyādi)
aṅgulyā nirdiśadbhirghaṭamukhamakhilaṃ vastu tattadbruvadbhi stacchabdāṃstadvibhaktīrapi ca taducite gocare pūrvavṛddhaiḥ | pitrādyaiśśikṣyamāṇāśśiśava iha muhussvātmanāṃ tattadartha
jñānaṃ tacchabdamātraśravaṇata uditaṃ prekṣya jānanti śaktim || 7 ||
iha - jagati | aṅgulyā, ghaṭamukhaṃ - ghaṭo mukhe agre upariṣṭāditi yāvat yasya tat tathoktam | tattat - sasaṃbandhikamasaṃbandhikaṃ ca | sasaṃbandhikasya prāthamyasiddhyarthaṃ vyadhikaraṇabahuvrīhyāśrayaṇaṃ, mukhaśabdasya upariṣṭādarthakatvāśrayaṇaṃ ca | vastu - karma | nirdiśadbhiḥ - upakṣipadbhiḥ | tacchabdān - ambādi vācakasasaṃbandhiśabdān, ghaṭādivācakāsaṃbandhiśabdāṃśca | tadvibhaktīrapi - yogyakriyākārakaviśeṣabodhikā dvitīyādivibhaktīśca | taducite - tatacchabdocite tattacchabdanirūpitaśaktipramāviṣaye | gocare - arthe, mātrādighaṭādyarthe | bruvadbhiḥ - uccārayadbhiḥ | pūrvavṛddhaiḥ - evameva svayamapi śikṣitaiḥ pitrādyaiḥ | ādyapadena mātṛmātulādiparigrahaḥ | śikṣyamāṇāḥ - taistaiśśabdaisteṣu teṣvartheṣūtpādyamānajñānāḥ | śiśavaḥ - bālāḥ | tacchabdamātrasya - kṛtsnasyāmbādiśabdasya, śravaṇataḥ - śravaṇāt | muhuḥ - niyamena | uditaṃ - jātam | svātmanāṃ tattadarthajñānaṃ - 
ambādighaṭādyarthaviṣayakasmṛtim | prekṣya - dṛṣṭvā, anubhūya | praśabdena śabdārthayossaṃbandhāntarādarśanaṃ saṃketayitṛpuruṣājñānaṃ ca vivakṣitam | śakiṃta jānanti - niścinvanti || ayamarthaḥ - bālā hi mātāpitṛprabhṛtibhiḥ śabdārthasaṃbandhabodhanāya pravṛttairambātātamātulādīn ghaṭārdīśca aṅgulyā nirdiśya enamavehi idamavadhāraya iti tattacchabdaistatadartheṣu bahuśaḥ śikṣitāḥ śanaiśśanaistattadartheṣu svātmanāṃ bodhotpattimanubhavanto dhūmavahnyoriva bodhyabodhakabhāvanirvāhakasaṃbandhamākāṅkṣamāṇāḥ
tādṛśānugatānatiprasaktasaṃbandhāntaramalabhamānāssaṃketayitṛpuruṣānajānantaśśabdārthayossaṃbandhaṃ śaktimeva niścinvanti iti ||
atra nirdiśadbhirityādivartamānena, yāvadaṅgulīnirdeśaśabdaprayogasāhacaryadarśanajanitavāsanābhūyastvena svayameva tattadartheṣu tatacchabdān prayoktumarthamātradarśanena śabdaṃ smartuṃ śabdamātraśravaṇena arthaṃ smartuṃ ca śiśavaḥ kṣamā bhavanti tāvaditi sūcyate | pūrvavṛddhairiti, bhramādyabhāvassūcyate | pitrādyairityanupekṣakatvaṃ sūcyate | (tattaditi) sasaṃbandhiśabdānāmasaṃbandhiśabdavyutpattyapekṣayā vyutpattiprāthamyaṃ ghaṭamukhaśabdena sūcyate | idaṃ ca kaṃcitpratipituranyaṃ pratyatathātvādyādṛcchikavyutpattāvasaṃbhavi | tacchabdamātreti kātsnaryoktyā śikṣiteṣu śabdeṣu na kasyāpyabodhakatvamiti vyajyate | avadhāraṇe vā mātraśabdaḥ śabdādanyenānutpattimāha | evaṃ ca buddhipūrvakavyutpatterdurapahnavatvātsiddhavastuni śaktiniścayenāpātapratītisaṃbhavādidaṃ śāstramārambhaṇīyamiti tātparyam | stragdharāvṛttam || 7 ||
janmādyadhikaraṇaṃ prastauti - atha janmādyadhikaraṇamiti |
atha janmādyadhikaraṇam |
prathamādhikaraṇe vyutpattyabhāvaḥ parihṛtaḥ | atra pratipattidausthyaṃ parihriyate | kāraṇatvasya viśeṣaṇatayā lakṣaṇatve viśeṣyabrahmanānātvaprasaṃgāt, upalakṣaṇatayā tattve copalakṣyākārābhāvājjanmādikāraṇatvaṃ brahmalakṣaṇaṃ na bhavatīti pūrvapakṣe - khaṇḍatāderviruddhaviśeṣaṇasya viśeṣyabhedāpādakatve’pyaviruddhaviśeṣaṇasya śyāmatāderviśeṣyabhedakatvaviraheṇa viśeṣyanānātvāprasaktyā janmādīnāṃ viśeṣaṇatayā lakṣaṇatvaṃ saṃbhavati, niratiśaya brahmatvasyopalakṣyākāratvasaṃbhavādupalakṣaṇatayāpi teṣāṃ lakṣaṇatvaṃ saṃbhavatīti, (lakṣaṇavirahāt) pratipattidausthyaparihārasiddhāntamabhiprayan
lakṣaṇavākyavyākhyānabhūtajanmādisūtravyākhyānātmakabhāṣyavākyeṣvacintyetyādivistarasya hetuṃ prayojanaṃ ca ślokadvayenāha - bhūtānītyādinā, asyetyuddiśya sautramityādinā ca ||
bhūtānītyādinā, asyetyuddiśya sautramityādinā ca ||
bhṛtānīti padena vastvavagatau satyāmimānīti yadgāyantyāmakhileśvaro’gaṇi jagajjanmādibhirlakṣaṇaiḥ |
tenādarśi vicitrataiva jagatassṛjyasya tenāpi tatsraṣṭurbrahmaṇa ujjvalā guṇagaṇā jijñāsyatāhetavaḥ || 8 ||
atra, yato vā imānītyādilakṣaṇavākye ityādiḥ | yato vetyādivākye, vastvavagatau - kāryavargaviṣayakaśābdabodhe | bhūtānīti padena satyāṃ - bhūtānītyanena jāte’pi | imānīti gāyantyām - (midamuktāviti śeṣaḥ | imānīti śrūyamāṇa idaṃśabde) jagajjanmādibhiḥ - ādipadena sthitilayāvucyete | lakṣaṇaiḥ asādhāraṇadharmaiḥ | akhileśvaraḥ - 
patiṃ viśvasya ityukto bhagavān | agaṇi - alakṣīti yat tena sṛjyasya jagato vicitratā - uccāvacaprakārakatvam | adarśye abodhyataiva | yadvā vicitrataivābodhyata na tvābhāsatā | tenāpītyādi | tena - jagadvaicitryeṇa | tatsraṣṭuḥ - vicitrajagatkāraṇabhūtasya | brahmaṇaḥ | jijñāsyatāyāḥ - upāsyatāprayuktavicāryatāyāḥ, hetavaḥ - tatprayojakaprāpyatopapādanena prayojakāḥ | ujjvalāḥ - sāmānyaviśeṣotsargāpavādopakramanayairniṣedhavākyānāmanyaparatvopapādanena nirvighātatayā śrutiṣu pratīyamānāḥ guṇagaṇāḥ - jñānaśaktayādiguṇānāṃ, gaṇāḥ - samūhāḥ | abodhyanta | anuṣaktasyādarśītyasya bahuvacanāntatayā vipariṇāmaḥ | kāraṇatva śrutyarthāpattyā guṇānāmākṣepāditi bhāvaḥ ||
atra vastupadena, śrutigatabhūtapadaṃ bhavanakriyāyogiparaṃ na prāṇimātraparamiti sūcitam | lakṣaṇairiti siddhavannirdeśāt pūrvoktapūrvapakṣasiddhāntāvapi sūcitau | lakṣaṇabhūtairjanmādibhiḥ ityanvayātsamudāyo lakṣaṇamiti sūcitatvānniratiśayabṛhattvasiddhiḥ vicitratā adarśyeva iti yojanayā, atyantāyogavyavacchedaḥ pratīyate | ayogasya ca prasaktiruddeśyaviśeṣaṇasya (grahādhikaraṇoktanyāyenā) vivakṣitatvānnedaṃpadena vicitratābodhasaṃbhava
iti | tadvyavacchedaśca grahaikatvādeḥ prāpakavākyavirodhinarutyāge’pi tadavirodhino’ṣṭavarṣatvāderna tyāga iti prakṛte
sṛjyavaicitryasya prāpakavākyasiddhatvānna tattyāga iti | jijñāsyatāhetava iti tu, tadbrahma tadvijijñāsasveti dvitīyatatpadārthaḥ, mumukṣūpāsyatvena prakṛtaṃ hi tacchabdārtha ityuktatvāt | ataḥ vijijñāsasva iti na vicārasya upāsanasya vā vidhiḥ ityanena virodho nāstīti mantavyam | jagata iti, sautraikavacanamanusṛtyoktam | akhileśvara iti ca, bhṛguvallīsthalakṣaṇavākyaikavākyasatyādivākyasthadvitīyāntānantapadayogarūḍhyarthanirdeśa iti || 8 ||
yato vetyatrasthemānītyasya, tanmūlakasya sautrasyāsyeti padasya ca prayojanakathanārthaṃ (idaṃ) | ayamarthaḥ - bhūtaśabdena kāryavarge’bhihite’pi imānīti padaṃ, kāryavargasya dharmigrāhakapramāṇasiddhākāraviśeṣaparaṃ; na tu jagata ābhāsatāparam | brahmaṇo jijñāsyatānupayogitvāt | vaicitryaparamimānīti padamīdṛśakāryaniṣpādanānuguṇaguṇotkarṣaṃ brahmeti jñāpanārtham | śrutivākyasthedaṃśabdena nirdiśataḥ sūtrakārasyāpīyameva vivakṣeti bhāṣyasthācintyetyādivistarasyedameva mūlamiti, tadidamāha - asyeti |
asyetyuddiśya sautraṃ padamatha tadacintyādibhāṣyaṃ vivavre
tattanmānaprasiddhaṃ jagadabhidadhatī tadvicitratvamāha |
śrautī sautrīdamuktistviti hṛdi nidadhattatra cācetanāṃśe
vaicitrīmāha pūrvaṃ padamatha niyatetyādikaṃ cetanāṃśe || 9 ||
te te mānena - pramāṇena prasiddhāḥ - dharmipratītidaśāyāmeva pratīyamānā yasya tattathoktaṃ, dharmigrāhakapramāṇasiddhākāraviśeṣayuktamityarthaḥ | jagat - bhūtaśabdoktakāryavargam| abhidadhatī - śaktyā bodhayantī | jagaddharmigrāhakamānasiddhākārayuktamabhidadhatītyanvayaḥ | śrautī - yato vetyādivākyasthā | sautrī - janmādyasya yata iti sūtrasthā ca | idamuktiḥ tuśabdaścārthako bhinnakrameṇa yojyaḥ, vaiṣamye vā | tuśabdo grahaikatvavyāvṛttamatratyoddeśyaviśeṣaṇamāha | idamuktiḥ - imānīti asyeti ca idaṃśabdaḥ, tasya - jagato vicitratvamāheti hṛdi nidadhadacintyādibhāṣyam, asyeti sautraṃ padamuddiśya - vyākhyeyatayopādāya tadvivavre - vyācakhyau | tatra - bhāṣye, acintyavividhavicitraracanasya niyatadeśakālaphalabhogabrahmādistambaparyantakṣetrajñamiśrasya iti
bhāṣyavākya ityarthaḥ | pūrvaṃ padaṃ - acintyavividhavicitraracanasyeti padam | acetanāṃśe - kāryavargaikadeśeṣvacetaneṣu vaicitrīmāha | atha - anantaram | niyatetyādikaṃ - niyatadeśakālaphalabhogabrahmādistambaparyantakṣetrajñamiśrasyeti padam | cetanāṃśe - kāryavargaikadeśeṣu cetaneṣu vaicitrīmāha | tadidamāhuḥ vyāsapādāḥ tatrācidaṃśavaicitryamāha acintyetyādinā, atha cidvaicitryamāha niyateti iti || 9 ||
śāstrayonitvādhikaraṇaṃ prastauti - atha śāstrayonitveti |
atha śāstrayonitvādhikaraṇam |
atra hi, ekadeśe’nuvādatvaṃ bādhaścāṃśāntare girām ityevaṃ pratyavasthāne vaktavyaṃ kiñciduttaramiti nimittasya ca karturanumānasiddhatvāttadaṃśe’nuvādatvaṃ nimittopādānayormitho bhedadarśanānnimittasyopādānatvaṃ bādhitamiti śaṅkāyām, karturanumānasiddhau viśeṣaṇavirodhāt, atyantātīndriye’rthe śrutereva pramāṇatvāt, ūrṇanābhādau tayoraikyasya dṛṣṭatvācca nimittopādānaśrutyornānuvādatvabādhāviti, mānāntareṇa brahmasiddhirnirākṛtā | tacchaṅkāparihārau pañcabhiḥ ślokairāha kāryatvādityādibhiḥ |
kāryatvāddhaṭavatsakartṛ vimataṃ kṣityādikāryaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ sāvayavatvatassa bhagavānādhārasiddhāntataḥ |
ityetanna ghaṭeta viśvajanane yatkartṛkālaikyayoḥ kāryatvavyabhicāribhedagaṇanāhetorvirodho’thavā || 10 ||
naiyāyiko’tra pūrvapakṣī sarvakartāramanumānena sādhayati | vimataṃ - vivādādhyāsitam |
kṣityādi - kṣitiḥ - mahī, ādipadena - mahīdharādiparigrahaḥ | nānāvyaktyanugamakaṃ vimatapadam | khātapūraṇādiniṣpādyasaṃpratipannāṃśavyavacchedaparaṃ vā | sakartṛ - upādānābhijñasamarthakartṛkam | kāryatvāt kṛtijanyatvāt | ghaṭavaditi | sakartriti
kapratyayavirahassamāsāntavidhiranitya iti śābdikokteḥ | evaṃ kāryatvahetunā kṣityādessakartṛkatvaṃ sādhitam | atha hetossvarūpāsidiṃdha vyudasyati | kāryaṃ ceti - tat kṣityādikaṃ, kāryaṃ sāvayavatvataḥ - avayavārabdhatvāt, ghaṭavadityanuṣajyate | cakāreṇa, mahattve sati kriyāvattvaṃ, mahattve sati mūrtatvaṃ ca hetvantaraṃ gṛhyate | nanu bhavatu kartussiddhiḥ,
īśvarasiddheḥ kimāyātam ityatrāha - sa bhagavāniti | saḥ - kartā, bhagavān - nityajñānādimānīśvarassidhyati | kuta ityatrāha - ādhārasiddhāntataḥ - adhikaraṇasiddhāntāddhetoḥ, pakṣadharmatābalāt nityajñānādisiddhirityarthaḥ | ādhārasiddhānto nāma, siddhāntapadārthaprabhedaḥ | sarvatantrapratitantrābhyupagamādhārasiddhāntabhedāccaturdhā siddhānta ityuktatvāt | tatra trayyantavidāṃ siddhāntatrayamabhimatam | vaiśeṣikasya tu turīyo’pi | taduktaṃ nyāyapariśuddhau trividhassarvatantrapratitantrābhyupagamasiddhāntabhedāt iti | kecitu - kasya citsādhane’nuṣaṅgasiddho’nya iti caturdhā vibhāgamāhuḥ | yathā kṣityādessakartṛkatvasiddhāvīśvarasiddhirityādi iti ca | evaṃ pūrvapakṣamupanyasya dūṣayitumupakramate - ityetadityādinā | ityetat - uktaprakāreṇa kāryatvātkarturanumānam | na ghaṭeta - na ghaṭanārham | kuta ityatrāha - yadityādi | yat - yasmāt | viśvajanane kṣityādikāryāṇamutpattau | kartṛkālaikyayoḥ - ekena nirmitatvarūpakartraikye, ekadā nirmitatvarūpakālaikye ca | kāryatvavyabhicāribhedagaṇanā - kāryatvarūpahetoḥ, vyabhicāribhedayossādhāraṇāsādhāraṇānaikāntyayoḥ, gaṇanā - dvitveneyattānirṇayaḥ | kāryatva mityapi paṭhyate | tadā kāryatvaṃ vyabhicārītyanvayaḥ | bhedagaṇanāditi ca pāṭhaḥ | bhedadarśanādityarthaḥ | idaṃ ca virodhe’nveti | athavā hetoḥ - kāryatvasya | virodhaḥ - anabhimatabahutvasādhakatvam | tata etanna ghaṭeteti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | ayamarthaḥ - kāryatvena karturanumitāvapi ekatvaṃ na sidhyati, pramāṇābhāvāt | na ca kāryatvameva pramāṇam | vikalpāsahatvāt | kiṃ kāryatvamātraṃ, utāsaṃbhāvitajīvakartṛkaṃ pakṣāsādhāraṇaṃ kāryatvaṃ, kiṃ vā vipulakāryatvam | prathame sādhāraṇānaikāntyaṃ, dvitīye tvasādhāraṇānaikāntya, tṛtīye vipulakāryeṣu kāladairghya kartṛbahutvadarśanādviruddhatvaṃ iti | athavetyuktestṛtīyaśirasyapyanaikāntyamiti sūcyate | ekakartṛkatvavirahe’pi vipulakāryatvadarśanāt | tadāhurvyāsapādāḥ - yadvā naikāntyam iti || 10 ||
astu lāghavādekatvasiddhirityatrāha - ( yadityādi)
yatkalpanāladhimalālasamānasānāṃ
kartraikyasiddhiriti nādaraṇīyametat |
tavdyāptimeva hi ruṇaddhi sakartṛkatvaṃ
kiṃ kalpyate laghimasaṃpadamīpsamānaiḥ || 11 ||
kalpanālaghimni - kalpanālāghavatarke, lālasā - mahānabhilāṣo yasya tattathoktaṃ mānasaṃ yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ naiyāyikānām | kurturaikyasya - ekatvasya siddhiḥ - lāghavatarkasahakāreṇa kartṛviṣayakānumitiviṣayatvamiti | yat - matam | etannādaraṇīyam - tatsiddhāntasthairapyanādaraṇīyam | hi yasmāt | tat - lāghavam | taditi, lāghavasāmānyaṃ parāmṛśyate | tallāghavam kartṛ vyāptimeva - kāryatvakartṛjanyatvayoḥ vyāptimeva | ruṇaddhi - (na grāhyati) | laghimasaṃpadaṃ - lāghavatarkākhyasaṃpadam | īpsamānaiḥ - āptumicchubhiḥ | sakartṛkatvaṃ, kiṃ kalpyate - kathaṃ sādhyate | bahukartṛkatvagauravaprahāṇena ekakartṛtve lāghavalābhavat tasyāpi prahāṇena lāghavalābhe saṃbhavati kathaṃ sa hīyate, śreyasi kena tṛpyata iti nyāyādityarthaḥ ||
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - kvacit samavāyyasamavāyinimittarūpakāraṇatrayasāpekṣotpattikatvadarśanāt, kvacicca nimittamātrāpekṣotpattikatvadarśanāt, kāryamātre, kāraṇeyattāniyamādarśanena, anyatra kāryopadhāyakakāraṇasaṃkhyāpekṣayā svalpasaṃkhyākakāraṇaireva kāryodayasya lāghavapakṣapātinyā buddhessvato lipsitatvena, aṅkurādau karturadarśanena ca, tamantareṇaivādṛṣṭasamāvahitaistaireva hetubhiḥ kāryodayaniścayasaṃbhavena, kartṛtvakāryatvābhyāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvāsiddhyā kāryatvasakartṛkatvayorvyāptyasiddhiḥ | kiṃca ghaṭādikāryeṣu ghaṭatvādijātyavacchedakalāghavamūlakatayā nānākāryakāraṇabhāvasyaiva prāmāṇikatayā tulyaśarīratāyāmeva sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyāvatāreṇa kartṛtvakāryatvābhyāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvāsiddhyā, noktavyāptisiddhiriti |
uktaṃ ca nyāyapariśuddhau tulyaṃ sarvavitkartṛpūrvakatvasādhane’pi tamantareṇāpi taistaireva hetubhiradṛṣṭasahakṛtaistattatkāryodayasaṃbhavāt iti | śārīrakasāre’pyuktaṃ lāghavātsyādakartṛkam iti | vyāptimevetyevakāreṇa,
vṛddhimicchato mūlahāniḥ iti nyāyassūcitaḥ | laghimasaṃpadityuktyā cāṇimādyantarbhūtalaghimākhyasaṃpatpratīteḥ pāśupatanirāso’pyatra gamyate | te’pi hyanumāneneśvaraṃ sādhayanti | ata eva tannirāsakasautrayuktīnāmatrākṛṣya bhāṣye’pyupanyāsa iti dhyeyam || 11 ||
ghaṭādau dṛṣṭānusāreṇa kṣityādessakartṛkatvasādhane, tadvadeva sattvādiguṇayukta karmavaśyaśarīrikartṛkatvasiddhiprasaṅgaḥ; kṣityāderyugapadutpattilayā - siddhiprasaṅgaśca | yadi
dṛṣṭānuguṇyānādaraḥ; tarhi kṣityādessāvayavatve’pi nityatvādikaṃ kalpyatām | atha darśanānuguṇyavirahaparāhataṃ taditi cetkarturapi sattvādiguṇavirahādikaṃ darśanānuguṇyavirahaparāhatamiti nānumānādīśvarasiddhiriti | imamarthe saṃgrahavistārābhyāṃ ślokatrayeṇāha - 
kṣmādāvaṃśini kāraṇāntaragaṇe yatne’pyadṛṣṭe tanau
pakṣe dṛṣṭavirūpatāstvaparadhā kalpyā na kartaryapi |
seyaṃ nyāyapathaprahīṇayugapatsargakṣayau nekṣitau
sarvasyeha na kalpanāparikarastannānumeyaḥ paraḥ || 12 ||
kṣmādau - mahīmahīdharādau | aṃśini - sāvayave vastuni, pakṣe | kāraṇāntaragaṇe - aṅkurādinirūpitakāraṇasamūhātmake,
pakṣe | yatne - yatnātmake, pakṣe | adṛṣṭe - adṛṣṭātmake, pakṣe | tanāvapi - śarīrātmake pakṣe ca | dṛṣṭavirūpatāstu - uttaraśloke vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa nityatvakartṛnirapekṣavyāpārakatvajñānacikīrṣānirapekṣatvakāryavidhānasamarthatvam | viprakṛṣṭatvādṛśyatvādikalpanādibhiḥ darśanānuguṇyaprahāṇamāpatati | aparadhā - dṛṣṭavairūpyasya jihāsitatve | kartaryapi - kāryatvahetunā siṣādhayiṣite kartaryapi | seyaṃ - dṛṣṭavirūpatā, sattvādiguṇādyaṃśe darśanānuguṇyaprahāṇam | na kalpyā - na kalpanīyam | kiṃ ca sarvasya - kṣityādeḥ kāryajātasya | nyāyapathaprahīṇau - anumānasaraṇerbhraṣṭau anumānāviṣayabhūtau, śrutyaikasamadhigamyau | yugapatsargakṣayau - ekakālasṛṣṭisaṃhārau | naikṣitau - na dṛṣṭau | dṛṣṭānuguṇyānusaraṇe tayoḥ kramikatvaprasaṃgaḥ | tadabhāve dṛṣṭavairūpasya prasaṃgaśca | iha - īśvarādiviṣaye | kalpanāyāḥ - anumiteḥ, parikaraḥ - sāmagrī | (nāstīti yat | tat - tasmāt | paraḥ - sarvakartā | nānumeyaḥ - anumānena na sidhyatītyarthaḥ || 12 ||
saṃgraheṇoktaṃ vistareṇa dvābhyāmāha - nityaṃ kṣityādītyādinā, dṛṣṭānuguṇyetyādinā ca - 
nityaṃ kṣityādi sāṃśaṃ bhavadapi ghaṭatāṃ kāraṇānāṃ pareṣāṃ
vyāpāraḥ kartrapekṣāvidhura iti bhavatvasya kartuḥ prayatne |
nāpekṣye dhīcikīrṣe tanubhṛta iha cādṛṣṭamīṣṭe vidhātuṃ
kāryaṃ dehī davīyānapi sṛjatu jagadvarṣma dṛśyetaradvā || 13 ||
sāṃśaṃ bhavadapi - sāvayavamapi | kṣityādi, nityaṃ ghaṭatāṃ nityaṃ syāt | prasiddhakartṛkabhinnatvāditi śeṣaḥ | sāvayavatvamastu kāryatvaṃ māstvityaprayojakatvaṃ vā | pareṣāṃ kāraṇānāṃ - kṣityādijanakahetūnām | paratvamiha ghaṭādikāraṇāpekṣayā | vyāpāraḥ kartrapekṣāvidhura iti bhavatu - kartṛnirapekṣakāryotpādaka iti bhavatu | kāraṇatvamastu kartṛsāpekṣitatvaṃ māstvityaprayojakatvam | pūrvataraślokoktalāghavatarkādvedam | asya kartuḥ, prayatne - yatnotpattau | dhīcikīrṣe - jñānamicchā ca | nāpekṣye - na kāraṇaṃ bhavatu | lāghavāditi śeṣaḥ | iha - īśvarānumāne | tanubhṛtaḥ - kṣetrajñasya adṛṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ vidhātuṃ, īṣṭa iti - samarthamiti | bhavatu - pratitarko bhavatvityarthaḥ | acetanatvāccetanādhiṣṭhitāveva dharmādharmau kāryakarau vāsivadityanupapannam; īśvarajñānaprayatnādau vyabhicārāditi bhāvaḥ | dehī - śarīrī, kṣetrajñaḥ | davīyānapi - atyantaviprakṛṣṭo’pi | dūraśabdādiyasuni dvādeśaḥ | jagat - aṅkurādikam | sṛjatu - saṃkalpādinā| viṣādiharaṇavattadupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | yadvā varṣma - śarīraṃ, sraṣṭuśśarīram | dṛśyetarat, astu - piśācasiddhādiśarīradadṛśyamastu | na tāvatā yogyānupalabdhiriti bhāvaḥ || 13 ||
dṛṣṭānuguṇyavirahādiha kalpanānā
māsāmasiddhiriti cedapi kartari syāt ||
janmakrameṇa dadṛśe jagataḥ kṣayo vā
klaptiśca darśanavaśādanayostathā syāt || 14 ||
iha - pūrvoktakṣityādinityatādiviṣayeṭa dṛṣṭānuguṇyavirahāt - darśanānusārābhāvāt, pratyakṣavirodhādityarthaḥ | āsāṃ kalpanānām - anumitīnāṃ, tarkāṇāṃ vā | asiddhiḥ - vyāptyabhāvādinā anutpattiḥ, aprāmāṇyaṃ vā | bhavati cet | kartaryapi - siṣādhayiṣite kartari ca | asiddhiḥ syāt - sattvādiśūnyatvākarmavaśyatvādiviṣayakānumityasiddhiḥsyāt | kiṃca, jagataḥ, janma utpattiḥ | krameṇa dadṛse dṛṣṭam | dṛśeḥ karmaṇi liṭ | jagata iti ghaṭādikaṃ vivakṣitam | kṣayo vā - layaśca | krameṇa dadṛśe | anayoḥ - jagajjanmakṣayayoḥ |
klṛptiḥ - anumitiḥ |
tathā syāt - kramaviṣayiṇī syāt | na (vā) smākaṃ tadabhimatamityarthaḥ || 14 ||
samanvayādhikaraṇaṃ prastauti (tattu samanvayāditi) | tatra siddharūpasya brahmaṇaḥ pravṛttinivṛttyanvayaviraheṇa bubhutsitagrāhakaśāstrapratipādyatvaṃ na saṃbhavatītyāśaṃkyasvataḥ puruṣārthabhūtasya anavadhikātiśayānandarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ śāstrapratipādyatvaṃ saṃbhavatīti samarthyate | tadāha - 
dhik kaumāriladarśanavyasaninaḥ prābhākaraprakriyāvikrantānapi hanta nāthavidhuraṃ viśvaṃ jagajjalpataḥ |
dūre kāraṇadoṣabādhakadhiyornāthe pumarthe svataḥ
prāmāṇyaṃ na kathaṃ bhajanti vidhivanmantrārthavādoktayaḥ || 15 ||
kaumāriladarśane - bhāṭṭaprakriyāyāṃ, vyasaninaḥ - durnirvāhārthanirvahaṇa pariśramaśālinaḥ | dhik - te nindyāḥ | prābhākaraprakriyāyāṃ, vikrāntānapi nirvighātagatīnapi | dhik - te pi nindyāḥ | kuta ityatrāha - viśvaṃ jagat, nāthavidhuraṃ - anātham | jalpataḥ - asaṃgatabhāṣaṇena bruvataḥ | pravṛttinivṛttiparatayaiva śāstrasya prāmāṇyādbrahmaṇaścātadrūpatvānna tasya śāstrapratipādyatvam; vedāntāstu karmasu kartṛbhūtātmapratipādanena karmavidhiśeṣatayaiva prāmāṇyaṃ bhajanta iti tairuktam | taddūṣati - dhigiti |
teṣāmubhayeṣāmapi abhyupagamāntareṣu mitho virodhe satyapi īśvarāpalāpe kathaṃ saṃvāda iti upālambho hanta ityanena vyajyate | kṣaṇabhaṅginaḥ karmaṇo bhāvisvargasādhanatvānupapattyā’pūrvaṃ kalpayantaḥ, kathamacetanasya tasya phalapradatvānupapattyā neśvaraṃ kalpayanti; śrutaṃ vā tadapalapanti; kathaṃ vā |
ātmasiddhyanukūlasya niyojyasya prasiddhaye |
kurvatsvargādikamapi pradhānaṃ kāryameva naḥ ||
ityuktarītyā prathamamananyārthatayā pratipannasya kāryasya tannirvāhāya svargādyarthatvaṃ vadantaḥ, sarvapradhānaṃ sarvaśeṣiṇaṃ sarvavedāntavedyaṃ jagannāthamapalapantīti, jalpata iti padena vyajyate |
siddhāntamāha - dūra iti | kāraṇadoṣasya - bhramapramādavipralambhātmakakāraṇadoṣasya, bādhakadhiyaḥ - nedamitibādhabuddheśca | dūre - viprakṛṣṭāḥ | mantrāḥ mantraṇādilakṣaṇalakṣitāḥ, mantratvena prasiddhā vā, arthavādāḥ - purākalpasarūpā bhūtārthaprati pādakāṃśāḥ, uktayaḥ - evaṃrūpavedāntavākyāni satyaṃ jñānaṃ sadeva somya ityādīni | vidhivat - vidhinātulyam | idaṃ pūrvottarānvayi | svataḥ pumarthe - puruṣārthasādhanatvena vināpi puruṣārthabhūte | nāthe - jagaccheṣiṇi | prāmāṇyaṃ kathaṃ na bhajanti | bhajantyevetyarthaḥ ||
tava gṛhe nidhirasti iti vākyasyevānavadhikātiśayānandarūpabrahmapratipādakasya vedāntasya puruṣārthaparatvātprāmāṇyaṃ nirbādham | vidherapi puruṣārthaparatvenaiva hi prāmāṇyam | na cārthasatyatvāniścayaḥ | nityanirdoṣāpauruṣeyatvasāmyādvidhivadeva
tanniścayāt | svataḥ pumartha iti pravṛttinivṛttivyāvṛttirucyate | puruṣārthasādhanatvena hi tayoḥ śāstraviṣayatvam | puruṣārtharūpatayā tu śāstraviṣayatvaṃ kaimutikanyāyasiddham | kiṃca, vidhyapekṣitaphalatayā phalapradatvena ca brahmaṇaḥ śāstrapratipādyatvaṃ nirbādhamiti pumarthe nātha ityābhyāṃ sūcitam || 15 ||
evaṃ catussūtrī vyākhyātā | sā hi śāstrārambhasiddhyarthā | īkṣaternāśabdam ityādi ca śāstram | tatra īkṣatyadhikaraṇādyarthānāṃ bhāṣyādiṣu sphuṭataratvādasphuṭārthānāṃ keṣāṃcit sphuṭapratipattaye (tatra ślokāducyante) tatra ca īkṣatyadhikaraṇaguṇasūtre pratijñāvirodhāt ityatra viṣayabhūtasyādeśaśabdasya paroktamarthaṃ pratikṣipan svasiddhāntābhimatamāha dvābhyām - tatra paroktamarthamanuvadati prathamena - 
chāndogye kecidāhurdiśatirutatamādeśamaprākṣya ityatrāṅpūrvastūpadeśaṃ prakaṭayati sato na praśāstiṃ ghañantaḥ |
no kartaryasti karmaṇyagaṇighañihasatkartṛśāstau na karma
syātsyātkarmopadeśe tadidamuditamādeśavācopadeśyam || 16 ||
kecidāhuḥ - mṛṣāvādino vadanti | kimityatrāha - chondogya ityādi | chāndogye - chandogānāmāmnāye | chandogaukthika ityādinā ñyapratyayaḥ | uta tamādeśamaprākṣya ityatra, āṅpūrvaḥ, ghañantastu ghañpratyayāntaśca | diśatiḥ dhātuḥ | sataḥ sadvidyāsthasacchabdavācyabrahmaṇaḥ | upadeśaṃ - tatkarmakamajñātajñāpanam | prakaṭayati - pratipādayati | praśāstiṃ - praśāsanam | bāhulakācchāseśśāstiḥ rājadaṇḍa iti vaiyākaraṇāḥ | yamo vaivasvato rājā iti bhagavanniyamanamapi hi rājadaṇḍaḥ | na prakaṭayati | kuta ityatrāha - ghañanta iti | idaṃ ca hetvarthagarbham | tadāha - no kartarīti | ghañpratyayaḥ kartari nāsti akartari ca ityukteḥ | ghañkarmaṇyagaṇi - karmārthe vihitaḥ kāraka ityukteḥ | yadyapi saṃjñāyāmityukteḥ karmaṇyasaṃjñāyāṃ ghañdurlabhaḥ; tathāpi bahulagrahaṇādasaṃjñāyāmapi ghañ sulabhaḥ | ko bhavatā lābho labdhaḥ | aparaṃ lābhaṃ svābhidheyāpekṣāvidhiniyamassaṃbandhamanuvartiṣyate ekaḥ pākaḥ puṭapākapratīkāśa ityādiprayogāt | iha - buddhisthabhavatpakṣe | sat - sacchabdavācyaṃ brahma | śāstau - śāsane | kartṛsyāt - 
kartṛtvārham | karma na syāt - karmatvānarham | na tasyeśe kutaścana iti śruterityarthaḥ | tat - tasmāt | āṅupasṛṣṭadiśate upadeśe’pi prayogāt, ghañaḥ karmaṇi prayogaprācuryāt praśāsanārthakatve brahmaṇaḥ praśāsanakarmatvavirahāt, ghañpratyayasya ca kartari vidhānābhāvādupadeśyatvasya ca brahmaṇyupapatteścetyarthaḥ | idaṃ - sacchabdavācyaṃ brahma | ādeśavācā tamādeśam ityādeśaśabdena | upadeśyamuditaṃ - upadeśyatvenoktamiti kecidāhuriti pūrveṇānvayaḥ ||
tamimamarthaṃ pratikṣipan svābhimatārthamāha - 
atra brūmaḥ praśāstiṃ vadati diśirasāvāṅmukho nopadeśaṃ
śāstau so’tiprasiddho na hi para iha cāpekṣitārthaprasaṃgaḥ |
yukto’sādhāraṇoktyā ghañagaṇikaraṇe’pyatra vaivakṣikatvaṃ
śābdoktaṃ kārakāṇāṃ nanu karaṇatayā kartari syādvivakṣā || 17 ||
 (atretyādi) atra brūmaḥ - asmin pūrvapakṣe siddhāntaṃ brūmaḥ | kimityatrāha praśāstimityādi | āṅmukhaḥ - āṅpūrvakaḥ | asau diśiḥ - diśidhātuḥ | praśāstiṃ - praśāsanam, ājñām | vadati - pratipādayati | āsāvāṅpūrvo diśidhātuḥ, upadeśaṃ na vadati - upadeśaṃ na bodhayati | kuta ityatrāha - śāstau so’tiprasiddha iti | sa āṅpūrvo diśiḥ praśāsane atiprasiddha ityarthaḥ | āṅpūrvo diśiḥ niyoktṛprayojanavacanaḥ, upapūrvastu niyojyaprayojanavacanaḥ iti nyāsakāroktiḥ | ādiṣṭo’smi adiṣṭā raghupuṅgavena ityādiprayogāccetyāśayaḥ | na hi para iti | pare - upadeśe saḥ - ādeśaśabdaḥ, na hi prasiddhaḥ | (tasyopadeśatvaprasiddhiḥ prasiddhetyarthaḥ) eṣa ādeśa eṣa upadeśaḥ iti
pṛthaṅnardeśāditi bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca iha - praśāsitṛtvārthakatve | apekṣitārthaprasaṃgaḥ - ekavijñānena sarva - vijñānapratijñāsiddhyarthaṃ nimittāntaravyudāsāyopādānasyaiva nimittatvābhidhānam apekṣitamityapekṣitārthasiddhirbhavati |
asādhāraṇasya - paramātmāsādhāraṇasya praśāsitṛtvasya, uktyā - pratipādanenāyamādeśaśabdaḥ praśāsane yukto bhavati | praṣṭavyatvoktayaivārthādupadeśyatvaṃ siddhamiti taduktiranapekṣitakathanaṃ syāt; saṃsāranivṛttibrahmatadupāsanānāmupadeśyatvaṃ sādhāraṇamiti taduktissādhāraṇoktiścasyāditi bhāvaḥ | karaṇe’pi, ghañ - ghañpratyayaḥ | agaṇi - vihitaḥ | upadeśe’janunāsikaḥ ityatra halaśca ityasaṃjñāyāmapi bahulagrahaṇātkaraṇe ghañ iti mahābhāṣyokteriti bhāvaḥ | kārakāṇāṃ - karmādīnām | vaivakṣikatvaṃ - vivakṣādhīnatattatkārakabhāvam | śābdaiḥ - vaiyākaraṇaiḥ, uktam | vivakṣātaḥkārakavibhaktayo nānābhavanti iti tairukteriti bhāvaḥ | nanvi tyavadhāraṇe | kartari karaṇatayā vivakṣā kāraṇatvaprakārakabodhanecchā | syādeva | akartarīti paryudāsastukartṛtvaprakārakabodhatātparye ghajpratyayasya asādhutvabodhanārtha iti bhāvaḥ ||
tadāhurvyāsapādāḥ upadeśapakṣe prakṛtyarthāsvārasyaṃ pratyayasvārasyaṃ ca | praśāsanapakṣetu pratyayāsvārasyaṃ pradhānabhūtaprakṛtyarthasvārasyamarthaucityaṃ ceti vaiṣamyam; ataḥ praśāsitaivādeśaśabdābhidheya iti svīkartuṃ yukta iti | atra vastutaḥ kartaryeva kartṛtvasya sādhakatamatvarūpakaraṇatvasya ca sattvāt kartṛtvāvivakṣayā karaṇatvamātravivakṣāyāṃ tatpratyayaḥ sulabhaḥ | yathā ghaṭādau ghaṭatvadravyatvādeḥ vivakṣānusāreṇa ghaṭādipadaprayogaḥ tadvaditi, vivakṣātaḥ kārakavibhaktayaḥ ityatrāropaprasaktyabhāvānnātra tatparihārāya ghañaḥ kartari lakṣaṇā | karaṇādeḥ kartṛtvavivakṣāpi nāroparūpeti samabhivyāhṛtatatkriyākārakāntarānadhīnatatkriyānukūlavyāpāravattvaṃ svātantryamiti pariṣkurvadbhistāntrikairuktam | na caivaṃ pratyayāsvārasyābhyupagamavirodha iti vācyam | praśāsanopadeśayoḥ prayoge satyapi prasiddhiprācuryāt praśāsane svārasyaṃ tadvirahāccopadeśe prakṛterasvārasyamitivat ghañaḥ karmakaraṇayoḥ prayoge’pi karmaṇi prasiddhiprācuryātsvārasyaṃ, karaṇe tu tadvirahādasvārasyamityadoṣāt | na capraśāsanakaraṇatvasya paramātmatadjñānasādhāraṇyānnāsādhāraṇyamiti vācyam | tathāpyupadeśyatvāpekṣayā syāsādhāraṇyāt karaṇāntaranairapekṣyeṇa praśāsanakartṛtve sati praśāsanakaraṇatvasyāsādhāraṇyākṣateśca | yadyapi karmaṇyapi ghañdurlabhaḥ tadvidhau saṃjñāyām imityukteḥ; tathā copadeśapakṣe’pi
pratyayasvārasyābhāvastulyaḥ; tathāpi prasiddhiprācuryatadvirahābhyāṃ svārasyāsvārasye nirbādhe | na caivaṃ bāhulakātkartaryeva ghañāśrīyatāmiti vācyam | karmakaraṇayoḥ saṃjñāyāmapi bahulagrahaṇāt, ghañupapādanavat kartari ghaño bāhulakatayā mahābhāṣyakārairanupapādanāt | na ca kārakāntarāṇāṃ kartṛtvavivakṣocitā, na tu kartuḥ kārakāntaravivakṣeti vācyam | ātmānamātmanā vetsi sṛjasyātmānamātmanā ityādau kartuḥ karaṇatvavivakṣetyuktatvāt, lūyate kedāraḥ svayam ityādau karmakarturapi karaṇatvavivakṣākathanāt, preṣite ca kilāyaṃ kriyāmakriyāṃ ca dṛṣṭvā iti bhāṣyavyākhyāne kartuṃradhikaraṇatvavivakṣayā saptamīti kaiyaṭenoktatvācca ||17 ||
ānandamayādhikaraṇe pucchabrahmaṇaḥ prādhānyaṃ paroktamanuvadati - 
ṣaṣṭhyānyatvamavedi nainamavadacchaloko na cājñātatā
jñātasyāsya tathāpi nāvayavitā tasyānya ātmā śrutaḥ |
śodhyatvaṃ ca vikāravācakavacastadbrahmapucchaṃ bhavennatvānandamayaḥ sa cānnamayavat kośo bahiḥ kathyate || 18 ||
asya - ānandamayasya | ṣaṣṭhyā - tasya puccham iti ṣaṣṭhyā | anyatvaṃ - brahmānyatvam | avedi - śrotṛbhirajñāyi, vyatirekaṣaṣṭhīsvārasyāditi bhāvaḥ | enaṃ - pūrvaṃ brahmānyatva (pratiyogitve) nopāttamimamānandamayam |
ślokaḥ - asanneva sa bhavati iti brahmaviṣayaślokaḥ | nāvadat - na pratipādayāmāsa, api tu pucchatayoktaṃ brahmaivāvadadityarthaḥ | jñātasya - priyamodādirūpeṇa lokaviditasyānandamayasya, asya | ajñātatā - (sadbhāvāsadbhāva - jñānāṃśakau prayojakamajñātatvaṃ na - na bhavatītyarthaḥ | tathāpi - yathākathaṃcidajñātatvopapādane’pi | tasya - niravayavasyaparamātmanaḥ | avayavitā - śiraḥpucchādyavayavavattā | na - na bhavatītyarthaḥ | tathā ca jñāto’vayavī ca ānandamayo na paramātmetyarthaḥ | tasya ānandamayasya anya ātmā śrutaḥ - tasyaiṣa eva śārīra ātmā ityātmāntaraṃ śrutam | paramātmano’pyā (hyā)tmāntarāsaṃbhavādātmāntaravānānandamayo na paramātmetyarthaḥ | asya - ānandamayasya | śodhyatvaṃ śrutaṃ - ānandamayo me śudhyantām iti vākyāvagatamityarthaḥ | śruta ityanuṣaktasya liṅgavipariṇāmaḥ | nityaśuddhe paramātmani śodhyatvānupapatteḥ, śodhyatvena pratīta ānandamayo na paramātmetyarthaḥ |
asyānandamayasya, vikāravācakavacaḥ - vikāratvabodhakamayaṭ
pratyayarūpaśabdaḥ | śrutamityanuṣajyate | paramātmano vikāratvānupapattervikāratayā śruto na paramātmetyarthaḥ | yata evaṃ tataḥ, pucchaṃ brahma tadbhavet - paramātmatayā pratipādyatvārhaṃ bhavati | yadvā tat - tasmāt pucchaṃ brahma paramātmā bhavedityadhyāhāreṇayojanā | ānandamayastu brahma na bhavet - paramātmatvānarhamityarthaḥ | sa ca - ānandamayastu, annamayavat - aparamātmabhūtānnamayādivat | bahiḥkośaḥ - antaḥsthitapucchabrahmavastuni buddhyavataraṇahetubhūtabuddhiviṣayasaṃpuṭarūpatayā kathyate - śrūyate ||
tadāhuḥ - tasya priyamiti saṃbandhavācinyā ṣaṣṭhyā vyatirekapratīteḥ, brahmaviṣayasyoparitanaślokasyānandamayaviṣayatvābhāvena pucchatayoktabrahmaṇa eva pradhānatvāvagamāt, priyamodādirūpeṇa lokaviditasyānandamayasya sadbhāvāsadbhāvajñānāśaṅkānupapatteḥ, niravayavasya paramātmano’vayavitvānupapatteḥ, avikārasya vikāratvānupapatteḥ, tasyaiṣa eva śārīra ātmā ityātmaviṣayavākyāvagatasyātmāntarasya paramātmatayā ānandamayasya paramātmatvānupapatteḥ, annamayaprāṇamayamanomayavijñānamayānandamayā me śudhyantām iti
śrutasya śodhyatvasya nityaśuddhe paramātmani anupapateśca nānandamayaḥ paramātmā, api tu pucchatayā nirdiṣṭameva brahma paramātmeti pucchabrahmavādahetusaṃgrahaḥ iti || 18 ||
tadidaṃ dūṣayati - pum̐lliṅgetyādi
pum̐lliṅgāntapradhānaprakṛtaparavacorūpaṇaṃ cāpyananyaiḥ
ślokāḥ pucchaṃ na cāhuḥ svarasamapi ca naḥ sūtramanyo’ntaro na
ṣaṣṭhyādiścāṃśabhāvānniravadhikatayā rūpaṇādānurūpyāt
paryāyatvādananyātmakavacanatayā pucchahetuḥ parāstaḥ || 19 ||
pum̐lliṅgāntaṃ, pradhānaprakṛtaparaṃ, vacaḥ - so’kāmayata iti vākyasthaḥ sa iti śabdaḥ | pum̐lliṅgāntetyanena ānandamayaparatvamāvaśyamityabhipretam | pradhānetyanena sarvaparyāyasādhāraṇasya tasyaiṣa eva śārīra ātmā ityuktasya na pradhānavācinā sarvanāmnā parāmarśasaṃbhava ityabhipretam | prakṛtetyanena tasmādvā etasmādātmanaḥ ityatrātmaśabdanirdiṣṭasya vyavahitasyāvyavahitapūrvaparāmarśinā tena parāmarśāsaṃbhava ityabhipretam | tathā ca so’kāmayata ityānandamayasya kāraṇatvapratīteḥ paramātmatvasiddhirityarthaḥ | asmin prakaraṇe, ananyaiḥ - 
svasmādanatiriktaiḥ svāvayavaiḥ | rūpaṇaṃ - śiraḥpakṣādirūpaṇaṃ, dṛśyata iti śeṣaḥ | tathā ca puccharūpāvayavatvena rūpitādbrahmaṇo’vayavitayā rūpitasyānandamayasyānatirekasiddhiriti ānandamayasya parabrahmatvasiddhirityarthaḥ | asmin prakaraṇe, ślokāḥ - tattatparyāyasthāḥ annādvai prajāḥ ityādiślokāḥ | pucchaṃ nāhuḥ, api tu pucchavantamāhuriti prakṛtaśloko’pi pucchavadānandamayaparaḥ; na tu pucchamātrapara ityarthaḥ | anena, pūrvapakṣiṇā nainamavadacchlokaḥ ityuktasya hetoḥ svarūpāsiddhirabhipretā | rūpaṇaṃ cāpyananyairiti tu avayavitayā rūpaṇamātraṃ na vastuto’vayavitvam | anyathā brahmaṇo’vayavatvaṃ prasajyetetyavayavitetyuktahetoḥ svarūpāsiddhirabhipretā | apica sūtram - ānandamayo’bhyāsāt iti sūtraṃ, na svarasam | ānandamayasya paramātmatvaṃ vadatāmasmākaṃ pakṣe anupahatasvārasyakaṃ, pucchabrahmaṇaḥ paramātmatve tvasvarasamityarthaḥ tathā sati brahmapucchamabhyāsāt iti hi bhavitavyam | asmatpakṣe tu ānandamayo netaraḥ iti vā nāśabdam iti vā pratijñāvākyayojanayā ānandamayaḥ paramātmeti sidhyati | abhyāsāditi ca sannihitānandābhyāsādityarthakatayā svarasam | tvatpakṣe tu ānandamayaśabdasya lakṣaṇayānandamayavākyaśrutapucchabrahmaparatvaṃ vaktavyamityasvārasyaṃ spaṣṭam | kiṃca, anyaḥ antaro na - tasmādvā etasmādanyo’ntara ātmā manomayaḥ ityādivadasyānandamayasyāntarātmāntaraṃ na śrutamityarthaḥ | antarātmaparamparopadeśasyānandamaye paryavasānādānandamayaḥ paramātmetyabhipretam | ṣaṣṭhyādiḥ ityādiśabdenājñātatvaṃ, mayaṭśravaṇaṃ, avayavitvaṃ, śodhyatvaṃ, brahmapadam, tasyaiṣa eva śārīra ātmā iti śarīrātmaśravaṇamityeteṣāṃ samudāyo gṛhyate | tatra ca krameṇāṃśabhāvādityādikamupapādakam | tasya pucchamiti ṣaṣṭhī, aṃśabhāvāt - avayavatvāt | avayavāvayavībhāvaḥ ṣaṣṭhyartha iti bhāvaḥ | ananyairityanatirekaḥ pūrvamuktaḥ | ajñātatvaṃ niravadhikatayā | priyādirūpeṇa jñātasyāparicchinnānandatvenājñātatvamapyupapannamiti bhāvaḥ | mayaṭśravaṇaṃ ca niravadhikatayā | prācuryārthe
mayaḍvidhānādvikāraśravaṇaheturasiddha iti bhāvaḥ | jñātatvahetorapyasiddhirniravadhikatayeti sūcitā | avayavitvaṃ rūpaṇāt | na vastuta ityarthaḥ | avayavitvaheturasiddha iti bhāvaḥ | anyathā brahmaṇaḥ pucchatvaprasaṃgaḥ | na ca pucchapadenādhāralakṣaṇā | rūpaprakaraṇavirodhāditi hṛdayam | śodhyatvaṃ ānurūpyāt |
ānandamayasya jīvatvapakṣe saṃsāranirharaṇavat tasya paramātmatvapakṣe jīvāparādhajanitakāluṣyanivṛttireva (prapadanādhīnasya) anurūpaṃ śodhyatvamityarthaḥ |
anenānandamayasyāparamātmatvasādhakaparoktaśodhyatvahetorasiddhirabhipretā | paryāyatvātbra hmapadaśravaṇam ānando brahmeti vyajānāt ānandamayamātmānam ityādisthānapramāṇenānandamayātmabrahmaśabdānāmaikārthye niścate sati, ānandamayaparyāyaśloke asanneva sa bhavati asadbrahma, iti brahmaśabdaśravaṇamānandamayaviṣaye, nānupapannamityarthaḥ | anena pūrvoktahetvasiddhirddaḍhīkṛtā | ananyātmakavacanatayā śārīrātmaśravaṇam | (prakārāntarā) tmopadeśaparamparāyā asminnānandamaye anyo’ntara ityuktyabhāvāt parisamāptyavagamena, tasyaiṣa eva iti vākyamananyātmakatvaparamityarthaḥ | sve mahimni pratiṣṭhitaḥ ātmeśvaram itivat | anenātmāntaravattvarūpahetvasiddhirabhipretā | yata evaṃ tataḥ, pucchahetuḥ - pūrvapakṣoktapucchabrahmatvahetuḥ, ānandamayasyābrahmatvaheturiti yāvat | jātāvekatvam, ṣaṣṭhyānyatvamavedi, ityādyuktahetava ityarthaḥ | parāstaḥ - siddhāntasādhakabalavaddhetubhiḥ svarūpāsiddhyā ca dūṣitā ityarthaḥ || 19 ||
antarādityadhikaraṇe ya eṣo’ntarāditye hiraṇmayaḥ puruṣo dṛśyate ityādyantarāditya (vidyāyām) viṣayavākye tasya yathā kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamevamakṣiṇī iti śrūyate | asya vākyasya ṣaḍarthā vṛttikṛtoktāḥ tānāha ṣḍibhiḥ ślokaiḥ - 
kapistvādityaḥ kaṃ pibati kiraṇairityapi kapi
rbabhastītyāmnātaḥ sa kapiramunāstaṃ yadiha tat |
pratīmaḥ kapyāsaṃ divasakaratejovikasitaḥ
sa padmaśrīmattvādagaṇi bhagavaccaśrurupamā || 20 ||
kaṃ pibati kiraṇairiti kapirādityaḥ - kaṃ jalaṃ pibatīti kapiriti sūryaḥ kapiśabdenocyata ityarthaḥ | śrautaprayogamāha - apītyādinā | sa - ādityaḥ | kapiḥ babhastīti - kapirbabhasti tejanam iti śrutyā | kapirāmnātaḥ - kapitvenābhyastaḥ, adhītaḥ | apissamuccaye | tena kapayo’rke bhavānarāḥ iti nānārtharalamālā gṛhyate | iha - asmin kapiśabdārthe sati, yadamunā - ādityena | astaṃ - vikasitam | asukṣepaṇe | karmaṇi ktaḥ | tat - karma | kapyāsaṃ - kapyāsaśabdavācyam | pratīmaḥ - niścinumaḥ | asyate kṣipyate ityāsam | karmaṇi ghañ | ghañabantaṃ puṃsīti prāyikam ityukteḥ klībatvam | arthamāha - 
divaseti | saḥ - kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkam ityuktaḥ | divasakaratejasā - sūryakiraṇaiḥ, vikasitaḥ padmaḥ - kamalaṃ vā puṃsi padmaṃ nalinam ityukteḥ puṃstvam | śrīmattvāt - śobhātiśayāddhetoḥ | bhagavaccakṣuṣoḥ - puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ iti prasiddhabhagavannetrayoḥ, upamā - upamānam | agaṇi - jñāpita ityarthaḥ | kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ yathā evaṃ tasyākṣiṇī iti yojanā | divasakarakiraṇavikasitapuṇḍarīkasadṛśe tasya netre ityarthaḥ | atra, kapistvādityaḥ ityādityaśabdanirdeśena, śrīmattvāt iti nirdeśena ca ādityakṣiptaṃ vā śrīmattvāt iti vṛttikāravacanaṃ smāritam | kapistvādityaḥ iti tu śabdena, asya vakṣyamāṇārthapañcakavyāvṛttissūcitā || 20 ||
arthāntaramāha - 
nālaṃ kapirbhavati kaṃ pibatīti tasmin
yasyāsa āsanamuśanti manīṣiṇastat |
kapyāsamujjvalamudāramidaṃ sarojaṃ
cakṣurnidarśanamudīritamīśvarasya || 21 ||
kaṃ - jalaṃ, pibatīti kapiḥ nālaṃ bhavati | nālamathāstriyām ityukteḥ klībatvam | jalapānakaraṇasya
tatkartṛtvavivakṣā | yasya - buddhisthasya | tasmin nāle | āsaḥ - jāsanaṃ, upadeśo bhavati | tat - karma | manīṣiṇaḥ - paṇḍitāḥ | kapyāsamuśanti - kapyāsaśabdārthamicchanti | vaśakāntau, kāntiricchā | kapau nāle āsa upaveśo yasya tatkapyāsaṃ, vyadhikaraṇabahuvrīhiḥ | āsa upaveśana iti paṭhitāt bhāve ghañityabhiprāyaḥ | yadvā phalitārthakathanamidam | āste upaviśatītyāsam pacāderākṛtigaṇatvādacpratyayaḥ kapāvāsaṃ kapyāsam | saptamīti yogavibhāgāt samāsaḥ | idaṃ - 
kapyāsaśabdoktam | ata eva ujjvalaṃ - śobhātiśayayuktam | udāraṃ - sphītam | apacitādapi paṅkajānnālasthapuṇḍarīkasya śobhātiśayo’stīti so’tra vivakṣita iti vyāsapādāḥ | sarojaṃ padmam | ayaṃ ca śrutisthapuṇḍarīkaśabdārthaḥ | īśvarasya - puṇḍarīkākṣatvajñāpyanirupādhikaiśvaryaśālino bhagavataḥ | taṃ cārunetraṃ puruṣaṃ pradhānam ityukteḥ | cakṣurnidarśanaṃ - netrayorupamānam | udīritaṃ - upamānatvenoktaṃ, śrutyeti śeṣaḥ |
pūrvatrātra ca, yābhirādityastapati tābhiḥ parjanyo varṣati | gaṇḍūṣapeyamiva kāntyamṛtaṃ
pipāsurinduḥ prasāritamayūkhamṛṇālakāṇḍaḥ iti sūryanālayossalilapānakartṛtvaprasiddherarthaucityācca kaśabde upapade pibaterbāhulakaḥ kipratyayassamāśritaḥ | arthāntareṣvapi hi āsaśabde ghañpratyayasya bāhulakakatvamaviśiṣṭam, arthaucityaṃ tvatra viśeṣaḥ | yathā tirodhiśabdasya bāhulakakipratyayāntatvaṃ tathā kapiśabdasyāpi bāhulakakipratyayāntatvamityadoṣaḥ | bhagavadguṇadarpaṇe, svayamapi tadanubhavasukhaṃ pāti pibatīti vā | in sarvadhātubhyaḥ | āto lopaḥ | kapiravyayaḥ iti bhaṭṭaparāśarapādā anujagṛhuḥ | teṣāmayamāśayaḥ sarvadhātubhya iti sūtravihitasya inpratyayasya gāṅkuṭādisūtreṇa ṅitve’pi koṭirityatra guṇahetutvaṃ yathā bāhulakaṃ tathātrākiḍitto’pyasyāllopaprayojakatvaṃ bāhulakam | ata eva matvarthācca ityatra bāhulakatvādakāralope’pi matvarthīya ityuktamiti | yadyapi vaiyākaraṇaiḥ kuḍikampyornalopaśca ityatra kapiḥ syātsiṃhake śākhāmṛge ca madhusūdane iti kośo likhitaḥ | prāṇa ejati nissṛtam kampanāt iti ca śrutisūtrasvārasyāt kampayitṛtvaṃ paramātmano’vagatam | tathā ca bhagavati kapiśabdaḥ kampayitṛtvanimittaka iti vaktumucitam; tathāpi sahasranāmādhyāye pūrvāparanāmasvārasyaparyālocanayā sukhapātṛtvanimittakatvameva, kapirityasyocitam, na hi vaiyākaraṇānāmupāyeṣvāgrahaḥ | upeyapratipattyarthā upāyā avyavasthitāḥ, iti tairevoktatvādityācāryāṇāmabhiprāyaḥ | evameva kapyāsamityatra | pyallopasiddhyā sarvadhātubhya in itīn pratyaya evāśrayituṃ yuktamiti dhyeyam | nanu uṇādivyutpannānāṃ saṃjñātvaniyamāt kapiśabdasyāditye bhagavati ca śrutisahastra, nāmaprayogeṇa jalasukhapātṛtvanimittatayā vṛttisaṃbhave’pi nāle kvāpi tasyāprayogeṇa uṇādisūtreṇa tadvyutpādanaṃ na saṃgacchata iti cenna | uṇādayo bahulam ityatra saṃjñāgrahaṇānuvṛttāvapi kvaciduṇādipratyayavidhau punassaṃjñāgrahaṇadarśanena auṇādikānāṃ pratyayāpāṃ kvacidasaṃjñāyāmapyanumānenādoṣāt || 21 ||
arthāntaramāha - kamityādi
kamudakamiha pyāso yasyāsanaṃ yadi vodbhavaḥ
kavibhiruditaṃ kapyāsaṃ tadgabhīrajalodayāt |
śiśiramadhuraṃ śrīmaddāmodarākṣi nidarśanaṃ
bhavati nalinaṃ luptākārastvapiḥ pihitādivat || 22 ||
(kam, udakaṃ bhavati) kaṃ śiro’mbunoḥ iti kośāt | iha - udake pyāsaḥ āsanaṃ sthitiḥ yasya tat| yadi vā - athavā, udbhavaḥ
utpattiḥ yasya tat | kapyāsaṃ - kapyāsapadavācyam | kavibhiḥ - krāntadarśibhiḥ | uditaṃ - uktam | kepyāso yasya tat kapyāsamiti vigrahe, vyadhikaraṇabahuvrīhiriti bhāvaḥ | itthaṃ vyutpattiḥ darśitā | atha vākyārthamāha - gabhīretyadinā | gabhirajalodayāt - nimnatamajale, utpatterhetoḥ | idaṃ sthiterupalakṣaṇam | śiśiramadhuraṃ - śītalasundaram upameye’pi tatsiddheḥ | tāpatrayāturāṇamupāsakānāṃ sakalasantāpaharaṃ bhagavannetramityarthaḥ | śrīmat - śobhātiśayayuktam | atiśāyane matup | nalinaṃ, dāmodarasya - lokatrayāśrayasya, yaśodayā baddhasya ca bhagavataḥ akṣṇoḥ - aiśvaryavātsalya raktayornetrayoḥ, nidarśanaṃ - upamānam | bhavati | nanu ko’yaṃ pyāso nāmetyatrāha - lupteti | api rupasargaḥ | apyupasṛṣṭe āsadhātau apyāṅupasṛṣṭe asadhātau vā pacādyaci upadeśārthaka utpattyarthako vā apyāsaśabdaḥ | vaṣṭi vāgurirallopam iti aperakāralope pyāsaśabdaḥ sādhuriti bhāvaḥṭa(tadupasṛṣṭenāsinā apyāsa iti nippannena | utpattirucyate | apiśabdasyatvakāro vaṣṭi vāgurirallopamavāpyorupasargayoḥ ityanuśāsanena luptaḥ | pihitaśabde
pidhānaśabde ca yathākāralopastathetyarthaḥ | abrahmapyācāryāt ityāpastambaprayogo’pyādiśabdābhipretaḥ | abrahmapi abrahmāpi pararūpaṃ katantavat; apervākāralopaḥ ityujjvalā |
udakotpattyuktāvapi sāmarthyādgabhīratvalābhaḥ ananodakasthitivacanena sthalakamalavyāvṛttyā śiśiratvādilābhaḥ | atra yasyāsanaṃ yadi vodbhava ityarthadvayamāsapadasyoktam; tatra yadyapi śrutaprakāśikāyāṃ, kaṃ jalam āsa upaveśana iti dhāturapipūrvaka ityuktam; tathāpi vedārthasaṃgrahe bhagavatā gambhīrāmbharamamudbhutetyabhihitam; tadvyākhyāyāṃ ca vyāsapādaiḥ
kam jalam asabhuvi apipūrvaka ityuktamiti pakṣadvayābhiprāyeṇa arthadvayoktiriti dhyeyam |
nanu ārdhadhātuke ityadhikṛtya asterbhūḥ iti sūtreṇārdhadhātukeparato’sterbhvādeśavidhānāt kapyāsamityatrāsterghañi pacādyaci vā parato bhūityadeśāpattyā kapibhāvamiti syāt, na tu kapyāsamiti rūpamiti cet, - na asterbhūḥ bruvo vaciḥ cakṣiḍaḥ khyāñ iti prakaraṇe, bahulaṃ saṃjñācchandasoḥ iti vaktavyamannavadhakagātravicakṣaṇājirādyartham iti vārtikavyākhyāne, (sarvaprakaraṇāpekṣametaducyate) annaśabde jagdhyabhāvaḥ, vadhaka iti ṇvuli vadhādeśaḥ gātramiti iṇa auṇādikeṣṭṛnigādeśaḥ, vicakṣaṇe khyāñabhāvaḥ, ajireti bhāvābhāvaḥ
iti kaiyaṭagranthe sarvaprakaraṇāpekṣametaducyata ityukteḥ | vārtike’jirādyārthamiti ādiśabdācca tatprakaraṇasthayoḥ astibrūño ścchandasi bhūbhāvavacyādeśayorabhāvena, brāhmaṇo bravaṇādityasya, prakṛte kapyāsamityasya ca siddheravighātādityācāryāṇāmabhiprāyāt | atra ca bhagavaccakṣurupamā - ityatra bhagavacchabdena bhagaśrīrityaiśvaryokteḥ, īśvarasya cakṣurnidarśanami tyatra aiśvaryokteśva prāyapāṭhanyāyena dāmodaretyatrāpi dāmāni lokanāmāni tāni yasyodarāntare | tena dāmodaro devaḥ iti nirvacanānusāreṇa aiśvaryavivakṣocitā | śrutaṃ cāsyāmapi vidyāyāṃ puṇḍarīkākṣasya sarvalokakāmeśatvamiti dhyeyam || 22 ||
evaṃ siddhāntābhimatāstrayo’rthā uktāḥ | athārthasvabhāvādeva pūrvapakṣatayā niścitāstra yo’rthā upanyasya nirākriyante - kapyāsamiti
kapyāsaṃ bhānubimbaṃ haribhajanapadaṃ hṛtsarojaṃ ca yadva
ttadvaccopāsakākṣidvayamiti tu na pattatra yatpuṇḍarīkam |
gauṇaṃ tasyākṣiṇī ityubhayapadanirākāṅakṣatānādareṇāpyadhyāhāraḥ samānādhikṛtapadagatityaktirūrdhvaṃ taduktiḥ || 23 ||
kaṃ pibatīti kapiḥ - sūryaḥ, tasya āsaṃ - āsanamadhikaraṇam | bahulagrahaṇādadhikaraṇe ghañ | tatkimityākāṅkṣāyāmāha - bhānubimbamiti | sūryamaṇḍalamityarthaḥ | atra puṇḍarīkamityādiḥ | tathāca kapyāsapadavācyamādityamaṇḍalaṃ, puṇḍarīkapadābhipretaṃ hṛtsarojaṃ - ca yadvat - yathā haribhajanapadaṃ - paramātmopāsanasthānam | tadvat tathā | upāsakasyākṣidvayam upāsanasthānamityayameko’rthaḥ | ayaṃ nopapadyata ityāha - iti tu na sa diti | ayamartho’tyantāsamīcīna ityarthaḥ | kuta ityatrāha - yadityādi | yat - yasmāt | puṇḍarīkaṃ puṇḍarīkapadam | tatra - pūrvanirdiṣṭehṛtsaroje | gauṇam - ākārasāmyarūpaguṇasāmyādāgatam; syāditi śeṣaḥ | lakṣaṇāpekṣayāpi gauṇīvṛttirjaghanyeti hi yūpasya svarūpaṃ karotītyatra nirṇītam | dūṣaṇāntaramāha - tasyetyādinā | tasyākṣiṇī ityanayoḥ padayo ravayavāvayavibhāvena mitho’nvitārthakayoritarapada nirākāṅkṣatā pratīyate | tāmanādṛtyādhyāhṛtābhyāmanvayābhyupagama iti śeṣaḥ | tasyetyasyādhyāhṛtopāsanasthānamityanenānvayaḥ | akṣiṇī ityanena cādhyāhṛtasyopāsakapadasyānvaya ityabhyupagamādityarthaḥ | śrutaprahāṇamaśrutakalpanaṃ ceti bhāvaḥ |
dūṣaṇāntaramapyāha - adhyāhāra iti, syāditi śeṣaḥ | anadhyāhāreṇopapattau adhyāhārakalpanaṃ doṣa iti bhāvaḥ | apiśabdo dūṣaṇāntarasamuccāyakaḥ | tathāhi - tasyetyupāsakaparatve satyaprakṛtaparāmarśitvāpattiḥ, anantaravākyasthaṣaṣṭhyantaśabdavirūpatāpattiḥ, divyāṅgavarṇanaprakaraṇarītibhaṅgāpattiḥ, ( yaścāyaṃ dakṣiṇe’kṣiṇī iti) yaścāyaṃ dakṣiṇe’kṣin itiṭadakṣiṇasyākṣṇa upāsanasthānatvaśravaṇādakṣiṇī iti dvivacanavirodhāpattiḥ | antarāditye ityādityamaṇḍalasya sthānatayā vihitatvādevamakṣiṇī ityakṣṇa sthānatve vihite sati sthānabhedena rūpabhedāt vidyābhedena vākyabhedāpattirityeteṣāmanuktānāmapiśabdassamuccāyaka iti dhyeyam | dūṣaṇāntaramāha - samāneti | adhikṛtam adhikaraṇam
| adhipūrvaḥ karotirādheyatve vartate | tasmādadhikaraṇārthe - kto’dhikaraṇe ca dhrauvyagatipratyavasānārthebhyaḥ iti ktapratyayaḥ samānam - abhinnam | adhikṛtaṃ - adhikaraṇaṃ, viśeṣyaṃ yayostathoktayoḥ padayoḥ kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamiti padayoḥ, yā gatiḥ - ca śabdādyabhāvenānapoditasāmānādhikaraṇyaprāptiḥ tasyāḥ, | tyaktiḥ - vaiyadhikaraṇyāśrayaṇena tyāgaḥ, syāditi śeṣaḥ | saṃbhavati sāmānādhikaraṇye vaiyadhikaraṇyamanyāyyamiti bhāvaḥ | dūṣaṇāntaramāha - urdhvamiti | atra rūpakathanādityādiḥ | rūpakathanādūrdhvam - uparitanasya buddhisthasya nāmnaḥ | uktiḥ - kathanam | uciteti śeṣaḥ nāmavidhivākyāt pūrvaṃ rūpakathanaṃ hyucitam | sthānavidhiparatve tvasya vākyasyaucityabhaṅga iti bhāvaḥ | atredaṃ tattvam - ūrdhvaṃ taduktiḥ - kapyāsamityādyetadvākyādūrdhvam upāsakākṣṇo haribhajanapadatvoktirityarthaḥ | nātastacchabdasya prakṛtārthaparatvahāniriti spaṣṭaṃ antarādityādhikaraṇaśrutaprakāśikāpariśramaśālinām | tatra hi ūrdhvamakṣividyāyā uktatvādatra taduktyanapekṣaṇādasya punaruktiśca ityanugṛhītam | yadyapi pūrvoktadūṣaṇamapi śrutaprakāśikārūḍhameva; tathāpyūrdhvaṃ taduktirityasya paunaruktyodbhāvana eva tātparyamityatrordhvoktiśabdābhyāṃ labhyata iti | tasmāditi tu na saditi pūrveṇānvayaḥ || 23 ||
arthāntaramupanyasya nirākaroti - 
kaścijjalpati markaṭasyajaghanaṃ kapyāsamakṣṇorayaṃ
dṛṣṭāntaḥ kathamiṣyate bhagavataḥsatyeva gatyantare |
sāmānādhikaraṇyamatra padayoḥ prāptaṃkutastyajyate
padmaṃ cedviśinaṣṭi tatsadṛśi tacchabdastu gauṇastava || 24 ||
kaścit - nāmagrahaṇānarhaḥ, pāpīyān | markaṭasya jaghanaṃ - vānarapṛṣṭhaṃ, karma | kapyāsaṃ jalpati - kapyāsapadavācyamāha | kapirmarkaṭaḥ tasyāsa āsyate upaviśyate’neneti vyutpattyā jaghanapradeśa ucyata iti kaścijjalpatītyarthaḥ | jaghanaṃ kaminīśroṇīpurobhāge kaṭisthale iti kośavaśāt jaghanaśabdaḥ puṣṭhe prayujyate | jalpatītyanena avācyaṃ vadato jihvā kathaṃ tava na śīryate ityetadabhipretam | taddūṣayati - 
akṣṇorityādinā | gatyantare - divaptakaratejovikasitamityādyuktaprakārāntare | satyeva, bhagavataḥ - heyapratibhaṭasya | akṣṇorayaṃ, dṛṣṭāntaḥ markaṭapṛṣṭharūpamupamānam | kathamiṣyate heyapratibhaṭabhagavaddivyanetrayormarkaṭapṛṣṭhaṃ nihīnopamānamityarthaḥ | atra - vākye | padayoḥ - kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamityanayoḥ | prāptaṃ - vibhinnavibhaktirāhityena svasvabhāvena prāptam | caśabdādyabhāvenānapoditaṃ ca | sāmānādhikaraṇyam - anekaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaikārthabodhakatvam | kutastyajyate kena hetunā upekṣyate | tacchabdaḥ - kapyāsaśabdaḥ | padmaṃ - puṇḍarīkapadavācyam | viśinaṣṭi cet - viśeṣayati cet | tacchabdaḥ tatsadṛśi markaṭapṛṣṭhasadṛśe gauṇastvatpakṣe syādityarthaḥ ||
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - markaṭapṛṣṭhaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ ca kimupamādvayam uta ekopamā | nādyaḥ - svataḥ prāptasāmānādhikaraṇyaparityāgaprasaṅgāt; ślāghyopamāsaṃbhave hīnopamāśrayaṇāyogācca | dvitīye, markaṭapṛṣṭhasadṛśapuṇḍarīkasadṛśe ityuktaṃ syāt | tadā kapyāsapadaṃ gauṇaṃ syāditi |
tuśabdaḥ śaṅkāntaraparihāraparaḥ | tathā hi - nanu prathamaśrutakapipadarūḍhyanusārāccaramaśrutasyāsapadasya gauṇatvamastviti cenna | āsapadamukhyārthaviśeṣaṇasamarpakasya kapipadasya vākyānanvayaprasaṅgāt | ekadeśānvayaprasaṃgāt | tātparyagrāhakamātratvaprasaṅgācca āsapade lakṣaṇāprasaṅgācca ||
nanu maṇḍapaṃ bhojaya ityatra maṇḍapānakartṛrūpayogārthāpekṣayā maṇḍapasthajane rūḍhipūrvikāyā lakṣaṇāyāḥ prābalyavadatrāpi nālasthityādiyogāpekṣayā markaṭapṛṣṭhasadṛśe rūḍhipūrvikāyāstasyā adoṣatvamiti cenna | yaugikāsapadasamabhivyāhāreṇa tannyāyasyātrāpravṛtteḥ | na hi maṇḍapaṃ bhojaya ityatra yaugikapadāntarasāhacaryamasti ||
nanu rūḍhiryogamapaharati iti nyāyena yogato nālādipratītyapekṣayā rūḍhyā markaṭapratītiḥ prabaleti cenna | kapipadavākyānanvayādirūpasya āsapade lakṣaṇāprasaṅgarūpasya ca rūḍhyarthavirodhiliṅgasya, puṇḍarīkasthityadhikaraṇatvarūpasya ca yogārthānukūlaliṅgasya sadbhāvenātra
rūḍheranunmeṣāt ||
nanu kapyāsapadaṃ markaṭajaghanamātraparam; tathā ca kapyāsaṃ yathā puṇḍarīkameva makṣiṇī iti yojanayā
markaṭapṛṣṭhasadṛśapuṇḍarīkasadṛśe iti labhyate | evaṃ ca na pūrvokta doṣaḥ | kiṃ tu rūḍhisaṃgraharūpaguṇaśceti cenna | yathaivaṃśabdayoḥ parasparapratinirdeśarūpatvasya yathā vai śyenaḥ yathaiṣikatūlam ityādau svarasato dṛṣṭasya bhaṅgaprasaṅgāt | evaṃśabdasya vākyārthapratigikasādṛśyabodhakatvena padārthapratiyogikasādṛśyābodhakatvāt | anyathā candra iva mukham itivat candra evaṃ mukhamityāpatteśceti || 24 ||
arthāntaramupanyasya nirākaroti - 
daravikasitaṃ kaścit kapyāsamāha gatistviyaṃ
bhavatu bhagavaccakṣurddaṣṭāntapaṅkajasaṅgatā |
tadapi ca na sattasminnarthe yato nanu kutracittadavayavaśaḥ saṃhatyā vā padaṃ vyudapadyata || 25 ||
kaścit daravikasitaṃ īṣadvikasitaṃ karma | kapyāsamāha - kapyāsaśabdārthamāha | bruvaḥ pañcānām iti ṇali āhādeśaḥ | (āheti padamuvācetyarthe vartata ityuktam | tadvā bhavatu) | atrākṣidṛṣṭāntapuṇḍarīkaviśeṣaṇatvamahimnārthaucityavaśena kapyāsaśabdasyeṣadvikasite rūḍhiśaktirgṛhyate | yathā loke kvacidapi avyutpannatve’pi yūpāhavanīyasphayakapālacaṣālacātvālādiśabdānāṃ vākyāntarapadāntarasamabhivyāhārāttattadarthaviṣayatayā vyutpattirgṛhyate tadvat | yadvā, prokṣaṇīśabdavadyogaśaktireva gṛhyate | īṣadarthe iti kādeśo’jādāvuttarapade’pi paratvādbhavatītyuktatvādīṣadapyāsamityatra īṣacchabdasya kādeśe kā apyāsamiti sthite, śakandhvāderākṛtigaṇatvāt pararūpaṃ, pṛṣodarāderākṛtigaṇatvādvā kāśabde’ntyalopa iti kapyāsaśabdasya yogaḥ saṃbhavatīti bhāvaḥ | atrārthaucityamabhyupagacchan śabdasyāvācakatvopapādanena dūṣayati - gatistvityādinā | tuśabdena pūrvoktānu citārthadvayavyāvṛttiḥ | bhagavaccakṣurddaṣṭāntapaṅkaje, saṃgatā upapannā | iyaṃ gatistu - ayaṃ prakārastu | bhavatu - prakṛtaucityādīṣadvikāso samādiṣṭa iti bhāvaḥ | evamabhyupagamārhāṃśo’bhyupagataḥ | atha dūṣyāṃśaṃ dūṣayati - tadapīti | api - tathāpi - tacca na sat - kapyāsaśabdasyeṣadvikāsavācakatvamasamīcīnam | cakāro dūṣitārthadvayasamuccayaparaḥ | kuta ityatrāha - yata iti | yataḥ - yasmāt | tatpadaṃ - 
kapyāsapadam | tasminnarthe - īṣadvikasite’rthe | avayavaśaḥ - avayavena | saṃhatyā vā - saṅghātena vā, samudāyena vā | kutracit - vaidikanighaṇṭau, laukikanighaṇṭau, vyākaraṇe vā | na vyudapadyata avyutpannamityarthaḥ | avayavena vyutpattiryogaśaktiḥ, samudāyena vyutpattistu rūḍhiśaktiḥ | avayaveva samudāyeneti tṛtīyā vaiśiṣṭyārthikā | vaiśiṣṭyaṃ cādhārādheyabhāvaḥ | tathā ca sphya diśabdavat vaidikanighaṇṭvādya (nanuśāsanā) na rūḍhiśaktiḥ | sati gatyantare śakandhvāditvaṃ pṛṣodarāditvaṃ cāgatikagatirūpamanucitamiti nāpi yogaśaktiriti bhāvaḥ | vyudapadyateti kartari laṅ | yato na vyudapadyate tato na sadityarthaḥ | śabdasya vācakatvābhāve prāmāṇike satyaucityamātramakiñcitkaramiti bhāvaḥ || 25 ||
evamukteṣu ṣaṭsvartheṣu ādyānāṃ trayāṇāṃ siddhāntatve’ntyanāṃ trayāṇāṃ pūrvapakṣatve ca hetumāha - 
ṣaṭsvartheṣu samīriteṣu caturāḥ kapyāsavācastrayo
gambhīrāmbha iti prakṛtya bhagavadrāmānujāṅgīkṛtāḥ |
tatsvīkārabahiṣkṛtāstaditare heyāstrayastadvidā
ślokaiḥ ṣaḍbhiramībhiritthamuditaḥ so’ya vibhāgo mayā || 26 ||
samīriteṣu - ukteṣu | ṣaṭsu kapyāsavāco’rtheṣu trayaḥ - kapistvādityaḥ nālaṃ kapiḥ kamudakam ityādiślokatrayoktāstrayo’rthoḥ |
gambhīrāmbha iti prakṛtya - prakramya, vedārthasaṅgrahe gambhīrāmbhassamudbhūta sumṛṣṭanālaravikaravikasitapuṇḍarīkadalāyatāmalekṣaṇaḥ ityanena vākyena, bhagavadrāmānujāṅgīkṛtā - etadvākyajñāpyabhagavadbhāṣyakārāṅgīkārāḥ, ata eva caturāḥ śobhamānāḥ, tadaṅgīkārajñāpyasauṣṭhavā ityarthaḥ | taditare trayaḥ - kapyāsaṃ bhānubimbam kaścijjalpati daravikasitam iti ślokatrayoktāstrayaḥ | teṣāṃ bhagava dbhāṣyakārāṇāṃ svīkārāda bhyupagamāt bahiṣkṛtāḥ - bahirbhūtāḥ, anuktijñāpitatadaṅgīkārāḥ | ataeva, heyāḥ - asamīcīnāḥ tadanaṅgīkārajñāpyadoṣā bhavantītyarthaḥ | tadvidā - sadācāryopadeśabalena bhāṣyābhiprāyavedinā | mayā itthamayaṃ sa vibhāgaḥ ;
ittham - uktaprakāreṇa | ayam - etacchlokadarśitaḥ | saḥ - saṃpradāyāgataḥ | vibhāgaḥ - siddhāntapūrvapakṣavivekaḥ | uditaḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ ślokairuktaḥ | ādyānāṃ prathamokteradūṣaṇāccasiddhāntatvam | antyānāmanantarokterdūṣaṇācca pūrvapakṣatvaṃ vyajyata iti (vibhāgo’tyuktiḥ) iti
bhāvaḥ |
atra kapayo’rke bhavā narāḥ iti kośasadbhāvāt, kapirbabhasti iti prayogasadbhāvāt, salilapānakartṛtvasya mukhyasya yogārthasya sadbhāvāt, bhagavaddi vyākṣyupamānatāyāṃ vikāsasyāntaraṅgatvāt, sūryakiraṇādhīnasyaiva ca tasya cārutaratvācca divasakaratejovikasitamityasya prathamamuktiḥ | avayavārthamātrasadbhāve’pi kośaprayogayorabhāvāt, avayavārthasyāpi karaṇatvarūpasyaiva sattvena kartari kṛt iti autsargikakartṛtvarūpasyābhāvāt, akṣyupamānatāyāṃ vikāsavannālasthiteḥ sākṣādupayogitvaviraheṇa mlānatānivṛttivyañjanenaivopayogitvācca nālaṃ kapirbhavati ityasyānantaroktiḥ | tadvadeva salilasthitestatra sākṣādupayogitvābhāvāt, (dhātvanatiriktārthakopasargasthāśrayaṇīyatvāt), tatrāpi śaktatāvacchedakānupūrvighaṭakayatkiṃcidvarṇalopasyābhyupagantavyatvācca kamudakamiha pyāsaḥ ityasya caramoktiḥ | ata eva pratīmaḥ kapyāsam iti prathamasya svīyatvoktiḥ, uśanti manīṣiṇaḥ kavibhiruditam ityantyayoḥ parakīyatvoktiśca | ata eva ca gambhīrāmbhaḥ ityādiśrīsūktau amātyāderantaraṅgatvatāratamyena narendrāsaktitāratamyavat, pradhānabhūtaviśeṣyasannidhitāratamyena ravikaretyādiviśeṣaṇānāmantaraṅgabahiraṅgabhāvo vyañjita iti dhyeyam | atra ca jātinaimittikaṃ yathāsthāna miti navamādhyāye nāvamikādhikaraṇe maudgaṃ caruṃ nirvapet śriyai śrīkāmaḥ ityāmnātāyām iṣṭau pauṇḍarīkāṇi barhīṣi bhavanti ityatra darmaiḥstṛṇīta haritaiḥ iti darbhapadasthāne puṇḍarīkapadaṃ haritapadasthāne raktapadamūhitavyamiti śābarabhāṣyokteḥ, puṇḍarīkaśabdasya raktāmbhoje’pi vṛtterniścayena, raktāntalocanaḥ kṣatajopamākṣa ityādibhirna virodhaḥ | kvacittu puṇḍarīkaviśālākṣau ityatra sitāmbhojaparatayā puṇḍarīkaśabdo vyākhyātaḥ, tadapyakṣimadhyapradeśābhiprāyakatayopapannam | evamubhayārthasādhāraṇyena vedārthasaṃgrahe puṇḍarīkapadameva nirdiṣṭaṃ bhagavatā | atrāpi tatsūcanāya sapadmaśrīmattvāt idaṃ sarojam bhavatī nalinam iti sāmānyapadāni nirdiṣṭānīti dhyeyam || 26 ||
īkṣatyadhikaraṇe pratijñāvirodhādityetadviṣayabhūtasyādeśaśabdasya parābhyupagatārthaḥ pratikṣiptaḥ chāndogye kecit ityādinā | ānandamayādhikaraṇe pucchabrahmaṇaḥ prādhānyaṃ pratikṣipyānandamayasya paramātmatvaṃ sādhitam ṣaṣṭyānyatvam ityādinā |
antarādityādhikaraṇaviṣayabhūtakapyāsaśruteḥ parābhyupagatārthaḥ pratikṣiptaḥ kapyāsaṃ bhānu ityādinā | atha jyotiradhikaraṇaguṇasūtrabhūte bhūtādipādavyapadeśopapatteścaivam ityatra viṣayabhūtasya gāyatrī vā idaṃ sarvam ityasya paroktārthapratikṣepeṇa svābhyupagatārtha ucyate - 
gāyatrī nāma sāmaśrutiśirasi paraṃ jyotiradhyāyi sidhya
ccātuṣpadyaṃ tu bhūtakṣititanuhṛdayaiḥ ṣaḍvidhaṃ tadvidhābhiḥ |
gānatrāṇādikābhiḥ śrutigatavacanavyaktibhedāttu siddhā
vākprāṇoktiḥ parārthā svarasanigamanaśrutyadhīnaṃ tadetat || 27 ||
atrāyaṃ śrutisandarbhaḥ - gāyatrī vā idaṃ sarvaṃ bhūtaṃ yadidaṃ kiṃca vāgvai gāyatrī vāgvā idaṃ sarvaṃ bhūtaṃ gāyati ca trāyate ca | yā vai sā gāyatrī iyaṃ vāva sā yeyaṃ pṛthivī asyāṃ hīdaṃ sarvaṃ bhūtaṃ pratiṣṭhitam | etāmeva nātiśīyate | yā vai sā pṛthivī iyaṃ vāva sā yadidamasmin puruṣe śarīramasmin hīme prāṇāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ | etadeva nātiśīyante | yadvai tatpuruṣe śarīram idaṃ vāva tadyadidamasminnantaḥ puruṣe hṛdayamasmin hīme prāṇāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ | etadeva nātiśīyante | saiṣā catuṣpadā ṣaḍvidhā gāyatrī iti |
asyāyamarthaḥ - gāyatrī vā idaṃ sarve bhūtamiti | gāyatrīśabdaḥ - catuṣpādbrahmaparaḥ | sarvabhūtaśabda ātmaparaḥ | ātmavargasya tatpādatvaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyenoktam | tasyaiva brahmaṇo gānatrāṇādibhiḥ ṣāḍvidhyaṃ ca vivakṣuḥ śrutirgānatrāṇopapādanāya vāgrūpatvamāha vāgvai gāyatrī iti | gāyatrīśabdavācyaṃ catuṣpādbrahma vāgrūpaviśiṣṭamityarthaḥ raso’hamapsu kaunteya ityārabhyocyate śabdaḥ khe pauruṣaṃ nṛṣu iti | tathā bhagavatparāśaravacanam śabdamūrtidharasyaitadrūpaṃ viṣṇormahātmanaḥ iti | tataḥ kiṃ vivakṣitaṣāḍvidhyasyetyatrāha vāgvā idaṃ sarvaṃ bhūtaṃ gāyati ca trāyate ceti | vāgviśiṣṭaṃ paraṃ brahma sarvabhūtaṃ gāyati
abhidhatte; hitāhitavidhiniṣedhamukhena trāyate cetyarthaḥ | evamekaḥ pādo gānatrāṇarūpaṃ vidhādvayaṃ coktam | tadanuvādapūrvakaṃ dvitīyaṃ pādaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyenāha - yā vai sā gāyatrī iyaṃ vāva seyaṃ pṛthivī iti uktākāraviśiṣṭaprakṛtadharmiparau yattacchabdau | yā sā sarvabhūtarūpaikapādā gāyatrī gāyatryākhyaṃ brahma, tadeva pṛthivītyarthaḥ | kathaṃ pṛthivyā brahmātmakatvamityatrāha - asyāṃ hīti | brahmātmakatvādeva hi sarvabhūtapratiṣṭhātvam | na hi kevalapṛthivyāḥ sarvabhūtadhāraṇaśaktirityarthaḥ | pratiṣṭhātvaṃ ca niyatamityāha - etāmeva nātiśīyata iti | pṛthirvī bhūtajātirnātivartate | karmavaśyātmanāṃ niyamena pratiṣṭhātvādaśakyātikramaṇetyarthaḥ | pṛthivīmayabrahmāṇḍodare hi bhoktṛvargaḥ parivartate |
evaṃ dvitīyaḥ pādo bhūtapratiṣṭhātvatadanativartyatvarūpaṃ vidhādvayaṃ coktam | atha tṛtīyaṃ pādamāha - yāvai sā pṛthivī iyaṃ vāva sā yadidamasmin puruṣe śarīramiti | puruṣaśabdaḥ śarīraviśiṣṭajīvaparaḥ (pṛthivī) pṛthivīrūpapādaviśiṣṭā yā, (sā) gāyatrī gāyatryākhyaṃ brahma, sā śarīraṃ, śarīrākhyapādaviśiṣṭetyarthaḥ | bhūtapṛthivyorgāyatrīsāmānādhikaraṇyena nirdiṣṭatayā (pṛthivīśabdasya) brahmaparyantatvena tadupasthāpanakṣamatvāt āgryaprāyanyāyādatrāpi (pādāntarasya tatsāmānādhikaraṇyena nirdeṣṭuṃ yuktatvācca) śarīrasya brahmātmakatvaṃ prāṇapratiṣṭhātvatadanativartyatvābhyāmupapādayati - asmin hīme prāṇāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ etadeva nātiśīyanta iti | caturthaṃ pādamāha - yadvai tatpuruṣe śarīraṃ idaṃ vāva tat yadidamasminnantaḥ puruṣe hṛdayam iti | śarīrākhyapādaviśiṣṭaṃ yadgāyatryākhyaṃ brahma tadeva (hṛdayam) ityarthaḥ | pūrvavat brahmātmakatvamupapādayati - asmin hīme prāṇāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ etadeva nātiśīyanta iti | prāṇāḥ prāṇāpānādayaḥ indriyāṇi vā | teṣāṃ hṛdayasaṃbandhināḍīdvārā hṛdayapratiṣṭhitatvam | evaṃ tṛtīyacaturthau pādau prāṇapratiṣṭhātvatadanativartyatvarūpaṃ vidhādvayaṃ coktam | uktaṃ catuṣpāttvaṃ ṣājhvidhyaṃ ca nigamayati - saiṣā catuṣpadā ṣaḍvidhā gāyatrīti | eṣā catuṣpadā ṣaḍvidhā sā gāyatrītyanvayaḥ | etadbrahma catuṣpadatvena ṣaḍvidhatvena ca prasiddhā gāyatrītyarthaḥ | vidheyānusārāt strīliṅganirdeśaḥ | indraśśacīpatiḥ | balena pīḍitaḥ | duścyavano vṛṣā | samitsusāsahiḥ iti catuṣpadā ṣaḍvidhā ca gāyatrī prasiddhā | tasyāśca ṣaḍvidhatvaṃ tatpādasya ṣaḍakṣaratvāt | catuṣpadeti gāyatrīpādasyaiva nirdeśaḥ | tasya padasyargvācakatvābhāve ṭābṛci iti vihitaṭābanupapatteḥ | ataścatuṣpadeti padaṃ, gabhīrāyāṃ nadyāmityatra gabhīrapadavat gāyatrīpadamukhyārthaṃ viśinaṣṭi - ṣaḍvidheti padamapi tatsamabhivyāhārāttadviśeṣaṇameva | evaṃ ca brahmaṇi gāyatryāṃ catuṣpāttvaṣāḍvidhyokterbrahmaṇi gāyatrī sādṛśye tātparyamavagamyata iti tamimaṃ śrutyarthamāha gāyatrī nāmeti |
sāmaśrutiśirasi - chāndogye | bhūtakṣititanuhṛdayaiḥ - bhoktṛvargapṛthivīśarīrahṛdayaiḥ | sidhyatparyavasyat, catvāraḥ pādā yasya tat catuṣpāt, tasya bhāvaḥ cātuṣpadyam; brāhmaṇāderākṛtigaṇatvāt ṣyañ; pādasya padādeśaḥ, catuṣpādityatra ca pādasya lopaḥ ityantyalopaḥ; catuṣpāttvamityarthaḥ; paryavasyaccātuṣpadyaṃ yasyeti vigrahaḥ | gānatrāṇādikābhiḥ - gānatrāṇabhūtapratiṣṭhātvatadanativartyatvarūpābhiḥ | tasya - catuṣpāttvasya, vidhābhiḥ - 
tadupapādakaprakāraiḥ | ṣaḍvidhaṃ sidhyat ṣāḍvidhyakam | paraṃ jyotiḥ - atha yadataḥ paro divo jyotiḥ ityuktaṃ paraṃ brahma | gāyatrī nāma - catuṣpāttvaṣaḍvidhatvābhyāṃ prasiddhagāyatrīsadṛśatvena | adhyāyi - adhītamityarthaḥ | vibhāṣā luṅlṛṅoḥ iti gāṅādeśābhāvaḥ | nātra gāyatryāṃ brahmadṛṣṭiḥ | pūrṇāmapravartinī śriyaṃ labhate iti mokṣaphalaśravaṇāt | nāpyākāśādhikaraṇanyāyena gāyatrīśarīrakabrahmavedanam | brahmaṇaścatuṣpāttvādyuktivaiyarthyāt | ato brahmaṇi gāyatrīsādṛśyānusandhānameva vidhīyata iti bhāvaḥ | atra sidhyatpadena - pādo’sya viśvā bhūtāni ityanantaramantre kaṇṭhoktacatuṣpāttvavyāvṛttiḥ | tuśabdena - vāk pādaḥ prāṇaḥ pādaḥ ityuktasya, agniḥ pādo vāyuḥ pādaḥ ityuktasya ca catuṣpāttvasya vyudāsaḥ | bhūtapṛthivyādīnāṃ kīrtanānantaraṃ catuṣpadeti nigamanasvārasyāt bhūtapṛthivyādīnāṃ pādatvaṃ pratīyata iti, idaṃ catuṣpāttvaṃ na kaṇṭhoktam; kintu paryavasānagatyā siddham | śrutigatavacanavyaktibhedāt - yā vai sā gāyatrī iyaṃ vāva sā idaṃ vāva tat iti śrutiśailīvailakṣaṇyāt | vāgvai gāyatrī asmin hīme prāṇāḥ ityetadrūpācchailīviśeṣāddhetoḥ | vākprāṇoktiḥ parārthā - gānatrāṇaprāṇapratiṣṭhātvādyupapādanārthā | natu bhūtādipadaṣaṭkapratipādanārthā | tadetat - pūrvoktaṃ, catuṣpāttvaṣaḍvidhatvābhyāṃ brahmaṇi gāyatrīsādṛśyānusandhānam | svarasaṃ - svataḥ pratītatvalakṣaṇarasayuktaṃ, nigamanaṃ - kīrtitopasaṃhārarūpaṃ yasyāṃ sā tathoktā śrutiḥ - saiṣā catuṣpadeti śrutiḥ tadadhīnaṃ - tatpramāṇakamityarthaḥ ||
atra śrutigatavacanavyaktibhedāt svarasanigamanaśrutyadhīnamityābhyāmasyāḥ śruteḥ paroktārtho’nupapanna iti sphoritam |
yādavaprakāśairasyā hyayamartha uktaḥ - gāyatrī vā idaṃ sarvam iti brāhmaṇaṃ na catuṣpāttvaparam, kintu sarvabhūtavākpṛthivīśarīrahṛdayaprāṇarūpavidhāṣaṭkaparam | saiṣā catuṣpadā ṣaḍvidhā iti vākyaṃ ca ṣāḍvidhyānuvādena catuṣpāttvavidhānaparam | catuṣpāttvaṃ ca pādo’sya iti mantravarṇapratipādyamiti | ayamanupapannaḥ - saiṣā catuṣpadā ṣaḍvidhetyubhayāṃśe nigamanapratīteḥ svārasikatvādekāṃśe’nuvādatvam, itarāṃśe vidhāyakatvamiti vairūpyāyogāt | vākprāṇayorgānatrāṇādivadanyārthatayā bhūtapṛthivyādivat prādhānyataḥ pratipādyatvābhāvena padārthaṣaṭkāsiddheśceti || 27 ||
atha antaryāmyadhikaraṇam - 
antaryāmyadhikaraṇaviṣayabhūte’ntaryāmibrāhmaṇe yasya tamaśśarīram ityatra
vākyārthānadhyavasāyaḥ; yasya cetanasya yaddravyam iti śarīralakṣaṇasya dravya tvaghaṭitatvāt tamasaśca dravyādravyatvayorvādivākyasaṃmohādatrāha - 
tamo nāma dravyaṃ bahulaviralaṃ mecakacalaṃ
pratīmaḥ kenāpi kvacidapi na bādhaśca dadṛśe |
ataḥ kalpyo hetuḥ pramitirapi śābdī vijayate
nirālokaṃ cakṣuḥ prathayati hi tadṛrśanavaśāt || 28 ||
bahulaṃ ca tadviralaṃ ceti vigrahe (khaṃjakubjādivat) samāsaḥ | bahulaṃ - sāndraṃ, viralaṃ - sāntarālam | ekavyaktau sāndratvādyabhāvādavayavasaghātātmakatvamuktaṃ bhavati | anena sāvayavatvamuktam | mecakaṃ - nīlaṃ calaṃ calanavat | pūrvavatsamāsaḥ | ābhyāṃ guṇakriyāvattvaṃ uktaṃṭabhavati | tamo nāma dvitīyāntamidaṃ, nāma prasiddhau | anena bhāvarūpeṇa bhāsamānatvamuktaṃ bhavati | dravyaṃ pratīmaḥ dravyatvena pratyakṣayāmaḥ | anuminuma iti vā | tamo dravyaṃ sāvayavatvāddhaṭavat; tamo dravyaṃ guṇakriyāśrayatvāt ghaṭavat; tamo na tejo’bhāvarūpaṃ vidhi rūpeṇa pratīyamānatvāt ghaṭavat; tamo na guṇaḥ guṇāśrayatvena pratīyamānatvāt, ghaṭavat; tamo na rūpapratītyabhāvaḥ vidhi rūpeṇa pratīyamānatvāt ghaṭavat; (vadi ityanumānatvāt ghaṭa) ityanumānāni vivakṣitāni | atra mecakamityanena viyadvyāptapārthivaparamāṇugatanailyapratītirevāndhakārapratītiriti pakṣaḥ pratikṣiptaḥ | tathā sati hi nailyamityeva tamoviṣayakadhīḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | tama iti bhāvarūpeṇa pratīyamānatvādityanena tejassaṃsargābhāvo rūpapratītyabhāvo vā tama iti pakṣau pratikṣiptau | hetvasidiṃdha pariharati - kenāpītyādinā | kenāpi | puruṣeṇeti śeṣaḥ | kvacidapī tyapiśabdena kadāpīti samuccīyate | bādhaḥ - rūpādiniṣedhadhīḥ | na dadṛśe - na dṛṣṭa ityarthaḥ | dṛśeḥ karmaṇi liṭ | bādhaśceti cakāreṇa kāraṇadoṣaḥ samuccīyate | tathā ca kāraṇadoṣabādhakapratyayavirahāttamasi rūpādipratītirna bhrāntirūpeti na hetvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | tamasi rūpādipratīterbhrāntitvaniyāmako doṣo na dṛśyate | tamasi rūpādipratīteḥ sarvānubhavasiddhatvena sarvatra tatkalpanaṃ ca gauravaparāhatam | ataḥ kāraṇadoṣo durvaca iti bhāvaḥ | nanu (tamasaḥ kāraṇanirūpaṇāttamo na dravyam) tathā hi tamaso rūpavattvānna tadrahitāni vāyvādīni tatkāraṇāni| sparśagandhavirahānnānyānyapi tatkāraṇāni | (kāraṇaguṇaprakrameṇa hi kārye vaiśeṣikaguṇārambha ityatrāha) - ataḥ kalpyo heturiti | tamasi rūpādipratīterabādhitatvāt dravyatvasiddhau tatkāraṇaṃ kalpyamityarthaḥ | loke kāryaṃ hi pratītaṃ kāraṇakalpanāyāṃ pramāṇam na punaḥ sphuṭāvadhāritaṃ kāryaṃ kāraṇanirūpaṇāyāpahnavamarhati iti nyāyāt | abādhitapratyayasiddhasya tamasaḥ kāraṇaṃ kalpyam | tatra svacchadravyatvādākāśakāryaṃ tamaḥ | rūpavattvaṃ tu pañcīkaraṇādupapadyate | yadvā tejaḥkāryaṃ tamaḥ rūpavattvāt; sparśānupalambhastu svacchatvāt iti vyāsapādāḥ | prabhāprabhāvadrūpeṇa tejodravyavat, pṛthivyeva tamorūpeṇa tadāśrayarūpeṇa ca sthitā | pṛthivīcchāyaiva tama iti meghanādārisūrayaḥ | nyāyasiddhāñjane’pi pṛthivīviṣaye tamaḥ parigaṇitam | ataḥ kāraṇaṃ kalpanīyam | nanu rūpopalabdhivelāyāṃ tamaso’nupalabdheḥ, tadanupalabdhikāle tadupalabdhirnimīlanavadapi saṃbhavatīti śaṅkitaṃ pratyakṣamityatrāha - pramitirapi śābdī vijayata iti | śāstrajanyaṃ pramātmakaṃ jñānamuktaśaṅkānirāsakamityarthaḥ | vijayo hi pratipakṣanirasanam | tamaḥ sasarja bhagavān ityādikamiha vivakṣitam | 
(apiśabdena yuktirapi saṃgṛhyate | ulūkādīnāmasmadādirūpopalabdhivelāyāṃ rūpānupalabdhyā asmadādīnāṃ tadrūpopalabdhikāle tadanupalabdhyā tayoḥ śaṅkitatvaprasaṅgāttamasi rūpapratītirna bhrāntirityarthaḥ) nanu tamo na dravyaṃ tasya grāhakābhāvāt | ālokānanugṛhītatvāt cakṣurna grāhakam ||
(ālokasannidhāvanavasthānāṃ nāsyāgrāhyatvamato rūpapratītirbhrāntireva) nimīlane nīlapratibhāsavadityatrāha - nirālokamityādi | nirālokaṃ - ālokāsahakṛtam | cakṣuḥ - kartṛ | tat - tamaḥ | prathayati - prakāśayati | kuta ityatrāha - darśanavaśāt - ulūkāderasmadādeśca rūpatejo’bhāvagrahaṇe cakṣuṣa ālokanirapekṣatvādityarthaḥ | ālokābhāvabhinnaviṣayakacākṣuṣatvenālokasaṃyogatvena kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ paramate | asmanmate tu ālokābhāvabhinnasthāne tamobhinnatvaṃ niveśya kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ | rūpapratīterbhramatvakalpanaṃ tvadhikaṃ paramata iti bhāvaḥ || 28 ||
bhūmādhikaraṇasiddhāntamāha - 
bhūmākhyaḥ pūruṣottamo yadadhikaṃ brahmeha satyāhvayaṃ
prāṇākhyādavarātmanastu balatastajadjñādhikassatyavit |
yaḥ satyaṃ vadatīti vāggamayituṃ na hyagnihotryantaraṃ
śaknoti prakṛtāgnihotryavagamādbhaṅgastu śabdaśriyaḥ || 29 ||
chandogāḥ samāmananti - yatra nānyatpaśyati nānyacchṛṇoti nānyadvijānāti sa bhūmā | atha yatrānyatpaśyatyanyacchṛṇotyanyadvijānāti tadalpam iti | ayaṃ bhūmaśabdo (vaipulyaviśiṣṭavācaka) bahuśabdādimanici niṣpanno, yadyapi vaipulyavāci; tathāpi (alpa)pratiyoginirdeśatvādvaipulyāśrayaparaḥ; tathā ca bhūmā vipula ityarthaḥ | bhūmaśabdārthaviśeṣyaścātmā; tarati śokamātmavit iti prakramya, bhūmānamupadiśya, ātmaivedaṃ sarvam ityupasaṃhārāt | tatra kimayaṃ bhūmaguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ pratyagātmā paramātmā veti saṃśayaḥ |
pratyagātmā bhūmeti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | ātmajijñāsave nāradāya prāṇopadeśānantaramanyānupadeśena prāṇaśabditajīva eva ātmopadeśasamāptipratīteḥ | pratyagātmano’pyavidyāmuktasya sukharūpatvamupapadyate | na ca eṣa tu vā ativadati yaḥ satyenātivadati iti tuśabdena prāṇātivādinamapekṣya satyātivādino’nyatvapratīteḥ prāṇaśabdoktajīvādadhike satyanāmani brahmaṇi ātmopadeśasamāptyavagamena bhūmā paramātmeti vācyam | sa vā eṣa evaṃ paśyan iti prakṛtaprāṇātivādinaḥ pratyabhijñānena tasya, eṣa tu vā ativadati iti satyavadanamaṅgaṃ vidhīyate | yathā eṣa tu vā agnī hotrī yaḥ satyaṃ vadati iti prakṛtāgnihotriṇaḥ satyavadanaṃ vidhīyate; tadvaditi |
siddhāntastu - paramātmaiva bhūmā eṣa tu vā iti prāṇātivādyanyatvapratīteḥ | na ca eṣa tu vā agnihotrī iti vākyanyāyāvatāraḥ | tatra hi dravyadevatāntarābhāvenāgnihotrāntarāpratīteragatyā tuśabdasvārasyabhaṅgaḥ | iha tu - prakṛtātivādanimittaprāṇātiriktasatyaśabdoktaparamātmarūpātivādanimittāntarasaṃbhavātkimiti tuśabdasvārasyabhaṅgaḥ kāryaḥ | satyaśabdo hi paramātmavācakaḥ | chāndogya eva tasya brahmaṇo nāma satyam iti śravaṇāt | ataḥ satyaśabdokte paramātmani ātmaśabdaparyavasānādbhūmā paramātmeti |
tamimaṃ siddhāntamāha - bhūmākhya ityādinā | bhūmā niratiśayasukhalakṣaṇatayā vede bhūmaśabdanirdiṣṭaḥ puruṣāṇāmuttamo nārāyaṇa eva | kuta ityatrāha - yaditi | yat - yasmāt |
iha - bhūmavidyāyām | prāṇākhyāt - prāṇasahacāreṇa prāṇaśabdopacaritādavarātmanaḥ | ārāgramātro hyavaro’pi dṛṣṭaḥ iti paramātmāpekṣayāpakṛṣṭājjīvāt | adhikaṃ - atiriktam | brahma, satyāhvayaṃ - satyaśabdābhihitaṃbhavati | tataḥ paramātmanyātmaśabdopadeśaparyavasānādbhūmā puruṣottama ityarthaḥ | puruṣaṃ satyam iti satyaśabdasāmānādhikaraṇyena puruṣaśabdanirdeśā tpuruṣottama iti sādhyanirdeśaḥ | saṃprasādādadhyupadeśāt iti sūtrānusāreṇādhikamiti nirdeśaḥ | nanu eṣa tu vā ativadati iti prāṇātivādinaḥ satyavadanaṃ vidhīyata ityatrāha - tubalata iti | satyavit - 
satyanāmakabrahmanimittakātivādahetusatyavedanavān tubalataḥ - tubalena | tatjñādhikaḥ - prāṇavedyadhikaḥ | tuśabdo hi pūrvoktādarthāntare svarasaḥ | ataḥ prāṇātivādino’nyaḥ satyavādī kaściducyate | prāṇātivādinamapekṣya satyātivādinaḥ
