prakarṣaśca, tadvedyaprāṇādetadvedyasatyaprakarṣanibandhana iti satyaśabdokte prakṛtātmopadeśaparyavasānāt bhūmā puruṣottama ityarthaḥ | eṣa tu vā agnihotrī iti vākyavaiṣamyamāha - ya iti | yaḥ satyaṃ vadatīti vāk - eṣa tu vā agnihotrī yaḥ satyaṃ vadati iti vākyam | prakṛtāgnihotryavagamādagnihotryantaraṃ - prakaraṇādavagamyamānādagnihotriṇo’nyamagnihotriṇam | gamayituṃ na śaknoti | dravyadevatābhāvādityarthaḥ | hiśabdo hetau | tasmāt, śabdaśriyaḥ tuśabdasvārasyasya | bhaṅgaḥ - tyāgaḥ soḍha iti śeṣaḥ | tadvat prakṛte’nupapattyabhāvena, tuśabdasvārasyabhaṅgo’nucita iti bhāvaḥ | ativāditvamatikrāntasvopāsyavastuvāditvam | tacca parimitasukhe pratyagātmani na saṃbhavati | kiṃ tu niratiśayasukhasvarūpe paramātmani saṃbhavati | ataḥ paramātmaiva bhūmeti || 29 ||
gaḍḍhandya ghddhaḍḍhadhtdṛdvadma daharādhikaraṇaviṣayasya atha yadidamasmin ityāderarthābhidhāne parapakṣāt svapakṣe vailakṣaṇyamāha - 
asvārasyacatuṣṭayaṃ paramate yacchabdaliṅgavyathā
tasminnityanapekṣayā vyavahite hṛtpuṇḍarīke’nvayaḥ |
apyākāṅkṣitakāmavedanavidhikṣepaṃsvapakṣe dvayaṃ
yacchabdasya yadaikaśeṣyamapi yā tatraikavadbhāvitā || 30 ||
atrāyaṃ śrutikramaḥ - atha yadidamasmin brahmapure daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma daharo’sminnantarākāśastasmin yadantastadanveṣṭavyam iti | asyāyamarthaḥ parairuktaḥ - asmin brahmapure - śarīre; yadidaṃ puṇḍarīkākāraṃ; daharam - alpaṃ; veśma; asmin - hṛdayapuṇḍarīkaveśmani; antaḥ - antarvartī; dahara ākāśaḥ - brahma; tasmin - hṛdayapuṇḍarīkākāśe; yat - daharākāśaḥ; sāmānye klībaṃ; astīti śeṣaḥ; tat - upāsyaṃ; tatpadamapi sāmānye napuṃsakamiti |
svābhimatārthastu - yadidaṃ śarīre padmākāraṃ hṛdayāyatanaṃ; asmin hṛdayāyatane antaro’ntarvartī; daharākāśaḥ - paraṃ brahma; tasminnityādi - astirbhavatipara ityastīti labhyate; yadasti tasminnantaścetyanvayaḥ, yo daharākāśo’sti; yattadantarvartīti tadarthaḥ; yaśca yacca yaditi puṃnapuṃsakayorekaśeṣaḥ; tadanveṣṭavyaṃ - sa ca taccānveṣṭavyaṃ; daharākāśastadantarvartiguṇajātaṃ copāsyamityarthaḥ | ātmānamanuvidya vrajantyetāṃśca satyān ityuttarānusārādyacchabdādiṣu triṣvapyekaśeṣāśrayaṇam | ekaśeṣavaśādekayatpadopasthitayorapi daharākāśatadguṇayoryogyatayā daharākāśasyāstītyanenānvayaḥ | guṇajātasya tasmin antarityanenānvayaḥ | puṣpavantau divā doṣā padmotpalavikāsakau ityādiṣviva yathāyogyamanvaya iti |
anayorarthayoḥ svārasyāsvārasyavivekamāha - asvārasyetyādinā atha yadidam ityādivākya iti śeṣaḥ | yacchabdasya - yadantariti yatpadasya, liṅgavyathā - pum̐lliṅgākāśaśabdanirdiṣṭadaharākāśaparāmarśitvaprāptapum̐lliṅgabādhanam | idamekamasvārasyaṃ paramate | svamate tu napuṃsakaśeṣānna doṣaḥ | yadyapyubhayatra śāstrīyaṃ klībatvam; tathāpi svataḥprāptapuṃstvabādhanamadhikaṃ paramata iti hṛdayam | tasminnitīti | anapekṣayeti sahārthe tṛtīyā | vyavahite - daharo’sminniti vākyavyavahite | hṛtpuṇḍarīke, anvayaḥ - parāmarśitvam | anapekṣaśabdena tadvaiyarthyaṃ lakṣyate | tathā ca tasminnityasya vaiyarthyaṃ,
vyavahitaparāmarśitvaṃ ceti doṣadvayamityarthaḥ | daharo’sminnantarākāśastadanveṣṭavyaḥ ityevālamiti | (tasminnityasya vaiyarthyaṃ vyavahitahṛdayapuṇḍarīkaparāmarśitvaṃ ceti bhāvaḥ) | asmākaṃ tu guṇapratipādanena tasminnantarityasya sārthakyam | daharākāśasyāpyupāsyatvārthaṃ yatpadaikaśeṣāśrayaṇamapi | daharo’sminnityavyavahitadaharākāśaparāmarśitvācca na vyavadhānadoṣa iti hṛdayam | ākāṅkṣitasya etāṃśca satyān ityuparyanuvādena purovādārthamapekṣitasya, kāmavedanavidheḥ - kāmyanta iti kāmāḥ kalyāṇaguṇāḥ, te cāpahatapāpmatvādayaḥ, teṣāṃ vedanaṃ - upāsanaṃ, tasya vidhiḥ - vihitatvānumānādvidhiviṣayatvasiddhiḥ, tasyāḥ, kṣepaḥ - nirāsaḥ | apiśabdassamuccaye | asmākaṃ tu guṇaguṇinorubhayorevopāsyatvānna tattyāga iti bhāvaḥ | kāmāḥ kāmyaspṛhāsmarāḥ ityabhidhānakośāt, kāmyaparasya kāmaśabdasya prakaraṇādapahatapāpmatvādibodhakatvam | etadasvārasyacatuṣṭayaṃ śaṅkaramata ityanvayaḥ | svapakṣa ityādi | yacchabdasya tacchabdāderupalakṣaṇam | yadaikaśeṣyaṃ - ekaśeṣatvaṃ brāhmaṇādiniveśādbhāve ṣyañ | tatra - ekaśeṣatvenābhyupagate yacchabdādau | yā ekavadbhāvitā - arthasyaikavadbhāvavidhānaprayuktamekavadbhāvavattvaṃ tadidaṃ dvayaṃ - akhārasyaṃ svapakṣa ityanvayaḥ | (uttarayatpadatvānna tatpadādhyāhāra iti bodhyam) ||
atra yaśca yaccetyādivigrahe yattadostyadādisūtreṇaikaśeṣaḥ | anveṣṭavyapade tu napuṃsakamanapuṃsakena iti sūtreṇa (ekavadinyarthasyai) ekavadbhāvavidhānāt | vyaśabdavattatsamānādhikaraṇayattadoścaikavacanāntatvaṃ sidhyati | puṃnnapuṃsakayostu paratvānnapuṃsaṃka śiṣyate iti klībatvamapi yattadossiddham | anugṛhītaṃ ca vyāsapādaiḥ tyadādīnisarvairnityam iti pulliṅganapuṃsakaliṅgayacchabdayorekaśeṣaḥ napuṃsakamanapuṃsakenaikavaccāsyānyatarasyām iti napuṃsakāntatvamekavacanāntatvaṃ ceti | atra punnapuṃsakayostu paratvānnapuṃsakaṃ śiṣyate ityetadvināpi uddiśyamānapratinirdiśyamānāyorekatvamāpādayanti sarvanāmāni talliṅgamupādadata iti kāmacāra ityukteḥ (kāṃsyabhojinyāyena) 
viśeṣaṇasyāpyanveṣṭavyapadasya, napuṃsakasūtreṇa kṛtaikaśeṣatayā niścitaliṅgasya vidheyaparatvena prādhānyāt, talliṅgamevāniścitaliṅgasya tyadādisūtreṇa kṛtaikaśeṣamātrasya uddeśyasamarpakasyāpi tatpadasya nyāyyamiti, tatparatantrasya yatpadasyāpi tadeva liṅgamiti sarvatra napuṃsakasūtrādhīnameva napuṃsakatvamityabhiprāyeṇānapuṃsakamityuktam | anveṣṭavye pade napuṃsakānapuṃsakasahoktivaśapravṛttena
napuṃsakasūtreṇārthasyaikavadbhāvātideśāttatsamānādhikaraṇasyāpyekavadbhāvasiddhyai tatpade ekavacanāntatvaṃ tatpāratantryācca yatpade’pītyabhiprāyeṇa ekavacanāntatvaṃ cetyuktamitidhyeyam | evaṃ cātra bhāvaprakāśikākṛduktakliṣṭanirvāho nādartavyaḥ | rakṣohāgamaladhvasandehāḥ prayojanam ityatra tu padasaṃskārapakṣāśrayaṇādantaraṅgatvāt prathamamupajāyamānetaretarayogadvandvaprayuktaṃ bahutvaṃ padāntarasambandhanimittakena bahiraṅgeṇaikavadbhāvena bādhituṃ na śakyamiti bahuvacanopapattiḥ | padavākyasaṃskārapakṣayośca lakṣyānurodhitvāt | kvacidvākyasaṃskārapakṣāśrayaṇena vyākaraṇamadhijigāṃsamānebhyaḥ prayojanamanvākhyāyata ityatra samānādhikaraṇatiṅantasyaikavacanāntatvaṃkṛtamityupadarśitavyāsapādasūkternirvāhāntarāṇi tu pārāśaryavijaye mahācāryairanugṛhītāni tatraiva draṣṭavyāni || 30 ||
devatādhikaraṇasiddhāntamāha - 
paṭutaravigrahādi vahatāṃ bhavatāpavatāṃ
bhagavadupāsanaṃ diviṣadāmapi saṃbhavati |
nanu śatamarthavādavacasāmapi mantragirāṃ
vidhivadiha pramāṇamapadoṣamapuṃprabhavam || 31 ||
atra devatānāṃ brahmavidyānadhikāro’rthitvādyabhāvāt tadabhāvaśca caitanyādyabhāvāt | mantrārthavādayoḥ (smṛtisatī) daṃparatvānna tatra tātparyam | caitanyarahitasyāpyuddeśyatvasaṃbhavānna tyāgātmakayāgavidhinā tatsiddhiḥ | kṣaṇadhvaṃsino’pi karmaṇo’pūrvadvārā kālāntarīyaphalasādhanatvanirvāhānna nirvāhaka prasādāśrayatayāpi tatsiddhiḥ | devatārahiteṣu keṣucidapūrvasyāvaśyakatvānnārthavādikaprasādādaraḥ | ato’śarīrāṇāmacetanānāṃ devatānāmanadhikaro brahmopāsaneṣviti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
siddhāntastu - sāmavidhibrāhmaṇe, mayi varca ityetena kalpena catvāri varṣāṇi prayuñjānastrayāṇāṃ lokānāmādhipatyaṃ gacchati iti indrapadasya phalatvaśravaṇāt, anyatrāpi marudādidevatāpadaprāptermadhuvidyāphalatvokteḥ, devatāvaśīkārasya ca tatra tatra phalatvokteḥ, phale ca tātparyāvaśyaṃbhāvāt, upāsanaprakaraṇasthairapi tau ha prajāpatim ityādibhiḥ karmavidhiśeṣabhūtaiśca vajrahastaḥ ityādibhirdevatānāṃ caitanyādisiddhiḥ | ata eva cārthavādikaprasādādarāvaśyaṃbhāvaḥ | devatārahiteṣu ca bhagavata eva svaprasādadvārā phalapradatvam |
mantrārthavādayośca vidhivadevāpauruṣeyatvādisāmyāt prāmāṇyam | smṛtistutī ca nirālambane na prasidhyataḥ | asatkīrtanena ca na prarocanā saṃbhavati | vedānteṣu devādicaturvidhasṛṣṭirāmnāyate | ato devatāvigrahādisadbhāvāt brahmavidyāyāmastyevādhikāro devatānāmiti | tamimamāha - paṭutareti |
paṭutaraṃ ca tat vigrahādi ceti vigrahe karmadhārayaḥ | ādipadena veda parigrahaḥ | brahma bṛhaspatiḥ ityādinā devānāṃ brāhmaṇyā dyavagamādbhavāntarādhigatavedāpramoṣeṇa vā tatsiddhiḥ | vigrahasya paṭutaratvaṃ jātaputraḥ ityuktāvasthāviśeṣarūpaṃ, viṣṇukramaṇādau yogyatārūpaṃ, apaṅgutvādikaṃ ca vivakṣitam | vidyāyāḥ paṭutaratvaṃ ca upadeṣṭṛnairapekṣyādirūpaṃ vivakṣitam | vigrahādīti dvitīyāntaṃ, sāmānye klībam | vahatāṃ - bhāratayā bibhratām | anena durapahnavatvaṃ vyajyate | bhavatāpavatāṃ - saṃsāratāpāturāṇām | atiśāyane matup | gajasyāpi svānurūpo vātaroga itivaditi bhāvaḥ | ābhyāmarthī samartho vidvāṃścetyuktamadhikāratrayamuktam | tathāvidhānāṃ diviṣadāmapi devatānāmapi | etadapekṣayā prakṛṣṭaviṣṭapasthatve’pi nityavibhūtyapekṣayā nirayasthatvātteṣāmupāsanecchā saṃbhavatīti sūcyate | bhagavataḥ - heyapratibhaṭakalyāṇaguṇākarasya anena upāyopeyatvaupayikaguṇaviśeṣā abhipretāḥ | upāsanaṃ - asakṛdāvṛttacintanam | saṃbhavati - prāmāṇikamityarthaḥ | atropāsanamityanena taduparyapi bādarāyaṇaḥ saṃbhavāt iti sūtrasthatacchabdārtha uktaḥ | diviṣadāmityupariśabdārthaḥ | manuṣyādhikāratvāditi pūrvasūtraprastutamanuṣyādhikārastatrātra ca, apinā samuccīyate | paṭutaretyādinā saṃbhavāditi śabdasyārtha
uktaḥ | arthavādavacasāṃ - vajrahastaḥ ityādīnāṃ | atha taddhobhaye devāsurāḥ ityādīnāṃ ca karmopāsanavidhiśeṣabhūtārthavādavākyānām | mantragirāṃ - devatāstutyanuṣṭheyaprakāśanaparamantravarṇānām | apiśabdena devatāpadaprāpti sādhanakarmavidhayo gṛhyante | śataṃ ānantyaparam (idaṃ vināpi mānāntaravirodhaṃ) bhūyasāṃ balīyastvaṃ na bādhitavyamiti bhāvaḥ | pramāṇam - abādhitārthapratipādakam (iti yojanā) | tatkathaṃbhūtamityatrāha - apadoṣamiti nirdoṣam (pauruṣeyaṃ ca) apuṃprabhavaṃ - apauruṣeyaṃ ca) vidhivat - vidhivākyavat pramāṇameva | nanvityavadhāraṇe | nirdoṣāpauruṣeyatvasāmyāt, mānāntaravirodhavirahācca arthavādādervidhivadeva prāmāṇyāt devatāvigrahādisiddherarthitvādisaṃbhavādastyeva brahmavidyādhikāro devānāmiti, mantrārthavādayorvidhivatprāmāṇyasthāpanasaṃrambheṇa kaṇṭhoktiḥ | vidhiśeṣāṇāṃ tu
pratiyogyabhyupagamādapiśabdena sūcanamiti dhyeyam || 31 ||
uktameva vistṛṇīte - 
stutiparamādyamatra nijasādhyavidheyaruciprajananasiddhaye svavacanīyaguṇādakhat |
taditarathā na sidhyati vidhiṣviva tattvavidāṃ
kathamasatā guṇena kathitena nutirbhavati || 32 ||
stutiparaṃ - guṇābhidhānaparam | atra - vidhiśeṣabhūtārthavādamantrasamudāye | ādyaṃ arthavādātmakaṃ kartṛ | nijasādhyāyāḥ svakāryabhūtāyāḥ; vidheyeṣu - yāgādiṣu ruceḥ icchāviśeṣasya, prajananasya siddhaye rucyutpattisiddhyartham | svavacanīyaguṇeṣu svapratipādyakṣepiṣṭhatvādiguṇeṣu ādaravat - tātparyavadityarthaḥ | stutiparamiti codyabījameva parihārabījatayā kathamasatetyādinocyate | prajananeti praśabdena kalpitastutiśaktikanirarthavādavidhisthalavyāvṛttirvivakṣitā | uktārthamarthāpatyā draḍhayati - tadityādinā | tat - vidhyapekṣitaṃ stutiparatvaṃ kartṛ | itarathā - guṇatātparyeṇa vinā | na sidhyati - na saṃbhavati | vidhiṣviva iti pūrvānvayī, yathā vidherguṇatātparyavirahānna stāvakatvaṃ tadvadarthavādasyāpi guṇatātparyavirahānna stāvakatvamiti bhāvaḥ | yadvā yathā vidhiṣu svārthatātparyeṇa vinā na pravartakatvasiddhistathārthavādeṣvapi svārthatātparyeṇa vinā na stāvakatvasiddhirityarthaḥ | atha vā vidhiṣviva ityuttarānvayī | tattvavidāṃ - pratipipādayiṣitārthaviparītaviṣayakapramātmakaviśeṣadarśanavatāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ | vidhiṣviva kaṣāyaṃ piba śikhā te vardhiṣyate ityādyupacchandanavākyeṣviva tatpratipādyālīkasādhyasādhanabhāveneva | kathitena - arthavādabodhitenāpi | asatā - niścitālīkabhāvena | guṇena, nutiḥ - stutiḥ (ruciḥ pūrvapravṛttiḥ) kathaṃ bhavati - na bhavatītyarthaḥ |
(tadidaṃ bhāṣitaṃ bhagavatā - na cāsatā guṇena kathitena prarocanā jāyate iti |) satāmeva guṇānāṃ kīrtanaṃ stutirnāsatām | tasminneva stutiśabdasya prayogaprācuryāt | nānṛtaṃ vadet iti niṣedhasya stutivyatiriktaviṣayakatvakalpane gauravācca | (kiṃ caivaṃ tulyanyāyatayā nindāpavādāderasaddoṣakathanatvāpattāvapavādi harerdoṣamityādyasvārasyaṃ spaṣṭam) | bhūtārthe katamā
stutiḥ ityetatūpakramagatavākyaśeṣānusārādyāvadartho vaktuṃ na śakyata ityetadarthakamiti bhagavadguṇadarpaṇe spaṣṭam | ata eva kavibhirapi, (arthavāda eṣa doṣaḥ tu kiṃcitkathayeti mithyābhiśastirūpavastusaddoṣakathanapratiyogitayā’rthavādo nirdiṣṭaḥ) |
anyathā hi vāstavaṃ tu kiṃcitkathayeti syāt | guṇābhidhānamātraṃ stotramityāśrayaṇe’pi nārthavādānāṃ svārthatātparye kiṃcidbādhakam, asatastu na prarocakatvamityabhyupagamāt | vedāntācāryāstu - stotravyākhyāne stoṣyāmīti - paramārthaguṇavarṇanena seviṣya ityarthaḥ tathā ca dramiḍabhāṣyam nāsatā stutirupapadyate iti āhuśca varadācāryāḥ - 
kathamasatā guṇena kathitena nutirbhavati iti prāhuḥ||
teṣāmayamartho’sya ślokasyābhipretaḥ | atra arthavādamantrātmakabhāgadvaye, prathamaṃ arthavādarūpaṃ nijasādhyavidheyaruciprajananasiddhaye stutiparaṃ sat, rucisiddhyarthastutiparatākaṃ sat, - svapratipādyaguṇeṣu satyatvatātparyarūpādarayuktaṃ, bhavati | tat - stutiparatvam | itarathā - guṇasatyatvatātparyavirahe | na sidhyati | stutitvasya paramārthaguṇavarṇanatvavyāpyatvādvyāpakābhāve vyāpyābhāvādityarthaḥ | tattvavidāṃ - yathārthadarśinām |
vidhiṣviva - upacchandanavākyeṣviva | kathitenāsatā guṇena, nutiḥ, kathaṃ bhavati - na kathamapītyarthaḥ | paramārthaguṇavarṇanaṃ hi stutiḥ | tatra guṇānāṃ ghaṭakatayā hetutvavivakṣayā tṛtīyā | na cāsatā guṇena kathitena prarocanā jāyate iti bhāṣye, prarocanāśabdaḥ stutiparaḥ | stutyaṃ stutibhirarthyābhiḥ ityatra tu guṇavarṇanamātre stutiśabdo gauṇaḥ | āśāsanāṃśeṣvarthopadhāyakatvaṃ vā arthyābhirityatrābhipretam vācamartho’nuvartate iti nyāyāditi || 32 ||
(vidhyarthavādamantrāṇāmiti kramānusāriṇī) arthavādavacasāṃ mantragirāmiti krame prathamanirdiṣṭasyārthavādasya svārthe (svapakṣe) prāmāṇyamupapādyānantaranirdiṣṭamantrāṇāṃ tadupapādayati
atha manavo vidheyaparibodhanakāryamukhādvidhimupakurvate hi diviṣadvapurādidhiyam |
upajanayanta enamupakartumalaṃ khalu te
kathamiva devatādhiyamupaiti vapurvidhurā || 33 ||
atha - anantaranirdiṣṭāḥ; yadvā arthavādaiḥ prarocanāyāṃ
kṛtāyāmanantaramanuṣṭhānakāle | manavaḥ - mantrāḥ | vidheyasya - anuṣṭheyasya paribodhanakāryamukhāt - prakāśanarūpasvakṛtyadvāreṇa | vidhimupakurvate - vidhivākyasya kiṃcitkurvanti | hīti pratiyogyabhyupagamaḥ sūcyate | te - anuṣṭheyārthaprakāśakatayābhyupetā mantrāḥ | diviṣadāṃ - devānāṃ, vapurādiṣu - vigrahādiṣu, dhiyaṃ - pramām upajanayantassantaḥ - prathamameva janayantaḥ santaḥ | enaṃ - sāmānyato mantropakāryatayā pūrvamupāttaṃ vidhim | upakartuṃ - tasya kiṃcitkartum | alaṃ - samarthāḥ, devatāvigrahādiviṣayakapramotpādana (hetuka) vidhyupakārakarā ityarthaḥ | hetau śatrādeśaḥ | khalviti durapahnavatvamasyocyate | tadāha - kathamiveti | vapurvidhurā - śarīrādiśūnyā | devatā - mantrastutyā yāgādyuddeśyā ca | dhiyaṃ - smṛtim | kathamupaiti - nirviśeṣāyāḥ smṛtyāroho duśśaka iti bhāvaḥ | mantrāṇāmanuṣṭheyārthaprakāśakatvasiddhyarthaṃ devatāvigrahādau dvārabhūte prāmāṇyamāvaśyakam | anyathā vaiyarthyaṃ syāt | ato na vākyabheda iti bhāvaḥ | idamapi bhāṣitaṃ bhagavatā na hi nirviśeṣā devatā dhiyamadhirohati (ityatra) | paṭutaretyādiślokatraye natkuṭakaṃ vṛttam | yatiniyatānajau bhajajalā iti natkuṭakamityukteḥ || 33 ||
hetvantareṇāpi devatānāṃ brahmavidyādhikāraṃ samarthayate - 
atha suranaratiryaksthāvarātmaprapañcaprajananagaṇanāsu chandasāmantabhāge |
prakaṭataramadhītā vigrahā devatānāmavakalaya gatiṃ copāsanaprakriyāsu || 34 ||
atheti yuktyantaraprastāve | chandasāṃ - vedānām | antabhāge - antapradeśe; chāndogyasubālādivedāntavākyaviśeṣeṣviti yāvat | surāḥ - devatāḥ, narāḥ - manuṣyāḥ, tiryañcaḥ - mṛgādayaḥ, sthāvarāḥ - vṛkṣādayaḥ ta ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya tathoktasya prapañcasya sarvalokasya, prajananaṃ - sṛṣṭiḥ, tasya gaṇanāsu buddhiviparivṛttirūpaprakaraṇeṣu |
devatānāṃ - agnīndrādīnāṃ vigrahāḥ - śarīrāṇi | āyudhāderapyupalakṣaṇamidam | devatābhedena vigrahabhedāt vigrahā iti bahuvacanam | ekasyā api devatāyā tattadyajñeṣu anekapratipattidarśanābhiprāyeṇa vā bahuvacanam | prakaṭataraṃ - sphuṭataram | anena parābhyupagatāpūrvavyāvṛttiḥ | aśrutamapi hi tatkalpitam; śrutaṃ punaridaṃ kathamapalapyata iti bhāvaḥ | adhītāḥ - avagatāḥ | kiṃ ca
upāsanaprakriyāsu - upāsanaprakaraṇeṣu | garti - devatādhikārapratipādanaprakāram | avakalaya ālocayeti prativādinaṃ pratyuktiriyam ||
atra (sadeva somyedamityādikaṃ, tanmādhye) puruṣastryakṣaḥ prājāpatyaḥ, candramā manaso jātaḥ; cakṣossūryo ajāyata; apānānniṣādāḥ, lalāṭātkrodhajo rudraḥ; devo bhūtvā devānasṛjat; ṛṣirbhūtvā ṛṣīn yakṣarākṣasagandharvān ityādikaṃ draṣṭavyam | atha taddhobhaye devāsurāḥ ityādikaṃ ca brahmopāsanaprakaraṇasthaṃ draṣṭavyam | tadidaṃ bhāṣitaṃ bhagavatā - dehendriyādimattvaṃ ca
brahmādīnāṃ sakalopaniṣatsu sṛṣṭiprakaraṇeṣūpāsanaprakaraṇeṣu ca śrūyate iti | atrottaratra ca mālinīvṛttam | nanamayayayuteyaṃ mālinī bhogilokairi tyukteḥ || 34 ||
prakārāntareṇāpi tāsāmadhikāraṃ vadan devatādhikāraṃ nigamayati - 
nigamaśatanidānairdevatāvigrahādi
sphuṭataramitihāsairdharmaśāstraiḥ purāṇaiḥ |
api gaditamanantopāsane’syādhikārastadayamaravargastatphalārthī samarthaḥ|| 35 ||
nigamaśatāni - saṃkīrṇabrāhmaṇamantrārthavādātmakāni bhūyāṃsi vedavākyāni, nidānāni - mūlāni yeṣāṃ tathoktaiḥ | itihāsaiḥ - purāvṛttapratipādakaśrīmadrāmāyaṇādibhiḥ | dharmaśāstraiḥ - manuyājñavalkyaviṣṇuprabhṛtiproktasadācārādipratipādakahitānuśāsanagranthaviśeṣaiḥ | purāṇaiḥ - sargādipratipādakapurāṇaratnaprabhṛtibhiḥ | api, devatāvigrahādi - yāgoddeśyabhūtendrādiśarīrādikam | sphuṭataraṃ - dvāradvāribhāvena vākyabhedaparihāranirapekṣam | gaditaṃ - paṭhitam | na śrutyarthāpattyā kalpanamiti bhāvaḥ | mānāntararapratirodhābhāvāt nātra virodhādhikaraṇanyāyapravṛttiriti bhāvaḥ | (atra yajñāgṛhārādhyāyādikamanusandheyam) | yata evaṃ tataḥ ayaṃ - pramāṇaprasiddhadehendriyādiḥ | amaravargaḥ brahmādidevatāgaṇaḥ | tatphalārthī - buddhisthopāsanaphalamokṣādyarthī | samarthaḥ ( brahma bṛhaspatiḥ ityādyuktabrāhmaṇyādiyogena, bhavāntarādhigatavidyāprabodhena vā vaiduṣyāt, agnimattvācca śakto bhavatīti | yattasmāt indrādyaiśvaryasya karmaviśeṣaphalatvaśravaṇāt, tadvaśīkārasya ca tattvaśravaṇāt, mantrārthaṃvādayoḥ svārthe prāmāṇyāt, vedānteṣu sṛṣṭiprakaraṇeṣu upāsanaprakaraṇeṣu ca dehendriyādiśravaṇāt, viprakīrṇavedamūleṣu itihāsādiṣu devatāvigrahādipratipādanāt, mānāntaravirodhavirahāt aṅgāpūrvanyāyena devatāprasādānāṃ paramāpūrvasthānīyaparamātmaprasādadvāratvāśrayaṇena pratimāpratimeyanyāyānavatārāt kalpāntare ca pūrvapūrvendrādiśarīrakaparamātmana eva prasādadvāraphalapradatvāśrayaṇena kṛtahānākṛtābhyāgamaprasaṅgavirahāccetyarthaḥ) asya - amaravargasya | anantopāsane deśakālavastuparicchedarahitabhagavadupāsane adhikāraḥ anuṣṭhānaprayatnaphalasvāmyam | astīti śeṣaḥ ||
amarapadena, anantapadena ca, yadyapyamarā asmadādyapekṣayā cirakālasthāyinaḥ prakṛṣṭaiśvaryayuktāśca; tathāpyaparicchinnabrahmānandānubhāvavirahāt teṣāṃ tadapekṣā tadupāyānuṣṭhānaṃ ca yujyata eveti sūcyate | vargapadena ca, vyavadhānena, sākṣādvā mokṣārthinyo vasvādidevatāstadanyadevatāśca vivakṣitāḥ | ata eva mokṣakāṅkṣiṇyanuktiriti dhyeyam || 35 ||
tatraivādhikaraṇeṭa
tatraivādhikaraṇa iti | devatādhikaraṇa ityarthaḥ | śabda iti cennātaḥ prabhavātpratyakṣānumānābhyām iti sūtra iti | śeṣaḥ | tatra hi indrādiśabdānāṃ arthaśūnyatvādiparihārāya vyaktiviśeṣasaṅketitatvanirāsenākṛtivācakatvaṃ sādhitam ||
tatra parairākṛtessaṃsthānātirekābhyupagamāttatra doṣadarśanātsaṃsthānasyaivānugatapratītyādiprayojakatvasaṃbhavācca tasyaivākṛtitvaṃ sādhyate (avayavasaṃsthānameva jātiriti yāvat) 
saṃsthānaṃ kalayākṛtiṃ tvanugataṃ tāvatpratīmo hi tadbrūmaścāparamapyadarśi na tathā pakṣāntare jātiṣu |
yadvadgotvapurassarīṣu dhiṣaṇā bahvīṣu sādhāraṇī
jātirjātiritīdṛśī phaṇitirapyevaṃ madīye pathi || 36 ||
tuśabda evakārārtho bhinnakramaḥ | saṃsthānaṃ tvākṛtiṃ kalaya - saṃsthānamevākṛtiṃkalaya | evakāraḥ prasiddhiparaḥ | ubhayābhyupetamavayavasaṃyogaviśeṣaṃ jātiṃ svīkurvityarthaḥ | yadvā vaiṣamye tuśabdaḥ | saṃsthānasya klṛptatvadṛṣṭatvābhyāṃ parābhyupetajātervaiṣamyaṃ vivakṣitam | atirekavāde’pi vyañjakatvāya saṃsthānamāvaśyakaṃ, dṛśyate ca taditi, klṛptaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ ca saṃsthānamiti tenaivānugatapratītyādyupapatternātiriktajātisvīkāra ucita iti bhāvaḥ | ākṛtiratra sāmānyam | jātyākṛti ityatra tvavayavasaṃsthānamākṛtiḥ | nātassiddhasādhanam | saṃsthānaṃ nāma vastunaḥ svāsādhāraṇaṃ rūpamiti bhāṣyam | atra svaśabdaḥ prakṛtaparaḥ | rūpamapṛthaksiddhaviśeṣaṇam | ghaṭasya dravyatvaṃ saṃsthānaṃ mā bhūdityasādhāraṇapadam | prakṛtatvaṃ ca tattadvyavahāragocaratayā buddhisthatvam | gotvaṃ gāṃ prati, khaṇḍatvaṃ khaṇḍaṃ prati, khaṇḍagavaṃ prati tu khaṇḍatvaviśiṣṭagotvaṃ saṃsthānam | etādṛśasaṃsthānameva jātinirvāhakamiti na tadvyaṅgyajātyākhyapadārthāntarasvīkāra ucita ityarthaḥ | nanu gotvajātessaṃsthānābhivyaṅgyatvānna saṃsthānasyaiva jātitvamiti cenna | vikalpāsahatvāt | kiṃ saṃsthānātirekiṇo dṛśyatvādevamucyate, uta kalpyatveneti | nādyaḥ | saṃsthānavyatirekeṇānekeṣu ekākārabuddhibodhyavastvapratīteḥ | anuvṛttapratyayabodhyaṃ hi sāmānyam | saṃsthānasyaivānuvṛttabuddhibodhyatvāt | nāpi dvitīyaḥ - saṃsthānenaiva tadvyavahāropapatteḥ | kalpakaṃ hyanyathāsiddham | ato nātiriktajātiriti |
tadidamāha - anugataṃ tāvadityādinā | tat - saṃsthānam | anugataṃ pratīmaḥ, brūmaśca (anugatapratītivyavahāraviṣayī karma ca) tāvacchabdaḥ kramārthaḥ | anugatapratītyanyathānupapattyā hi jātiḥ kalpyā | sā tu saṃsthānānugamenānyathāsiddhettyarthaḥ | darśanādapi kalpane parasya saṃrambhātiśaya iti prathamaṃ tannirāsaḥ | hiśabdena saṃsthānānugamasya prasiddhirucyate | atha darśanaṃ nirasyatiaparamiti | asmābhirityādiḥ | asmābhistvayā mayā anyaiśca | aparaṃ - saṃsthānātiriktaṃ jātyākhyaṃ vastu |
nāpyadarśi nāpi dṛṣṭamityarthaḥ |
tathāśabdaḥ kalpanāvyudāsasya dṛṣṭāntatvaparaḥ | yathā na kalpanaṃ tathā na darśanamityarthaḥ | atra vyāsapādāḥ - ayaṃ sāsnādimān ayamapi sāsnādimān, ayaṃ pṛthubudhnodarākāraḥ ayamapi pṛthubudhnodarākāra iti sāsnādirevānuvṛttabuddhivyavahāraviṣayo dṛśyate | tadatirikto’nuvṛttadhīvyavahāraviṣayo na kaściddṛśyata ityanujagṛhuḥ | nanu vyāvṛttaṃ saṃsthānamanuvṛttadhīvyavahārayoḥ kathaṃ viṣaya ityatrāha - pakṣāntara ityādi | yadvat - yathā | pakṣāntare - atiriktajātivāde | bahvīṣu - anantāsu | gotvāśvatvapurassarīṣu - gotvāśvatvādikāsu jātiṣu, parābhyupetasāmānyeṣu | sādhāraṇī - samānā | jātirjātiriti dhiṣaṇā - jātirjātirityekākārā pratītiḥ | īddaśī phaṇitirapi - jātirjātirityekākāravyavahāro’pi bhavati | madīye pathi - saṃsthānajātivāde | evaṃ jātau jātipratītivat sausādṛśyamūliketyarthaḥ | atra gaurgaurityanugatapratītiranugataśabdaśceti śeṣaḥ | jātiṣu jātitvaprakārakapratītivyavahāravadeva gavādiṣvapi gotvādiprakārakapratītivyavahāropapattiriti bhāvaḥ | jātiṣvityupalakṣaṇam | vyaktyādiṣvapīti | yathā vyaktitvajātivirahe’pi bahvīṣu vyaktiṣuvyaktiśabdaprayogaḥ, yathāvā khaṇḍādiṣu khaṇḍatvajātyabhāve’pi khaṇḍaśabdaprayogaḥ; yathā ca nityadravyeṣu viśeṣamicchatāṃ viśeṣatvajātivirahe’pi viśeṣeṣu viśeṣaśabdaprayogaḥ; tadvat gavādiśabdaprayogopapattiriti | nanu kimidaṃ sausādṛśyamiti cedatra vyāsapādāḥ - sadṛśavyavahārāsādhāraṇaheturviṣayadharmassādṛśyam | sa ca bhrātṛtvavat pratiyoginirūpyapadārthaḥ prativyaktivyāvṛttaḥ | ato na (vyā) vṛttivikalpādidaurghaṭyam | nirdiṣṭākārapauṣkalyatassādṛśyaṃ sausādṛśyamityāhuḥ | megha nādārisūribhiśca atha sāsnādīnāṃ ka upādhiriti cet - saṃsthānasādṛśyameveti brūma ityuktam || 36 ||
imāveva pūrvapakṣasiddhāntau vistareṇa ślokatrayeṇāha - 
ekā vyaktiṣu bhūyasīṣvanugatā pūrṇā prativyakti yā
saṃsthānādaparā nirādinidhanā sā jātirāsthīyatām |
gaurgaurityanuvṛttabuddhivacasī no cedvaṭete kathaṃ
vyutpattiśca kathaṃ gavādivacasāṃ vyaktiṣvasaṃkhyāsu naḥ || 37 ||
yā bhūyasīṣu vyaktiṣu, anugatā - anuvartamānā | prativyakti - 
vyaktauvyaktau | pūrṇāparisamāpya vartinī | nirādinidhanā - ādirutpattiḥ, nidhanaṃ nāśaḥ, tābhyāṃ nirgatā | nirādayaḥ krāntādyarthe iti samāsaḥ | anena prāgabhāvāpratiyogitve sati dhvaṃsāpratiyogitvarūpaṃ nityatvamuktam | ekā - sarvāsu vyaktiṣvabhinna | yā evaṃvidhā bhavati sā saṃsthānādaparā jātiḥ - ubhayābhyupagatasaṃsthānātiriktā jātiḥ | āsthīyatāṃ - pratijñāyatāṃ, vakṣyamāṇānugatapratītyādyanyathānupapattyā kalpyatām | pratijñā hi sādhyaviśiṣṭapakṣabodhanam | āṅpūrvakastiṣṭhatiḥ pratijñāyāṃ vartate | ā ṅaḥ pratijñāyām ityukteḥ | anena nityamekamanekānugataṃ sāmānyami ti sāmānyalakṣaṇamuktaṃ bhavati | pūrṇetyanena vattivikalpaḥ pratītiparāhata ityuktaṃ bhavati | saṃsthānādaparetyanena saṃsthānasya nityatvādyanupapattyā tadatiriktajātyupagama āvaśyaka ityuktaṃ bhavati | atha hetumāha - gaurgaurityādinā | no cet - pūrvoktajātirnābhyupeyate cet | gaurgaurityanuvṛttabuddhiḥ anuvṛttavacaśca, kathaṃ ghaṭete - kathamupapanne syātām | anugatapratītivyavahārayoranugatadharmaṃ vinānupapatteḥ tābhyāṃ tādṛśadharmasiddhirityarthaḥ | gavādivacasāṃ - gavādiśabdānām | asaṃkhyāsu - anantāsu, vyaktiṣu | naḥ asarvadarśināmasmākam | vyutpattiḥ - śaktigrahaḥ | kathaṃ - ghaṭata ityarthaḥ | sarvopasaṃhāreṇa śaktigrahasiddhyarthe jātirāvaśyakītyarthaḥ | cakāreṇa vyāptigrahaṇaṃ, kāryakāraṇabhāvagrahaṇaṃ, śuktyādau rajatādibhramaḥ, gavādiśabdānāṃ prathametaravyaktiṣu gauṇatāvirahaśca samuccīyate | sarvasyāpyasyānanugatasaṃsthānenānirvāhāt || 37 ||
siddhāntaṃ vistṛṇīte dvābhyāṃ - 
atra brūmaḥ pratīmaḥ kimapi kimaparaṃ vastu sāsnādimattvādgotvaṃ nāmānuvṛttavyavahṛtiviṣayaṃ taddhi tādṛkṣamaikṣi |
gotvā’jatvā’śvatādiṣvanugatamaparaṃ vastu jātiṣvanekāsvekaṃ jātyādiśabdavyavahṛtighaṭakaṃ jātivāde kimūce || 38 ||
atra brūmaḥ - asmin pūrvapakṣe siddhāntaṃ vadāmaḥ | sāsnādimattvādaparaṃ saṃsthānātiriktam | sāsnā galakambalaḥ | ādinā śṭaṅgādiparigrahaḥ | anena saṃsthānamucyate | saṃsthānātiriktaṃ gotvaṃ nāma - gopadapravṛttinimittatvena siddhaṃ kimapi aparam, anuvṛttavyavahṛtiviṣayaṃ - anugatavyavahāragocaram | dvitīyāntamidam | vastu pramāṇasaṃbandhārhaṃ, karma | pratīmaḥ - kiṃ pratītiviṣayīkurmaḥ | kiṃ kṣepe (ayaṃ kiṃśabdaḥ) na paśyāmaḥ; darśanābhāvānnātiriktajātirityarthaḥ | tarhi vyavahārānyathānupapattyā kalpyata ityatrāhataddhītyādi
| tatsaṃsthānam | tādṛkṣaṃ - sausādṛśyenānugatavyavahārādiprayojakam | aikṣi - dṛṣṭamityarthaḥ | anena kalpanānyathāsīddharuktā | hiḥ prasiddhau | vyañjakatvāya sausādṛśyaṃ pareṇāpi puraskṛtamiti bhāvaḥ ||
sausādṛśyaviśiṣṭasaṃsthānena vyāptigrahaṇādikaṃ suśakam | bhramotpattiścāvirodhisaṃsthānapratipattau sulabhā | tannivṛttiśca virodhisaṃsthānapratītyā sulabhā | sādṛśyaleśogauṇatāpādakaḥ, sausādṛśyaṃ tu mukhyatāpādakam | atassausādṛśyaviśiṣṭasaṃsthānenopapattau nātiriktakalpanamityarthaḥ | atra vyāsapādāḥ - kasmiṃścidarthe kasyacicchabdasya vyutpattiḥ tatsusadṛśe’rthe susadṛśasya tasyaiva śabdasya prayogaheturiti yathādarśanamabhyupagantavyam | apratipannamarthāntaraṃ kalpayitvā vṛttivikalpādidoṣāṇāṃ pratītiśaraṇatayā parihārādapi paridṛṣṭa eva vastuni pratītibalena codyaparihārau lāghavanyāyādupapadyete iti | paridṛṣṭaṃ ca vastviha saṃsthānasausādṛśyam | atra sausādṛśyenaikīkṛtena saṃsthānalakṣaṇākāreṇa sarveṣāṃ kroḍīkārasaṃbhavāditi bhāvaprakāśikāyāmuktam | mahābhāṣye’pi pratyāhārāhnike, rūpasāmānyāt (rūpasāmānyādvā) siddhametat; tadyathā - tāneva śāṭakānācchādayāmaḥ; tāneva śālīn bhūñjamahe; ye madhurāyāṃ ye māgadheṣu tadeva bhavataḥ kārṣāpaṇaṃ; yanmadhurāyāṃ gṛhītaṃ anyasmiṃścānyasmiṃśca rūpasāmānyāttadevedamiti bhavati ityuktam | kaiyaṭena ca vyaktinirdeśe’pi bhedāparāmarśena lokavadabhedavyavahāro’stīti siddhamiṣṭam | vyaktivyatiriktaṃ tu sāmānyaṃ bhavatu vā mā vā bhūt sarvathā loka iva śāstre vyavahāra ityuktam | kaustubhe’pi ihoccāritokārādiḥ susadṛśīḥ ṣaḍvyaktīḥ sādṛśyākhyasaṃbandhena upasthāpayati ityuktam | ataḥ sādṛśyasya vyaktyantarasaṃgrāhakatvaṃ sāmānyakāryadhurīṇatvaṃ ca sarvasaṃmatam | tadidamabhipretya taddhi tādṛkṣamaikṣī tyuktam ||
anuvṛttavyavahārādau vyāvṛttasaṃsthānasya kathaṃ prayojakatvamityāśaṅkya, jātiṣu jātyantaravirahe’pi jātivyavahārasya yathātiriktajātivāde sausādṛśyenopapattiḥ; tadvat saṃsthānajātivāde’pi saṃsthānasausādṛśyenopapattiriti parihārābhiprāyeṇāha - 
gotvā’jatvā’śvatādiṣviti | anekāsu gotvādijātiṣu |
anugataṃ - vartamānam | ekaṃ sādhāraṇam - jātyādiśabdavyavahṛtighaṭakaṃ - jātyādivyavahāraprayojakam | aparaṃ vastu jātyantarātmakaṃ vastu, sausādṛśyātiriktaṃ vā vastu jātivāde - 
atiriktajātipakṣe | ūce kiṃ - svīkṛtam kim ? | kṣepavācī kiṃśabdaḥ; | na svīkṛtaṃ hītyarthaḥ | tathā ca tadvadeva gavādiśabdānāmapi vyutpattiḥ sausādṛśyenaiva upapadyata iti bhāvaḥ || 38 ||
anyasyādarśanena vyavahṛtighaṭanasyānyathaivopapattyā
kalpyatvāsaṃbhavenāpyubhayasamayasaṃgītasaṃsthāvalambī |
yukto jātyādiśabdaḥ susadṛgiha mithaḥ sā hyanusyūtadhīvā
gvyutpattyarhaiva yādṛkparasamayamatāmākṛtiṃ sā vyanakti || 39 ||
vyā - anyasya - saṃsthānātiriktasya jātyākhyasya | adarśanena, anena darśanaṃ netyuktam | kalpanaṃ vyudasyati - vyavahṛtīti | vyavahṛtighaṭanasya - vyavahārānugamasya | anyathaivopapattyā - ubhayābhyupetasaṃsthānasausādṛśyenaivopapattyā | yaḥ kalpyatvāsaṃbhavaḥ tenāpītyarthaḥ | darśanābhāvādanumeyatvāsaṃbhavāccetyarthaḥ | jātyādiśabdaḥ - gavādiśabdaḥ | ādinā śuklādiśabdaparigrahaḥ jātyajātisādhāraṇaṃ hi saṃsthānaṃ niruktam | ubhayasamayasaṃgītasaṃsthāvalambī yuktaḥ - saṃsthānajātivādātiriktajātivādātmakapakṣadvayābhyupagatasusadṛśasaṃsthānakṛtānugamakaḥ sannupapanna ityarthaḥ | jātyādīnāṃ śabda iti ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣaḥ | ato gavādiśabdalābhaḥ | ihetyādi - iha - saṃsthānajātivāde | mithaḥ - anyonyam | susadṛk - sausādṛśyavatī | saṃsthā, saṃsthānam | anusyūtadhīvāgvyutpattyarhaiva - anugatapratītivyavahāraśaktigrahajananādiyogyaiva | bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | hīti prasiddhau | yādṛksā - yatsausādṛśyaviśiṣṭaṃsaṃsthānam; kartṛ | parasamayamatāṃ - paramatasvīkṛtām | ākṛtiṃ - saṃsthānātiriktajātim | vyanakti - vyañjayati | tādṛksaṃsthānam anusyūtadhīvāgvyutpattyarhamiti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | sausādṛśyaviśiṣṭasaṃsthānasya paramate jātivyañjakatvavat asmatpakṣe’nugatabuddhyādihetutvaṃ saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ ||
susadṛśāvayavasaṃyogaviśeṣarūpasaṃsthānasya jātyabhivyañjakatvaṃ hi parairapyāstheyam | taduktaṃ meghanādārisūribhiḥ - abhivyaṅgyatvapakṣe’pyayaṃ parihāraḥ samaḥ | gavayādau na kathaṃ gotvāderabhivyaktiriti codite; tatrābhivyañjakāvayavābhāvādityuttare datte; kathaṃ tatra tadabhivyañjakāvayavābhāva iti punaścodite; susadṛśāvayavābhāvādityevottaraṃ syāditi
ākṛtigrahaṇā jātiḥ anugatasaṃsthānavyaṅgyetyarthaḥ iti ca tāntrikairuktam | atassausādṛśyaviśiṣṭasaṃsthānasya jātyabhivyañjakatvavadanugatapratītyādinirvāhakatvaṃ saṃbhavatīti bhāvaḥ | sādṛśyasya klṛptapadārtheṣu antarbhavo bahirbhāvo vā’stu | sarvathāpi klṛptasādṛśyenopapattau atiriktajātikalpanaṃ gauravaparāhatamiti hṛdayam | siddhānte tu sādṛśyaṃ nātiriktam | adravyaṃ, sattvarajastamaśśabdasparśarūparasagaṃdhasaṃyogaśaktibhedāt daśaive tyuktatvāt | meghanādārisūribhistu - śaktisaṅkhyāsādṛśyāni guṇe’ntarbhūtāni ityuktam | tathā śaktisādṛśyaparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvāśrayāśrayibhāvādayaḥ, cidacitsādhāraṇā guṇā tairevoktam | tadgatabhūyodharmavattvaṃ sādṛśyamiti pakṣe’pi, sādṛśyabhūtā ye bhūyāṃso dharmāḥ tadatiriktaṃ tadatiriktavastvabhivyaṅgyaṃ jātyākhyaṃ vastvaṅgīkriyata iti gauravam | asmākaṃ tu taireva dharmairanugatavyavahārasidhyāśrayaṇāt lāghavam | tadgatadharmeṣu sādṛśyaṃ, sadṛśāvayaveṣu vyañjakatvaṃ, vyaktiṣu jātiriti trayāṇāṃ vṛttivikalpādeḥ pratītiśaraṇatayā parihāraḥ paramate | asmanmate tu tadgateṣu dharmeṣu sādṛśyaṃ, sādṛśyavadavayaveṣu anugatavyavahāraprayojakatvaṃ ceti dvayoreva vṛttivikalpādeḥ pratītiśaraṇatayā parihāra iti lāghavam sādṛśyaṃ guṇa iti pakṣe’pi, sādṛśyasyāvayaveṣu, sadṛśāvayaveṣu anugatapratītiprayojakatvasya ceti dvayoreva pratītibalena tatparihāra iti lāghavamastyeva | yadyapi sādṛśyaṃ prativyakti vyāvṛttam; tathāpi vyāvṛttasyāpi tasyānuvṛttadhīhetutvaṃ svabhāvādevopapadyate; yathā paramate vyāvṛttānāmanuvṛttajātivyañjakatvaṃ tadvat | taduktaṃ jātipratītyabhāvānnābhivyañjakatvamiti keṣāṃcitsaṃsthānānāmeva gotvādyabhivyañjakatvaṃ svabhāva iti vā | evamatrāpi gavayādau tatsaṃsthānādgotvādyapratītissvabhāvādeveti || 39 ||
prathamasūtrabhāṣye ato vastusaṃsthānarūpajātyādilakṣaṇabhedaviśiṣṭaviṣayameva pratyakṣam iti vākye
arthadvayamuktam; vastusaṃsthānarūpā jātiḥ; jātireva bheda iti ca | tatra saṃsthānameva jātirityayamarthaḥ saṃsthānātirekiṇaḥ ityādibhāṣyeṇopapāditaḥ | jātireva, bheda ityayamarthaśca jātigrahaṇenaiva bhinna iti vyavahārasaṃbhavāt ityādibhāṣyeṇopapāditaḥ | tatra gotvādivyatiriktabhedamaṅgīkurvatā pareṇāpi abhāvābhāve, pratiyoginirapekṣabhāvarūpapadārthasyaiva pratiyogisāpekṣābhāvatvamaṅgīkṛtam | anyathānavasthānādanupalambhācca | tadvadbhedasyāpi pratiyoginirapekṣabhāvarūpagotvādirūpatvaṃ saṃbhavatītyuktam | tatra tulyanyāyatayā saṃsargābhāvasyāpi bhāvarūpatvamabhipretam | tadidaṃ saṃsthānajātinirūpaṇaprasaṃgādāha dvābhyām - 
abhāvasyābhāvo bhavati khalu bhāvaḥ paramate
ghaṭāderapyevaṃ kathaya kimabhāvo na bhavati |
ghaṭasya pradhvaṃso bhavati hi kapālatvamaparastvabhāvaḥ piṇḍatvaṃ kimiha dadṛśe kiṃcidaparam || 40 ||
abhāvasyetyādi | paramate - neyāyikādimate | abhāvasyābhāvaḥ - ghaṭābhāvābhāvaḥ bhāvo bhavati khalu - bhāvarūpaghaṭātmakaḥ abhyupagataḥ kila | anyathānavasthānādanupalambhācceti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ - ghaṭābhāvābhāvasya ghaṭātmakatvavat | ghaṭāderabhāvo’pi bhāvaḥ - bhūtalarūpādiviśeṣarūpaḥ | kiṃ na bhavati - kuto na bhavati, bhavatyevetyarthaḥ | pratiyogisāpekṣasyābhāvasya pratiyoginirapekṣabhāvarūpatvamanupapannamityanupapattirhi tenātiriktābhāve hetukartavyā | sā ghaṭābhāvābhāvasya ghaṭātmakatvaṃ vadatā tenaiva nirākṛteti kalpakābhāvānnātiriktābhāvaklṛptiriti bhāvaḥ | ghaṭasyetyādi | satkāryavādābhyupagamāt, ghaṭadhvaṃsaḥ kapālatvāvasthārūpaḥ | aparastvabhāvaḥ - ghaṭaprāgabhāvaḥ | piṇḍatvāvasthārūpaḥ | hiśabdaḥ prasiddhau | ghaṭaprāgabhāvadhvaṃsasya ghaṭasattākāle ghaṭarūpatvaṃ tenāṅgīkṛtam | tathā ghaṭadhvaṃsaprāgabhāvasya ghaṭasattākāle ghaṭarūpatvaṃ ca tenāṅgīkṛtam | tasmāt dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayorapi bhāvarūpatvamupapannamiti bhāvaḥ | tarhi darśanabalāttatsiddhirityatrāha - kimiha dadṛśe kiṃcidaparam | iha - bhūtalakapālapiṇḍeṣu | aparaṃ kiṃcit - bhūtalarūpādiviśeṣādyatiriktaṃ saṃsargābhāvarūpaṃ vastu | dadṛśe kiṃ - kiṃ dṛṣṭaṃ, na dṛṣṭamityarthaḥ | tathā copalambhā nupapattyorabhāvānnātiriktābhāvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ || 40 ||
nanu ghaṭābhāvasya bhūtalarūpādyātmakatve ghaṭasannidhikāle bhūtalarūpasya nāśāpasaraṇayorabhāvenābhāvapratyayaḥ syādityāśaṅkyātiriktābhāvavāde’pi tadā nityasya niṣkriyasya tasya nāśāpasaraṇayorabhāvādabhāvapratītissyāt, yadi ghaṭasattākālāvacchinnabhūtalasyābhāvīyaviśeṣaṇatāsaṃsargavirahānna tadgraha ityupeyate; tarhi tatkālāvacchinnabhūtalarūpādeḥ ghaṭābhāvānātmakatvopagamānnānupapattirityāha - 
saṃsargābhāvamevaṃ viduriha sudhiyo bhāvameva tvabhāvaṃ
manvānāḥ kumbhavadbhūtalata itarabhūbhāgabhedastvabhāvaḥ |
yastattatkālabhedavyatikaritagṛhaprāṅgaṇādipradeśaḥ
kumbhābhāvāśrayo’nyairagaṇi sa iha naḥ kvādhikasyopalambhaḥ || 41 ||
bhāvameva tvabhāvaṃ manvānāḥ sudhiyaḥ - ubhayābhyupagatabhūtalarūpādirūpameva ghaṭābhāvaṃ prāmāṇikatvena niścinvānāḥ, paramapuruṣakaṭākṣādhīnasamīcīnaprajñāyuktā asmadācāryāḥ | iha - abhāvasya bhūtalarūpādirūpatve ghaṭasattādaśāyāmapi ghaṭābhāvaḥ pratīyeteti śaṅkāyām | saṃsargābhāvaṃ, evaṃ viduḥ - vakṣyamāṇaprakāraviśiṣṭaṃ svīkurvanti | tuśabdaḥ klṛptatvadṛṣṭatvarūpavaiṣamyadyotakaḥ | evakāreṇa abhāveṣu kasyacidbhāvarūpatvam anyasya tvabhāvarūpatvamiti parābhyupagatamardhajaratīyaṃ vyāvartyate | vakṣyamāṇaprakāraḥ ka ityatrāha - kumbhavadbhūtaleti | (dhaṭādhikaraṇabhūtalādikabhūbhāgabheda itarabhūtalagataviśeṣaḥ) | tuśabdaḥ evakārārthaḥ | abhāvaḥ - kumbhābhāva ityarthaḥ | ghaṭādhikaraṇabhūtalabhinnabhūtalarūpādirūpo ghaṭābhāva ityarthaḥ | atra paramataṃ dṛṣṭāntayati - ya ityādi | anyaiḥ - atiriktābhāvavādibhiḥ | yaḥ, tattatkālabhedena - ghaṭānadhikaraṇakālaviśeṣeṇa, vyatikaritaḥ avacchinnaḥ, gṛhaprāṅgaṇādipradeśaḥ aṅgaṇaṃ - catvaram |
kumbhābhāvāśrayo’gaṇi - ghaṭābhāvādhikaraṇatvena svīkṛtaḥ | ghaṭānadhikaraṇakālāvacchinnabhūtalasyaiva ghaṭābhāvasaṃsargatvāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ | saḥ - taddeśakālavarttī ghaṭānadhikaraṇakālāvīcchannagṛhāṅgaṇarūpādireva | naḥ - asmākam | iha - abhāvasthāne | atra bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | sa hi sa na iti pāṭhe sa hi - pradeśaḥ | naḥ - asmākaṃ mate | saḥ - kuṃbhābhāva ityarthaḥ | hiśabdo dārḍhye | taddeśavarttī rūpādirevāsmākaṃ ghaṭābhāva ityarthaḥ | adhikasya - atiriktasyābhāvasya | upalambhaḥ kva - na kvāpīti bhāvaḥ | prābhākaramate adhikaraṇasyaivābhāvatvāṅgīkārāt bhūtale ghaṭo nāstīti ādhārādheyabhāvapratīterbhrāntitvam | asmākaṃ tu bhūtalādivṛttirūpādyātmakatvasya ghaṭābhāve’bhyupagamāttatpratīteḥ pramātvamiti dhyeyam | itarabhūbhāgaśabda itarabhūvartiparaḥ | sa iti ca tadvartipara iti kapālatvaṃ piṇḍatvamiti pūrvānusārādjñāyate | tatrāpi kapāle ghaṭo dhvastaḥ iha mṛtpiṇḍe ghaṭo bhaviṣyati iti pratītyoḥ pramātvamityabhipretam || 41 ||
jātiguṇavācipadayorvyaktiguṇiparyantābhidhānamākṛtyadhikaraṇasiddham | jātyādimātraśaktatvaṃ tu na sūtrārūḍham | kiṃ tvarvācīnavyākhyātṛbhiruktam | atra śarīravāciśabdānāmākṛtyadhikaraṇanyāyaviṣayatānirūpaṇaparāṇi - avasthiteriti kāśakṛtsnaḥ aṃśo nānāvyapadeśāt carācaravyapāśrayastu ityādīni sūtrāṇi | atra tattvamasi sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma ityādiṣu cidacitprakāritvaṃ prātipadikabodhyam | ( carācaravyapāśrayaḥ aṃśo nānāvyapadeśāt ityādibhirullāpitādākṛtyadhikaraṇanyāyā) tatra samānavibhaktyā tvavasthādvayaviśiṣṭavastvaikatvamucyate aruṇādhikaraṇanayāditi siddhāntaḥ | atra pūrvaślokeṣu ākṛtiśabdārtho nirūpitaḥ | asmin śloke ākṛtyadhikaraṇanyāyaḥ śarīravāciṣu pravartate, carācaretyādisūtrādityucyate | uparitaneṣu triṣu ślokeṣu aruṇādhikaraṇanayādviśiṣṭavastvaikyaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyārtha iti ucyate | (avasthiterityaṃśo nānetyādisūtrāti ucyata iti śeṣaḥ) ||
sacca tyacceti sṛṣṭvā viśasi cidacitau tena cokto’si viśvaṃ
tvajjatvāttvallayatvāttvamiti ca nikhilaṃ tvaccharīraṃ kileti |
taistaiśśāstrairabhedavyavahṛtihṛdayaṃ vistṛṇānairnirastā
bhedābhedāvabhedaṃ bhramamapi bhagavan kāraṇaṃ kalpayantaḥ || 42 ||
he bhagavan - nirdoṣakalyāṇaguṇākara, puruṣottameti saṃbuddhiḥ | pūrvaṃ brāhyasaṃsmaraṇena vākyamuktam | atra bhagavatsaṃbodhanena vākyamucyate | avalokya mukhaṃ teṣāmādityamavalokayet iti smṛtyartho’trānuṣṭhito bhavati | acyutabhānunā ityuktamādityatvamatra bhagavacchabdoktadoṣapratibhaṭatvena vyajyate | atra bhakārādyarthabhartṛtvasraṣṭṛtvādikamapi vivakṣitam | cidacitau - cetanān prakṛtivikārāṃśca | sṛṣṭvā - utpādya | viśasi - praviśasi | tatsṛṣṭvā tadevānuprāviśat anena jīvenātmanānupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi ityādiśrutyartho’trābhipretaḥ | tena - svasṛṣṭacetanācetanānupraveśena | sacca tyacceti viśvamukto’si - sacca tyaccābhavat tatteja aikṣata tā āpaḥ tato’si sarvaḥ ityādinā viśvatvena bodhito’si; viśvaśarīrakatvena viśvavācakaśabdairabhihito’sītyarthaḥ ||
sacca tyaccābhavaditi tattacchabdavācyamabhavadityarthaṃ ityuktam | pitrādiva nanupraviṣṭena nāmarūpavyākaraṇasaṃbhavādanupraveśena tanorvyākaraṇaṃ tayoḥ svaparyantatvasiddhyartham | tatra nāmnaḥ svaparyantatvaṃ - svamukhyaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvam | rūpasya tu
svāpṛthaksiddhaviśeṣaṇatvamityevaṃ sāmarthyasiddho’rthaḥ | kaṇṭhato’pyuktaḥ - tadanupraviśya sacca tyaccābhavat iti | tadidamuktaṃ - tena cokto’si viśvam iti | castvityarthakaḥ | tasya hṛdaye’nvayaḥ | aṃśo nānā ityatra cetanācetanayorbrahmaśarīratayā tadapṛthaksiddhaviśeṇatvaṃ nirṇītam | carācaravyapāśrayaḥ ityatra loke cetanācetanaparyavasitatayā gṛhītaśabdānāṃ tatprakārakabrahmaparyantatvaṃ nirṇītam | ato na lakṣaṇā śaktyantaraṃ veti; tadatroktam | sarvaṃ khalu iti śrutyartha ucyate - tvajjatvāt ityādinā |
sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalāniti śāntaḥ ityatrāyamarthaḥ - tajjalānidaṃ sarvaṃ brahma khalvityanvayaḥ | jāyata iti jaṃ, līyata iti lam | anyebhyo’pi dṛśyate ityubhayatrāpi ḍapratyayaḥ | anityatra tu ana prāṇana ityasmādvicpratyayaḥ | jalāniti ca nimnonnatādivatsamāsaḥ | yato vā imāni ityādiśrutyanusārādvibhaktitrayasyāpi vivakṣitatvāt tasya caikatrāsaṃbhavāt tasya
jalāniti śeṣaṣaṣṭhyā samāsaḥ | ananaṃ ca jīvanaṃ, atrāntaḥpraveśo vivakṣitaḥ | antaḥ praviṣṭaśśāstā ityukteḥ | tajjatvāttallatvāttadanatvācca, idaṃ sarvaṃ brahma khalu, sarvaśarīrakaṃ khalu brahma | yadvā brahmaśarīraṃ khalu sarvamiti | tadidamatra bhagavatsaṃbodhanena ucyate ||
tvajjatvāt - tvatta utpannatvāt | tvaditi vyastā samastā vā pañcamī | tvallatvāditi - līyata iti li | līyateḥ kartari kvipi hnasvo napuṃsake iti hnasvaḥ | li iti hnasvāntam | anitītyanaṃ, pacāderākṛtigaṇatvādac pratyayaḥ | li ca anaṃ ceti khañjakubjādivatsamāsaḥ | tvallyanamiti śeṣaṣaṣṭhyā samāsaḥ | tvatsandhilayavatvāttvatsaṃbandhyananavatvāccetyarthaḥ | saṃbandhaśceha krameṇa adhikaraṇatvaṃ hetutvaṃ ca | tvamitīti | nikhilaṃ tvamiti - nikhilaśarīrakastvamiti, nikhilaṃ tvaccharīramiti ca, sarvaṃ tvaccharīramiti vā caśabdo vāśabdārthaḥ | kilaśabdaḥ - śrutisthakhaluśabdavyākhyānam | sarvaṃ brahma khalu | asyāṃ yojanāyāṃ sarvaśabdaḥ śarīriparyantaḥ | atra ca tajjalāniti janmalayānvitastacchabdārthaḥ sūkṣmacidacidviśiṣṭaṃ brahma | ananānvitastu brahmatvaguṇayogibrahmeti vivekaḥ | sarvaṃ brahma khalu sarvaṃ jagadbrahmāpṛthaksiddhaṃ khalu | asyāṃ yojanāyāṃ, sarvaśabdo niṣkarṣakaśabdo jaganmātravācī | brahmaśabdaśca brahmaśarīre lākṣaṇikaḥ | tadidaṃ yojanādvayaṃ tvamiti ca nikhilaṃ tvaccharīraṃ kila ityuktam | iti śabdadvayārthaścā bhedavyavahṛti hṛdaye abhedenānveti | evaṃbhūtaṃ vedānteṣu jīvabrahmaṇoracidbrahmaṇoścābhedanirdeśatātparyaṃ, vistṛṇānaiḥ - 
vistareṇa pratipādayadbhiḥ | taistaiśśāstraiḥ - yasyātmā śarīraṃ (miti) yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ ityādighaṭakaśrutirūpaiḥ, tadanupraviśya sacca tyaccābhavat ityādibhiḥ, sarvaśarīrakatayā sarvaśabdavācyaṃ brahmeti pratipādayadbhiśca nityanirdoṣāpauruṣeyavedāntātmakahitānuśāsanaśāstraiḥ kartṛbhiḥ nirastā ityatrānvayaḥ | bhedābhedau - jagadbrahmaṇorbhedābhedau | abhedaṃ - jagadbrahmaṇorabhedam brahmaṇaśca svagatanānātvabhramam atadākārāropaṃ ca | kāraṇam - abhedanirdeśahetum | api śabdena jagadbrahmaṇoḥ prakāraprakāribhāvaṃ vinā kevalabhedaṃ abhedaśruteśca rājā rāṣṭramitivat svasvāmibhāvaṃ ca hetum vadanto vivakṣitāḥ | nirastāḥ - śrutyaiva pratyākhyātā ityarthaḥ ||
atra bhedābhedaśabdena aupādhikasvābhāvikabhedābhedavādinau bhāskarayādavau vivakṣitau | abhedaṃ bhramamiti śaṃkaro vivakṣitaḥ | apiśabdena vaiśeṣikādayo vivakṣitāḥ | aupādhikabhedābhedapakṣe kleśakarmādyaspṛṣṭasyeśvarasya tadarhānīśvaratādātmyaṃ vyāhatam | abhedavadbhedasyāpi sattvāt khaṇḍamuṇḍayoriva na sāmānādhikaraṇyamiti dūṣaṇam | svābhāvikabhedābhedavāde guṇavat doṣāśca svābhāvikā bhaveyuḥ | bhogyabhoktṛniyantṛṇāṃ trayāṇāmapi brahmāṃśatvābhyupagamāt acijjīvayoḥ acidīśvarayoḥ jīveśvarayośca sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ghaṭaśśarāva itivadanupapannamiti dūṣaṇam | śaṃkarapakṣe svayaṃprakāśabrahmaṇo doṣasaṃbandhastanmūlabhramaśca na saṃbhavataḥ | sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca vyartham | ekena padena svarūpasyāvagatatvāt | viruddhaṃ ca - padadvayāvagatadharmadvayavattāpratipādanaparatvāttasya | bādhārthatve ca tasya padayoḥ svarūpalakṣaṇā nañarthalakṣaṇā ceti dūṣaṇam | kiṃ ca bhramamityanena sāmānādhikaraṇyasyātadākārāropaṇanibandhanatvasyāpyukatyā vākyāprāmāṇyaprasaṃgaśceti dūṣaṇam | vaiśeṣikādipakṣe sāmānādhikaraṇyasyāmukhyatvaṃ spaṣṭam | eteṣāṃ caturṇāṃ (śrutyakta) śrutigatasāmānādhikaraṇyatātparyaviruddhatātparyakalpanaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ dūṣaṇam | śrutyuktaṃ cābhedaśrutitātparyaṃ darśitameveti || 42 ||
atha prāsaṅgikamaruṇādhikaraṇam ṭa
aruṇādhikaraṇaṃ vyācikhyāsustasya paroktavyākhyānaprakriyāmanuvadati - 
unnītaṃ guruṇāruṇādhikaraṇe na hyekahāyanyagādāruṇyānvayamābhidhānikamapi tvākṣepataḥ prāpitam |
yadbrūte’ruṇayeti kevalaguṇaṃ vāgdravyanirdeśavadvākyasthā na girastu kārakavibhaktyantā mithaḥ saṃgatāḥ || 43 ||
arthaikatve dravyaguṇayoraikakarmyānniyamaḥ syāt iti pūrvakāṇḍasūtram | asyaivamadhvaramīmāṃsakairvyākhyānaṃ kṛtam - ākṛtyadhikaraṇanyāyena jātiguṇādiviśiṣṭaparāṇāṃ śabdānāṃ viśeṣaṇajātyādiśaktatvaṃ, tatropāttadravyakavākyasthanīlādiśabdānāṃ guṇamātraparatvam | dravyānupādāne tu dravyasya lakṣaṇayā bodhaḥ yathā śvetamālabheta iti |
tatrāyaṃ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ - aruṇayeti padamāruṇyaśaktam | asyāruṇyasya yogyatve’pi avyutpannatvānna dravye’nvayaḥ |
kārakāṇāṃ kriyayaivānvayāt | vyutyannatve’pi amūrttasyāyogyatvānna kriyāyāmanvayaḥ | ato vicchinnasyāsya (prakaraṇakalpitaśrutānumitaikadeśaniṣpannena vākyenāruṇayā prakṛtāpūrvasādhanībhūtadravyaparicchedaṃ bhāvayedityākārakeṇa prākaraṇikasarvadravyāṅgatvamiti| )
siddhāntastu - na yogyatājñānaṃ śābdabodhahetuḥ | api tu ayogyatāniścayasya pratibandhakatāmātram | ataśca pratibandhakābhāvasattve āruṇyasyāpi kriyānvayopapatteḥ | paścāttayoryogyatāgaveṣaṇāyām āruṇyasya pāÐṣṭhakadravyasaṃbandhabodhopapatteḥ ekahāyanyanvayaniyama iti | tamimaṃ siddhāntamāha - unnītamiti | yat - yasmāt | dravyanirdeśavadvākyasthā - upāttaikahāyanīdravyakavākyasthitā | aruṇayeti vāk - aruṇayeti padam | kevalaguṇaṃ brūte - lakṣaṇayā dravyāpratipādanena śaktyāruṇyamātramabhighatte | kārakavibhaktyantā girastu - kārakavibhaktyantapadāni tu | mitho na saṃgatāḥ - kriyānanvayenānyonyamanvayānarhāḥ | bhavantīti śeṣaḥ | kārakāṇāṃ kriyānvayaniyamāt | tasmāt ekahāyanī, ābhidhānikam - śābdam | āruṇyānvayaṃ, nāgāt - nābhajat | api tvākṣepataḥ amūrtasya, krayasādhanatvānupapattyā aparicchinnasya tatsādhanatvānupapattyā vānumānena guṇe dravyavyapekṣe ca dravye ca guṇakāṅkṣiṇi iti nyāyena pāÐṣṭhakamāruṇyānvayamagāditi atrā(ruṇayaikahāyanyetanayoḥ) yugapadanvayānna guṇāvarodhanyāya iti guruṇā aruṇādhikaraṇe unnītam utprekṣitamityarthaḥ || 43 ||
tadidaṃ dūṣayan vakṣyamāṇaṃ svābhimatavyākhyānaṃ stauti - 
aruṇādhikaraṇasaraṇiḥ
samagaṇi rāmānujāryamunibhiryā |
uciteyamanucitānyā
pariṣadi viduṣāṃ sphuṭībhavati || 44 ||
rāmānujāryamunibhiḥ yā aruṇādhikaraṇasaraṇiḥ
tadadhikaraṇapūrvapakṣasiddhāntaprakriyā | samagaṇi - cintitā | iyamucitā nyāyyā | anyā pūrvoktā| anucitā - na nyāyyā | viduṣāṃ pariṣadi - prāmāṇikānāṃ sadasi | sphuṭībhavati - krameṇa ucitetyanuciteti ca vyaktībhaviṣyati |
atrānuciteti vadato’yaṃ bhāvaḥ - upāttadravyakavākyasthaguṇavācakapadaṃ guṇamātraparamiti vadatāṃ vākyabhedena pūrvapakṣe aruṇāpadasyāruṇyaparatvaṃ vyāhatam | guṇe śuklādayaḥ puṃsi iti guṇamātraparatve puṃlliṅgatvāpattiśca | ataḥ pūrvapakṣo’nupapannaḥ | siddhānto’pyanupapannaḥ | (svata evāyogyasyāyogyatāpratisaṃdhāne śābdānutpatteḥ) kiṃ ca daśabhiḥ krīṇāti iti vākyaprāptakrayānuvādena yugapadanekakārakavidhānaṃ na saṃbhavati | na ca tasya krayasamuccayaparatvamiti vācyam | vidhāyakatvaucittyāt | kiṃ cāruṇyasyāpi karaṇatve daśabhirityanuvādavirodhaḥ | tasya dravyamātraparatve tvasvarasyam | krayabhedasyāprāmāṇikatvādeva vijātīyakramasādhanatvenāruṇyasya ekahāyanyanvayaniyamo’pi durvacaḥ | viṣṇave śipiviṣṭāya ityādau kārakavibhaktyantasyāpi dravyānvayasvīkārādatrāpi taducitamiti || 44 ||
svabhimataprakriyāmāha
upātte’pi dravye visadṛśavibhaktivyapagamāt
prakṛtyā vaiśiṣṭyaṃ pariṇamati khalvākṛtinayāt |
vibhaktistvarthaikyaṃ kathayati samānaiva padayo - 
strirūpā sā saṃkhyānvayamiva diśet kārakadhiyam || 45 ||
ayamarthaḥ - paramate śvetamālabheta ityādiṣu guṇavācipadasya, guṇiparyantatvamuktam | paṭasya śuklaḥ ityādiṣu
tadapavādakatvena upāttadravyakavākyasthatvamabhyupetam | tasyāpyapavādakatvena paṭaḥ śuklaḥ ityādiṣu sāmānādhikaraṇyamuktamiti prayojakatrayakalpanam | asmatpakṣe tu śvetamālabheta paṭaḥśuklaḥ ityādiprayogeṣu apṛthaksiddhadharmavācitvameva guṇiparyantatve prayojakam | paṭasya śukla ityādiṣu asamānavibhaktinirdeśastadapavāda iti prayojakadvayamiti lāghavam | tathā cāsmanmate pūrvapakṣe siddhānte cāruṇāpadasya dravyaparyantatvam ||
tadihāyaṃ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ - aruṇāpadaṃ dravyaparyantameva; ākṛtyadhikaraṇanayāt | athāpi aruṇayetyasya ekahāyanyanvayo na saṃbhavati | sa hi sākṣādvā krayānvayadvārā vā vaktavyaḥ | tatra na sākṣādanvayaḥ | kārakavibhaktitvavirodhāt; kārakāṇāṃ kriyānvayaniyamāt | nāpi krayānvayadvārā; prāptakrayānuvādenāruṇādravyaikahāyanīdravyayorubhayorvidhāne vākyabhedāpatteḥ | caśabdādyabhāvena yadagnaye ca prajāpataye ca ityatreva samudāyavidhānaśaṅkāyā apyayogāt, aruṇayetyatra dravyaviśeṣāpratītyā viśeṣarūpeṇopasthitaikahāyanīdravyasya jhaṭiti krayānvayena guṇāvarodhanyāyena ekahāyanyavaruddhe āruṇyānanvayāt | nāpyekahāyanyā krīṇāti tayā cāruṇayā bhavitavyamiti vākyārthaḥ saṃbhavati | kraye ekahāyanyā ekahāyanyāmāruṇyasya ca vidhāne vākyabhedāpatteḥ | ata āruṇyasya prākaraṇikasarvadravyānvayānnaikahāyanyanvayaniyama iti ||
siddhāntastu - āruṇyasya ekahāyanyāmevānvayaḥ | samānavibhakteḥ padadvayārthaikyaparatvāt | na ca kārakavibhaktivirodhaḥ | kriyanvayikārakabodhakatvasyākṣateḥ | na ca kriyayaivānvayaniyamaḥ | vibhaktyarthaikatvasya kārakānvayābhāvapraṃsagāt | na caikavacanatvena ekatvabodhakatvānna kārakavibhaktitvena tadbodhakatvamiti vācyam | samānavibhaktitvenaikyabodhakatve’pi virodhābhāvat | tadihāruṇyādiviśiṣṭatvaṃ prātipadikāvaseyam | padadvaya pravṛttinimittabhūtadharmadvayāśrayavastvaikyaṃ samānavibhaktivācyam | kriyākārakasaṃbandhastu vākyāvaseya iti na vākyabheda iti ||
tamimaṃ siddhāntamāha - upātte’pītyādinā | dravye - ekahāyanīdravye | upātte’pi - nirdiṣṭe’pi | apiśabdena paroktapūrvapakṣe dravyanirdeśavadvākyasthatvameva nāstīti, tatsiddhānte ca yadyapi tadasti, tathāpi tasya guṇamātravācakatāprayojakatvaṃ paṭaḥśukla ityatra tadabhāvāditi ca sūcyate | visadṛśavibhaktivyapagamāt - asamānavibhaktinirdeśavirahāt | apṛthaksiddhaguṇavācitvarūpotsargasyāpavādābhāvādityarthaḥ | prakṛtyāsamānavibhaktiprakṛtibhūtāruṇāpadādinā ākṛtinayāt - ākṛtyadhikaraṇanyāyāt | vaiśiṣṭyam - guṇayuktatvam | pariṇamati khalu - śābdabodhaviṣayībhavati | padayoḥ - aruṇayaikahāyanyeti padayoḥ | samānā vibhaktiḥ, padayorarthaikyaṃ - tadīyaśaktigrahajanyopasthitiviśeṣyaikyam | kathayatyeva pratipādayatyeva | ayamevādhikaraṇārtha ityevakāreṇa bodhyate | tuśabdaśca ākṛtyadhikaraṇāt prayojanabhedadyotanārthaḥ | trirūpā - kārakavibhaktitva - ekavacanatvasamānavibhaktitvarūpākāratrayayuktā | sā - vibhaktiḥ | saṃkhyānvayamiva - ekatvādiprakārakabodhamiva | kārakadhiyaṃ - karaṇādiviṣayakabodham | diśet - dadyāt | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ padadvayārthaikyaṃ diśedityapi draṣṭavyam | trirūpetyukteḥ | yadvā saṅkhyānvaya iva diśediti pāṭhaḥ | saṅkhyānvaya iti - saptamyantam | ekatvādibodhakatāyāmivārthaikyabodhakatāyāmapi kārakabodho nānupapanna ityarthaḥ | diśediti śaki liṅ | tathā ca ekasyā api śaktatāvacchedakabhedācchaktitrayaṃ saṃbhavatīti bhāvaḥ ||
uciteyamiti pūrvaśloka uktam | tasyāyamāśayaḥ - aruṇāpadasya guṇaviśiṣṭavācitvādeva na guṇe śuklādayaḥ puṃsi ityanuśāsanavirodhaḥ | ata eva guṇavacanānāmāśrayato liṅgavacanāni ityanuśāsanamapi svarasam | amūrtasya krayānvayānabhyupagamādeva nāyogyatāniścayavirahadaśāpratīkṣaṇamapi | samānavibhaktyaikyabodhakenaikahānīdravyameva krayoddeśena vidhīyata iti na vākyabhedadoṣo’pi | ata eva daśabhiri ti na daśadravyānuvādavirodhaḥ | ata eva ca vijātīyakrayasādhanatvenārthikaikahāyanyanvayāśrayaṇakleśo’pi na | guṇāt krayabhedaścāprāmāṇikaḥ | daśabhiḥ krīṇāti ityasya ca krayāṃśe vidhāyakatvaṃ svarasam | daśabhiriti ca tattadvākyaprāptadaśatvānuvādaḥ krayadravyasamuccayārthaḥ | samānavibhaktiḥ padadvayārthaikatvabodhinīti na guṇāvarodhanyāyavirodhaḥ | viṣṇave śipiviṣṭāya ityādau ananaivāruṇādhikaraṇanyāyena kārakavibhaktyantayormitho’nvayānna vairūpyam |
saṅkhyāmuṣṭyādhikaraṇe ca - parimāṇaviśiṣṭavācakapadasamabhivyāhṛtasaṅkhyāviśiṣṭavācakapadārthasaṅkhyāviśiṣṭasya padārthaikadeśe’pi parimāṇa evābhedānvayo na parimite, vyutpattivaicitryāt | anyathā dve prasthe vrīhayaḥ iti vrīhīṇāṃ bhūyastvena bādhāpatteḥ | tathā ca caturo muṣṭīn ityatra parimitadravyaviśeṣaṇasyāpi muṣṭiparimāṇasya
catuṣṭvaṃ prati viśeṣyasya bādhāpekṣayā viśeṣaṇasya catuṣṭvasyaiva bādho nyāyya iti pūrvapakṣe; saṅkhyāviśiṣṭasyāpi parimitadravya evānvayaḥ | anyathā ekadeśānvayāpatteḥ | na ca bādho; muṣṭiparimitatvāvacchedenānvayāt | svato’saṅkhyātānāṃ taṇḍulānāṃ deśādyupādhinaiva saṅkhyeyatāyāḥ saṃbhavāt | muṣṭiparimitatvasya copādhitvasaṃbhavāt tadavacchedenaiva catuṣṭvasyānvayopapatteḥ | mahāśūraḥ ityādau mahattvasya śūratvadvāreva muṣṭiparimāṇadvārā vānvayaḥ | tathā ca parimeyadravyaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ catuṣṭvasyopapannam | ataścatuṣṭvasya muṣṭiparimāṇasya ca ubhayoreva dravyaviśeṣaṇatve’pi prathamaśrutacatuṣṭvābādhanenānantaraśrutamuṣṭiparimāṇasyaiva bādha iti saṅkhyāmuṣṭyadhikaraṇavirodho’pi nāsti | ata iyamucitā anyā tvanucitetyuktam ||
kiṃ ca krayasamuccayaṃ vadatām krayaṇeṣu tu vikalpaḥ syādekārthatvāt iti dvādaśādhikaraṇavirodhaḥ | tatra dravyasamuccayasyaiva pratīteḥ | na ca krayaṇāni krayā iti vaktuṃ yuktam | tathā sati krayeṣvityeva nyāsāpatteḥ | karaṇādhikaraṇayośca iti krayaṇaśabdasya karaṇalyuḍantatvasvārasyāt | ata eva iyaṃ gauḥ somakrayaṇī iti mantravarṇānurūpyamiti | ata eva tadadhikaraṇabhāṣye’pi dravyasamuccaya evādhikaraṇasiddhāntatayoktaḥ | na ca nirguṇakrayamātravidhāyakavākyāntarābhāvena tattadvākyānāmeva krayavidhāyakatvāvaśyaṃbhāvena utpattiśiṣṭatattadvākyasthaguṇāvarodhena guṇāntarāniveśāt guṇāt krayabhedāvaśyaṃbhāvena ekārthatvasya krayeṣveva paryavasānamiti vācyam | taṃ vai daśabhiḥ krīṇāti iti vākyasya krayotpādakatvopapatteḥ | na ca vaiśabdena vidhitvabhaṅgaḥ | vidhistu dhāraṇe’pūrvatvāt ityadhikaraṇoktanyāyenārthāprāptivaśādeva vidhitvasiddheḥ | gavā krīṇāti ityādiṣvapi hi liṅādyaśravaṇenārthāprāptivaśādeva vidhitvasya vaktavyatvāt | krayāṇāmapi bhede gauravācca | daśabhirityaṃśastvanuvādaḥ | sa ca samuccayārtha ityuktameva ||
kiṃ cāruṇādivākyānāṃ vibhinnakrayavidhāyakatve’pi anyoddeśena guṇaviśiṣṭadhātvarthakaraṇakabhāvanāvidhānarūpaṣaṣṭhaprakārāpattiḥ | mama tu dhātvarthoddeśenānyakaraṇakabhāvanāvidhirūpatṛtīyaprakāraḥ | daśaḥ krīṇati ityanyoddeśenabhi
śuddhadhātvarthakaraṇakabhāvanāvidhānāddvitīyaprakāraśceti | na ca vākyāntaraprāptakrayānuvādenāruṇādivākyena dravyavidhāne nirapekṣatvabodhakatṛtīyānupapattyā dadhyādīnāmiva gavādīnāṃ vikalpaprasaṅga iti vācyam | tattaddravyajanya (vikretrānatīnāṃ) mitho vilakṣaṇatayā dvārabhedādeva nirapekṣatvasyākṣatervikalpānāpatteḥ | ata eva paramate’pyāruṇyaguṇaikahāyanīdravyayoryugapadvidhāne’pi dravyaparicchedādilakṣaṇadvārabhedena dravyaguṇayoḥ parasparanairapekṣyādubhayatra tṛtīyopapattirityuktam |
nanvevam taṃ vai daśabhiḥ krīṇāti iti vākyena daśadravyaviśiṣṭakrayo vidhīyate | saṅkhyāyāḥ karmasāmānādhikaraṇyavirahānna saṃkhyayā karmabhedaḥ | daśabhirityaṃśe’pi vidhitvānna vairūpyamapi | kāni tāni daśa dravyāṇītyapekṣāyāṃ gavādivākyairdravyaviśeṣavidhirbhavatviti cenna | ajñātajñāpanāpravṛttapravartanarūpa prameyadvayavato vidherihājñātajñāpanamātrārthakatvena pravṛttiviśeṣakaratvānāpatteḥ | yadyapi daśabhiḥ trīṇāti iti dravyaviśiṣṭakrayotpattigavādivākyaṃ ca gavādīnāṃ pākṣikyāmaprāptau prāpakam, ata eva sarve dravyavidhayo niyamavidhaya ityetadatra saṃgacchata iti vaktuṃ śakyate; tathāpi dravyaviśeṣamātrāvidhānena niyamasiddherna dravyasāmānyavidhyapekṣā anyoddeśena śuddhadhātvarthakaraṇakabhāvanāvidhānāddvitīyavidhiprakārasaṃbhave’nyoddeśena guṇaviśiṣṭadhātvartha karaṇakabhāvanāvidhānarūpaṣaṣṭhavidhiprakārāśrayaṇaṃ ca na nyāyyam | ato daśabhiri ti saṅkhyādvārā dravyasamuccayānuvādaḥ | krīṇātipadena cānyoddeśena śuddhadhātvarthakaraṇakabhāvanāvidhirūpadvitīyaprakāra iti yuktam ||
idaṃ tu cintanīyam | daśabhiḥ krīṇāti ityasya vidhāyakatvāśrayaṇe, ādhāne sarvaśeṣatvāt iti dvaitīyīkādhikaraṇasiddhāntavirodhaḥ | tatra hyādhānasya ya evaṃ vidvānagnimādhatte ityanenāgnyuddeśenādhānavidhāyinā prāptatvāt, vasante brāhmaṇo’gnīnādadhīta ityādīni vākyāni brāhmaṇakartṛtve nimitte vasantādikālasyādhānāṅgatvena vidhāyakānīti pūrvapakṣe ya evaṃ vidvān iti vākyasya vartamānopadeśarūpatvena vidhāyakatvānupapatteḥ, pratyakṣavidhiyuktavasantādivākyānāmeva vidhāyakatvena brāhmaṇakartṛtvādernimittatvābhāvādguṇaviśiṣṭādhānasya brāhmaṇādīnāṃ vidhānamiti siddhāntitam | uktarītyā yacchabdādyupabandhavato vidhāyakatve tadvirodhaḥ ||
kiṃ caivaṃ dravyaṃ cotpattisaṃyogāt iti pūrvādhikaraṇasiddhāntavirodhaḥ | tatra hi sphyaśca kapālāni ca ityanukramya
etāni vai daśa yajñāyudhāni iti vākyasya yajñāyudhaśabdasya
yajñasādhanavācitayā sphyādīnāṃ yajñasādhanatvāvagamakatvāttasya ca sākṣādasaṃbhavādaviśeṣeṇa sarvāṅgāvatārāt yogyasarvakriyāsādhanatvaṃ sphyādīnāṃ sphacenoddhanti ityādīni avayutyānuvāda iti pūrvapakṣe; bahūnāmuddhananādivākyānām ānarthakyāpatternityaprāptyabhāvena nityavacchravaṇavirodhāpatteḥ etāni iti vākye vaiśabdopabandhācca uddhananādivākyaṃ vidhāyakam; yajñāyudhāni iti vākyaṃ tvanuvādakamiti siddhāntitam | tadvadatra vaiśabdayuktasya vidhāyakatvādbahvīnāṃ krīṇātīnāṃ vaiyarthyāpatteśca daśabhiriti vākyaṃ na vidhāyakamiti ||
atrocyate - daśabhiḥ trīṇāti iti vākyaṃ somoddeśena krayavidhāyakam | daśabhiḥ iti tu vākyāntara (vā) prāptadaśadravyānuvādaḥ | na ca pūrvoktādhikaraṇadvayavirodhaḥ | bhūyo vaiṣamyāt | tathā hi ādhānādhikaraṇe, ya evaṃ vidvān iti vākyaṃ tāvat atha saṃbhārān saṃharanti iti saṃbhāravidhiśeṣaṃ na vidhāyakaṃ bhavitumarhati | naivaṃ daśabhirityādivākyaṃ vidhyantaraśeṣatayā śrutamitīdamekaṃ vaiṣamyam | vasantādivākye vidhiśravaṇam | naivaṃ gavādivākye vidhiśravaṇamitīdamaparam | pramāṇāntaraprāptādhānānuvādena brāhmaṇādikartṛkatve nimitte vasantādikālavidhāne kālasyānupādeyatvātprāpte’pi karmaṇyupādeyatvaṃ kāle tu vidheyatvamiti vaiyadhikaraṇyāpattiḥ vasantādivākye | gavādivākye tu naiva vidheyatvopādeyatvayorvaiyadhikaraṇyaṃ, gavāderupādeyatvādityanyadapi vaiṣamyam | vasantādivākye sarvakartṛkatvavat sarvakālasyāpyādhāne prāptisaṃbhavena brāhmaṇakartṛkatve kālavidhiḥ kāle nimitte kartṛvidhirityatra vinigamanāvirahādubhayaviśiṣṭādhānavidhirāvaśyakaḥ | gavādivākye tu naivaṃ vinigamanāviraha iti itaradapi vaiṣamyam | evaṃ etāni vai daśa yajñā yajñāyudhāni iti vākyaṃ yajñāyudhāni saṃbharanti ityetadvidhiśeṣaṃ na vidhirbhavati | ākhyātaṃ ca nāsmin vākye’sti | vaiśabdaścātra śrūyate | sphyādivākyasyānuvādakatve bahūnāṃ vākyānāṃ vaiyarthyamuddhananādernityaprāptyabhāvānnityavacchravaṇavirodha iti uddhananādivākyānāṃ vidhāyakatvam | prakṛte tu daśabhiriti vākye krīṇātītyākhyātaṃ śrūyate | asya ca vidhāyakatvābhāve vākyavaiyarthyam | vidhāyakatve’pyasya na gavādivākyānāṃ vidhāyakatvahāniḥ | krīṇātīnāṃ bahūnāmanuvādakatvaṃ tu, daśabhiḥ krīṇāti iti vākyasyānuvādakatvāśrayaṇādvaram | padādvākyasya pradhānatvāt | ata evāgatyā vasantādivākye ṣaṣṭhavidhiprakārāśrayaṇam | prakṛte tu gatisattvānna tadyuktamiti ||
atra ca taṃ vai daśabhiḥ krīṇāti iti vākyavihitakrayānuvādena
gavādivākyavihitadravyāṇāṃ kathaṃ tasminneva vākye daśabhiriti anuvāda iti tu na śaṅkanīyam | vāyavyaṃ śvetamālabheta iti vākyavihitavāyavyayāgānuvādenālambhasya codakaprāptasya vāyavyavākya evānuvāda iti siddhāntāt | ata eva dvaitīyīkādhānādhikaraṇapūrvapakṣe tāntrikairuktam | saṃbhāreṣvagnimādadhāti ityādhānaprāpako vidhiḥ | atra hi na saṃbhārāvidheyāḥ, pramāṇāntaraprāptatvāt | nāpi tadadhikaraṇakatvam| tasminnādhīyatāmiti mantravarṇādeva prāptatvāt | ata ādhānamātravidhirayamiti | evaṃ ca saṃbhāreṣvagnimādadhāti iti vākyotpannādhānānuvādena saṃbhāravākyamantravarṇābhyāṃ prāpitayoḥ saṃbhāratadadhikaraṇakatvayostasminneva vākye saṃbhāreṣvityanuvādābhyupagamāt | iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ paramate gavādivākyānāṃ tattadguṇaviśiṣṭavibhinnakrayavidhāyakatvābhyupagamāt gavādiśabdānāṃ matvarthe lakṣaṇāvaśyaṃbhāvena taduttaratṛtīyayā krayagatakaraṇatvasyaiva bodhyatvāpattiḥ; matvarthalakṣaṇāyāmapi kriyākārakasaṃbandhāparityāgopapādanakleśaśca | asmanmate tu, taṃ vai daśabhiḥ ityādivākyaprāptakrayānuvādena guṇavidhānābhyupagamānna doṣadvayamiti ||
nanu evamekahāyanyā krīṇāti, tayāruṇayā bhavitavyamityāśrayaṇe vākyabhedāpattiriti siddhāntyabhiprāyonnayanapūrvakaṃ taddūṣitavatā pūrvapakṣiṇā kathamāruṇyasya prākaraṇikasarvadravyaniveśaḥ svīkriyate | pakṣadvaye’pi vākyabhedasya tulyatvāditi cenna | kraye ekahāyanyāḥ tasyāṃ cāruṇyasya vidhānamiti siddhāntino vidhyāvṛttilakṣaṇavākyabhedaḥ | mama tu vākyabhaṅgalakṣaṇavākyabhedaḥ | vākyabhaṅgācca pradhānabhūtavidhyāvṛttiḥ pradhānadoṣa iti pūrvapakṣyāśayāt | aruṇayā piṅgākṣyā iti ca samānavibhaktyā śrauta evāruṇyaikahāyanyoḥ saṃbandhaḥ | krayānvayastu ekahāyanīdravyasya vākyāvaseya iti na vidhyāvṛttilakṣaṇavākyabheda iti siddhāntino vāstavāśayena vākyabhaṅgalakṣaṇavākyabhedapūrvapakṣoparyuktavidhayā siddhāntakaraṇopapatteḥ ||
nanvevamapyaruṇayeti tṛtīyāyā viśeṣaṇavibhaktitvena sādhutvamātrārthatvāpattirabhedasya saṃsargatvāditi cenna | samānavibhakterabhedārthakatvopagamāt | anyathā anumitipratibandhakatāyāṃ kalpanāgauravāpatteḥ | na ca tathāpi
viśeṣyavibhaktyaiva karaṇatvasya bodhitatvādviśeṣaṇavibhakteḥ kārakabodhakatvaviraha iti vācyam | saṃbandhenānyataravaiyarthyam iti nyāyena ubhābhyāmapyekasyaiva karaṇatvasya bodhanāt | nīlena jalamāharati ityādau nīlādipadottaravibhakteḥ karaṇatābodhakatvamāvaśyakam | ghaṭena jalamāharati ityādau ca
ghaṭādipadottaravibhaktestadāvaśyakam | nīlena ghaṭena jalamāharati ityādau ubhābhyāmeva vibhaktibhyāmekaṃ karaṇatvaṃ bodhyate | pratyekamavadhṛtasāmarthyayoranyatarasyākaraṇatve mānābhāvāt | na ca tathāpyananyābhihitakaraṇatānabhidhāyakatvādvibhakteḥ kiṃcidasvārasyamiti vācyam| ananyakaraṇakakriyākaraṇatābhidhāyitva eva svārasyadhurāviśrāntestadabhāvena tvanmate’pyasvārasyāvarjanīyatvāt | liṅgabādhanaṃ tvadhikaṃ tvanmate | aruṇāpadasya gumamātraparatve strīliṅgānupapatteḥ | liṅgasya vibhaktyapekṣayā prāthamyena jaghanyavibhaktyanusāreṇa prathamasthaliṅgasvārasyabādhanānupapatteḥ | ata eva na prathamayajñe pravṛñjyāt ityatra prathamaśrutaprathamaśabdasvārasyānurodhenānantarayajñapadasya prayogaparatvaṃ nirṇītam ||
yattvatra kaiściduktaṃ liṅgasya prātipadikādyarthatve prābalyahetoḥ prathamapratītatvasyābhāvāt strīliṅgāsādhāraṇena aruṇayeti vibhaktivikāreṇa ṭāppratyayamunnīya svayaṃvācakena prātipadikādyarthadyotakena vā liṅgasya pratyetavyatvāditi, tanna | aruṇayetyatra vibhaktervikārābhāvāt, prakṛtereva hi vikāraḥ | kiṃ ca rūpamālādinā kāvyapāṭhena ca vyutpattidaśāyāmeva madhurayā madhubodhitamādhvī ityādau ākarāntastrīliṅgastṛtīyaikavacanānta ityevaṃrītyā vyutpatteḥ | anyatrāpi pratisandhānācca liṅgasyaiva prathamapratītervibhaktiprāthamyāsiddheḥ | anuśāsanavidāṃ tu ṅyāpprātipadikāt ityanuśāsanena svārthadravyaliṅgasaṅkhyākārakāṇāṃ kramikatvena bodhyakrameṇa bodhakakrama iti śaiṣikakramanyāyena vā liṅgaprāthamyāvagamenānyatrāpi prathamāvagatakramānupardenaiva pratisandhānācca vibhaktiprāthamyāsiddheḥ | kiṃ ca svarūpeṇa vibhaktivikārajñānaṃ na ṭābunnāyakam | vibhaktivikāratvena tadjñānaṃ tvanuśāsanānanudhyāyināṃ durlabham | tadanudhyāyināṃ tu pūrvoktadiśā liṅgāvagatiprāthamyameveti prathamayajñanyāyapravṛttau na kiṃcidbādhakam ||
kiṃ caivamaruṇādiśabdānāṃ dravyalākṣaṇikatve, guṇavacanebhyo matupo lugiṣṭaḥ ityanuśāsanānarthakyam | nirūḍhalākṣaṇikatve ca śakyārthamātravivakṣayā prayogānāpattiḥ | nanvevaṃ yatrāruṇā piṅgākṣī meṣī labdhā kevalamekahāyanī gauśca labdhā, tatra, paramate krayaṃ pratyāruṇyasyāpi dravyavat karaṇatvāt prathamaśrutāruṇyānurodhena dravyasya tyāgaḥ | bhavanmate tu āruṇyasya dravyaśeṣatvāt dravyaguṇavirodhe ca dravyasya tyāgāyogāt dravyānurodhena guṇasyaiva tyāga ityanuṣṭhānabhedāpattiḥ | na ca tadyuktaṃ - devatā vigrahādyabhyupagame’pi dravyadevatāsāmye dravyasāmyasyātideśahetutvaṃ dravyasya sannikṛṣṭatvāt | na tu devatāsāmyasya | svarūpato
viprakṛṣṭatvādityanuṣṭhānaikyaparitrāṇāditi cenna | aruṇayaikahāyanyetyatra krayadravyaṃ pratyaruṇimaguṇo gotvajātiścetyubhayamapyaṅgam | tatrobhayoreva dravyāṅgatvāviśeṣe’pi prāthamyādevāruṇyānurodhenānantaraśrutajātibādha ityanuṣṭhānabhedābhāvāt | atra uciteyamanucitānyā ityuktam | unnītaṃ guruṇā iti pūrvoktaparāmarśasaukaryāya uciteyamanucitā seti vaktavyam tathāpyanyetyuktiḥ prakārāntareṇa paroktamaruṇādhikaraṇavyākhyānamapyanucitamiti vyañjanāya | tathā hi - kecidamūrtasya krayānvayānupapattyāruṇāpadasya lakṣaṇayā dravyaparatvāvaśyaṃbhāve piṅgākṣitvaviśiṣṭevāruṇyaviśiṣṭāpyekahāyanī yugapadeva krayārthatayā vidhīyata iti pūrvapakṣaṃ paramatavadeva guṇamātraparatvena siddhāntaṃ ca āhuḥ ||
aruṇāpadasya aikarūpyābhyupagamenaiva pūrvapakṣasiddhāntayoḥ pravartanaucityādidamapi anucitamiti bodhayitumanyetyuktam iti || 45 ||
vaiṣamyanairghṛṇye na sāpekṣatvāt kartā śāstrārthavattvāt parāttu tacchateḥ kṛtaprayatnāpekṣastu ityādyadhikaraṇārthamāha - 
ādāvīśvaradattayaiva puruṣassvātantryaśaktyā svayaṃ
tattadjñānacikīrṣaṇaprayatanānyutpādayan vartate |
tatropekṣya tato’numatya vidadhattannigrahānugrahau
tattatkarmaphalaṃ prayacchati tataḥ sarvasya puṃso hariḥ || 46 ||
brahmakāraṇavāde paroktadūṣaṇoddhāro’yam | asyārthaḥ - ādau sṛṣṭau | puruṣaḥ jīvaḥ | jātyekatvam | sarvasya puṃsa
iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | īśvaradattayā sarvaśeṣiṇā, sarvaniyantrā, bhagavatā svakaruṇayaivotpāditayā | anena sṛṣṭiyaugapadyamanugrahanibandhanamiti sūcitam | dattayetyanenoktārthalābhaḥ | svātantryaśaktyā icchāyāṃ satyāmanivāryatvaṃ svātantryaṃ, saiva śaktiḥ - kāryopayuktakāraṇagatadharmaḥ, tayā | yadyapi śaktiḥ adravyaprabhedaḥ; tathāpi kāryopayogyapṛthaksiddhaviśeṣaṇamiha śaktirityupacaryate | tattadjñānacikīrṣaṇaprayatanāni - tattatpadārthaviṣayakajñānacikīrṣāprayatnāni | kartumicchā - cikīrṣā | pravṛttyudyogaḥ prayatnaḥ | utpādayan - janayan, vartate | ābhyāṃ prathamapravṛttiḥ dvitīyādipravṛttayaśca vivakṣitāḥ | tatra - prathamapravṛttau upekṣya - anivāraṇamanutpādanaṃ ca kṛtvā | tataḥ - dvitīyādipravṛttiṣu | anumatya - sahakāribhāvaṃ prāpya | tannigrahānugrahau - ādyapravṛttau svatantrasya puruṣasya nigrahaṃ
duṣkarmajanyanarakādipradānasaṃkalpaṃ, anugrahaṃ satkarmajanyasvargādipradānasaṃkalpaṃ ca | vidadhat - kurvan | tattatkarmaphalaṃ - saṃkalpānusāreṇa svarganarakādirūpapuṇyāpuṇyaphalam | sarvasya puṃsaḥ - sarvapuruṣāṇām | prayacchati - dadāti | ka ityatrāha - hariḥ - 
brahmāṇaṃ śitikaṇṭhaṃ ca yamaṃ varuṇameva ca |
prasahya harate yasmāttasmāddhariritīryate ||
ityuktasarvapraśāsitā bhagavānityarthaḥ
īśvaradattayā svātantryaśaktyetyanena,
vaiyarthye yāvatā na syādvidhānapratiṣedhayoḥ |
niyantṛtvaśrutestāvān saṃkoco na tvataḥ param ||
bāhyāntaḥkaraṇavyāptitacchaktyādhānadhāraṇaiḥ |
sādhāraṇopakāraiḥ syādicchādeḥ kāraṇaṃ paraḥ ||
bhogāddaṣṭopanītārthasvābhāvyādvāsanānvitāt |
icchādermanasotpattau yuktodāsīnatā vibhoḥ ||
abhisandhyantaraprāptavastunyanyatra kenacit |
upayuktavyudāste hi tasya prāpayitā pumān ||
sāmarthye satyavāryatvāt svatantro jīva ucyate |
sa hyodane viṣajñānāt svecchāmapi niyacchati ||
sannidhāpakavaidhurye’pyarthe budiṃdha satāṃ nayan |
prayojako’numantā ca bhavatīśastadā tadā ||
pūrvapravṛtteśca phalamanumatyādikaṃ bhavet |
dayādīnāṃ guṇānāṃ ca sulabhaṃ viṣayāntaram ||
amātyasya svatantratve rājñastannāpahīyate |
evameva parasyāpi jīvasvātantryadāyinaḥ ||
na svātantryaṃ parādhīnasvarūpāt pracyutaṃ bhavet |
svātantryadāyinā puṃsā tatpravṛttyanivāraṇāt ||
iti vyāsapādābhihitasaṃgrahārthaḥ sūcitaḥ | ato jīvānāṃ svātantryaṃ vidhiniṣedhaśāstrasāphalyaṃ, paramātmanaḥ sarvakāraṇatvaṃ niraṅkuśasvātantryavattvaṃ ca sarvamupapannam || 46 ||
tarhi anivartanādikaṃ doṣaḥ syādityatrāha - 
duṣkarmasvanivartanānumanane puṃsaḥ karotyacyutaḥ
svātantryeṇa niraṅkuśena sa guṇaḥ śrutyā na doṣo hareḥ |
dṛṣṭaścāriṣu nigraho guṇatayā loke na doṣātmanā
na syādāśrayasiddhiraupaniṣadaṃ no cet pramāṇaṃ vacaḥ || 47 ||
pramāṇatarkābhāsāvalambanairbāhyakuddaṣṭibhirapracyāvanīyakāraṇatvaniyantṛtvādimahimā bhagavān | niraṅkuśena - apratihatena pāratantryāsamānādhikaraṇena | svātantryeṇa - icchāyāṃ satyāmanivāryatvena hetunā | puṃsaḥ - pūrvoktarītyā karaṇakalebaravitaraṇādirūpaiḥ sādharaṇopakārairupakṛtasya vāsanāvaśādduriteṣu pravartamānasya puruṣasya | duṣkarmasu - pāpeṣu | apādānasyādhikaraṇatvavivakṣayā saptamī | anivartanānumanane - 
ādyapravṛttāvanivāraṇaṃ dvitīyādipravṛttiṣvanumatiṃ ca | atra karmatvāśrayasyādhikaraṇatvavivakṣayā saptamī, duṣkarmasviti | karoti - tadvān bhavati | saḥ - svātantryam | śrutyā - nityanirdoṣāpauruṣeyavedena | hareḥ - heyapratibhaṭasya sarvaniyanturbhagavataḥ | guṇaḥ - utkarṣāpādakadharmaḥ | guṇāpekṣayā sa iti puṃstvam | saḥ - svātantryam | doṣo na - apakarṣāpādakadharmo na | śrutyetyanuṣajyate | dharmigrāhakaśrutyaiva svātantryasya guṇatvaṃ doṣatvavirahaśca siddhamityarthaḥ | śrutestarkānugrahamāha - dṛṣṭaśceti | caḥ kiṃ cetyarthakaḥ | loke ariṣu - svaśāsanātivṛttiviṣaye | nigrahaḥ - aparādhānuguṇaduḥkhaprayojakatā | guṇatayā ddaṣṭaḥ | rājñāmiti śeṣaḥ | doṣātmanā - doṣatvena ātmasvabhāvo na ddaṣṭaḥ | rājñāṃ svānabhimateṣu uttarottaramaparādhānujñānamapi hi nigraharūpaṃ ddaṣṭam | evaṃ pāpeṣvanumananamapi prāktanaduritajanyanigrahaphalarūpamiti yathā karmaphaladāyitvaṃ svātantryaṃ guṇa eva na tu doṣa iti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ svātantryasya guṇatve śrutiranukūlatarkaśca uktau | atrāyaṃ vikalpo’bhipretaḥ - kimīśvarānabhyupagamena svātantryaṃ doṣa ityucyate ? uta tadabhyupagameneti | tatrādyaṃ dūṣayati - āśrayasiddhirna syāditi | īśvarā(na)bhyupagamena svātantryaṃ doṣa ityucyate - ityāśrayabhāvādanabhyupagamakalpo na kalpata ityarthaḥ | dvitīyaṃ pratikṣipati - aupaniṣadamiti | aupaniṣadaṃ - upaniṣadbhavam | vacaḥ vākyam | pramāṇaṃ no cet - tvayā anabhyupagataprāmāṇyakaṃ cet | āśrayasiddhirna syāt svātantryarūpadharmasyāśrayo na sidhyet |
avyavadhānena brahmapratipādakavedabhāgaviśeṣa upaniṣat | upa niṣīdatītyupaniṣat | gahane hīyamupaniṣaṇṇetyuktam | gahane brahmaṇyupaniṣaṇṇeti tadvyākhyānam | upaniṣatpadādbhavādhikārīyo’ṇpratyayaḥ | bhavatvaṃ ca ghaṭakatvam | atra svātantryeṇa niraṅkuśena sa guṇaḥ śrutyā iti na syādāśrayasiddhiraupaniṣadaṃ no cet pramāṇaṃ vacaḥ iti ca vadato’yamabhiprāyaḥ | anumānena svātantryasya doṣatvaṃ vadatā tvayā kiṃ śrutiḥ pramāṇam ityabhyupagamyate, na vā | ādye dharmigrāhakapramāṇabhūtaśrutyaiva bhagavatsvātantryasya guṇatvaṃ siddhamiti tasya doṣatvānumānaṃ kālātyayāpadiṣṭaṃ syāt | dvitīye śrutyaikasamadhigamyasyeśvarasyāsiddhyā svātantryaṃ kasya doṣa ityucyata iti hetorāśrayāsiddhiḥ syāditi | idaṃ ca vistareṇa vyāsapādairanugṛhītam | tathā ca dharmigrāhakapramāṇasiddhaguṇabhāvasvātantryaprayuktatvāt janmāntaraduṣkṛtaphalapradānarūpatvācca bhagavato jīvasya duṣkarmasvanivartanamanumatiśca guṇo na tu doṣa iti bhāvaḥ (ityādi) || 47 ||
dayādiguṇānāṃ viṣayamāha - 
duṣkarmavyavasāyatastu virato yastasya puṃsaḥ purā
bhūyojanmasamarjitānyagaṇitānyāgāṃsyanādṛtya yat |
tasyānantasukhāptaye ca yatate lakṣmīsahāyasvayaṃ
tatkāruṇyapurassaro guṇagaṇastasyāyamujjṛmbhate || 48 ||
yaḥ - puruṣaḥ | duṣkarmavyavasāyataḥ virataḥ - trividhāpacārakartavyatāniścayānnivṛtto bhavati | tuśabdaikavacanābhyām asya durlabhatvaṃ śraiṣṭhyaṃ cocyate | lakṣmīsahāyaḥ - devyā kāruṇyarūpayā rakṣakaḥ ityuktarītyā kāruṇyādiguṇodbhāvikayā lakṣmyā yuktaḥ | aṅgīkartṛtvamanumantṛtvaṃ ca patnyāḥ | tāntrikairuktam | lakṣmītantre’pi - 
ekāhaṃ parām śaktistasya devī (sanāsanī)
karo(ti) nikhilaṃ kṛtyaṃ sarvabhāvānugāminī ||
iti sarvakṛtyeṣu bhagavadanuvidhānaṃ svayamevoktam | mūlaprakṛtibhagavadbuddhiśaktyābhimānitvādinā cāsyāḥ kāraṇatvādivyapadeśa iti tatratatroktam | evaṃbhūto bhagavān, tasya - atimātraṃ prātikūlyavyavasāyanivṛttasya, purā - prācīnai rbhūyobhiḥ bahutaraiḥ janmabhiḥ | samārjitāni vācikāditrairūpyeṇa dhanavadicchāpūrvakaṃ saṃgṛhītāni | agaṇitāni - sarvajñena
bhagavatāpi iyattayā durjñeyāni | āgāṃsi - aparādhān | anāddatya - daṇḍāprayojakatayā saṃkalpya | tasya - puruṣasya | anantasukhāptaye - aparicchinnabrahmānubhavatatpūrvakatatparicaryājanyaniratiśayānandaprāptaye | prāptiṃ kartum | svayaṃ ca yatate - svayameva pravartate | svayamiti lakṣmyā, eva kāreṇa sukṛtasya ca vyāvṛttiḥ | anena prayojayitṛtvasya viṣayo darśitaḥ
| iti yat tasmāt - lakṣmīpateḥ kāruṇyapurassaraḥ kāruṇyaprabhṛtikaḥ | anenāśrayaṇīyatāpādakeṣu guṇeṣu dayāyāḥ prādhānyamuktam | atra hi dayā (bhiḥ) svārthanirapekṣāparaduḥkhāsahiṣṇutā iti bhāṣitam | atra ca paraduḥkhānāṃ (asatyatā) labdhā | sā ca pratikūlatayā vedanīyatvarūpaiveti paraduḥkhaduḥkhitvarūpā dayetyuktaṃ bhavati | etaccātra kāruṇyaśabdopādānena sūcitam | ṛṣeḥ puṇyātmanastasya kāruṇyaṃ samapadyata śokaḥślokatvamāgataḥ iti hyuktam | ayaṃ pramāṇasiddhaḥ; svaviṣayatayā svānubhavasiddhirvā granthakṛto’bhipretā | guṇagaṇaḥ - mokṣapradatvaupayikavātsalyādiguṇaprakaraḥ | ujjṛmbhate - asaṃkucitaviṣayalābhena prakāśate | anena dayādīnāṃ viṣaya uktaḥ | tadidaṃ bhāṣitaṃ bhagavatā svaśāsanātivṛtti vyavasāyamātreṇānādyanantakalpopacitadurviṣahānantāparādhānaṅgīkāreṇa niratiśayasukhasaṃvṛddhaye svayameva prayatate iti || 48 ||
atha māyāvādinirāsaprakaraṇam |
evaṃ brahmakāraṇavāde paroktadūṣaṇāni parihṛtāni | atha paramataṃ dūṣayitumupakramate - 
ādau bhedaśrutīnāmanṛtaviṣayatā lakṣaṇā caikyavācāṃ
dūreṇaiva prahāṇaṃ tadubhayaghaṭanātatparāṇāṃ ca vācām |
pratyakṣādipramāṇasvarasagatihatistarkabādhaśca bhūyānmāyāvāde tadetat sakalamitarathā lakṣmaṇācāryapakṣe || 49 ||
ekasya māyāvādasya dūṣaṇena bauddhānāṃ sāṅkhyasya yādavabhāskarayośca pakṣāḥ pratikṣiptā bhavantīti sa evātra pratikṣipyate | vaiśeṣikaśca saṃsthānajātinirūpaṇaprasaṅgena pūrvameva nirastaḥ | śaivapakṣastu kastvaṃ tattvavidasmi ityādinā vistareṇa dūṣayiṣyate | ataḥ parapakṣapratikṣepavādārthaḥ sarvo’pyukto bhavati ||
tathā hi - lakṣaṇā caikyavācām ityuktam | tatra śaṃkarapakṣe lakṣaṇā nāsti | viśeṣyaikyaparatvābhyupagamāt | kiṃ tu viśiṣṭābhidhānasvarasasya śabdasya
viśeṣaṇāviṣayakabodhajanakatvamātram | evaṃ cātra lakṣaṇāśabdasyāsvarasyaparatvaṃ vaktavyam | lakṣaṇāśabdamukhyārthasyāpi saṃbhave so’pi grāhya eveti, lakṣaṇā caikyavācā mityetadyādavabhāskarasaṃgrāhakam | tatra yādavamate sanmātrabrahmapariṇāmajīveśvaravācipadayorghaṭaśarāvapadayoriva sāmānādhikaraṇyamanupapannamityanyatarasya upādānasanmātrabrahmalakṣakatvamāśrayaṇīyam | bhāskarapakṣe’pi aupādhikabhedābhedābhyupagamāt jīveśvaravācipadayorghaṭākāśapaṭākāśapadayoriva tadanupapannamiti anyatarasyānupahitalakṣakatvamāvaśyakam | (śaṃkarapakṣe ca viśeṣaṇāṃśaprahāṇādasvārasyam | ) ato lakṣaṇā caikyavācā mityatra yādavabhāskarayoḥ saṃgrahaḥ |
(pratyakṣāt nyādinā) boddhānāṃ dūṣaṇaṃ spaṣṭam | padārthānāṃ sthityutpattyoḥ sarvalokapratyakṣasiddhayostairapalāpāt | doṣādīnāṃ mithyātve’pi bhramāṅgīkāraśca pratyakṣaviruddhaḥ | cinmātrasya satyatvam anyasyāsatyatvaṃ ca śaṃkarayogācārayoraviśiṣṭam | sāṅkhyaśaṃkarayośca nirvikārasvaprakāśaikarasacinmātrasya svavyatiriktasākṣitvamaviśiṣṭam | ata eva vipratiṣedhāccāsamañjasam iti sūtre bhagavatā bhāṣyakṛtā śaṃkarapakṣe sāṅkhyapakṣadūṣaṇamatidiṣṭam | teṣāmapyuktarītyāvidyāsākṣitvādhyāsādyasaṃbhavādasāmañjasyamevetyataḥ śaṃkarapakṣadūṣaṇena sarvaṃ dūṣitaṃ bhavatīti sa evātra spaṣṭaṃ dūṣyate | taduktam sāṃkhyasaugatacārvākasaṃkarācchāṅkarodayaḥ | dūṣaṇānyapi tānyatra bhūyastadadhikāni ca || iti ||
ādāviti - māyāvāde brahmabhramahetubhūtānirvacanīyānupapannaikaveṣāvidyāṅgīkārapakṣe śaṃkaramata ityarthaḥ | ādau - prathamam | vakṣyamāṇadoṣāpekṣayā prādhānyaṃ sūcitam | bhedasya śrutaya iti vigrahaḥ | tatpratipādakatvaṃ, tadvyāpyadvitvādipratipādakatvaṃ ca ṣaṣṭhyarthaḥ | tāsāṃ, anṛtaviṣayatā - mithyābhidheyakatā | śaradaḥ kṛtārthatā itivat puṃvadbhāvaḥ | idaṃ rajatamityādivākyavat asadarthakatvaṃ pradhānadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ | tattvāvedakatvalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ hīyeteti hṛdayam | atra bhedaśrutiśabdena nānāvidhadravyadevatādeśakāletikartavyatāphalādhikāriprāyaścittādyupaskṛtoccāvacayāgadānahomādyātmakakarmavidhiparaḥ kṛtsno’pi pūrvabhāgaḥ, uttarabhāge’pi - cetanācetanatatsṛṣṭyāditatphalasukhaduḥkhataddhetuśabdasparśāditadubhayaniyantṛ - īśvaraguṇavigrahatatprāptyupāyavidyāviśeṣatatphalagativiśeṣāditatpūrvakaparipūrṇabrahmānubhavatatpūrvakatatparicaryātadupapādakadivyamahiṣīpariccha
dādiparasahasrabhedapratipādakasandarbhāśca vivakṣitāḥ | ekyavācām
abhedapratipādakasāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśayuktatattvamasyādivākyānām | lakṣaṇā - viśeṣaṇāṃśatyāgaḥ | cakāraḥ padāntaravaiyarthyasamuccayaparaḥ | śaktigrahānusāreṇa pravṛttinimittadharmaprakārakopasthitiḥ prathamabhāvinī | anantaraṃ taduktajātiguṇādyanyonyaikyāpattyādikutarkajanitakṣobheṇa, viśeṣaṇāṃśatyāgena viśeṣyasvarūpamātrabodha iti, vilaṃbitatvasāmyādviśeṣaṇāviṣayakaviśeṣyamātraviṣayakajñānajanakatvena lakṣaṇāśabdaprayogaḥ | prayojanaṃ tūktameva ||
tadubhayetyādi - tāsāmubhayīnāṃ - bhedaśrutīnāmabhedaśrutīnāṃ ca ghaṭanāyāṃ bhedābhedayorviṣayabhedapradarśanena virodhaśamanapūrvakaṃ prāmāṇyopapādane, ( tarkaparāṇāṃ ) tātparyavatīnām | vācāṃ - yasyātmā śarīram ityādiśrutīnām | sarvanāmno vṛttimātra iti tadubhayeti puṃvadbhāvaḥ | vastvabhiprāyeṇedamuktam | bhedaśrutibhirjīveśvarabhedāvagamāt, abhedaśrutibhiśca tayorabhedāvagamāt, bhedābhedayośca virodhādanyatarabādhanaṃ vinānupapattau yasyātmā śarīram ityādibhiścidacitorbrahmaśarīratvāvagamāt, śarīrasya ca jātiguṇayordravyaṃ pratīva śarīriṇaṃ pratyapṛthaksiddhaprakārataikasvabhāvatayā prakāravāciśabdānāṃ prakāriparyantaśaktatvāt tattvamasi ityādau śvetaketuśarīrakasya sārvajñyādiviśiṣṭasya caikyaṃ samānavibhaktyā bodhyate yathā nīlamutpalamityādau nīlatvaviśiṣṭasya utpalatvaviśiṣṭasya ceti | bhedaśrutibhiśca prakāraprakāriṇormitho bhedaḥ pradipādyate | ato bhedābhedaśrutyorna virodha ityayamartho ghaṭakaśrutibhiravagamyata iti vastusthitimanurudhya tadubhayaghaṭanātatparāṇāmi tyuktam | na tu ghaṭakatvaṃ parābhyupetamiti mantavyam ||
dūreṇaiva prahāṇam - atyantaparityāgaḥ | yasyātmā śarīram ityādīnāmapi bhedaśrutitvāttatprayuktamaprāmāṇyamekaṃ, jagadbrahṇaṇorvyāvahārikakāryakāraṇabhāvābhyupagamavat tathāvidhaśarīraśarīribhāvābhyupagamābhāvāttadaṃśe’pyaprāmāṇyaṃ dvitīyaṃ, bhedaśrutīnāmasatyaviṣayakatvādabhedaśrutīnāṃ coktarītyā svarūpamātropasthāpakatvādbhedābhedayorabhāvena virodhasyāprasaktyā tacchamanāya ghaṭakānapekṣaṇāddhaṭakatvenāpyaprāmāṇyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ, tadidaṃ dūreṇaiva prahāṇami tyanena vivakṣitam | dūreṇa prahāṇe prahāṇahānayorarthasiddhatvāt | kiṃ ca tadubhayetyanena aikyavācāṃ sāmānyena parāmarśādanantaraṃ yogyatayā viśeṣaniścayasiddhiḥ | tatraikyaśrutiranekavidhā | kācitsamānavibhaktinirdeśenābhedabodhikā | yathā - tattvamasi brahmadāśā ityādikā | kācicca
sajātīyadvitīyarāhityātmakaikatvabodhikā | yathā ekaśśāstā advitīyam ityādikā | kācittu bhedaniṣedhikā | yathā - yatra tvasya sarvam neha nānāsti ityādikā | trividhāyā apyaikyavācastātparyaṃ ghaṭakaśrutyā bodhyate | tatra viśeṣaṇadvayāśrayavastvabhedastvabhedaśrutyarthaḥ | śarīratayā cidacidviśiṣṭatvena sajātīyavastvantaraṃ nāstīti sajātīyadvitīyaśūnyatvātmakaikatvaśrutyarthaḥ | bhagavatprakāratayā vinā svaniṣṭhaṃ kiṃcidapi nāstīti bhedaniṣedhaśrutyartha iti trividhaikyaśrutitātparyaṃ yasyātmā śarīram ityādiśrutyā bodhyate | sarvasyā apyaikyaśruterbhedaśrutyā saha virodhena ghaṭakaśrutyā vinā parasparānupamardena prāmāṇyāsiddheḥ | tatra viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyayorviśeṣaṇānāṃ mitho bhedaḥ prathamadvitīyayorbhedaśrutyarthaḥ | tṛtīye tvabrahmātmakatvaniṣedhe’pi svarūpāniṣedhāttadbheda eva bhedaśrutyarthaḥ | ato jagadbrahmaṇoḥ śarīraśarīritayā viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvavodhakaghaṭakaśrutibhirbhedaśrutyabhedaśrutyoḥ virodhasamādhānena prāmāṇyamupapādyata iti tadubhayaghaṭanetyādyuktam ||
pratyakṣaṃ - indriyajanyaṃ, ādi padenānumānaṃ gṛhyate tathāvidhasya, pramāṇasya, svarasagatiḥ - saviśeṣavastubodhakatvasvārasyaṃ, tasyāḥ, hatiḥ - vyāghātaḥ, tyāga ityarthaḥ | pratyakṣasya, sanmātra (viṣayakā) bhyupagamāt, anumānena nirviśeṣavastupakṣīkaraṇena nityatvādisādhanāt, prapañcamithyātvānumānena dharmigrāhakapratyakṣabādhābhyupagamācca pratyakṣādipramāṇasvārasyahānirityarthaḥ | tarketyādi | tarkonāma vyāpyāropeṇa vyāpakāropaḥ | tarkayāmyāpādayāmītyādyanubhavasākṣiko vyāptigrahādidvārā pramāṇānugrāhako jñānaviśeṣaḥ | tasyāpi saviśeṣaviṣayatvāt nirviśeṣavastuvādināṃ tadanādaraḥ spaṣṭaḥ | pratyakṣasya sanmātragrāhitve sarvapratītīnāmaviśeṣaprasaṅgāt pravṛttinivṛttivyavasthāvirahaprasaṅgaḥ | nirviśeṣavastunyanumānenaikatvādisādhane sādhyasādhanadharmaiḥ saviśeṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ | brahmaṇaḥ svayaṃprakāśatve tirodhānāsiddhiprasaṅgaḥ | tirodhāne’pyanivṛttiprasaṅgaḥ | prakāśasvarūpasya tirodhāne svarūpanāśaprasaṅgaḥ | brahmaṇo jñānaviṣayatve tadjñānānmokṣāsiddhiprasaṅgaḥ | anirvacanīyotpattyabhyupame tasya pārśvasthairupalabhyamānatvaprasaṅgaḥ | pravṛttyādyabhāvaprasaṅgaśca śabdasyāpyaparokṣajanakatve pratyakṣalakṣaṇādisāṃkaryaprasaṅgaḥ | dṛkddaśyayoḥ saṃbandhānupapattirityuktagrāhyalakṣaṇayogākhyatarkābhyupagame caitanyavyavahārāsiddhiprasaṅgaḥ | bhedapadārthanirāse brahmajaḍayoraikyāpattirityāditarkāṇāṃ tairanādarāt tarkabādhaśca bhūyān ityuktam | cakāreṇa
pramāṇānāmayathāvasthitaprābalyadaurbalyavyavasthāvyatyāsassamuccīyate | sautrāṇāṃ ca
nyāyānāṃ bādhaḥ spaṣṭa eva | so’pi cakāreṇa saṃgṛhyate ||
tadidaṃ sarvaṃ svapakṣe nāstītyāha - tadetaditi | bhedaśrutirabhedaśrutirghaṭakaśrutiḥ pratyakṣādipramāṇaṃ tarkaśceti pūrvoktaṃ sarvamidam | lakṣmaṇācāryapakṣe - bhagavadrāmānujasiddhānte | itarathā pakṣāntaroktaprakārātiriktaprakārayuktaṃ krameṇa satyaviṣayakatvaviśiṣṭābhidhāyitvavirodhaśāmakatvasaviśeṣaviṣayatvapratitarkaparāhatyabhāvarūpasamīcīnaprakārayuktaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | tadupapādanaṃ tu granthagauravabhiyā na likhyate | atra tadetat sakalamitarathā - ityanena
ādau bhedaśrutīnāmṛtaviṣayakatā mukhyatā caikyavācām
ārādevānurodhastadubhayaghaṭanātatparāṇāṃ ca vācām |
pratyakṣādipramāṇasvarasagatimitistarkabodhaśca bhūyān
iti pāṭhyamiti sūcyate | māyāvāde lakṣmaṇācāryapakṣe ityābhyāṃ svaparapakṣayorlakṣmaṇameghanādanyāyaḥ sūcitaḥ || 49
gaḍḍhandya ghddhaḍḍhadhtdṛdvadma
pūrvaślokoktānyeva paramatadūṣaṇāni krameṇa bahubhiḥ ślokairvistareṇāha na dvaitaṃ pratipādayantyupaniṣadvācaḥ prasiddhaṃ hi tat
kiṃtvadvaitamananyagocaratayā tadvedyamāsthīyatām |
aprāpte khalu śāsramarthavaditi vyarthaḥ prayāso yataḥ
prakhyātāditarastu śāsraviṣayo bhedastvadadvaitavat || 50 ||
