upaniṣadvācaḥ - vedāntavākyāni | dvaitaṃ - bhedam | na pratipādayanti - na bodhayanti | dvayorbhāvo dvitā, dvitaiva dvaitam | prajñāditvāt svārthe’ṇpratyayaḥ | dvitvavācinā ca dvaitaśabdena tadvyāpako bhedo lakṣyate | pṛthakatvaniveśitvāt saṅkhyayā karmabhedaḥ syādityukteḥ | vedāntabākyāni na bhedapratipādakānītyarthaḥ | kuta ityatrāha - prasiddhaṃ hīti | tat - dvaitam | prasiddhaṃ hi - pratyakṣadyavagataṃ khalu | ato bhedo na śāstrapratipādya ityarthaḥ | śāstraṃ tvasannikṛṣṭe’rthe śabdavijñānāt iti sūtramatrābhipretam ||
asannikṛṣṭavācā ca dvayamatra jihāsitam |
tādrūpyeṇa paricchittistadviparyayato’pi vā
ityuktarītyā śāsrapratipādyatvarūpeṇa mānāntarābodhitatvasya śāstrapratipādyatve’pekṣitatvāttadabhāvādasya na śāstrapratipādyatvamiti bhāvaḥ | atra ghaṭapaṭādibhedasya pratyakṣasiddhatvaṃ nāhamīśvara iti īśvarabhedapratyakṣatvaṃ ca vivakṣitam | tatra hi śāsrapratipādyaṃ kimityatrāha - kiṃ tviti | kiṃ tu - bhedasya śāstrapratipādyatvābhāve | advaitam abhedaḥ | ananyagocaratayā - śāsretarapramāṇāviṣayatvena hetunā | tadvedyam upaniṣatpratipādyam | āsthīyatām - pratijñāyatām yuṣmābhiriti śeṣaḥ | kuta idamityatrāha - aprāpta ityādi | śāstram ajñātajñāpanaikapravaṇaṃvedarupaṃ hitānuśāsanam | aprāpte - sādhakabādhakapramāṇāviṣaye’rthe | arthavatkhalu - ajñātajñāpanarūpaprayojanavat kila | ato mānāntarānadhigatatvādabheda eva śāsrabodhya ityarthaḥ | iti prayāsaḥ - uktārthapratipādanaprayuktaḥ kleśagarbhaḥ prayatnaḥ | yasu prayatne | vyarthaḥ - niṣprayojanaḥ | kuta idabhityatrāha - yata iti | prakhyātāt - mānāntaraviṣayāt ghaṭapaṭadibhedāt | itaro bhedaḥ - kāryakāraṇabhāvaśeṣaśeṣibhāvaniyantṛniyamyabhāvopāsyopāsakabhāvaprāpyaprāpakabhāvarūpo bhedastu | tvadadvaitavat śāsraviṣayaḥ tvadabhimatajīveśvarābhedena tulyaṃ,
śāsrapratipādyatvarūpaśāsrasaṃbandhabhāgītyarthaḥ | visinotīti viṣayaḥ ṣiñ banghane ata stena tulyaṃ kriyā cedi ti vatipratyayopapattiḥ | adhyāhṛtabhavatikriyayā vā tadupapattiriti | yathā so’yaṃ devadatta ityādyaikyapratyakṣe’pi jīvaparayoraikyasya tadaviṣayatvāt śāsrapratipādyatvaṃ tvayā svīkriyate; tathā ghaṭapaṭādibhedasya pratyakṣādisiddhatve’pi jīvaparayorniyantṛniyamyabhāvādirūpabhedasya tadaviṣayatvācchāsrapratipādyatvamasmābhiḥ svīkriyate | ato na dvaitaṃ pratipādayantītyādinoktaḥ prayāsastava vādivijayānupayuktatvādviphala iti mṛṣāvādinaṃ pratyuktiriyaṃ siddhāntinaḥ ||
tadidamāhuḥ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādāḥ - yadi bhedamātrasya lokasiddhatvāt aprāpte hi śāsramarthavat iti nyāyādabheda eva śāsravedya ityucyate; tarhi so’yaṃ devadatta ītyādāvabhedamātrasya lokasiddhatvāt aprāpte śāsrasyārthavattvācca śāsreṇāmedapratipādanamayuktam | devadattādyaikyaṃ hi lokasiddham | na jīvaparayoḥ | atastayorabhedaḥ śāsravedya iti cettarhi ghaṭapaṭādibheda eva lokasiddho na tu jīvaparayorniyamyatvaniyantṛtvadhāryatvadhārakatvaśeṣatvaśeṣitvavyāpyatvavyāpakatvalakṣaṇabhedo na lokasiddha iti sa bhedaḥ śāsrapratipādya eveti || 50 ||
jīvānāṃ mitho bhedasya śrutipratipādyatvamāha - 
yacchvetāśvataraśrutirnṛbahutāmeko bahūnāmiti
brūte tatra vidhirviśiṣṭaviṣayastvaṣṭākapālādivat |
nityatvātmabahutvamatra nigamādanyatra vidyaḥ kathaṃ
pratyaktavena parāktayā svaparayormuktau ca bhedaḥ sphuṭaḥ || 51 ||
śvetāśvataraśrutiḥ - nityo nityānāṃ cetanaścetanānāmeko bahūnāṃ yo vidadhāti kāmān iti śvetāśvataropaniṣadgatamantraḥ | eko bahūnām iti eko bahūnāmityaṃśena | nṛbahutāṃ - ātmabahutām | brūte - bodhayati iti yat | tatra - niruktaśruterātmabahutvabodhakatāvāda ityarthaḥ | aṣṭākapālādivat - yadāgneyo’ṣṭākapālaḥ
ityādividhivat; aṣṭākapālatvaviśiṣṭāgneyapuroḍāśavidhivadityarthaḥ | atrāpi vatipratyayopapādanaṃ pūrvavad draṣṭavyam | viśiṣṭaviṣayovidhiḥ - nityabahucetanaviśiṣṭa(pradhāna)viṣayājñātajñāpanarūpavidhiḥ | āsthīyate - pratijñāyate | āsthīyatāmityanuvṛttasya vibhaktivipariṇāmena sabandhaḥ | tuśabdaḥ śaṅkāvyudāsakaḥ | nityo nityānāṃ
cetanaścetanānāmeko bahūnāṃ yo vidadhāti kāmān ityasya kāmavidhānaparatvānna nityatvabahutvayostātparyam; tathā sati vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt; anepekṣitaṃ cedaṃ cetananityatvabahutvapratipādanamiti śaṅkādvayam; viśiṣṭavidhitvāśrayaṇenānityānāmalpānāṃ kāmapradānādapi nityānāṃ bahūnāṃ kāmapradāne kathite hi kāmapradānasyātiśayo bhavatīti kāmapradāne’tiśayabodhalipsayāpekṣitatvopapādanena ca vyudastamiti sūcanāya tuśabdaprayogaḥ | nanu ātmabahutvasya lokasiddhatvānna tatra śāsrasya tātparyaṃ, aprāpte hi śāsramarthavadi ti śaṅkāyāmāha - nityatvetyādi | nityatvasahitā ātmānaḥ nityatvātmānaḥ | supeti vā tṛtīyeti vā yogavibhāgāt samāsaḥ | nityatvaviśiṣṭātmanāṃ bahutvaṃ nityatvātmabahutvam | atra - loke, nigamādanyatra - upadarśitaśruteranyataḥ, pratyakṣādita ityarthaḥ | kathaṃ vidmaḥ - na vidma ityarthaḥ | hetoradhikaraṇatvavivakṣayānyatreti saptamī ||
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - acitsaṃsṛṣṭātmabahutvapratītirlokasiddhā | pariśuddhātmasvarūpabahutvasya tu lokasiddhatvābhāvāt tatra śāsrasya tātparyaṃ saṃbhavatyeva | śrutau hi nityānāṃ bahūnāṃ cetanānāmiti saṃbandhādviśiṣṭasya vaiśiṣṭyamiti rītyā nityatvaviśiṣṭasya bahutvalābhānnityatve bahutvāvacchedakatā labhyate | aupādhikākārasyānityatvānniṣkṛṣṭasvarūpasyaiva nityatvāttadavacchedenātmabahutvabodhane sati pariśuddhātmasvarūpabahutvaṃ siddhaṃ bhavatīti tatra śāsratātparyaṃ saṃbhavatyeveti | na ca śrutau nityānāṃ nityaḥ cetanānāṃ cetana iti saṃbandhaḥ sānnidhyāditi vācyam | saṃbhavati sābhānādhikaraṇye vaiyadhikaraṇyasyānucitatvāt eko bahunāmityatra vairūpyāpatteśca tadayogāt | sānnidhyaṃ tu nityānāṃ kāmavidhānaṃ nityasyocitamityaucityavyañjanopakṣīṇamiti na sāmānādhikaraṇyabādhakam | sannidhiśca durbalapramāṇatvānna śrutirūpaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ bādhitumalam | śrutau nityānāṃ bahūnāmityatra viśiṣṭasya vaiśiṣṭyamiti rītyaiva bodha iti sūcanāyaiva śloke nityatvātmabahutvamiti tṛtīyāsamāsagarbhaḥ sandarbhaḥ svīkṛtaḥ na tu nityātmabahutvamiti karmadhārayagarbhaḥ | yadvā nityatvaṃ cātmabahutvaṃ ca nityatvātmabahutvamiti samāhāradvandvaḥ | tasya ca samūhārthakatvāt samūhasya caikakālāvacchedenaikadeśasthānekavastvātmakatvānnityatvabahutvayorātmanyekakālāvacchinnavṛttikatvasiddhyā pariśuddhātmasvarūpabahutvalābhaḥ | śrutau ca nityānāṃ bahūnāmiti sāmānādhikaraṇyopagamāt | tatra ca kālaikyamapi bhāsata iti tāntrikābhyupagamānnityatvabahutvayorātmasvekakālāvacchinnavṛttikatvalābhācchrati -
gatasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ śloke samāhāradvandvaprayogeṇa vivṛtam | taduktaṃ sudarśanabhaṭṭapādaiḥ - nityatvasamānādhikaraṇabahutve’bhihite pariśuddhātmasvarūpabahutvaṃ hyuktaṃ bhavati iti | ataḥ pṛthaktvaniveśitvanayādātmabahutvamātma bhedalambhakamiti hṛdayam ||
mūktāvapi bhedaśravaṇānniṣkṛṣṭātmanāṃ mitho bheda ityāha - pratyaktavenetyādinā | pratyaktavena - ahaṃbuddhibodhyatvena | parāktayā - idaṃbuddhibodhyatvena svaparayoḥ muktānāṃ svasya cānyasya ca mitho bhedaḥ sphuṭaḥ śrutiṣu spaṣṭa ityarthaḥ | pratyakatvaparākatvarūpaviruddhadharmādhyāsānmokṣadaśāyāmātmabhedhaḥ, śrutisiddha iti bhāvaḥ | śrutiṣu muktānāmahamidaṃvyavahāraḥ tatra tatra draṣṭavyaḥ | (yadvā svaparayorityantaṃ saṃsāradaśāyāṃ bhedasiddhiparam) muktāviti mokṣadaśāyāṃ tatsiddhiparam | idaṃ ca bhedaśrutīnāmanṛtaviṣayatānirasanaṃ bhedaniṣedhārthaṃ bhedānuvādarūpāṇi bhedaśravaṇāni iti pakṣanirāsasyāpyupalakṣaṇam || 51 ||
ādau bhedaśrutīnāmanṛtaviṣayatā ityuktaparābhyupagamo vistareṇa dūṣitaḥ | atha lakṣaṇā caikyavācām ityuktaṃ tatpakṣaṃ vistareṇa dūṣayati - 
sāmānādhikaraṇyabhāgiṣu padastomeṣu sarveṣu kiṃ
pratyāyyaṃ vyatiricyate pratipadaṃ vaktavyamadvaitinā |
yadvā neti na cedanarthakatayā nekātiriktaṃ padaṃ
paṭhyeta prativādinastvitarathā yuktiḥ svamāsīdati || 52 ||
vyā - parairhi samānādhikaraṇavākyasyākhaṇḍārthatvamuktam | tathā hi - sāmānādhikaraṇyasyaikārthaparatvavyutpatteraikārthyasyaca padānāṃ viśeṣaṇamātraparatve viśiṣṭaparatve cāsiddheḥ svarūpapātraikyaparatvaṃ satyādisamānādhikaraṇapadānāmiti | tadidaṃ nirasyati - sāmānādhikaraṇyetyādinā | sarveṣu - vyadhikaraṇeṣu
samānādhikaraṇeṣu ca | padastomeṣu - vākyeṣu madhye | sāmānādhikaraṇyabhāgiṣu - samānādhikaraṇeṣu padeṣu, samānamekamadhikaraṇaṃ vācyaṃ teṣāṃ tathoktāni samānādhikaraṇāni, teṣāṃ bhāvaḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ, tatra bhāgaḥ saṃbandhaḥ tadvatsvityarthaḥ karaṇasyaivādhikaraṇatvavivakṣayā saptamī | pratyāyyaṃ bodhyam | pratipadaṃ - tattatpadeṣu | vyatiricyate bhinnaṃ bhavati | yadvā - athavā | neti - na vyatiricyata iti praśne | advaitinā - nirviśeṣavastvabhyupagamavatā mṛṣāvādinā | kiṃ vaktavyaṃ - kiṃśabdaḥ kṣepārthakaḥ | uktakalpayoḥ naiko’pi kalpo vaktuṃ śakyata ityarthaḥ | samānādhikaraṇapadeṣu kiṃcitpadabodhyasya taditarapadabodhyādviśeṣo’sti vā na veti praśne
mṛṣāvādinā kiṃcidapyuttaraṃ vaktuṃ śakyamityarthaḥ | kuta ityatrāha - na cedityādi | buddhisthatvāt dvitīyaṃ śiraḥ prathamaṃ dūṣyate | na cetpratyāyyaṃ - na vyatiricyate cedityarthaḥ | ekātiriktaṃ - vivakṣitatattadekaikapadātiriktam | padam anarthakatayā padāntarābodhitārthabodhanarūpaprayojanaviraheṇa hetunā | na paṭhyeta - nādhīyītetyarthaḥ uktārthānāmaprayogaḥ iti nyāyāditi bhāvaḥ tathā ca paṭhyamānasya vaiyarthyaprasaṅga iti hṛdayam ||
idamupalakṣaṇaṃ - padānāṃ paryāyatvaprasaṅgaḥ | pravṛttinimittabhedābhāvena sāmānādhikaraṇyalakṣaṇahāniprasaṅgaḥ | sāmānādhikaraṇyapratyāyyasya aikyasya svarūpātireke sakhaṇḍatvaprasaṅgaḥ | anatireke tasya pratipadikāvagatasvarūpāvirodhānna viśeṣaṇatyāga ityapi bodhyam | sarveṣu padastomeṣvityanena sarveṣveva vākyeṣu padānāṃ vyutpattisiddhārthasaṃsargaviśeṣamātraṃ pratyāyyamiti samānādhikaraṇapadeṣu kevalaṃ vyutpattisiddhapravattinimittaprahāṇaṃ nocitam | samānavibhaktyavagatamaikyaṃ tu prātipadikāvagataviśeṣaṇasaṃbandhāvirodhena pradhānabhūtāviśeṣyaikya paramityayamarthaḥ sūcitaḥ ||
prathamaṃ śiro dūṣayati - itarathetyādinā | itarathā - pratyāyyaṃ pratipadaṃ bhinnaṃ cet satyādipadānāṃ tattatpadārthavirodhivyāvṛttimātraparatve virodhivyāvṛttyavacchinnasvarūpaparatve cetyarthaḥ prativādino yuktiḥ asmatprativādino’khaṇḍavākyārthasādhakatvenābhimataḥ samānavibhaktyavagataikyāsiddhirūpo hetuḥ | svam - prativādinaḥ svīyaṃ pakṣam | āsīdati - vyāghatakabhāvena prāpnotītyarthaḥ | satyādipadānāṃ virodhivyāvṛttimātraparatve pratiyogibhedena vyāvṛttibhedādaikyāsiddhiḥ, vyāvṛttyavacchinnasvarūpaparatve ca vyāvṛttīnā mupalakṣaṇatve padāntaravaiyarthyam, viśeṣaṇatve tvekārthatvāsiddhiriti dūṣaṇaṃ prativādinaḥ svapakṣe’pyāpatatīti bhāvaḥ | viśeṣaṇabhūtārthabhede’pi viśeṣyabhūtapradhānārthaikārthyasiddhiriti ca pakṣadvaye’pi samānam | paraṃ tu siddhānte padānāṃ mukhyatā, pakṣāntare tu tattadvirodhivyāvṛtti lakṣakatvamiti viśeṣa iti tuśabdārthaḥ || 52 ||
itthaṃ ślokatrayeṇa, ādau bhedaśrutīnāmanṛtaviṣayatā lakṣaṇā caikyavācām ityuktaparābhyupagamadvayaṃ vistareṇa dūṣitam | dūreṇaiva prahāṇaṃ tadubhayaghaṭanātatparāṇāṃ ca vācām ityetattu, ghaṭakaśrutīnāmapi bhedaśrutitvena bhedaśrutyanṛtaviṣayatādūṣaṇenaiva dūṣitamiti pṛthaṅ na dūṣitam | dūreṇaiva ityādinā pṛthaguddeśasūcitam yasyātmā śarīram ityādiṣu śarīrādiśabdānāṃ
tādadhīnyalakṣakatāyāḥ parābhimatatvena tadaṃśe lakṣaṇāpyāpatatītyevaṃrūpamadhikadūṣaṇaṃ tu dūreṇaiva prahāṇam ityatraivoktamiti na tatra vistarāpekṣā | ato’tra nirdeśakrameṇa pratyakṣādi pramāṇasvārasyahānim idārnī viśadayati - 
dṛśyatvādanṛtaṃ vigītamiti yaddaṣṭāntayanto jaguḥ
śuktyāropitarūpyamatra katicittatredamācakṣmahe |
dharmigrāhakamānadhikakṛtamidaṃ sopādhikatvaṃ punaḥ
saitraṃ vyaktamabhaṅguraṃ vyabhicaraliṅgaṃ ca bhaṅgāya vaḥ || 53 ||
parairitthamuktam - vigītaḥ prapañco mithyā dṛśyatvāt śuktirūpyavat ityanumānena prapañcamithyātvasiddhiḥ | svābhāvādhikaraṇe pratīyamānatvaṃ, svasamānādhikaraṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ, jñānanivartyatvaṃ vā mithyātvamiha sādhyadharmaḥ | jñānaviṣayatvarupaddaśyatvaṃ hetuḥ | dravyasamavetapratyakṣe saṃyuktasamavāyasyāvaśyakatvena tadabhāvena rajatatvānyathākhyāterasaṃbhavādanirvacanīyaṃ sākṣibhāsyaṃ śuktāvapūrvaṃ rajatamutpadyate | tacca dravyaṃ dṛṅnivatrtye ceti tadeva vyāptiniścayabhūmitayā ddaṣṭānta iti | tadidamanuvadati - ddaśyatvādi tyādinā | atra katicit mṛṣāvādina ityarthaḥ | śuktyāropitarūpyam - śuktiviśeṣyakabhramaprakārabhūtaṃ rajatam; yadvā rajatakāraṇatayā klṛptasāmagryabhāvena,
pārśvasthānupalambhena ca tatrāsaṃbhāvitotpattikameva kevalaṃ svīyakusṛṣṭyā śuktāvutpannatayā kathitaṃ rajatam | ddaṣṭāntayantaḥ santaḥ - vyāptiniścayabhūmibhūtadṛṣṭāntamācakṣāṇāḥ santaḥ | ddaṣṭāntasya sādhyavaikalyarūpasiddhāntyabhimatadūṣaṇasphoraṇāya śuktyāropitetyuktam | utpattayarthādrohateḥ ṇijantāt karmaṇi ktaḥ | atra ca dṛṣṭāntayanta ityatra tatkaroti tadācaṣṭe iti sūtreṇākhyānārthako ṇic | jñānapratyāsattyupagamāt, pañjīkaraṇasiddharajatākyavasattvopagamādvā rajatānyathākhyāteḥ, yathārthakhyātervo upagamāt rajatagrahopapatteśca na tatra rajatītpattyapekṣeti hṛdayam | vigītaṃ vivādādhyāsitam | jagaditi śeṣaḥ | uttaraśloke jagata ityukteḥ | ayaṃ pakṣanirdeśaḥ | anṛtaṃ - jñānanivatrtyatvarūpamithyātvavat | idaṃ sādhyasamarpakam | ddaśyatvāt jñānaviṣayatvāt | iyaṃ hetūktiḥ | iti yat vimataṃ jaganmithyā ddaśyatvāt śuktirūpyavat ityākārakaṃ yadanumānam | jaguḥ avadan | śiśupaśuprāyapuruṣarañcakatvāt gānatvoktiḥ | tasya vaidikasadaḥ śikhāmaṇeragrataḥ kvaparavādisaṃbhavaḥ iti pūrvoktadiśā jaguriti parokṣanirdeśaḥ | tatredamācakṣmahe - tasminnanumāne kālatyayāpadeśasopādhikatvavyāghātātmakadūṣaṇatrayamidam
uparitanasārghaślokena sūtrakārahṛdayānusāriṇo vayaṃ vadāma ityarthaḥ | tatrāsya ślokasyottarārghena prathamaṃ kālātyayāpadeśaṃ sopādhikatvaṃ cāha - dharmigrāhaketyādinā | idaṃ - jagatpakṣakadṛśyatvahetukamithyātvānumānam | dharmigrāhakamānena mithyātvarūpasādhyadharmaṃ prati dharmibhūtaprapañcagrāhiṇā pratyakṣeṇa prapañcasatyatvagrāhakeṇa, dhikkṛta - sādhyābhāvagrahaṇādbādhitam | ata idamanumānaṃ duṣṭamityarthaḥ | tadāhuḥ sudarśanabhaṭṭapādāḥ - tatrāśrayaḥ pratīto vā na vā ? na cedāśrayāsiddhiḥ | ( pratyakṣādipramāṇaiḥ satyatveneti tasya mithyātvasādhanaṃ dharmigrāhakapramāṇabadhitam iti | tathā caṇḍamārūte’pyuktaṃ - prapañcaviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ svaprakāśaṃ svataḥ pramāṇaṃ ca | ato ghaṭādiviṣayakatvapramātvagrāhiṇāpratyakṣeṇa pramāviṣayatvaṃ ghaṭādau gṛhītamiti uṣṇatvapratyakṣeṇānuṣṇatvānāmānasyeva mithyātvānumānasyāpi bādhaḥ iti sopādhikatvamityādi - (punaśaśabdastu śabdārthakaḥ | cakāro viśeṣaṇatrayasamuccayaparaḥ) sautraṃ - vaidharmayācca na svapnādivat iti sūtroktam | abhaṅguraṃ - abhagnaṃ, pakṣāvṛttitvasādhyasamavyāptyoḥ sattvenāpratyākhyepādhitvakam | vyabhicarat sādhyābhāvavadvṛtti, liṅgaṃ dṛśyatvāditi heturyena tattathoktam | jñāpyatvaṃ vigrahavākyasthātṛtīyārthaḥ | hetuvyabhicārajñāpakamityarthaḥ | upādhervyabhicāronnāyakatvādevamuktam | sopādhikatvaṃ pratipannopādhau bādhitatvarūpopādhimattā | puna śśabdo vākyālaṅkāraḥ | cakāraḥ pūrvoktadoṣasamuccāyakaḥ | vaḥ - vādināṃ yuṣmākam | bhaṅgāya - parājayahetuḥ | kālātyayāpadeśasya sākṣātsopādhikatvasya cānaikāntikadvārā vādiparājayāvahatvāditi bhāvaḥ | athavā kālātyayāpadiṣṭaṃ dṛśyatvaṃ ca abhaṅguraṃ sautraṃ tasya sopādhikatvaṃ ca, punaśśabdaścaśabdārthakaḥ; brahmaṇyanaikāntikaṃ ddaśyatvāditi liṅgaṃ ca vaḥ parājayaheturiti yojanā | śrutissṛtisvārasyāt brahmaṇi ddaśyatvaṃ vartata ititthamuktam | tadāhuḥ - ddaśyatvaṃ ca brahmaṇi vyabhicāri iti | liṅgamiti sāmānyoktyā vyāvartamānatvāderapi dūṣaṇaṃ siddham tadidaṃ sopādhikatvaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādairvistareṇoktama | tattu tatraivānusandheyam | vyaktami tyanena bālānāmapi idaṃ sopādhikatvaṃ sphuṭamiti vādinaḥ prayogākauśalatiśayāviṣkaraṇam || 53 ||
atha vyāghātamāha - 
mithyātvaṃ bhrāntisiddhaṃ yadi khalu jagataḥ pādhyate siddhasādhyo
hetuḥ satyaḥ prapañco bhavati hi yadi vā satyamadvaitahāniḥ
sādhyaṃ brahmasvarūpaṃ yadi bhavati tadā siddhasādhyatvameva
syādevaṃ ca prapañcavyudasanasaraṇirdūrataste nirastā || 54 ||
atra yattaditi padadvayamadhyāharthavyam | tathā ca jagato yanmithyātvaṃ sādhyate tadbhrāntisiddhaṃ yadīti yojanā | bhrāntisiddhaṃ bhrāntiprakārabhūtaṃ, mithyābhūtamiti yāvat | jagataḥ siṣāghayiṣitasya mithyātvasya mithyātvaṃ cedityarthaḥ | hetuḥ - ddaśyatvādikaḥ | siddhasādhyaḥ - siddhaṃ mānāntarāvagataṃ sādhyaṃ yasyeti vigrahaḥ | atra syādityapakarṣaḥ | atra yathāśrute hetoḥ siddhasādhanatvaṃ doṣa iti pratīyate | tattu nāsti | taduktaṃ caṇḍamārute vastuto mithyātvasya mithyātve’pi tena rūpeṇa mithyātvasyāsādhyatvāt siddhasādhanāsaṃbhavādato’rthāntarameva iti | mithyātvasya mithyātve mithyābhūtasyāropitapītimnaḥ śvaityene parāmārthikasatyatvenāvirodhādarthāntaram iti ca | ato’tra siddhasādhyo heturityasyārthāntaraṃ syādityarthaḥ | tatphalitamāha - satyaḥ prapañco bhavatīti | āropitamithyātvasya pāramārthikasatyatvena virodhābhāvānna prapañcasatyatvahānirityarthaḥ | yadi
vetyādi | tat mithyātvaṃ satyaṃ yadītyarthaḥ | advaitahānirityatra tadā tasya brahmasvarūpātireka ityadiḥ | siṣādhayiṣitasya mithyātvasya satyatve tasya brahmasvarūpātireke ca sati brahmaṇaḥ satyena tena sadvitīyatvaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ | satyasyaiva tasya brahmasvarūpānatirekamāśaṅkya nirākaroti - sādhyaṃ brahmasvarūpaṃ yadi bhavatīti | brahmasvarūpānatireke hi nādvaitahāniḥ, na vā jagataḥ satyatvamiti śaṅkāśayaḥ | siddhasādhyatvameva syāt - arthāntarameva syāt | brahmasvarūpasyāsmābhiraṅgīkārādityarthaḥ | evakāreṇa pūrvoktadoṣa evāpatatīti sūcyate | tataḥ phalitamāha - evaṃ ceti | evam - uktarītyā te prapaṃcavyudasanasaraṇiḥ tvaduktajaganmithyātvānumānaprakiyā | dūrato nirastā | sādhyadharmapāramārthyāpāramārthyāvikalpādinā vyāghātaprasaṃjanāt dūṣitetyarthaḥ | mādhyamikoktajñānabādhyatvasya bhavatā bādhyatvābhyupagamānneyamapramāṇi kadharmaparamparāpādanena dūṣaṇarūpanityasameti sūcanāya caśabdaprayogaḥ | tadidamāhurbhaṭṭasudarśanapādā - sādhyadharmasya pāramārthyāpāramārthyavikalpe kṛte pāramārthyaṃ cedadvaitahāniḥ | ddaśyatvahetoranaikāntyaṃ ca; paramārthe’pi tasya vṛtteḥ | apāramārthye prapañcasatyatā | bādhyakabādhe bādhyasvarūpābādhaḥ iti nyāyāt ityādinā || 54 ||
nanu vaidharmyācca na svapnādivat iti sūtrasya yogācārokte jāgaritapratyayo nirālambanaḥ vedanatvāt, svapnapratyayavat ityanumāne sopādhikatvapradarśanaparatvāt kathaṃ mṛṣāvādyuktamithyātvānumāne sopādhikatvapradarśanaparatoktiriti cet ? atrāhuḥ - mṛṣāvādināṃ cogācārāṇāṃ cārthamithyātvābhyupagamasyāviśeṣānna doṣa iti | taduktaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ pratītatve sati bādarhatvasya ubhayābhimatatvācca
iti kāraṇadoṣabādhapratyayayoranirvācyapakṣe’pyupādhitvopapatteraviśiṣṭatvāt iti ca | tathā caṇḍamārute’pyuktam - jāgaritapratyayo nirālambanaḥ vedanatvāt svāpnapratyayavat iti bauddhānumānam vaidharmyācca na svapnādivat iti, sūtrabhāṣye bhagavatā dūṣitam | māyinā prapañco mithyā ddagviṣayatvāt śuktirajatasvāpnādyarthādivat ityuktam | evaṃ ca jñānatvena mithyāviṣayatvasādhanasya jñānaviṣayatvena mithyātvasādhanasya cāviśeṣāt, jñānatvahetudūṣaṇānyeva tadviṣayatvahetorapi bhavanti iti ataḥ sautraṃ sopādhikatvamityetadupapādanāya yogācārāṇāṃ mṛṣāvādināṃ ca prapañcamithyātvopagatāvaviśeṣa māha - 
cinmātravyatireki vastu sakalaṃ mithyeti joghuṣyate
yogācāramate yadaupaniṣadīkartuṃ tadākṛṣyate |
māyāvādibhirapratītanigamanyāpapramāṇantaraprasthānairapahastayanti hi saha nyāyaiḥ pramāṇāni tat || 55 ||
yogācāramate - vaibhāṣikasautrāntikayogācāramādhyamikānāṃ caturṇāṃ buddhaśiṣyāṇāṃ madhye tṛtāyasya yogācārākhyasya mate | cideva cinmātram | mayūravyaṃsakāditvānmātraśabdena samāsaḥ | mātre kātrsyo’vadhāraṇe itikośaḥ | tavdyatireki - (svarūpātiriktasvagatabhedaśabditayāvadviśeṣaśūnye) saṃvivdyatiriktam | sakalaṃ - jñātṛjñeyādirūpam | vastu - padārthajātam | siddhāntena vastutvoktiḥ sāṃpratikasatyatvena parābhimatena vā vastutvoktiḥ | mithyeti yat joghuṣyate - yanmathyātvānumānaṃ paunaḥpunyenocyate | ghuṣighātoḥ kriyāsamabhihāre yaṅntāt karmaṇi yatprayayaḥ | jāgaritapratyaya ityādikaṃ yadyogācārairasakṛduktaṃ jaganmithyātvānumānam | tat - mithyātvānumānaṃ, karma | idaṃ ca kartumityatrānveti | apratītam apramitaṃ | nigamānāṃ pramañcasatyatvaparāṇāṃ svaniṣṭhavastuniṣedhaparāṇāṃ brahmaṇi heyaguṇaniṣedhaparāṇāṃ ca vedānāṃ | nyāyānāṃ sāmānyaviśeṣotsargāpavādopakramavirodhādhikaraṇasarvaśākhāpratyādinyāyānām
apacchedajartilādinyāyānāṃ ca | pramāṇāntarayoḥ pratyakṣānumānayośca | prasthānaṃ prameyaparijñāpanaparipāṭīyaistayoktaiḥ | māyāvādibhiḥ anirvacanīyabhāvarūpājñānavīdibhiḥ śāṅkaraiḥ kartṛbhiḥ | aupaniṣadīkartum - upaniṣatpratipādyaṃ kartum | cvipratyayena upaniṣadāmatatparatvaṃ sūcyate ākṛṣyate upaniṣaditi śeṣaḥ | prapañcasatyatvapravaṇānām ata evānicchantīnāṃ tapasvinīnām upaniṣadāṃ cākṛṣya jaganmithyātvātmakamahākūpapātanaṃ cikīrṣyata ityarthaḥ | jagadapalāpe tulye’pi śrutidroho māyāvādināṃ yogācārebhyo viśeṣa iti hṛdayam | tathā ca sautraṃ sopādhikatvaṃ māyāvādipakṣe’pyaviśiṣṭamiti bhāvaḥ | tatredamācakṣmahe ityādinoktaṃ mithyātvānumānadūṣaṇaṃ nigamayati - apahastayantītyādinā | pramāṇāni - pratyakṣānumānāgamarūpāṇi| nyāyaiḥ saha - svaparikarabhutaiḥ anukūlatarkairmīmāṃsāśātragrathitopakramādinyāyaiśca sākam | tat prapañcamithyātvānumānaṃ, karma | apahastayanti - hastāpakarṣaṇena prāmāṇikasadaso nivarthayantītyarthaḥ ||
yadvā taditi prathamāntam | tanmithyātvānumānam aupaniṣadīkartum upaniṣadanugrāhakatarkarūpaṃ kartum | ākṛṣyate - anicchadapi balādānīyate | tat - ākṛṣyamāṇaṃ mithyātvānumānaṃ, karma | pramāṇāni nyāyaiḥ sahāpahastayanti hastākarṣeṇa nivartayanti ityarthaḥ | atra copaniṣadityasya nādhyāhāraḥ | pūrvayojanāyāṃ hi; nikṛṣṭasyotkṛṣṭapadaprāpaṇaṃ nākarṣaṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | nikṛṣṭasya hi utkṛṣṭapadaprepsayā svata eva pravṛtteḥ karṣaṇānapekṣaṇāt | ato nikṛṣṭaṃ padaṃ manasāpi cintayitumanicchantyāḥ paramotkṛṣṭāyā upaniṣada eva tatrākarṣaṇamucitam | kasminnarthe pātayāma iti tatprasiddheḥ ityato’dhyahāra āśritaḥ | asyāṃ yojanāyāṃ tu tathā brahmaṇabruvaiḥ kaiścidāgrahanigṛhītaiścaṇḍālajātīyaiḥ kaścit svanarhatānusandhānena svayamanicchannapi brāhmaṇakulamāropayitumākṛṣyate taṃ ca brāhmaṇottamā svānuyāyibhiḥ kṣattriyādibhiḥ saha dūrādeva nirasyante; tadvat mṛṣāvādibhiryogācāroktaṃ mithyātvānumānam arthasvābhāvyāt svayameva śithilam aupaniṣadīkartumākṛṣyate | tacca pramāṇāni nyāyaiḥ sahāpahastayantītyākarṣaṇokatyupapatternādhyāhāraḥ | hiśabdaśvārthe vartate | pūrvayojanāyāṃ tu prasiddhau hiśabdaḥ | (kartṛkaraṇāddhātvartha iti nimic) || 55 ||
avaṃ - nanu cidaṃśe yogācārairnānātvakṣaṇikatvayorupagamānmṛṣāvādibhiśca tatra nityatvaikatvayorupagamāt kathaṃ cinmātravyatireki vastu sakalaṃ mithyā ityādikamuktaṃ ityāśaṅkya civdyatiriktatvāviśeṣācciddharmabhūtanānātvāderapi mithyātvaṃ matadvaye’ṣyaviśiṣṭamiti
cinmātravyatireki vastu sakalaṃ mithyā ityādikaṃ vaktuṃ yuktameveti pariharati - 
nānātvakṣaṇikatvayorūpagaterekatvanityatvayorapyekatra mate paratra ca dhiyo vaiṣamyamastīti cet |
māyāvādimatasya saugatamatādetanna cattācviko
dharmastatra matadvaye’pi na dhiyaḥ kaścit svarūpādṛte || 56 ||
vyā - prasaṅgāccidaṃśe’pi svarūpātiriktadharmānabhyupagamānmṛṣāvādināṃ yogācārairatyantasāmyamiti teṣāṃ vaidikabahirbhāvamāha - nānātvetyādinā | ekatra mate - yogācārapakṣe | dhiyaḥ - saṃvidaḥ | nānātvakṣaṇikatvayorupagateḥ - bahutvadvitīyakṣaṇavṛttidhvaṃsapratiyogitvayoḥ svīkārāt | paratra mate mṛṣāvādipakṣe | dhiyaekatvanityatvayorupagateḥ - sajātīyadvitīyaśūnyatvasarvakālāvacchinnatvayośca svīkārāddhetoḥ | saugatamatāt sugatabhaktayogācārapakṣāt | māyāvādipakṣasya asatyāvidyāvādipakṣasya | vaiṣamyaṃ bhedaḥ | astīti cedetanna - ukta vaiṣamyaṃ na prāmāṇikamityarthaḥ | kuta ityatrāha - yadityādi | yat - yasmāt | tatra matadvaye’pi pūrvoktayogācārapakṣamāyāvādipakṣarūpamatadvaye’pi | svarūpāddate - dharmisvarūpādanyaḥ | kaścit eko’pi | dharmaḥ - nānātvādikaḥ ekatvādikaśca | tāttviko na bhavati satyo na bhavati | jñānavyatiriktatvāddharmāṇāṃ, dhiyaśca svagatabhedaśūnyatvādityarthaḥ | ataścinmātravyatirekītyādyuktirnānupapannā | cidaṃśe’pyabhyupagamataulyāt yogācāramāyāvādināmatyantavaidikabahirbhāva ityarthaḥ | atra ca yogācārairmṛṣāvādibhiścānyonyaṃ prati sādhyasya nānātvaikatvāderdharmisvarūpātirekānatirekavikalpe prathame śirasi bāhyārthāstitvasaviśeṣatvayoḥ, antye tu
arthāntarasya ca prasaṅgādanumānāsvārasyamiti sūcyate || 56 ||
dharmigrāhakamānadhikakṛtam ityanena pratyakṣapramāṇasvārasyahāniḥ , cinmatravyatireki ityādinānumānasvārasyahāniścoktā | atra mukhāntareṇa pratyakṣasvārasyahānirucyate | satyayorevādhiṣṭhānadoṣayorbhramahetutvaṃ ddaṣṭam | ato doṣamithyātve’pi bhramotpattiḥ pratyakṣaviruddhā | tathā satyadhiṣṭhānamithyātve’pi bhramotpatti prasaṅgena mādhyamikapakṣavijayaprasaṅgaḥ | doṣakalpakadoṣāpekṣaṇāccānavasthāprasaṅgaḥ |
svarūpānāditvādinānavasthāparihārastu aparamārthādhiṣṭhānavāde’pi tulya iti pratyakṣasvārasyahānyā saha mṛṣāvādināṃ vaidikabahirbhāvakāṣṭhāmāha - 
mū svarūpānāditvasvaparaghaṭane durghaṭanatā
pravāhānāditvaṃ punariti catasro hi gatayaḥ |
asatyāvidyāyā gaditumanavasthāparihṛtau
matāstvetāstulyāḥ sphuṭamasadadhiṣṭhānasaraṇau || 57 ||
atra mṛṣāvādināmityapekṣyate | asatyāvidyāyāḥ - asatyā ca sā avidyā ca asatyāvidyā, tasyāḥ | pāramārthye hyadvaitahāniprasaṅgenāsatyatvena svīkṛtāyā bhāvarūpājñānaśabditāyā jīvabrahmāśrayāyā avidyāyāḥ, (jīvabrahmāyā sā avidyāyā) anavasthāparihṛtausvayamapi kalpitatvena mūladoṣāpekṣaṇāt yā anavasthā prasaṃjitā tasyāḥ parihārāya | catasro gatayo gadituṃ muṣāvādināṃ matā hīti saṃbandhaḥ | svarūpānāditvaṃ ca svaparaghaṭanā ca svarūpānāditvasvaparaghaṭane | itaretarayogadvandvaḥ | svarūpeṇānāditvaṃ svarūpānāditvaṃ, prāgabhāvāpratiyogitvamityarthaḥ | janyatve hi janakāpekṣā, ajanyasya tu na mūlāpekṣeti iyamekā gatiḥ | iyaṃ ca brahmājñānavādinā uktā | taduktaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ brahmājñānavādinā svarūpānāditvena parihṛtaḥ iti | svaparayorghaṭanā - svaparaghaṭanā - svakalpane prapañcakalpane ca svayameva doṣa iti doṣatvena svaparanirvāhakatvam | svānirvāhakatvena paranirvāhakamātratve hi svasmin nirvāhakāntarāpekṣā, svaparanirvāhakatve tu na tadapekṣeti iyamanyā gatiḥ | taduktaṃ - doṣasya svaparanirvāhakatvena doṣāntaranairapekṣyācca nānavasthā iti | durghaṭanatā - durghaṭatvam | upapattinirapekṣatvam | upapattisāpekṣatve hyupapādakāpekṣā | tannirapekṣetve tu nopapādakāpekṣeti iyamaparā gatiḥ | tadaktaṃ paramārthasya hyupapattyapekṣā | prāsādādinigaraṇeṣu alpāsyena vilulataraprasādādinigaraṇamanupapannamiti yuktirna pravartate | ata upapattinirapekṣatvamapāramārthyasya sādhakamiti | pravāhato’nāditvaṃ pravāhānāditvam - utpattivināśayogisajātīyavyaktipāramparyam | tacca svāśrayapūrvakālatvavyāpakābhāvapratiyogitānavacchedakadharmavattvamiti | tātparyadīpikākṛtaḥ | bījāṅkuranyāyānnānavastheti iyamitarā gatiḥ | iyaṃ ca jīvājñānavādinoktā | taduktaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ jīvājñānavādinā bījāṅkuranyāyāt pravāhānāditvena parihṛtā iti | atrādyā brahmājñānavādinoktā | antyā tu jīvājñānavādinoktā | madhyame tūbhābhyāmuke iti vivekaḥ |
iti catasro gatayaḥ - yuktayaḥ asatyāvidyāyā anavasthāparihārarūpasādhye mṛṣāvādibhirgadituṃ vaktuṃ matā iṣṭā hītyarthaḥ | icchārthena yogāttumun | etāścatasro gatayo avidyāyāḥ svarūpānāditvasvaparanirvāhakatvopapattinirapekṣatvapravāhānāditvarūpā anavasthāparihārahetavaḥ | asadadhiṣṭhānasaraṇau - prapañcabhramādhiṣṭhānabhūtasaṃvinmātrāpāramārthyavādimādhyamikapakṣe’pi | tulyāḥ samānā iti | sphuṭaṃ - bālānāmapi spaṣṭamityarthaḥ ||
tadidamuktaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ - apāramārthye svarūpānāditvamapi nānavasthāparihāraḥ | tathā sati mādhyamikapakṣavijayaprasaṅgāt | tatrādhiṣṭhānasya bhrāntisiddhatvena tasyā bhrānteradhiṣṭhānāntarāpekṣā syāttasyāpi bhrāntisiddhatvena tathā syādityanavasthayāhyadhiṣṭhānasya pāramārthyaṃ sādhitam | mādhyamikenāparamārthādhiṣṭhānasya svarūpānāditve svīkṛte’navasthā parihṛteti adhiṣṭhānāpāramārthyameva syāt iti | doṣasya svaparanirvāhakatve’dhiṣṭhānamapi tathā syāt iti ca durghaṭatvamanavasthāparihāraścedadhiṣṭhānāpāramārthye’pi anavasthā parihṛtā syāt iti ca | pravāhānāditvapakṣe’pyadhiṣṭhānāpāramārthye’navasthā suparihārā | pitrādiparamparāvat adhiṣṭhānaparamparānāditvābhyupagamāt iti ca | tuśabdastu svaparanirvāhakatvadurghaṭatvayoradhiṣṭhānasya doṣavaiṣamyaśaṅkāvyudāsakaḥ | tadāhuḥ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādāḥ - paraṃ pratyadhiṣṭhānatve siddhe hi svanirvāhakatvamiti cetparaṃ prati doṣatve hi svanirvāhakatvaṃ, tadeva nāsti iti tulyāḥ ityādinā tatrāparamārthatvaprayuktopapattiravarjanīyetyadhiṣṭhānāpekṣā syādevetyanavasthitiriti vaktavm | tatra doṣe’pi tulyamiti ca, tadidaṃ sarvamabhipretya bhāṣitaṃ bhagavatā - yāvadbrahmavyatiriktapāramārthikadoṣānabhyupagamo na tākadbhrāntirupapāditā bhavati iti || 57 ||
sarvaśūnyatvasādhakahetoriva cinmātravyatiriktāpāramārthyasādhakahetorapi svasvarūpotsādakatayā hetoḥ svarūpāsiddhiriti punarapyanumānāsvārasyamucyate | tatra mṛṣāvādipakṣasādhakayuktināṃ mādhyamikapakṣasādhakatvaṃ pūrvatroktam | atra tu mādhyamikapakṣanirāsakayuktīnāṃ mṛṣāvādipakṣanirāsakatvamucyate - ityādi ṭa
khaṇḍanakāra khaṇḍaya bhavatpakṣaḥ pratikṣipyate
tarkaistvatkathitaistataḥ kimiti cedasmanmataṃ tiṣṭhati |
yadvanmādhyamikoktacidvyudasanapratyāsatastvanmataṃ
viṣṭhatyeva yathā ca bādhakahatau loke svarūpasthiti || 58 ||
re iti sāvajñāhvānam | dviruktyā nikarṣātiśayo vyajyate | khaṇḍanākhyagranthakartaḥ | khaṇḍaya |
abhyanujñāyāṃ loṭa | na naḥ kiṃcicchinnamityarthaḥ | kuta ityatrāha - bhavadityādi | tvatkathitaistarkaiḥ - pramāṇavyāptyādibhaṅgāvasare mādhyamikaṃ prati tvaduktaistarkaiḥ | bhavatpakṣaḥ tvatsvīkṛtaṃ cidvyatiriktasya mithyātvam | pratikṣipyate - nirasyate | sādhanadharmapāramārthyāpāramārthyavikalpādibhistvaduktamanumānādikaṃ nirasyata ityarthaḥ | tataḥ kimiti cediti | svamatakhaṇḍanābhyanujñānena svapakṣasthāpanāvirahitāt parapakṣadūṣaṇādbhavatāṃ vaitaṇḍikatvameva prasajati | na tu prayojanaṃ sidhyatīti cedityartha | pariharati asmanmatamityādinā | asmanmatam asmadabhimataṃ jagatsatyatvam | tiṣṭhati - sthiraṃ bhavati | parapakṣadūṣaṇaṃ svapakṣasthāpanā cedīdaṃ dvayamapi tantreṇa siddhamiti na vaitaṇḍikatvaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ | asminnarthe tatpakṣameva ddaṣṭāntayanti yadvetyādinā | (yathā) mādhyamikoktasya śūnyavadinoktasya, cidvyudasanasya - adhiṣṭhānamithyātvasya, pratyāsataḥ pratikṣepāt | pramititatsādhanasaddhāvādivikalpamukhena tvatkṛtāt | tvanmataṃ tvadabhimatamadhiṣṭhānabhūtasaṃvitsatyatvam | tiṣṭhati; tadvadityarthaḥ | ddaṣṭāntāntaramāha yathā ceti | loke apavarakādau | bādhakasya ghaṭābhāvabodhakavākyasya | hatau tatraiva ghaṭasadbhāvapramitijanakena vākyena svabodhyābhāvabodhane sati | svarūpasya pratiyogino ghaṭādeḥ |
sthitiḥ styatvaṃ yathā tadvadityarthaḥ | kārakajñāpakayorvaiṣamyāt na hi devadattahantari hate devattasya punarunmajjanam iti nyāyasya na virodha | tadidaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ, etatkhaṇḍanakārādyuktakutarkanirasanopalakṣaṇarthamuktamityādinā vistareṇoktaṃ tatraivānusandheyam || 58 ||
punarapyanumānāsvārasyamāha - 
adhītvaṃ vedyatve prasajati dhiyaḥ kumbhavaditi
svasiddhiḥ sā dhītvāditi yadiha ko’pyāha tadasat |
svamiddhiḥ svādhāraṃ prati hi ni paraṃ pratyapi naraṃ
parajñeyā seyaṃ tadapi na matitvaṃ vighaṭate || 59 ||
ko’pi - nāmagrahaṇānarhaḥ kaścinmṛṣāvādītyarthaḥ | iha - prāmāṇikabhūyiṣṭhe
jagati | dhiyaḥ - anubhūteḥ | vedyatve - anubhavāntaraviṣayatve sati | adhītvaṃ ananubhūtitvam | prasajati āpādanaṃ bhajati | kumbhavat - kumbhasyeva | ṣaṣṭhyantādvatiḥ | anubhūtiryadyanubhāvyā syāttarhi ananubhūtiḥ syāt ghaṭavadityayamanukūlatarko vakṣyamāṇānumānāṅgattayokta iti hetoḥ, uktarkāddhetoḥ | athānumānamāha - svasiddhirityādinā | sā - anubhūtiḥ | ayaṃ pakṣanirdeśaḥ | svasiddhiḥ - svenaiva siddhiḥ vyavahārānuguṇyaṃ yasyāstathoktā | svayaṃprakāśatve sati, (jñānāntaraviṣayatvaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ) avadhāraṇagarbhatvāduktārthasiddhiḥ, tacca vedyatve parajñeyeti ca pūrvaparānusārādāśritam | ayaṃ ca sādhyānirdeśaḥ | dhītvāt anubhūtitvāt | hetunirdeśo’yam | kumbhavaditi pūrvasmādanuṣajyate | vyatirekadṛṣṭāntaścāyam | ghaṭādau hi jñānāntaraviṣayatvābhāvasvaprakāśatvayorabhāvo’nanubhūtitvaṃ ca dṛṣṭamitīdaṃ vyatirekayanumānam | iti yadāha pūrvokākārakaṃ yadanumānamuktavān, vakti vā | tadasat | tat - anumānam asat - duṣṭaṃ, siddhasādhanakālātyayāpadeśasopādhikatvarūpadoṣayuktamityarthaḥ | atra ca sādhyaṃ svayaṃprakāśatvaṃ kiṃ svaśrayaṃ prati, utānyaṃ prati | ādye siddhasādhanaṃ, antye bādhaḥ | parajñānasya paraṃ prati jñānamantareṇāprakāśāt | anubhūterjñānāntaraviṣayatvābhāvaśca bādhitaḥ | puruṣāntarajñānānāṃ puruṣāntarīyānumityādiviṣayatvāt | anyathā laukikavaidikasakalavyavahāravirodhāpatteḥ | ghaṭādeścānanubhūtitvamajñānāvirodhitvaprayuktaṃ nānubhāvyatvaprayuktamiti sopādhikaṃ cedam | tadetadāha - svasiddhirityādinā | svādhāraṃ prati - ahaṃ jānāmītyatra jñānāśrayatayā pratītamahamarthaṃ prati | svasiddhiḥ - svayaṃprakāśasvāśrayabhūtāhamarthakartṛkasvakarmakavyavahāre svayameva heturityarthaḥ | śeṣitvavācinā pratiśabdenāśrayakartṛkatvalābhaḥ | śeṣatvavācitā ca lakṣaṇetthaṃ bhūtākhyāna bhāgavīpsāsu pratiparyanavaḥ ityukteḥ | ata eva svādhāram ityatra dvitīyā | sādhyatāvacchedakaprakāreṇa mānāntarasiddhiparahiśabdena siddhasādhanatvaṃ sūcyate | na paramityādi; paraṃ naraṃ pratyapi svāśrayapuruṣādanyaṃ puruṣaṃ pratyapi (puruṣāntaraṃ pratyapi) | svasiddhirna bhavati hītyarthaḥ | hiśabdo madhmamaṇinyāyāt pūrvāparānvayī | ataḥ kālātyayāpadiṣṭamidamityarthaḥ parejñeyetyādi; seyaṃ - svāśrayaṃ prati svasiddharūpā puruṣāntaraṃ pratyatathāvidhā ca dhīḥ | parajñeyā - parayā - svabhinnānubhūtyā, jñānāntareṇetyarthaḥ | jñeyā viṣayīkāryā, jñānāntaraviṣayatvavatītyarthaḥ | sarvanāmno vṛttimātre puvaṃdbhāvaḥ parā cānubhūtidvayī samānādhikaraṇā vyadhikaraṇā ca | tayā dvividhayāpi vedyatvamanubhūteḥ | tatra
tatpuruṣīyātītānāgatānubhūtestatpuruṣīyavartamānānubhūtiviṣayatvādādyayānubhāvyatvaṃ, puruṣāntarajñānānāṃ puruṣāntarānumeyatvadarśanādanyayānubhūtyānubhāvyatvamiti samānādhikaraṇavyadhikaraṇānubhūtidvayasaṃgrahāya parayā jñeyeti vyākhyātam | ato’nubhūterjñānāntaraviṣayatvābhāvo bādhita ityarthaḥ | tadapītyādi; atra dhiya ityanuṣajyate | tadapi - tathāpi, parajñeyatve’pi, jñānāntaraviṣayatve satyapīti yāvat | dhiyaḥ - anubhūteḥ matitvaṃ, na vighaṭate - na virudhyate | anubhūtitvamanubhāvyatvādhikarānubhūtivṛttyabhāvāpratiyogītyarthaḥ | ghaṭāderanubhūtitvābhāvo nānubhāvyatvaprayuktaḥ | kiṃ tu ajñānāvirodhitvaprayuktaḥ | anubhūtistu ajñānavirodhinyeveti sopādhikatvamiti bhāvaḥ || 59 ||
anubhūteranubhūtitvamasādhāraṇadharmaḥ | anubhūtitvaṃ ca vartamānadaśāyāṃ svasattayaiva svāśrayaṃ prati prakāśamānatvam | anubhāvyatvaṃ tu ghaṭādisādhāraṇaṃ, na hyasādhāraṇākāro dharmigrāhakamānanasiddhiḥ sādhāraṇākāre satyapagacchati | tathā sati (ghaṭāderanubhāvyatvena dhaṭatvādiprasaṅgādityanubhāvyatvānanubhāvyatvayoḥ) vyāptiṃ dūṣayati - 
tathā hi pratyakṣaprabhṛtimitivargaprakaṭitaḥ
svabhāvo bhāvānāmapasarati vedyāsta iti kim |
svabhāvapratyāso yadi bhavati vedyatvavaśataḥ
paṭasya jñeyatve ghaṭavadapaṭatvaṃ prasajati || 60 ||
tathā hi - tadapi na matitvaṃ vighaṭate iti yatpūrvamuktaṃ tattathaivetyarthaḥ | kuta ityatrāha - pratyakṣetyādi | te - bhāvāḥ, padārthāḥ | vedyā iti - jñānaviṣayā iti | itiśabdo hetvarthakaḥ | vedyatvātkāraṇādityarthaḥ | bhāvānāṃ - padārthānām | pratyakṣaprabhṛtimitivargaiḥ - sākṣātkārānumitiśābdātmakapramāsamūhaiḥ | prakaṭitaḥ - pūrvaṃ sanneva prakāśitaḥ | tena vaijñānikatvavyudāsaḥ | vargaśabdena bahūnāmanyathākaraṇamanucitamiti labhyate | svabhāvaḥ - asādhāraṇadharmaḥ | apasarati kiṃ nāpagacchatītyarthaḥ | padārthānāṃ prameyatvādirūpasādhāraṇadharmo na pramāṇasiddhaḥ tattadasādhāraṇadharmaviruddha ityarthaḥ | tathātve’ti
prasaṅgamāha - svabhāvetyādinā | dhiya ityādiḥ | dhiyo vedyatvaśataḥ - anubhāvyatva yogāt, jñeyatvarūpasādhāraṇadharmayogādityarthaḥ | svabhāvasya - svāsādhāraṇadharmabhūtānubhūtitvasya, pratyāsaḥ - pratikṣepaḥ
asu kṣepaṇe iti paṭhitāt pratipūrvāt bhāve ghaña | bhavati yadi - bhavati cedityarthaḥ | jñeyatvarupasādhāraṇadharmo’nubhūtitvarūpāsādhāraṇadharmābhāvavyāpyaścedityarthaḥ | paṭasya pratyakṣasiddhapaṭatvarupāsādhāraṇadharmavataḥ | jñeyatve sati - sādhāraṇāt prameyatvādityarthaḥ | ghaṭavat - prameyatvadharmayogighaṭavat | apaṭatvaṃ paṭatvābhāvaḥ | arthābhāve’vyayībhāvaḥ | prasajati - āpadyate | jñeyatvasya ghaṭe paṭatvābhāvasāmānādhikaraṇyamātreṇa paṭe’pi tatsāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ pratyakṣaviruddhamityarthaḥ | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ - dhūmānumitasya parvatīyavahnerjñeyatvāt parvatīyavahnitvābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | kalpyāpūrvasya vāde tasya jñeyatvādapūrvatvābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | śabdaikavedyasya svargasya prameyatvāt svargatvābhāvaprasaṅgaścetyapi bodhyam | pratyakṣaprabhṛtimitivargetyuktatvāt | evaṃ ca ghaṭādāvanubhāvyatvasyānubhūtitvābhāvasāmānādhikaraṇyamātreṇa svaprakāśāyāmanubhūtāvapi anubhāvyatvenānubhūtitvābhāvaḥ pratyakṣabādhita iti bhāvaḥ | ghaṭādau tvajñānāvirodhitvamevānubhūtitvābhāvaprayojakaṃ, nānubhāvyatvam | tadidaṃ bhāṣitaṃ bhagavatā eva tarhyajñānāvirodhitvamevānanubhūtitvanibandhanaṃ, nānubhāvyatvamityā sthīyatām iti | ato’nubhāvyatvānanubhūtitvayorna vyāptiriti hṛdayam || 60 ||
pratyakṣādipramāṇasvarasagatihatiḥ ityatrādiśabdavivakṣitānumānāsvārasyamuktaṃ dṛśyatvādanṛtam ityādinā | atra tadbrādhaśca bhūyān ityuktaṃ vivṛṇoti - prakāśa ityādi
prakāśaḥ kumbhādau jāḍimavati tadgocaramitistirodhiścaitasyā viratirajaḍe brahmaṇi punaḥ |
svarūpaṃ cinmātraṃ sphuraṇamapidhiśca svanidhanaṃ
jaḍaṃ vā brahma syādapidhividhuraṃ vāpyaparathā || 61 ||
brahmasvarūpasya nityatvanirviśeṣatvasvaprakāśatvāni, bhāvarūpājñānena tirodhānaṃ, jñānena tannivṛttiśca mṛṣāvādibhirabhyupagatāni | tatrānyonyaṃ dharmāṇāṃ vyāhatirucyati - 
prakāśaḥ kumbhādāvityādinā | jāḍimavati - jaḍatvavati, niyataparaprakāśyatvavati | kumbhādau - ghaṭādau | prakāśaḥ - bhāti prakāśate iti vyavahāraviṣayo dharmaḥ | tadgocaramitiḥ - tadviṣayakajñānaṃ bhavati | kumbhādau tirodhiśca ghaṭādiniṣṭhaṃ tirohitatvaṃ tu, na prakāśata iti vyavahāraviṣayabhūtaṃ tirohitatvaṃ | etasyāḥ - ghaṭādigocarabuddheḥ | viratiḥ - abhāvo bhavatītyarthaḥ | evaṃ prāmāṇikaṃ ghaṭādīnāṃ prakāśatirodhānasvarūpaṃ darśitam | atra ca ghaṭādisvarūpātiriktatvāttadabhāvarupaṃ tattirodhānaṃ na tatsvarūpanāśātmakamiti vakṣyamāṇārthavaiṣamyamuktaṃ bhavati | astvevaṃ tataḥ kimityatrāha - ajaḍa ityādi | ajaḍesvaprakāśe | brahmaṇi punaḥ - brahmaṇi viṣaye tu | cideva cinmātraṃ - svarūpātiriktaprakāśādidharmaśūnyam | svarūpaṃ - dharmisvarūpam | sphuraṇaṃ - prakāśa, bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | apidhiśca apidhānaṃ ca, tirodhānaṃ tvityarthaḥ | apipūrvakāddadhāteḥ upasarge ghoḥ kipratyayaḥ | svanidhanaṃ - svarūpanāśa eva, atra syāditi śeṣaḥ | prakāśasya svarūparūpatvāttirodhānaṃ tannāśa eva syāditi nityatvādīnāṃ mitho vyāghāta iti bhāvaḥ | prakāśaikasvarūpasya prakāśatirodhānaṃ prakāśanāśa eva iti ca bhagavatā bhāṣitam | jaṅaṃ vetyādi | aparathā - brahmaṇaḥsvarūpātiriktajñānaviṣayatvābhyupagame; svarūpātiriktaprakāśasya nityasphuraṇābhyupagame ca, krameṇa brahma jaḍaṃ vā syāt, apidhividhuraṃ vā syāt - ghaṭādivat jaḍatvaprasaṅgo vā, tiroghānābhāvaprasaṅgo vā brahmaṇa iti bhāvaḥ | tirodhānasya nivartakānupapattinivṛttyanupapattyoḥ, api śabdena sūcanam | atra ca jñānarūpaṃ paraṃ brahma ityādikamanusandheyam | tiraśśabdasyānupasargatve’pi bāhulakaḥ kipratyaya iti vā, sarvadhātubhya in iti inpratyayasya bāhulakaṃ kitvamiti vā dhātunirdeśe ikpratyayaḥ | dadhāteścārthalakṣakatvamiti vā samādheyam | tirodhipadasādhutvamityāhuḥ | prayuktaṃ ca bhagavadyāmunapādaiḥ - svayāthātmyaṃ prakṛtyāsya tirodhiḥśaraṇāgatiḥ iti || 61 ||
ācchādanaṃ vikṣepaśceti kṛtyadvayamavidyāyā brahmaṇi mṛṣāvādibhiruktam | tatrācchādanāṃśaḥ pūrvatra dūṣitaḥ | vikṣepāṃśo’tra dūṣyate | vikṣepāścādhyāsaḥ | sa cātadūpāropaḥ | tasyātra svarūpanivṛttyorasiddhirucyate - 
purovartidravyasphuraṇamapi rūpyabhramasahaṃ
na śuktitvasphūrtirbhramavihataye yā prabhavati |
yadi brahma prāgvanna khalu jagadadhyāsaviratiḥ
kva sidhyedadhyāso yadi bhavati śuktitvasadṛśam || 62 ||
puro vartata iti purovartti | supyajatau iti ṇiniḥ | purovartinaḥ dravyasya śuktyādidravyasya, sphuraṇaṃ idaṃtva śuktitvā)dinā grahaṇam | rūpyabhramasahaṃ - rajatabhramāvirodhi | pratyuta tadupayogītyarthaḥ | api śabdastu sphuraṇabhramayoḥ samānaviśeṣyakatvaprayuktavirodhasūcakaḥ | sa ca virodhasphuraṇasyāsādhāraṇadharmāviṣayakatvena parihṛtaḥ | na śuktitvetyādi | śuktitvasphūrtiḥ - śuktyasādhāraṇaśuktitvasphuraṇam | na rūpyabhramasahaṃ - rajatabhramavirodhītyarthaḥ | kuta ityatrāha - bhrameti | yā - śuktitvasphūrtiḥ | bhramavihataye - rajatabhramanivṛttaye, anutpādāya prabhavati - śaktetyarthaḥ | tathā ca sādhāraṇākāragraho bhramotpādakaḥ, asādhāraṇākāragrahastu tadvirodhīti sarva siddhamityarthaḥ | tayordvayorapi brahmaviṣayakatve’niṣṭamāha - yadītyādinā | brahma - svaprakāśaikarasaṃ brahma | prāgvadyadi - pūrvārdhe prathamopāttapurovartidravyasphuraṇasadṛśaṃ cedbhramahetudharmigraharūpaṃ cet | tadā jagadadhyāsaviratiḥ na khalu prapañcabhramanivṛttirna syāt | bhramahetujñānasya tadanivartakatvādityarthaḥ | tathā cānirmokṣaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ | brahma śuktitvasadṛśaṃ yadi bhavati - asādhāraṇadharmabhūtaśuktitvagrahatulyaṃ cet , bhramavirodhigraharūpaṃ cet | adhyāsaḥ - prapañcabhramaḥ kva sidhyet pratibandhakasattvānnotpadyetetyarthaḥ | tathā ca pratyakṣavirodha ityarthaḥ | brahmasvarūpaṃ na svarūpato’dhyāsavirodhi, kiṃ tvaparokṣavṛttyavacchinnamityetattu na saṃbhavati | viṣayabhedābhāvāt | brahmasvarupāviṣayīkṛtāparokṣavṛttyavacchinnatadviṣayīkṛtaḥ kaścidākāro’sti vā na vā ādye brahmaṇaḥ saviśeṣatvam, antye svarūpasyaiva nivartakatvamityādikaṃ tu vedāntavijayādiṣu draṣṭavyam || 62 ||
brahmaṇi acetanādhyāsaḥ cetanādhyāsaśceti adhyāsadvayaṃ tanmate svīkṛtam | tatra ghaṭapaṭādhyāso’cetanādhyāsaḥ
| kartṛtvamoktṛtvādiyūktapuruṣādhyāsastu cetanādhyāsaḥ | tatrācidadhyāsaḥ na khalu jagadadhyāsaviratiḥ iti pūrvatra dūṣitaḥ | atra tu kartṛtvabhoktṛtvādiyuktajīvādhyāsānupapattistannivṛttyanupapattiścocyate - 
idaṃ māyāvāde vividhamuditaṃ dūṣaṇamiha
svayaṃjyotirbrahma vyapagataviśeṣaṃ yadi katham |
mate tasminnaramaddṛśi tu saviśeṣaḥ puruṣa ityatadrūpāropastaduparatirityādi ghaṭate || 63 ||
vyā - iha mate - māyāvādapakṣe brahma, svayaṃjyotiḥ vyapagataviśeṣaṃ yadi - nirviśeṣasvaprakāśarūpaṃ cet | tadā asmaddṛśi tasmin - asmatpratyaye nirviśeṣasvaprakāśasvarūpe brahmaṇi | asmatpratyayagocare viṣayiṇi iti hi taduktiḥ | tuśabdo’dhiṣṭhānatvopayuktadharmaśūnyatvadyotakaḥ | saviśeṣaḥ -; viṣayatayā kartṛtvabhoktṛtvādidharmayuktaḥ | puruṣa ityatadrūpāropaḥ - puruṣa iti yadatadrūpaṃ puruṣatvarūpaṃ yadadhiṣṭhānabhūtabrahmasvarūpāvṛttirūpadharmaḥ tasyāropo’dhyāsaḥ | taduparatiḥ - tannivṛttiḥ | ityādi - tatprabhṛti pūrvoktaṃ sarvam | kathaṃ ghaṭate - kathamupapadyate |
brahmaṇo nirviśeṣatvena sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇadharmadvayābhāvāt, svaprakāśaikasvarūpatvena tayoḥ sphūrtyasphūrtyorabhāvācca na bhramasaṃbhavaḥ | saṃbhave vā na tannivṛttiḥ; tadavirodhinastannivartakatvāyogāt | na ca vaiśadyasyādhyāsanivartakatvaṃ yuktam | tasya jaḍatvenānivartakatvāt | jñānamevājñānanivartakamiti yuṣmābhirupagamāt | tanmātraviṣayakajñāsyaiva nivartakatve ca tasyānāditvādanādinivṛttiprasaṅgaḥ | svamātrasākṣijñānasya ca nivartakatve tasyotpādyatvaprasaṅgaḥ | svamātrasākṣitvasya cotpādyatve tasyaiva nivartakatvaprasaṅgaḥ | svamātrasākṣitvasya nivartakatāvacchedakatvamapyayuktam | śuktyādijñānasyādhiṣṭhānātiriktaviṣayasyāpi nivartakatvadarśanādikaṃ caṇḍamārutādāvanusandheyam ||
tasminnasmaddṛśi tvityasyaivaṃ vārthaḥ | tuśabdaścakārārthakaḥ | tasmin nirviśeṣasvaprakāśe brahmaṇi | asmaddṛśi ca asmatpratītiviṣaye ahaṃkārākhyadravye ca | saviśeṣaḥ - kartṛtvādiviṣayakaḥ | puruṣa ityatadrūpāropaḥ jīvādhyāsa ityādyayaṃ bhāvaḥ | na tāvadantaḥ karaṇe citpratibimbāt kartṛtvādiyuktajīvasiddhiḥ | cito nirviśeṣatvena tatra kartṛtvādyabhāvāt nīrūpatvena pratibimbāsaṃbhavācca | nāpi citsvarūpe’ntaḥkaraṇapratibimbāt kartṛtvādiviśiṣṭajīvasiddhiḥ | antaḥkaraṇasyācetanatvena (kartṛ) kartṛtvādyabhāvāt | na ca jñātṛtvakartṛtvādiśūnyasya jñānasyāntaḥkaraṇe pratibimbājjīvasiddhiḥ | jñānasyācākṣuṣatvena pratibimbāsaṃbhavāditi atadrūpāropaḥ taduparatirityādi ghaṭata itīdaṃ vividhaṃ māyāvāde dūṣaṇamuditam uktamiti māyāvāda ityupakrāntavistaro nigamitaḥ || 63 ||
avaṃ - nanu bhavatpakṣe kṣetrajñānāṃ nityaṃ sphuraṇamasti tathā ca teṣāṃ svarūpatirodhānena vinā na dehātmabhrama iti tulyānupapattirityatrāha - 
adhiṣṭhāne puṃsi svayamahamiti sphurjati vapuḥ - 
prakārādhyāropaḥ paśurahamahaṃ mānuṣa iti |
aṇutvādau dehavyatikaravidūre ca vihite
tirodhiḥ sakoco nanu bhavati tadgocaradhiyaḥ || 64 ||
puṃsi - kṣetrajñātmani | adhiṣṭhāne viśeṣye, jīvātmarūpe bhramaghamiṃṇi | ahamiti svayaṃ sphurjati - ahamiti svataḥprakāśamāne satītyarthaḥ | ṭuosphūrjā vajranirghoṣa iti paṭhitāt laṭaḥ śatrādeśaḥ | svayaṃprakāśatvaprayuktātmasphuraṇasyātyantadārḍhyadyotanāya vajranirghoṣatvavyapadeśaḥ | etenātmano jaḍatvaṃ nirastam atra ca bhramāpekṣitādhiṣṭhānasphūrtirūktā | bhramavirodhidharmāsphūrtimāha - aṇutvetyādinā | dehavyatikaraḥ - dehātmanoritaretaratādātmyabhramaḥ, vidūro nirvartyo yasya tathokte | anyonyasaṃsargavācino vyatikaraśabdasya sāmarthyāt tadbhramaparatvat aṇutvādīnāṃ dehāvṛttitvāt tajjñānaṃ dehatādātmyabhramavirodhīti dehavyatikaretyādyuktam | aṇutvādau | ādinā nityatvaniravayavatvayoḥ parigrahaḥ | vihitesati ca tirohite sati ca, sphurati sati cetyarthaḥ | cakāro’dhiṣṭhānasphuraṇaṃ pūrvoktaṃ samuccinoti | aṇutvādīnāmāgamagamyatvādasphuraṇoktiḥ | te hi nāhaṃ pratyayagocarā iti | evaṃ bhramāpekṣitadharmiphphuraṇaṃ virodhidharmāsphuraṇaṃ coktam | atha bhramotpattimāha - paśurityādinā | ahaṃ paśurahaṃ mānuṣa iti capuḥ - prakārādhyāropaḥ
tādātmyena dehaprakārakabhramaḥ | atra bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | abhedasaṃsargeṇa paśumanuṣyādidehaprakārakātmaviśeṣyakabhrama upapanna iti bhāvaḥ | prakāratvamihāgṛhītāntaratve satyapradhānatayā bhātattvam | svarūpātiriktadharmabhyupagamāt | tadaprakāśe’pi nātmasvarūpanāśaprasaṅga iti dehātmabhramopapattiḥ | pakṣāntare tu tatra svarūpātiriktadharmānabhyupagamāt svarūpanāśaprasaṅgādinā tadanupapattirdurvāreti bhāvaḥ | tadgocaradhiyaḥ - ātmavṛttyaṇutvanityatvaniravayavatveṣu viṣayatayā prasaraṇārhasya dharmabhūtajñānasya saṃkocaḥ - karmaṇā, śrutyādisahakāryasamavadhānena ca teṣu prasaraṇaviraha eva | nanvityavadhāraṇe | tirodhiḥ - aprakāśaḥ; atra bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | aṇutvāderaprakāśopapatteḥ, dharmiṇaḥ
prakāśopapatteśca ātmani dehatādātmyabhrama upapanna iti hṛdayam | tadidamāhurbhaṭṭasudarśanapādāḥ - satyaṃ svarūpasphuraṇamastyeva | na tasya tirodhānamabhyupagamyate | na ca svarūpasphuraṇaṃ bhramavirodhi | kintu nityatvāṇutvaniravayavatvānyeva bhramavirodhīni | tānyāgamagamyāni | teṣāṃ tirodhistadviṣayajñānaprasarābhāvarūpaḥ sekocaḥ | ataḥ svarūpātiriktākārāntarābhyupagamānnātmanāśaprasaṅgaḥ iti || 64 ||
nanu ahaṃtvadharmabhūtajñānayorātmāsādhāraṇākārayoḥ sphuraṇādbhramānupapattirityatrāha - 
mū ahaṃtvajñātṛtve nanu puruṣadharmau natu vapurguṇau vastusthityā tadapi na tathātvaṃ nanu tayoḥ
anāderadhyāsādavagatamidānīṃ śrutinayasvayogādyairanyaḥ sphurati vapurādestu puruṣaḥ || 65 ||
ahaṃtvajñātṛtve - pratyaktvenāhaṃbuddhibodhyatvaṃ dharmabhūtajñānāśrayatvaṃ cetyete vastusthityā | vastusattayā | itthaṃbhūtalakṣame tṛtīyā | prakṛtyāditvādvā tṛtīyā | vyutpattisvābhāvyācca padārthaikadeśe’pi puruṣadharmatve tṛtīyārthānvayaḥ | puruṣadharmau nanu - vastusadbhāvaviśiṣṭātmadharmatākāveva |
puruṣāvātmamānavau iti kośaḥ | (vastusthityā puruṣadharmāvityanenāgrahaṇasaṃbhāvitamastvamātmavṛttitvasya vyudasyate) | na tu vapugurṇau - na tu dehadharmau | nityatadāśritatvāt guṇatvavyapadeśaḥ | atrāpi vastusthityeti saṃbadhyate | vastusattāyuktadehadharmatvābhāvake ityarthaḥ | antarālāgrahaṇasaṃbhāvitaṃ dehadharmatvaṃ cātra vyudasyate | ābhyāṃ vākyābhyām ātmavṛttitve sati dehāvṛttitvarūpamātmāsādhāraṇākāratvamahaṃtvajñātṛtvayoruktaṃ bhavati | yadyapīti yādau pūraṇīyam | ahaṃtvajñātṛtve yadyapi puruṣadharmāveva vapurguṇau ca na bhavata iti yojanā | asādhāraṇākārasphuraṇāt bhramānupapattiriti codyaparamidaṃ vākyam | pariharati - tadāpītyādinā | ( tathāpi) tayora haṃtvajñātṛtvayostathātvamātmavṛtitve sati dehāvṛttitvaṃ, ātmāsādhāraṇatvamityarthaḥ | anādeḥ - bījāṅkuranyāyena pravāhānāditvavataḥ adhyāsāt dehātmabhramāddhetoḥ | nāvagatam - na gṛhītam | ato bhramopapattiriti śeṣaḥ |
vastuto’sādhāraṇākāratve’pi tayostathātvasyāgrahānna bhramavirodhaḥ | śuktitvāderasādhāraṇyagraha eva hi rajatādibhramanivartaka iti bhāvaḥ | ahaṃtvāderātmāsādhāraṇyāgrahāddehātmabhramaḥ, dehātmabhramāttadagraha ityanyonyāśraya parihārāyānāderityuktam | tadāhurbhaṭṭasudarśanapādāḥ - jñānasyātmāsādhāraṇākārasya sphuraṇāt bhramānupapattiriti cenna; tasyāsādhāraṇatvāsphuraṇāt | asādhāraṇatayā sphurato dharmasya hi bhramavirodhitvaṃ dṛṣṭam | yathā śuktitvādeḥ | śuktitvaṃ śuktyasādhāraṇatayā pūrvaṃ jñātaṃ paścādbhramaṃ nivartayati | na tāvadjñānaṃ dehātiriktātmāsādhāraṇadharmatvena kvacidṛṣṭacaram | ato bhramaḥ sebhavati | ātmasādhāraṇadharmatvādarśanādadhyāsaḥ; adhyāsādasādhāraṇadharmatvāgrahaṇamityanyonyāśrayo’nāditayā parihṛtaḥ iti | tarhyasādhāraṇyagrahādinā
gaḍḍhandya ghddhaḍḍhadhtdṛdvadma "" atadrūpāropastaduparatirityādi ghaṭate "" ityatra bhramāvirodhino na tannivartakatvaṃ, śābdāparokṣasya tu na tannivartakatā, śabdapratyakṣapramāṇalakṣaṇasāṅkaryaprasaṅgena tasyaivāsaṃbhavādityādikamabhipretam | śābdāparokṣasya bhramānivartakatvādeva jīvanmuktirapi na saṃbhavatītyucyate | yathā bhāṣitaṃ bhagavatā - ""yato vākyādāparokṣyajñānāsaṃbhavādvākyārthajñānādavidyā na nivartate tata eva jīvanmuktirapi dūrotsāritā"" iti | ""adhiṣṭhāne puṃsi"" ""ahaṃtvajñātṛtve""sa ityādiślokadvayaṃ tu svapakṣe dehātmabhramopapādanaparatvāt prāsaṅgikam | jīvanmukteḥ pṛthaṅnirapekṣatvadyotanāya ca ""idaṃ māyāvādaḥ "" iti nigamanaṃ kṛtam - 
jīvanmuktimataṃ na jīvati yataḥ śastreṇa śāstrātmanā
lūnaṃ lokaviruddhasiddhi ca yatastenedamādāvapi |
āpastambanirastamaupaniṣadaṃ prasthānamātasthuṣāmācāryo’pi nirācakāra khalu taddvaipāyanākhyo muniḥ || 66 ||
jūvataḥ saśarīrasya, muktiḥ mokṣaḥ, tasya mataṃ prāmāṇikatvenābhyupagamaḥ, na jīvati - prāṇādhāraṇaṃ na karoti | balavatpramāṇajātaistasyāprāmāṇikatvabodhanāt; svaviṣayasatyatvalakṣaṇaprāṇadhāraṇaśūnyamityarthaḥ | kuta ityatrāha - yata ityādi | yataḥ śāstrātmanā ""tasya tāvadeva ciram"" ityādibhūyaśśāstrakṣalakṣaṇena śastreṇa lūnaṃ - chinnam | chinnasya hi prāṇādhāraṇaṃ duśśakamiti bhāvaḥ | uttarayaccha bdasya tacchabdanirapekṣatvācchāstrātmanā śastreṇa cchinnatvānna jīvatīti labhyate | yato lokaviruddhasiddhi - yataḥ pratyakṣaviruddhātmalābhavat | vākyārthajñānamātrādbandhanivṛttyadarśanāt | (lokaviruddhasiddhitvaṃ) cakāraḥ śāstrapratiṣedhaṃ samuccinoti | tena - śāstrapratyakṣabādhitasvarūpasattākatvena | ādau - pūrvam | idaṃ - jīvanmuktimatam | āpastambanirastamapi bhavati | āpastambena niṣiddhaṃ ca bhavatītyarthaḥ | tathā ca bhagavānāpastabaḥ - ""vedānimaṃ lokamamuṃ ca parityajyātmānamanvicchet | buddhe kṣemaprāpaṇaṃ tacchāstrairvipratiṣiddham | buddhe cet kṣemaprāpaṇamihaiva na duḥkhamupalabheta"" ityādinā jīvanmukiṃta nivāritavān | vedāṃstrivargasādhanakarmaṇi | imaṃ lokamamuṃ ca tatphalamaihikāmuṣmikaiśvaryaṃ buddhe vākyārthajñāne
kṣemaprāpaṇaṃ mokṣaḥ | kiṃ ca tat - śrutismṛtiniṣiddhaṃ jovanmuktimataṃ karma | upaniṣada idam aupaniṣadam | "tasyedam" ityaṇpratyayaḥ | durgrahabrahmapratipādanacaturavedāntabhāgīyaṃ prasthānaṃ tattvanirdhāraṇapāragamanaprārambhaṃ panthānaṃ vā | ātasthuṣām - āsthitavatāṃ bodhāyanaṭaṅkadramiḍabhāruciguhadevaprabhṛtīnām | ācāryaḥ - śāstrārthopadeśānuṣṭhāpanādikartā | dvaipāyanākhyaḥ - "" dvīpe badarikāmiśre"" ityādidiśā dvaipāyana ityākhyā yasya tathokto miniḥ - mananaśīlaḥ vyāsa ityarthaḥ | nirācakāra khalu - nirākṛtavān khalu | śārīrakamīmāṃsāturīyalakṣaṇadvitīyacaraṇe hi bhagavatā vyāsena ""nopamardenātaḥ"" iti sūtritam | tacca sūtraṃ jīvanmuktinirasanaparaṃ bhāṣitam bhagavatā - 
""ataḥ "yadā sarve pramucyante kāmāye’sya hṛdi sthitāḥ |
atha martyo’mṛto bhavati atra brahma samaśnute""
iti vacanaṃ na bandhopamardenāmṛtatvaṃ vadati"" iti | atra bhaṭṭasudarśanapādāḥ - ""jīvanmuktinirācikīrṣātiśayādidaṃ sūtramuktam | upāsanavidhānāttasyāprayāṇādanuvṛttiśruterutkrāntiśruterdeśaviśeṣaprāptyā sarvabandhavimokṣaśruterīdṛśānekaśrutīnāṃ bādhaprasaṅgāt pratyakṣavirodhācca jīvanmuktiranupapanneti bhāvaḥ"" ityanujagṛhaḥ | ātasthuṣāmiti āṅpūrvāt tiṣṭhaterliṭaḥ kvasuḥ || 66 ||
""bhedaśrutīnāmanṛtaviṣayatā"" lakṣaṇā caikyavācām"" ityābhyāṃ vākyābhyāṃ mṛṣāvādibhirvedaprāmāṇya(ma) (mitya)bhyupagantuṃ na śakyam; abādhitārthakatvarūpasya tattvāvedakatvasya pūrvabhāge durlabhatvāt; (yasya yadrūpaṃ tasya tasmin pratipādakatvarūpasya ca ) tasyādvaitavākyeṣvabhāvāt tebhyo niṣprakārakajñānasyaiva taiḥ svīkārāt; prātibhāsikārthānyatve sati vyadhikaraṇāprakārakatvasyāpi abādhitārthakatvaparyavasānāduktadoṣa iti sūcitam | kiṃ ca te hi śailūṣvadvaidikabhūmikāṃ parigṛhya, vedaprāmāṇyaṃ vadanta iva praviśya, vedasya vyavahārikārthaviṣayatvenāprāmāṇyāparaparyāyaṃ prāmāṇyamuktvā, kātsnaryaina tathoktau vaidikairbahiṣkāro bhaviteti kasyacitattvāvedakatvaṃ vadanta iva vaidikagoṣṭhīmadhyāruhya, tasyāpi
tattvāvedakatayābhimatatattvamasyādeḥ saṃsargabodhakatvamapahṛtya, padārthasyaiva vākyārthatāṃ vadantaḥ, paunaruktyaprasaṅgena tadapi tyājayanto bodhakatvameva nāsti kutaḥ prāmāṇyamiti vyañjayantīti mahācāryacaraṇānugṛhītarītirapi tābhyāmabhipretā bhavati | atra tu mukhāntareṇa
vedaprāmāṇyaparigrahānupapattisteṣāmucyate - (ketyādi)
kva vedāntaḥ kvāmīnihitamatayaḥ śaṃkaramate
vadantaḥ prāmāṇyaṃ bhramavilasitaṃ vedavacasām |
aho yadvadbuddhagrathitaphaṇitīnāmatha ca te
kaligrāhagrastairagaṇiṣata vedāntina iti || 67 ||
vedāntāḥ kva - bhramapramādavipralipsākaraṇāpāṭavādimūlatvasaṃbhāvanāvidhurā upaniṣadaḥ kasmin prakāre; 1 atra vartanta iti śeṣaḥ | aprāmāṇyagandhenāpi śūnyā ityarthaḥ | śaṃkaramate - śaṃkarābhyupagatamāyāvādapakṣe nihitamatayaḥ - buddheḥ svārasikaprasaraṃ nirudhya atikṛcchreṇa nikṣiptabuddhayaḥ | etena śraddhālūnāmapi pūrvāparavyāghātavaśāddirārohatvaṃ tanmatasya vyajyate | vedavacasāṃ nirdoṣāpauruṣeya vedavākyānām | buddhagrathitaphaṇitīnāṃ yadvat vipralipsunā buddhena nibaddhānāṃ girāmiva | bhramavilasitaṃ bhramaviṣayabhūtaṃ, doṣamūlatvena mithyābhūtam | prāmāṇyaṃ vadantaḥ - buddhadoṣamūlakatvādbuddhāgamaprāmāṇyaṃ mithyā; evaṃ brahmadoṣamūlakatvādvedaprāmāṇyamapi mithyeti yathā paryavasyati tathā vadantaḥ | amī adūraviprakṛṣṭā durvādinaḥ | kva kasmin prakāre vartante ? upadarśikamahācāryacaraṇānugṛhītadiśā vedaprāmāṇyābhyupagamagandhenāpi śūnyā ityarthaḥ| śaṃkarastu na tatheti | atra ca doṣamūlatvādvedaprāmāṇyaṃ mithyeti sukhāntareṇa vedaprāmāṇyaparigrahānupapattiruktā bhavati | evaṃ vedāntānāṃ śāṃkarāṇāṃ ca pramāṇapramātṛbhāvalakṣaṇasaṃbandhasyātyantāyogamupapādya teṣāṃ vedāntiśabdavyavahartavyatāyāmanuśete - atha cetyādinā; atha ca tathāpi teṣāṃ vedāntaiḥ saha upadarśitasaṃbandhāt sattāyoge’pi | te śāṃkarāḥ | kaligrāhagrastaiḥ - kalidoṣaprayuktāgrahagrṛhītaiḥ kalidoṣalakṣaṇanakrākrāntairvā mandaiḥ| vedāntina ityagaṇiṣata - pramāṇaprāmātṛbhāvalakṣaṇavedāntapratiyogikasaṃbandhavanta iti svīkṛtāḥ | aho - vismaye | tacchabdapravṛttinimittaleśenāpi śūnyeṣu tacchabdaprayogo vismayanīya ityarthaḥ | kaligrāhetyādinā nāyaṃ vyavahartavyānāmaparādhaḥ; kiṃ tu vyavahatarṝṇāṃ tanmūlabhūtakaleśceti vyajyate | tadidaṃ bhāṣitaṃ bhagavatā - ""tattu bhrāntimūlamiti cedetadapi bhrāntimūlamiti tvayaivoktam"" iti | agaṇiṣateti gaṇayateḥ karmaṇi luṅ || 67 ||
yadyapyapramāṇatvaṃ tatpakṣeṇa śrutīnām; tathāpi, prāmāṇikajanavañcanārthaṃ tairvedārthanirṇayaupayikatayā kṛtaṃ mīmāṃsānyāyasaṃcāraṃ dūṣa yati - (yadityādi)
yatsāmānyaviśeṣavatsarmasu guṇapratyāsavidhyātmasu
trayyanteṣu janārdanapraṇayiṣu prābalyadaurbalyayoḥ |
prastāvaṃ pratipedire katicanāpacchedanīticchalā
nmīmāṃsāpadavī pariśramakathā dūreṣu tacchobhate || 68 ||
katicana śaṃkaramatanihitamatayo vādinaḥ | sāmānyaṃ vyāpakadharmāvacchinno vidhiḥ, viśeṣaḥ tadvyāpyadharmāvacchinno vidhiḥ, tau nirṇeyatayātra sta iti sāmānya viśeṣaḥ ṣaṣṭhāntyādhikaraṇanayaḥ arśaāditvādcapratyayaḥ; tasya vartmasu viṣayabhūteṣupa | yadvā sāmānyaviṣayakeṣu viśeṣaviṣayakeṣu cetyarthaḥ | guṇasāmānyaguṇaviśeṣavidhiniṣedhalābhāya tu guṇapratyāsetyatra guṇaśabdaḥ | guṇapratyāsavidhyātmasu guṇaniṣedhavidhyātmakeṣu | janārdanapraṇayiṣu - vyākhyātṝṇāṃ prayatnena vināpi bhagavannārāyaṇapareṣu | trayyanteṣu - askhalitavākyapadavarṇānupūrvikeṣūpaniṣadbhāgeṣu |
atra guṇasāmānyaniṣedho guṇaviśeṣaniṣedhaparyavasāyīti guṇaviśeṣaniṣedhavākyanirguṇavākyayoḥ sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyaviṣayatvamiti sūcanāya sāmānyetyādyuktiḥ| sāmānyaviśeṣetyanena vidhiniṣedhasūcitavirodhena ca guṇasāmānyaniṣedhasya guṇaviśeṣavidheśca utsargāpavādanyāyaviṣayatvaṃ sūcyate | guṇapratyāsavidhyātmasvityanena niṣedhārthatvaṃ na guṇavidheryujyate; svainaiva vihitānāṃ svenaiva niṣedhe śruterunmattapralapitatvāpatteḥ; "prakṣālanāddhi" nyāyavirodhāpatteriti ca sūcyate | niṣedhyasamarpakatvamapi guṇavākyasya na yuktaṃ, nirguṇamityādivākyasthaguṇādipadānāmeva tatsamarpakatvāditi ca guṇapratyāsetyanena sūcyate | ata eva guṇavigrahādividhiniṣedhayorbhinnaviṣayatvenāvirodha ityapi sūcitam | sāmānyaviśeṣaviṣayatvādvidhiniṣedhayorna ṣoḍaśīgrahaṇāgrahaṇavākyavanniṣedhānusāreṇa
vidhinayanamityapi sūcitam | 6yyanteṣvityanena niyatapaurvāparyasyānuṣṭhānātmakatvasya ca pratīteḥ, aniyatapaurvāparyānuṣṭhānaviśeṣaviṣayakāpacchedanyāyāviṣayatvamanayoriti sūcyate | janārdanapraṇayiṣvityanena saguṇavākyānāmaparabrahmaviṣayatvaṃ nirguṇavākyārthānāṃ tu parabrahmaviṣayatvamiti viṣayavibhāgo na yuktaḥ | "eṣa ātmā" ityādivākyenobhayorekaviṣayakatvabodhanasvārasyāditi sūcitam | janārdanetyanena sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyasiddhaṃ devatāpāramārthyamapi prasaṅgāt sūcitam | vedāntānāṃ bhagavatparatvādapi saviśeṣaparatvamiti ca janārdanetyanena sūcitam | kiṃ ta nopāsanārthatvaṃ guṇavidheryuktam | tathā sati svarūpaparavākyasyāpi tadāpatteḥ | ato bhagavatsvarūpadharmatayaiva guṇānāṃ saguṇavākyapratipādyatvamiti ca janārdanapraṇayiṣvityanena sūcitam | sarvasyaiva coktārthasya sūcanāya sāmānyaviśeṣavatmasvityādīni viśeṣaṇānyupāttānīti bodhyam ||
apacchedanīticchalāt - apacchedādhikaraṇoktanyāyāpadeśena | chalapadena saguṇanirguṇavākyayorapacchedavākyavaiṣamyaṃ dyotyate | prābalyadaurbalyayoḥ krameṇa guṇaniṣedhakavākyānāṃ saguṇavākyabādhakatāprayojakabalatvasya saguṇavākyānāṃ nirguṇavākyabādhyatāprayojakadurbalatvasya ca, prastāvaṃ prasakiṃta, pratisandhānamiti yāvat| pratipedire - bhejire | parīkṣakāṇāṃ prābalyadaurbalyapratisandhānaṃ guṇaniṣedhavidhivākyeṣu jāyata iti jajñire vā iti yattat, mīmāṃsā dvādaśalakṣaṇyā(tma)kā karmanirṇayaupayikanyāyapratipādakasūtrasandarbharūpā; saiva padavī vedārthanirṇayopāyaḥ - 
""dharme pramīyamāne tu vedena karaṇātmanā |
itikartavyatāmārgaṃ mīmāṃsā pūrayiṣyati ||""
ityādibhiriktāḥ; tatra pariśramaḥ sadātanādhyayanādhyāpanādikleśaḥ; tasya kathā vārtā; tayā dūreṣu rahiteṣu mandeṣu śobhate | ramaṇīyaṃ bhavati | mīmāṃsānābhijñā eva idaṃ vaktuṃ śrotuṃ cārhantītyarthaḥ | paribhramakathetyapi pāṭhaḥ | mīmāṃsādhvani nirantaragatāgataprasaktītyādyarthaḥ ||
atra sudarśanabhaṭṭapādāḥ - "" apacchedanyāyo hyaniyatavirodhapaurvāparyaviṣayaḥ| udgātṛpratihrtrorubhayorapacchedaniyamo nāsti | tatrāpi krameṇāpacchedo’pyaniyataḥ | niyame sati hi sarvasvadakṣiṇākatvādakṣiṇākatvavirodhaniyamaḥ | ubhayorapacchede tasya
kramabhāvitve’pyudgātrapacchedaḥ pūrvabhāvī, itarastu caramabhāvīti viparyayeṇeti vā niyamo’pi nāsti | ato’pacchedanyāyaviṣayaḥ prayogāvadhikavirodhaḥ | saguṇanirguṇavākyavirodhastu svarūpaprayuktaḥ | vidhiniṣedharūpaniyata (niyata)paurvāparyaśca| ato virodhābhyupagame’pyaniyatavirodhapaurvāparyaviṣayo’pacchedanyāyo nātra pravartate"" ityāhuḥ |
ullaṅghitaścāyaṃ nyāyo mahācāryacaraṇaiḥ - ""krameṇāpacchede upakramanyāyaḥ kathaṃ na pravartate ? paratvāt prābalye kathamuprakramanyāyānutsādaḥ | ato nyāyadvayasya mitho vyāghāte sati itthamiha nirṇayaḥ kāryaḥ - anupasaṃjātavirodhitayā pūrvasmādarthapratītau satyāmupasaṃhārasyotpattidaśāyāmeva sandehāvaṣṭabdhatvāt pūrvanirṇayānusāreṇottarārthanirṇayaḥ kārya ityayamupakramanyāyaḥ | naimittikaśāstradvayārthabodhadaśāyāṃ tacchāstrayoḥ paurvāparyābhāvānnopakramanyāyasya tatra pravṛttiḥ | tataśca prayogaviśeṣaparyālocanayā viruddhayornaimittikayoḥ kimanuṣṭhoyamiti viśaye naimittikānuṣṭhānakālaṃ prati sannihitatvāt dvitīyanimittahetukameva kartavyam| ata uttarakāle pratihartrapacchedābhāve udgātrapacchede itthaṃkartavyamuttarakāle udgātrapacchedābhāve pratihartrapacchede itthaṃ kartavyamiti prāyaścittaśāstrārtha iṣyate | ayamapacchedanyāyaḥ - "pūrvābādhena" ityatra bādhaśca
pūrvanaimittikakartavyatābuddhinaimittikānuṣṭhānakālayormadhye dvitīyanaimittike kartavyātābuddhyutpattyā pūrvakartavyatābuddheravyavadhānamito’tiriktabādhānirūpaṇādupakramanyāyānavaṣṭambhasiddheśca""ityādinā, ato mīmāṃsānabhijñānāmevedaṃ saguṇanirguṇavākyayorapacchedanyāyaviṣayatvakathanaṃ ramaṇīyamiti bhāvaḥ |
guṇapratyāsavidhyātmasviti sāmānyaviśeṣavarmasviti ca viśeṣaṇadvayena guṇaviśeṣavidherguṇasāmānyavidheśvoktyā brahmaṇyārthaguṇaniṣedhanirāso vyañjitaḥ | jñānasya jñānāśrayatvādarśanādbrahmaṇo jñānamātratvapratipādanena nirguṇatvaṃ hi phalitamityayamārthaguṇaniṣedhaḥ | tannirāsaścettham - jñānasya jñānāśrayatvābhāvaḥ kiṃ vyāptyā kathyate ? uta śrutismṛtiprābalyāt | vyāptyā cet jñānānāśrayabhūtaṃ viṣayakāśakaṃ jñānaṃ kasyaciddharmabhūtameva dṛṣṭamiti dharmatvenāṅgīkriyatām | yadi śrutiprābalyāt svatantratvenāṅgīkriyate tarhi śrutibalādeva jñānāśrayatvamapygīkriyatāmiti so’yamārthaguṇaniṣedho guṇavighiśrutyupādānena
nirastaḥ || 68 ||
evamapacchedanyāyāviṣayatvokteḥsāmānyaviśeṣanyāyotsargāpavādanyāyaviṣayatvasya ca vakṣyamāṇatvācca guṇādividhiniṣedhavākyānāṃ bhinnaviṣayatvamāha
yadbrahmaṇo guṇavikāraśarīrabhedakarmādigocaravidhipratiṣedhavācaḥ |
anyonyabhinnaviṣayā na virodhagandhamarhanti tanna vidhayaḥ pratiṣedhabādhyāḥ || 69 ||
yadvā - śrutyaiva vihitānāṃ śrutyaiva niṣedhe śruterunmattapralapitatvāpatterguṇādividhiniṣedhayorbhinnaviṣayatvāvaśyaṃbhāvena niṣedho na vidhibādhaka ityucyate | yat - yasmāt | brahmaṇaḥ - paramātmanaḥ | guṇaśca, vikāraśca śarīraṃ ca bhedaśca karma cādayo yeṣāṃ tathoktāste gocarā viṣayā yayosta yoktayostayo rvidhipratiṣedhayo rbhāvayorvācaḥ bodhakavākyāni | anyonyabhinnaviṣayāḥ satyo mitho vibhinnavidheyaniṣedhyakāḥ satyaḥ, ta eva virodhagandhaṃ bādhyabādhakabhāvanayojakamekaviṣayatvavirahātmakaṃ virodham | narhanti noktavirodhayogyā bhavanti | tat - tasmāt vidhayo guṇādisadbhāvabodhakavācaḥ pratiṣedhabādhyā na bhavanti guṇādyabhāvabodhakavākyabādhyā na bhavanti | ato’pi nāpacchedanyāyapravṛttirityarthaḥ ||
guṇavidhayaḥ ""varṣāyutairyasya guṇāḥ"" ""samastakalyāṇaguṇātmako’sau" ityādayaḥ| guṇapratiṣedhāḥ - ""nirguṇaṃ niṣkriyam"" ityādayaḥ | vikāravidhayaḥ - ""tadātmānaṃ svayamakuruta"" ""yathorṇanābhiḥ sṛjate gṛhṇate ca"" ""juṣṭaṃ yadā paśyati"" ""dhātuḥ prasādānmahimānam"" ityādayaḥ | vikāraniṣedhāḥ - ""avikārāya"" ""satyaṃ jñānam"" ""satyasya satyam"" ityādayaḥ | śarīravidhayaḥ - ""ādityavarṇam"" ityādayaḥ | śarīraniṣedhāḥ - ""aśarīram"" ""niṣkalam"" ityādayaḥ, ""na tasya
kārye karaṇaṃ ca vidyate"" ityādayaśca | bhedavidhayaḥ - ""pṛthagātmānam"" ityādayaḥ | bhedaniṣedhāḥ - ""neha nānāsti"" ityādayaḥ | karmavidhayaḥ - "" ekaḥ śāstā"" ""viṣṇoḥ karmāṇi"" ityādayaḥ| karmaniṣedhāḥ - ""niṣkriyam"" ityādayaḥ ādipadena śyāmādivarṇaparigrahaḥ | tadvidhiḥ - ""nīlatoyadamadhyasthā"" ityādiḥ | tanniṣedhāstu - ""agotram"" ityādayaḥ | atra guṇaśabdaḥ sattvādyarthakaḥ | vikāraśabdo mṛtāvasthārthakaḥ | viśaraṇayogidehavāci | neha nāneti nānāśabdo vinārthakaḥ | kriyāśabdaḥ parispandārthaka iti vā | guṇādiśabdāḥ sāmānyaparā iti vā | ādye bhinniviṣayatvānnotsargāpavādanayaḥ | dvitīye tu tannayaḥ | ataḥ eteṣu cotsargāpavādanyāyasāmānyaviśeṣanyāyayoḥ pravṛttyā guṇādiniṣedhānāṃ vihitaguṇavigrahādivyatiriktaheyaguṇavigrahādivikhaṣayatvam; pravṛttyā guṇādiniṣedhānāṃ vihitaguṇavigrahādivyatiriktaheyaguṇavigrahādiviṣayatvam; ""sattvādayo na santi yatra ca prākṛtā guṇāḥ"" ""na bhūyaḥ saṃghasaṃsthāno
deho’sya paramātmanaḥ"" ityādyuktaviśeṣaniṣedhaparyavasāyitvaṃ vā yuktam | bhedaniṣedhasya ca prakāraprakāriṇoḥ prakārāṇāṃ ca mitho bhedasya prāmāṇikatvādabrahmakātmakasvatantravastvātmakabhedaviṣayakatvaṃ vihitaikyavirodhibhedaniṣedhaucityādyuktam | vikāraviṣayo’pi paramaiśvaryāvahajagadākāratārūpavihitapariṇāmavyatiriktaheyavikāraviṣayako "yajeta" upasīta" ityādividhisiddhayāgopāsanādijanyaprītyātmakavikāravyatiriktaviṣayakaścetyādikamanusandheyam || 69 ||
""yatsāmānyaviśeṣavartmasu"" ityatrabhipretāṃ guṇādividhiniṣedhayorutsargāpavādanyāyasāmānyaviśeṣanyāyaviṣayatāṃ kaṇṭhato vakti - agrīṣomīyetyādi)
agrīṣomīyahiṃsā nijavidhivihitā kiṃ niṣiddhā na hiṃsyādbhūtānītyetayoktyā paśupadaviṣayaśchāga eva nyarūpi |
chāgo vā mantravarṇāditi ca tadubhayanyāyato heyadharmakṣepārthā nirguṇoktiḥ śrutaśubhaguṇako nirmala brahmaṇi syāt || 70 ||
yadvā yatparairuktaṃ kāraṇatvarūpeṇa saguṇatvaṃ svarūpeṇa nirguṇatvamiti
guṇavidhiniṣedhayorviṣayabhede sati notsargāpavādanyāyaḥ | kiṃ ca "asthūlam" ityādiniṣedhasyāpi viśeṣaviṣayatayā na padāhavanīyanyāyapravṛttiḥ | ata eva na sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyaviṣayatvamapīti tanna; guṇopāsanasyātāttvikajñānatvena(tayā) mokṣāsiddhiprasaṅgāt | tathā sati "svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyata" ityādi virudhyeta | tataśca mokṣopāyabhūtopāsanaviṣayatvāt saguṇatvasya tāttvikatvamāvaśyakamiti guṇavidhiniṣedhayorekaviṣayatvamavarjanīyamiti viṣayabhedāyogādutsargāpavādanyāyapravṛttiravighātā | kiṃ cetyādikamapyanupapannam | nirguṇamityādivākyāpekṣayā tannyāyasaṃbhavāt |
tadetadāha - agnīṣomīyetyādinā | na hiṃsyādbhūtānītyetayoktyā - "" na hisyāt sarvā bhūtāni"" iti vākyena | nijavidhivihitā - "" agnīṣomīyaṃ paśumālabheta"" iti vākyavihitā | agnīṣomīyasya paśorhiṃsā | niṣiddhā kim? kimiti kṣepe | tathā ca niṣiddhetyarthaḥ | agnīṣomīyahiṃsātiriktahiṃsāniṣedhakatvaṃ na hiṃsyādityādivākyasyeti bhāvaḥ ||
taduktaṃ daśame - ""aviśeṣeṇa yacchāstramanyāyyatvādvikalpasya tu saṃdigdhamārādviśeṣaśiṣṭaṃ syāt"" iti | atra ""yadāhavanīye juhoti"" iti sāmānyaśāstrasyāpi ""pade juhoti"" ityādiviśeṣaśāstravat kalṛptatvapratyakṣaśrutatvābhyāṃ liṅgātideśavaiṣamyādbādhyatvāyogena tulyabalatvādvikalpaḥ |
""sāmānyasyāpi labdhatvāt pratyakṣeṇa viśeṣavat |
nāsti liṅgādivadbīdho nāpi bādho’tideśavat ||"" iti pūrvapakṣe,
""prāptiḥ sāmānyaśiṣṭasya viśeṣe lakṣaṇāvaśāt |
śrutyā viśeṣaśiṣṭasya tasmādasti balābalam ||""
iti sāmānyadharmaprakāreṇa viśeṣadharmāvacchinnasyopasthityapekṣayā viśeṣadharmaprakāreṇa tadupasthiteḥ śīghrabhāvitvena, patnīsaṃyājādihomeṣu tattvena jhaṭiti prāptā padādisthānatā homasāmānye(namanthara) prāptamāhavanīyaṃ ruṇaddhīti siddhāntitam | tannayāyena hiṃsāsthale’pi
pravartanānivartanayorvirodhādviśeṣataḥ śrutāgnīṣomīyahiṃsāyāṃ pravartanānvayena tadvyatiriktahiṃsāyāṃ nivartanānvayādagnīṣomīyahiṃsā na niṣiddhā kiletyarthaḥ | ayamevotsargāpavādanyāyaḥ ||
sāmānyaviśeṣanyāmāha paśupadeti | ""chāgo vā mantravarṇāt"" iti ṣaṣṭhānyādhikaraṇena chāga eva, paśupadaviṣayaḥ - paśuśabdavācyaḥ | nānyarūpi - nirṇitaḥ | nipūrvādrūpayatervicārārthakāt karmaṇi luṅ taśabdalopaśca | ""chāgo vā
mantravarṇāt"" ityatra ""sāmānyāvasitā tāvacchodanāvākyabhedataḥ | mantrastadavirodhena pākṣiko yaugiko’pi vā"" iti ""agnīṣomīyaṃ paśumālabheta"" iti vidhiḥ sāmānyaparyavasita iti tatparatantro’pi chinattyāgacchatīti yogavṛttyā chāgadravyakaprayogaviṣayakatayā vā upapādyata iti pūrvapakṣe - 
""codanā paśusāmānyamaṅgatvenāvabodhayet |
chāge’pi vikalaṃ tattu prativyaktisamāptitaḥ ||
mantraḥ prakaraṇāmnānānnityamaṅgaṃ prapatīyate |
klṛptayā rūḍhiśaktyā ca sacchāgasyaiva vācakaḥ ||
tena taṃ tādṛśaṃ gṛhnan vidhirātmavirodhinam |
vidhate chāgamevāṅgaṃ tasmācchāgopasaṃgrahaḥ ||""
iti siddhāntitatvena paśuśabdaḥ sāmānyavacano’pi chāgarūpaviśeṣaparyavasāyīti nirṇītamityarthaḥ | cakāraḥ pūrvoktasamuccāyakaḥ | bhavatu nyāyadvayam, tataḥ kimityatrāha - tadubhayetyādi | tābhyāmubhābhyāṃ nyāyābhyāmi tyarthaḥ | ubhayaśabdādvṛttāvayacpratyayaḥ svārthikaḥ | ubhayaśabdayogādeva dvavacanāntādapi nyāyaśabdāttasirāśritaḥ | śrutaśubhaguṇake - śrutibodhitakalyāṇaguṇe | "śeṣādvibhāṣā" iti kvip | nirmale malaṃ svāśrayanikarṣāvahadharmaḥ, tadrahitatvena śrute | brahmaṇi, nirguṇoktiḥ - nirguṇamityuktiḥ | heyadharmakṣepārthā syāt śrutakalyāṇaguṇavyatiriktaheyadharmābhāvabodhikā, heyadharmābhāvabodhaprayojanikā vā bhavituṃ yuktetyarthaḥ | kṣepo yadyabhāvastarhyartho’bhidheyaḥ | yadyabhāvabodhanaṃ kṣepastathā tvarthaḥ | prayojanamiti vivekaḥ ||
atra krameṇa śrutakalyāṇaguṇake heyadharmakṣepārthetyutsargāpavādanyāyasiddhārthakathanam; nirmale heyadharmakṣepārtheti sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyasiddhārthakathanam; nirguṇamiti
guṇasāmānyābhāvabodhakasya sattvādi(rūpaguṇa) svāśrayanikarṣāvahadharmābhāve’pyavasānasyocitatvāt | atra ca śrutaśubhaguṇakatvanirmalatvayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyādekavacanānnirguṇoktirityukteśca pūrvoktaparakīyacodyanirāsasiddhiriti dhyeyayam | hiṃsāvākyasya utsargāpavādanyāyaviṣayatvakathanaṃ tu bhaṭṭābhyupagataprakriyāmanurudhya kṛtam | na tu svapakṣeṇa tathā kṛtam | paśusaṃjñapanasya hiṃsātvābhāvasiddhāntāt | (taduktamaśuddhamiti cenna) | ""śabdāt"" iti sūtre bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ - ""ayameva samīcīno duṣpradharṣaṇaḥ parihāraḥ | ataḥ śyenāgnīṣomīyavaiṣamyaṃ cedameva | alpaduḥkhado’pyatiśayitābhyudayasādhako vyāpāro rakṣaṇam | anarthodarko vyāpāro hiṃsetyarthaḥ"" iti | uktaṃ ca vedāntakaṇṭakoddhāre campakeśagurubhiḥ - "" vastutastu, paśusaṃjñapanaṃ na hiṃseti siddhāntavyavasthitau mīmāṃsakavyavahārānurodhena nyāyadarśanamātramiti na tatrāgrahaḥ"" iti ""maraṇoddeśyakavyāpārasya hiṃsatve mānābhāvāt; balavadiṣṭājanakāniṣṭaphalakavyāpārasyaiva hiṃsātvāt"" iti ca | saguṇanirguṇavākyeṣu virodhikaraṇopakramādhikaraṇanyāyapravṛttistu paroktavirodhapaurvāparyaniyamamupajīvyābhyuccayatayā vaktavyeti nātra taduktiriti dhyeyayam || 70 ||
""ādau bhedaśrutīnām"" ityārabhyaitatparyantaṃ sūtrakāraiḥ sāṅkhyayogācārādipakṣaviṣayodbhāvitadoṣasāmyaṃ mṛṣāvādipakṣasyābhisandhāya mṛṣāvādapakṣadūṣaṇaṃ vistareṇoktavān | idānīm ""patyurasāmañjasyāt"" iti sūtranirṇītadiśā vedāntānāṃ bhagavatpāramyaprāvaṇyena devatāntarapāramyaṃ vedāntavirodhādanupapannamiti vaktuṃ vedāntānāṃ tatparatvamāha - 
kastvaṃ tattvidasmi vastu paramaṃ kiṃ tarhi viṣṇuḥ kathaṃ
tattvedaṃparataittirīyakamukhatrayyantasandarśanāt |
anyāstarhi giraḥ kathaṃ guṇavaśādatrāha rudraḥ kathaṃ
taddṛṣṭyā kathamudbhavatyavataratyanyat kathaṃ nīyatām || 71 ||
""janārdanapraṇayiṣu"" ityatra saṃgraheṇoktamupaniṣadāṃ bhagavatparatvamatra kiṃcidvistareṇocyate - 
""sṛṣṭisthityantakaraṇīṃ brahmaviṣṇuśivātmikām |
sa saṃjñā yāti bhagavāneka eva janārdanaḥ ||""
ityetatpratyabhijñāpanāya, rudraśarīrako bhagavānevātharvaśiraśśikhoktopāsanadvayopāsya iti, ""sphuṭamapunarbhāvāya puravairikalebariṇaḥ"" iti vakṣyamāṇārthapratipattisaukaryāya tu ""janārdanapraṇayiṣu"" ityatra janārdanaśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ | ""agrataḥ kva paravādisaṃbhavaḥ"" ityuktatvādvijigīṣupraśnāsaṃbhavena kaṃcana jijñāsuṃ prati varadagurubhirukto’yaṃ praśnaprativacanamālārūpaśloko vakṣyamāṇavistarasya saṃgraharūpaḥ ||
kastvam - iti jijñāsoḥ praśnaḥ | tasyottaraṃ tattvavidasmi - iti | tattvaṃ paradevatāpāramārthyaṃ tadvettīti tattvavit | tattatvaviduktyā svābhiprāyaparikalpitadevatāpāramyavāditvavyudāsaḥ | tarhi tattvavittve
kiṃ paramaṃ vastu - sarvasmāt paraṃ svāpekṣayā paravastuśūnyaṃ ca nirupādhikasattāyogi kimityarthaḥ | kā devatā paradevateti praśnārthaḥ | vastuśabdānusārāt kimiti napuṃsakaliṅgatā | śrutivaividhyādayaṃ praśnaḥ | tasyottaraṃ - viṣṇuḥ iti | paramaṃ vastvityanuṣajyate | viṣṇureva sarvasmāt paraḥ | svāpekṣayā pararahitaśca | atra ca ""viṣṇustadāsīddharireva niṣkalaḥ"" ityanuvādatvaśaṅkayāpi śūnyavākyaviśeṣasphoraṇāya viṣṇuśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ | kathamiti - atra viṣṇuḥ paramaṃ vastvityanuṣajyate | śrutivaividhyadarśanāt kathaṃ viṣṇoḥ pāramyanirṇaya iti praśnārthaḥ| tasyottaraṃ - tattvedaṃpareti | idaṃparaṃ tātparyaviṣayabhūtaṃ yasya tadidaṃparaṃ, tattve devatāpāramārthye, idaṃparaṃ tattvedaṃparaṃ, tathāvidha taittirīyakaṃ nārāyaṇānuvākarūpaṃ mukha mādiryeṣāṃ tathoktānāṃ trayyantānāṃ puruṣasūktasubāloraniṣanmahopaniṣadādīnāṃ, sandarśanāt - ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgopetatayā pramitatvalakṣaṇasamīcīnadarśanāt, viṣṇupāramyanirṇaya iti prativacanārthaḥ ||
tattvedaṃparetyanena upasanavidhyanyaparavākyavyāvṛttiḥ | ""śaṃbhurākāśamadhyedhyeyaḥ"" ityādikaṃ dyupāsanavidhiparaṃ nopāsyaviśeṣanirṇayaparam | nārāyaṇānuvākastu tadekapara iti bhāvaḥ | taduktaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ - ""juhvā juhoti"" iti vākyasya homasādhanatvamātraparatvena
dravyaviśeṣanirṇaye na tātparyam; ""yasya parṇamayī juhūḥ"" ityetadvākyasya dravyaviśeṣanirṇayaparatvena tadanuguṇārthatvam ""juhvā juhoti"" iti vākyasya bhavati; tathā ""śaṃbhurākāśamadhye dhyeyaḥ" ityādīnāṃ vākyānāmupāsanavidhiparatvānnopāsyaviśeṣanirṇayaparatvam | nārāyaṇānuvākasya tu upāsanavidhyanyaparatvābhāvādupāsyaviśeṣanirṇayaikaparatvamasti | atastadanuguṇārthatvam ""śaṃbhurākāśamadhye"" ityādivākyānāmabhyupagantavyam"" iti ||
pṛcchati - anyā ityādinā | tarhi - bhagavata eva paratve | anyā giraḥ katham ""na sanna cāsacchiva eva kevalaḥ"" "śaṃbhurākāśamadhye dhyeyaḥ" ityādīni vākyāni kathaṃ saṃgacchanta iti praśnārthaḥ | śivādiśabdānāṃ rūḍhibhaṅgāpatteriti bhāvaḥ | tasyottaraṃ - guṇavaśāditi | atra saṃgacchanta iti śeṣaḥ | ayogyārthatvarūpasya rūḍhibhaṅgaliṅgasya sadbhāvānmaṅgalatvasukhabhāvayitṛtvādyarthāntarasādhāraṇyācca kāraṇatvaśaṅkitadoṣavyāvṛttaye apekṣitamaṅgalatvādiguṇādhīnā śivādiśabdānāṃ bhagavati vṛttiriti bhāvaḥ | pṛcchati - atrāhetyādinā | rudraḥ, atra - paratvaviṣaye, kathamāha - ""nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatiriktaḥ"" ityādikaṃ pāramyasya svavṛtthitvasvetarāvṛttitvapratītikaraṃ vākyaṃ kathamāhetyarthaḥ | tasyottaraṃ - taddṛṣṭyeti | ""so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat"" ityuktatvena prahṇādavāmadevādivat svaśarīrakaparamātmana eva svaprayuktāhamādipadajanyabodhaviśeṣyatvā(tsvo)ktapāramyaṃ tatraiva bhaviṣyatīti buddhyā rudro ""nānyaḥ kaścit" ityādikamuktavānityarthaḥ | āhetyuvācetyarthakamavyayam | bravītyarthakaṃ laḍantaṃ vā | pṛcchati - kathamudbhavatīti | atra viṣṇurityanuṣajyate | viṣṇuḥ kathamudbhavati - ""brahmaviṣṇurudrendrāste sarvai saṃprasūyante"" iti vākyoktā bhagavadutpattiḥ kathaṃ bhagavatpāramye saṃgacchata iti praśnārthaḥ | tasyottaram - avataratīti; viṣṇuriti vartate | ""ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate"" iti śrutyā bhagavato na karmamūlakaṃ janma, kiṃ tu icchayāvatārarūpamityarthaḥ | ""brahmaviṣṇurudrendrāḥ"" ityasyopakrame ""brahmaviṣṇurudrendrāḥ"" ityasyopakrame ""kaśca dhyeyaḥ"" iti dhyeyavidhiparatvamavagatamiti kāryatvamanūdyate | anuvādaśca
prāpakānurodhena syāt | lakṣmīpativiṣaya uttaranārāyaṇe bhagavadutpattiravatārarūpā śrūyata ityasmin vākye bhagavadavatārānuvādaḥ | brahmaśivayoravatāraśrutyabhāvādindravaruṇāditulyotpattiranūdyata iti bhāvaḥ | pṛcchati - anyat kathamiti | ""tato yaduttarataram"" ""tasmin yadantaḥ"" ityādikaṃ rudrādināmanirvacanaṃ
bhasmadravyavidhānaṃ cetyevamādi kathamupapadyata iti praśnārthaḥ | tasyottaraṃ - nīyatāmiti; "tataḥ" ityasyāvadhiparatvābhāvena hetuparatayā, ""tasmin yadantaḥ"" ityasya ""etāṃśca satyān kāmān"" ityuktaguṇāṣṭakaparatayā, rudrādināmanirvacanasyoparitanavidyāṅgabhūtapraṇavaviṣayatayā, bhasmavidhānasya paśupatiśarīrakabhagavadupāsanāṅgatayā ca nayanaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ || 71 ||
uktamarthaṃ caturviśatiślokyā prapañcayati - 
ya ukto yājñikyāmupaniṣadi nissīmamahimā
sa uktaḥ puṃsūkte paramapuruṣaḥ kāraṇatayā |
punaḥ saubālikyāmupaniṣadi māhopaniṣadī
gatiścaivaṃ tadvat kalaya kaṭhavallīprabhṛtayaḥ || 72 ||
yājñikyāmupaniṣadi - taittirīyake | yo nissīmamahimā paramapuruṣaḥ uktaḥ | saḥ - paramapuruṣaḥ | kāraṇatayā, puṃsūkte puruṣasūkte ukta ityarthaḥ | ""yamantassamudre vidyutaḥ puruṣādadhi,"" "hnīśca te lakṣmīśca palayau"" ""viśvarūpāya te namaḥ""yaḥ paraḥ sa maheśvaraḥ"" "puruṣasya vidmaḥ sahasrākṣasya"ityādinā "sahastraśīrṣam"ityādinā ca taittirīyakaṃ bhagavatparamiti niścīyate | samudraśayitvavidyudvarṇatvalakṣmīpatitvānāṃ bhagavalliṅgatvāt, viśvarūpaśabdasya ca "viṣvakseno viśvarūpo murāriḥ śauriḥ śārṅgī padmanābho mukundaḥ"iti halāyudhakośena bhagavati rūḍhyavagamāt, "akāro viṣṇuvācakaḥ"ityādiṣu bhagavadvācakatayā prasiddhenākāreṇa bhagavantamanūdya tasya paramaiśvaryavidhānāt "puruṣasya vidmaḥ"iti bhagavatprāpterabhidhānāttadupāyatayā "mahādevasya dhīmahi"iti "śaṃkarājjñānamanvicchet"ityuktarudradhyānasyoktatvācca | "yasya parṇamayī juhūḥ"iti vākyasya yathā "juhvā juhoti"iti vākyavihitahomasādhanasya dravyaviśeṣaparatvam; tathā tattadvidyopāsyavastuviśeṣanirṇayaparatvaṃ nārāyaṇānuvākasyeti taitthirīyakasya bhagavatparatvaṃ sidhyati | paramapuruṣa iti - "yasmāt paraṃ nāparamasti kiṃcit" ityādivākyasiddhārthakathanam | nissīmamahimeti - 
"sa maheśvaraḥ"ityādisiddhārthakathanam | puruṣaśabdasya ca bhagavadasādhāraṇyaṃ śrutismṛtikośanyāyaiḥ siddham | "puruṣo vai rudraḥ"ityetattu puruṣasya bhagavato rudraśarīrakatvaparam |
"madhye bhedaṃ mahāghoraṃ mahābdherambasi sthitam |
dhyātvā pradoṣakāle tu śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt ||"
ityetadapi tāmasatvānna grāhyam | śrutiprasiddhasyaiva saṃbhave tasyaiva grāhyatvāt | tasya dhyānaśeṣatayā tathātvāsiddheśca | ataḥ samudraśāyitvādibhistaittirīyakaṃ bhagavatparam | nārāyaṇānuvākārthapratyabhijñānāt puruṣasūktamapi bhagavatparam |
"śrutismṛtisamākhyānaviniyogopabṛṃhaṇaiḥ |
sūktasya pauruṣasyāsya bhagavatparatāṃ viduḥ ||"
ityuktadiśā śrutyādibhiḥ puruṣasūktaṃ bhagavatparamiti siddham | vistarastu vedāntavijayādipūrvācāryagrantheṣvanusandheyaḥ | tatra ca bhagavataḥ kāraṇatvam "tasmādvirāḍajāyata" ityādibhiruktam | tadidamatroktam - sa uktaḥ puṃsūkte paramapuruṣaḥ kāraṇatayā - iti ||
saubālikyāmupaniṣadi - subālopaniṣadi | paramapuruṣaḥ kāraṇatayokta ityanuṣajyate | punaśśabdastu puruṣasūktoktikriyāmapekṣya subālopaniṣaduktikriyāvṛttiparaḥ | subālopaniṣadapi sahasraśīrṣāpuruṣādipadayogādbhagavatparaivetyarthaḥ | māhopaniṣadī gatiścaivaṃ - mahopaniṣadastattvapratipādanaritiścoktaprakāreṇa bhagavatpāramyāvalambinītyarthaḥ | "eko ha vai
nārāyaṇa āsīt"ityananyasādhāraṇanārāyaṇaśabdena viśeṣavidhānadbhagavata eva kāraṇatvaṃ vadatītyarthaḥ | kaṭhavallīprabhṛtayastadvat kaṭhavallyādayo’pi bhagavatparamyaparā ityarthaḥ | kaṭhavallyām "sarve vedā yatpadamāmananti"" ityupakramya "so’dhvanaḥ pāramāpnoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam"ityukteḥ kaṭhavallayapi bhagavatparā | prabhṛtiśabdena maitrāyaṇīyādiśrutayo vivakṣitāḥ | evam "saviturvareṇyaṃ ghravamacalamamṛtaṃ viṣṇusaṃjñaṃ sarvādhāraṃ dhāma"iti ghruvatvācalatvādiviśiṣṭatayā vedāntoditasya vastuno viṣṇusaṃjñatvaṃ vidhīyata iti maitrāyaṇīyaśrutirmagavatparā | "atha puruṣo ha vai nārāyaṇo’kāmayata prajāḥ sṛjeya"ityādikā nārāyaṇopaniṣat, "atha puruṣo ha vai nārāyaṇo’kāmayata prajāḥ sṛjñeya"iti "athāpaḥ sasarja"ityādikā mahānārāyaṇopaniṣat, "eko nārāyaṇa āsīt na brahma na śaṃkaraḥ"ityādikaṃ paiṅgirahasyaṃ ca prabhṛtiśabdagrāhyāṇi | ( kalaya) - taittirīyakapuruṣasūktasubālopaniṣatkaṭhavallīprabhṛtayo bhagavatkāraṇatvaparā iti kalaya jānīhīti kastvamiti
pṛṣṭavantaṃ jijñāsuṃ prati svoktārthasya śraddheyatvadyotanāya vidhiḥ | "paśyamṛgo dhāvati"ityatreva vākyārthasya karmatvam ||
nanu kā mahopaniṣadgatirityatrāha - 
vyājahne jagadekakāraṇatayā sākūtameko ha vā
ityārambhavatā mahopaniṣadudghoṣeṇa nārāyaṇaḥ |
brahmeśadyuvasundharoḍusalilasvāheśasomāṃśumatpūrvāśeṣajagatpraṇāśayati yaḥ kāle samunmīlati || 73 ||
nārāyaṇaḥ - " āpo nārā iti proktāḥ"ityādinā manusmṛtiniruktadviparītanirvacanotprekṣānarhānitarasādhāraṇābhidhānako bhagavān | sākūtaṃ - sābhiprāyam | " eko ha vā " ityārambhasavatā - "eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt"ityādinā | mahopaniṣadudghoṣeṇa - vispaṣṭādhītenāpalāpanarheṇa sphuṭārthakena mahopaniṣadvākyena | jagataḥ, ekakāraṇatayā ekamabhinnanimittopādānatvena sajātīyadvitīyarahitaṃ yatkāraṇaṃ tattvena | vyājahne - vyāhṛtaḥ |
"sadeva somyedamagra āsīt"iti sadvidyāyāṃ sataḥ kāraṇatvaṃ pratipannam | "brahma vā idamekamevāgra āsīt"iti tadeva sadbrahmatvena viśeṣitam | idamekameva agra āsīdityanuvādaḥ, brahmeti vidhiḥ | tathā "ātmā vā idameka evāgra āsīt" iti tadevātmatvena viśeṣitam | atrāpi ātmeti vidhiḥ, idamityādiranuvādaḥ | tathā etadevamahopaniṣadi "eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt"iti nārāyaṇatvena viśeṣitam, atrāpi nārāyaṇa iti vidhiḥ, eka ityādiranuvādaḥ | atra ca sarvaśākhāpratyayanyāyena, sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyena ca sarveṣāṃ kāraṇavākyānāṃ bhagavatyeva paryavasānādbhagavāneva kāraṇamiti mahopaniṣadādibhyo’vagatamityarthaḥ | hiraṇyagarbhādayaḥ śabdā ākāśaprāṇādiśabdavadayogyārthāḥ | śivādiśabdāśca bahuṣvaryeṣu prayogāt sādhāraṇā iti sadādiśabdavanna viśeṣopasthāpanakṣamāḥ | ato nārāyaṇaśabda eva yogyaviśeṣaikasvārthaka iti
tameva nirdiśatīti śrutyabhisandhiḥ sākūtamityanenoktaḥ ||
kiṃ ca "yaḥ paraḥ sa maheśvaraḥ"ityatra hi akāravācyatvaprasiddhyanurodhena "yaḥ paraḥ" iti bhagavantamanūdya maheśvaraśabdarūḍhyarthavivakṣāyāṃ nirjñātabhedayorabhedāsaṃbhavāt, rudraśarīrakatvasya ca viśiṣyātra vidhāne prayojanābhāvāt, maheśvaraśabdasya ca yogārthavivakṣayānyatra pracuraprayogadarśanāt, viśiṣyaiva bhagavati prayogaprācuryadarśanācca niratiśayaiśvaryalakṣaṇamahāguṇo maheśvaraśabdena vidhīyata iti yuktam | atra tu sadādiśabdoktasāmānyasya vyaktiviśeṣaparyavasānākāṅkṣitvāt nārāyaṇaśabdasya ca rūḍhyarthena vinā kvacidapi prayogādarśanādbhagavata eva ca sarvaśarīkatvenāntaryāmibrāhmaṇādiṣu uktatvenāparyavasānavṛttyāpi kasyacit pratipādyatvāsaṃbhavāt, "eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt"ityatra nārāyaṇaśabdena sadādiśabdoktavastuni tādātmyena paramakāraṇatvayogyanārāyaṇa eva vidhīyate | ataḥ
kāraṇatvasya nārāyaṇatvavyāpyatvānnārāyaṇādanyasya na kāraṇatvasaṃbhava ityayamabhiprāyaśca sākūtamityatra vivakṣitaḥ | arthāprāptivaśādevāsya vidhitvopapatteḥ na "ha vai" śabdamātreṇāsyānuvādatvāpattirityabhiprāyo vā sākūtaśabdenoktaḥ | asyaiva tvabhiprāyasya udghāṭanamuttaraślokena kriyata iti na punaruktiḥ | kāraṇavastuno nārāyaṇatvavidhāne’pi samānasaṃvitsaṃvedyatayā (nārāyaṇasya kāraṇatvasiddhā) jagadekakāraṇatayā nārāyaṇo vyajahna (ityuktam) ||
