(atra cottarayacchabdasya tacchabdanirapekṣatvānna tacchabdādhyāhāraḥ) yaḥ kāle pralayārambhakāle | samunmīlati - svasaṃkalpamelanalakṣaṇasāmīcīnyayuktodayavati sati | pradhānakāraṇavāde tu pralayakālā vyavastheti samityasya bhāvaḥ | brahma - caturmukhaḥ, īśo rudraḥ, dyaurākāśaḥ, vasundharā bhūmiḥ, uḍūni - nakṣatrāṇi, salilam āpaḥ, svāhā - vahnipriyā, tasyā īśo ‘gniḥ, somaḥ candraḥ, aṃśumān sūryaḥ, (pūrva ādayo) yasya tattathoktaṃ ca tadaśeṣaṃ ca tajjagacceti vigrahe (karmadhārayā) dvitīyā | prāṇaśayati pralīnaṃ karoti | nārāyaṇaḥ kāraṇatayā vyājahne sa eva jagatpralayakartetyarthaḥ | atrottarārdham " na brmā neśāno
nemedyāvāpṛthivī na nakṣatrāṇi nāpaḥ"ityādimahopaniṣadgatavākyārthakathanaparam | ataścaturmukheśānādīnāṃ saṃhāryatvādipratītesteṣāṃ jagatkāraṇatvāsaṃbhavāddhiraṇyagarbhādiśabdānāṃ yogenāparyavasānavṛttyā vā nārāyaṇaśabdoktaviśeṣa eva paryavasānamiti bhāvaḥ || 73 ||
ākūtamudghāṭayati - 
aprāptatvānmahatyāmupaniṣadi jagatkāraṇasyānuvādo
na syādeko ha vā ityayamupari hi devebhya ityādinītyā |
vākyasvārasyato hi vyagaṇi bhagavataḥ kāraṇatvaṃ pareṣāṃ
kāryatvaṃ cāviruddhaṃ tadapi na paramastyatra kiṃcidvidheyam || 74 ||
jagatkāraṇasyāprāptatvāt - sadādiśabdoktakāraṇavastuno nārāyaṇatāyā vākyāntarānavagatatvāt | mahatyāmupaniṣadi mahopaniṣadi | eko ha vā ityayam " eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt"ityayamaṃśaḥ | upari hi devebhya ityādinītyā "upari hi devebhyo dhārayati"yadāgreyo’ṣṭākapālaḥ"ityādivākyeṣu "vacanāni tvapūrvatvāt""vidhistu dhāraṇe’pūrvatvāt"iti sūtrābhyāṃ nirṇītena, yacchabdahiśabdādyanuvādatvasūcakaśabdayoge’pi arthasya mānāntarānadhigatatvena vākyasya vidhitvameva nānuvādatvaṃ, yatra tvarthasya mānāntarāvagatiḥ tatra tu prayuktaśabdānāṃ pramāṇāntaraprāptānuvādatvasūcakatayā gaticintanamiti nyāyena | anuvādo na syāt - anuvādatvānarha ityarthaḥ | nanu aprāptatvaṃ hi sādhakabādhakamānāntarāgocaratvam; tatra sādhakapramāṇāviṣayatve’pi bādhakapramāṇasadbhāvānna vidhiḥ; "na sanna cāsacchiva eva" iti hi vidhitvabhañjakaṃ vidyata ityatrāha - vākyetyādi | aviruddhaṃ - pramāṇāntarāpratiṣiddham | bhagavato nārāyaṇasya | kāraṇatvaṃ, vākyasya svārasyam "aprāpte śāstramarthavat" iti vidhitvasya svataḥprāptatvarūpam tena, vyagaṇi hi vihitaṃ khalu | tathā, pareṣāṃ brahmeśānādīnām | aviruddhaṃ mānāntarāpratiṣiddham | kāryatvaṃ ca vākyasvārasyato vyagaṇi hi | ataḥ pramāṇāntarābādhitatvādvidhitvamityarthaḥ | śivaśabdasya tu sādhāraṇatvāt sadādiśabdavanna vidhitvabhañjakatvamiti bhāvaḥ | evamarthasthitiruktā; "ha vai" śabdasya vidhitvabhañjakatve’pi | atra param "eko ha vai nārāyaṇa
āsīt"" iti vākye tu | vidheyaṃ kiṃcidapi nāsti ""viṣṇustadāsīddhariraiva niṣkalaḥ" iti svasamanārthavākyāntaravihitārthānuvādakatvamasyopapannam | na tu vibhinnāyogyārthakasādhāraṇaśivādiśabdayuktavākyoktārthānuvādakatvamityarthaḥ ||
atra paramityanena yadāgneyādivākyeṣu arthāprāptivaśādvidhitve’pi, "
eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt"ityasyānuvādakatvamupapannamiti yadāgneyādivākyavaiṣamyaṃ sūcyate | tadidamanugṛhītaṃ vedārthasaṃgrahatātparyadīpikāyāṃ
bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ - "nanveko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt"ityatrānuvādakatvasūcakahavaiśabdaśravaṇāt, "sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtānyākāśādeva samutpadyante"iti vākyavadidamapi vākyamanuvādaparamiti vākyāntarasāpekṣam | tatra "tadvṛttamevakāraśca syādupādeyalakṣaṇam" iti vidhitvasūcakaivakāravatā "na sanna cāsacchiva eva kevalaḥ"iti vākyenoktaḥ jīva evānūdyate"iti | atrocyate - "aprāpte hi śāstramarthavat" iti nyāyena svataḥ prāptasya vidhitvasya havaiśabdo na bhañjakaḥ | "yadāgneyo’ṣṭākapālaḥ"ityatra "yadvṛttayogaḥ prāthamyamityādyuddeśyalakṣaṇam"ityanuvādasūcakayacchabdasadbhāve’pi "vacanāni tvapūrvatvāt" iti sūtreṇa vidhitvaṃ sthāpitam | pramāṇāntarāprāptatvāttathā "upari hi devebhyo dhārayati"ityatra hiśabde satyapi "vidhistu dhāraṇe’pūrvatvāt" iti sūtreṇa vidhitvaṃ sthāpitam | tasmānna svataḥprāptavidhitvabhañjako havaiśabdaḥ, api tu pramāṇāntareṇa vidhitve bhagne prayuktaśabdasya gatipradarśakaḥ | ākāśakāraṇatvavākye tu ayogyatvādinā vidhitvabhaṅgāt havaiśabdonuvādarūpatājñāpakaḥ | atra tu pramāṇāntarāprāptatvādvidhitvabhañjakābhāvācca havaiśabdo na svataḥ prāptavidhitvabhañjakaḥ | śiva evetyanena vidhitvabhaṅga iti cenna | "śivāste santu panthānaḥ""śivaṃ karmāstu""sivaḥ śivānāmaśivaḥ śivānām" ityādiṣvarthāntaraparatvadarśanena, śivaśabdasya sādhāraṇatvāt; sādhāraṇo’pi vidhitvabhañjakaśca sadevetyādiśabdāchiva evetyādivākyasyānuvādatā syāt | kiṃ cānuvādarūpatvamastu; tathāpi "viṣṇustadāsīddharireva niṣkalaḥ"ityādisvasamānārthavākyaśravaṇāttadanuvāditvameveti na viruddhārthaparavākyānuvāditvam"iti |
yadvā bhagavataḥ kāraṇatvaṃ pareṣāṃ kāryatvaṃ ca vākyasvārāsyato vyagaṇihi, tadapi bhagavataḥ kāraṇatvaṃ pareṣāṃ kāryatvaṃ - ca aviruddhaṃ
mānāntarāvirodhādvidhānārhamityarthaḥ | na kevalaṃ svārasyāvirodhābhyāṃ vidhānārhatvaṃ, kiṃ tvasmin vidheyāntarābhāvādapi bhagavatkāraṇatvameva vidheyamityāha - na paramastvayatra kiṃcidvidheyamiti | vidhitvasya svataḥ, prāptatvāt, havaiśabdasya vidhitvabhañjakatvābhāvāt, bhagavatkāraṇatāyāḥ pramāṇāntarānavagatatvāt, caturmukhādīnāṃ kāryatvasya ca mānāntarāviruddhatvāt, bhagavatkāraṇatvasya ca mānāntarāviruddhatvāt, asmiṃstu vākye vidheyāntarābhāvācca bhagavatkāraṇatvaṃ vidheyameveti bhāvaḥ | upāsanavidhyanyaparāṇi anyāni tvetadanurodhena neyānīti hṛdayam || 74 ||
nanvastu bhagavataḥ kāraṇatvam | kāraṇabhūtasyaiva bhagavato nārāyaṇasyākāśaśabdavācyatayaprasiddhasyāntarvartī kaścana tattvaviśeṣo mumukṣūpāsyatayā chāndogye śrutaḥ | "atha yadidamasmin brahmapure daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma daharo’sminnantarākāśaḥ tasmin yadantastadanveṣṭavyaṃ tadvāvavijijñāsitavyam"ityasyākāśasya nāmarūpayonirvoḍhṛtvaśravaṇāt, puruṣasūkte puruṣasya nāmarūpayoḥ kartṛtvadarśanācca, ākāśaparyāyabhūtāt puruṣādanyasyānveṣṭavyatayopāsyatvaṃ pratīyata itīmaṃ vyomātīta vādaṃ pratikṣipati - 
yatsaṃjñāmūrtikartuḥ paramapuruṣataḥ kiṃcidākāśanāmno
hṛtpadme syādupāsyaṃ śrutiśirasi paraṃ sāmagānāmagāyi |
tatra praśnottarābhyāmaghavihatimukhān satyasaṃkalpatāntānaṣṭau śiṣṭāṃstadīyānanidamudayinastadguṇānudgṛṇanti || 75 ||
sāmagānāṃ śrutiśirasi paraṃ - chāndogye tvityarthaḥ | taittirīyāderapi vyomātītaparatvāśrayaṇe mūlaṃ chāndogyameveti parābhimānasūcanāyātuśabdārthakasya paramityasya nirdeśaḥ | paramityetadbhinnakramatayā paramapuruṣataḥparamiti vā yaujanīyam | ākāśanāmnaḥ - ākāśanāmakāt, saṃjñāmūrtikartuḥ - nāmarūpakārakāt | ata eva paramapuruṣataḥ ākāśakartṛkatayā śrutayornāmarūpayoḥ kartṛtayā puruṣasūktoktāt paramapuruṣāt, param anyat
| kiṃcit - ekam | yat hṛtpadme hṛdayapuṇḍarīke | upāsyaṃ syādityagāyi - anveṣṭavyatayā vihitam | gāyateḥ karmaṇi luṅi ciṇ | "tadanveṣṭavyam"iti śrutau tavyapratyayānusāreṇāstikriyāyā liṅntapadādhyāhāra ucita iti pradarśanāya syādityuktam | evaṃ "athayadidam"ityādivākye śaṅkāhetubhiḥ saha paraśaṅkitārthasya dharmī nirdiṣṭaḥ | atha tasya dharmiṇaḥ
paroktatattvaviśeṣatvaṃ na saṃbhavati; kiṃ tu "tasmin yadantaḥ" iti guṇajātamucyata ityāha - tatretyādinā | tadguṇānityatra taditi guṇāniti padadvayam | anyathā tadīyānityanena paunaruktyāpatteḥ | pūrvayacchabdasya tacchabdāpekṣāniyamena tacchabdādhyāhārāpatteśca | taditi ca dvitīyāntam | yadagāyi, tat karma | guṇān udgṛṇanti guṇatvena vadantītyarthaḥ | śrutisūtrakāravākyakārāstadanusāriṇo’smadācāryāścetyādiḥ | tatra - chāndogye | praśnottarābhyām "kiṃ tadatra vidyate"iti codyagarbhapraśnena "yāvān vā ayamākāśaḥ"
ityādinā tatparihāragarbheṇa uttareṇa ca | aghavihatimukhān - apahatapāpmatvādīn | satyasaṃkalpatāntān - satyasaṃkalpatvaparyantān | śiṣṭān - "apahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyuviṃśoko vijighatso’pipāsaḥ satyakāmaḥ satyasaṃkalpaḥ""etāṃśca satyān kāmān""asmin kāmāssamāhitāḥ"ityādibhirbodhitān | tadīyān - daharākāśabhūtaparamapuruṣīyān | anidamudayinaḥ asmin kāle udayina āvirbhāvavanta idamudayinaḥ te na bhavantītya nidamudayino nityārvibhūtān | aṣṭau guṇānudgṛṇanti, ("tasmin yadantaḥ" iti nirdiṣṭaṃ dharmiṇaṃ daharākāśabhūtaparamapuruṣaguṇagaṇa) iti vadantītyarthaḥ | ato na tattvaviśeṣasyopāsyatvaprasaktiriti bhāvaḥ ||
tatratyapraśnaprativacanavivaraṇaṃ ca mahācāryaiḥ kṛtam - "taṃ cet brūyuryadidamasmin brahmapure daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma daharo’sminnantarākāśaḥ kiṃ tadatra vidyate yadanveṣṭavyaṃ yadvāva vijijñāsitavyam"iti codyagarbha evāyaṃ praśnaḥ | anyathā yadidamityādyanuvādavaiyarthyāt | ataḥ sūkṣmatvāddaharākāśe kiṃ vartitumarhatīti praśnābhiprāyaḥ | evaṃ sati kimiti svaramataḥ pratīyamānadharmipraśnarūpatāpyujjīvitā | "yāvān vā ayamākāśastāvāneṣo’ntarhṛdaya ākāśaḥ"iti hetvasiddhyā codyasya pārihāra uktaḥ | tāvānityuktyā paricchedaśaṅkāyām
"ubhe asmin dyāvāpṛthivī"ityādinā sarvādhāratvoktyā pūrvavākyasyāparicchede tātparyaṃ vyañjitam | evaṃ codye niraste "asmin kāmāḥ samāhitāḥ"iti praśnasyottaramuktam | anantaram "ātmāpahatapāpmā"ityādinā kāmān vavṛtya "atha ya ihātmānamanuvidya vrajantyetāṃśca satyān kāmān" iti satyapadena dyāvāpṛthivyādayo vyāvartitāḥ"iti ||
apahatapāpmatvaniṣkarṣaśca bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ anugṛhītaḥ | "kṣetrajñeṣu pratyavāyakarapāpasajātīyānāṃ karmaṇāṃ svalīlākṛtānāmapi phalajananaśaktipratibhaṭatvaṃ nāma kaścidayamīśvarasya svabhāvaviśeṣaḥ | pariśuddhātmaviṣayasyāpyapatapāpmaśabdasyāyamevārthaḥ | sa tu tasya nirodhānārhaḥ | pratibandhakanivṛttāvāvirbhavati | īśvarasya tirodhānānarho nityāvirbhūta iti viśeṣaḥ"iti ||
tadidaṃ pariṣkṛtamadvaitavidyāvijayavyākhyāyāṃ vīrarādhavagurubhiḥ - "nityasiddhaṃ cāpahatapāpmatvaṃ vyadhikaraṇānavacchinnaviṣayatāsaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākaḥ pāpmābhāvaḥ | tasya cāvirbhāvatirobhāvau pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyatadasāmānādhikaraṇye | athavā apahatapāpmatvaṃ pāpakāraṇasamavadhānakālīnapāpānutpādaprayojakībhūtadharmaḥ | tasyāvirbhāvaḥ prayojakatāvacchedakadharmavaiśiṣṭyam | niruktānutpādaprayojakaṃ hi caramadehaviyogaprākkālāvacchinneśvarīyabandhasaṃkalpaviṣayatābhāvaviśiṣṭātmatvam | anyathā muktāvaśloṣānupapatteḥ | na ca vidyayā tadupapattiḥ | tasyāḥ prāmādikāghāśleṣamātraprayojakatvāt | nityeśvara sādhāraṇyācca | niruktānutpādasya cācetane bhāvānna viśeṣyavaiyarthyam | satyasaṃkalpatvaṃ ca svaviṣayotpāde prayatnāntarānapekṣapariṇāmayogyadravyāśrayatvam | tacca dravyaṃ saṅkalpajñānameva tasya cāvirbhāvastādṛśāvasthāvaiśiṣṭyam"iti ||
anidamudayina ityanenoparitanaprajāpativākyokta(jīviyā)pahatapāpmatādivyudāsaḥ | te hṛdya tirohitāḥ | aṣṭāviti ca pradarśanārthaṃ dyupṛthivyādistraṣṭṛtvadhārakatvādiguṇagaṇasya | taduktaṃ vedārthasaṅgrahatātparyadīpikāyāṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ - "uktadyupṛthivyādīnāṃ straṣṭṛtvadhārakatvādiguṇagaṇo’pahatapāpmatvādiguṇagaṇaśca vivakṣita ityarthaḥ"iti | śrutiruktā | "kāmādītaratra tatra cāyatanādibhyaḥ"iti sūtram |
vākyakāraśca "tasmin yadantaḥ"iti kāmavyapadeśa ityāha | "kāmyanta iti kāmāḥ apahatapāpmatvādayo guṇā ityarthaḥ"iti bhagavadbhāṣyakārāḥ | ataḥ śrutisūtrakāravākyakārabhagavadbhāṣyakārāḥ "tasmin yadantaḥ" ityantaravasthita na tattvāntaram; kiṃ tu paramapuruṣaguṇaguṇa iti vadantītyarthaḥ | "tasmin yadantaḥ"iti daharākāśamātramucyata iti pakṣaḥ pratikṣiptaḥ pūrvam "asvārasyacatuṣṭayam"ityādinā | atra tu daharākāśāntarvartinastattvaviśeṣatvaṃ nirastamityapaunaruktyam | udgṛṇantīti graśabda iti kryādau paṭhitasya bahuvacane śnāpratyaye ca sati rūpam || 75 ||
śvetāśvataraśruteradhikavastuparatvavādaṃ niracaṣṭe - 
prastutyāmṛtahetumādipuruṣaṃ nyakkṛtya mārgāntaraṃ
tasyaitadghaṭanāya niṣpratibhaṭāmudghuṣya sārveśvarīm |
uktistatra tato yaduttarataraṃ tanmuktidāyīti yā
tacchravetāśvatarāgame nigamanaṃ proktasya hetūktimat || 76 ||
atrāyaṃ prakṛtopayuktaśvetāśvataropaniṣadgatavākyasaṃdarbhaḥ - 
vedāhametaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam |
ādityavarṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt ||
tameva viditvātimṛtyumeti |
nānyaḥ panthā vidyate’yanāya ||
yasmātparaṃ nāparamiti kiñcit |
yasmānnāṇīyo na jyāyo’sti kaścit ||
vṛkṣa iva stabdho divi tiṣṭhatyeka
stenedaṃ pūrṇaṃ puruṣeṇa sarvam |
tato yaduttarataraṃ tadarūpamanāmayaṃ
ya etadviduramṛtāste bhavanti ||
athetare duḥkhamevāpiyanti |
sarvānanaśirogrīvaḥ sarvabhūtaguhāśayaḥ ||
sarvavyāpī sa bhagavāṃstasmāt sarvagataḥ śivaḥ |
mahān prabhurvaipuruṣaḥ sattvasyaiṣa pravartakaḥ || iti ||
atra yasmāt paraṃ nāparamasti kiṃcit iti vākye puruṣāt paraniṣedho vakṣyamāṇaparavyatiriktaviṣayaḥ | tato yaduttarataram ityatra tu pūrvaprastutāt puruṣādyaduttarataraṃ paraṃ śivātmakaṃvastvityarthaḥ | tasmāt sarvagataḥ śivaḥ vakṣyamāṇatvāditi pūrvapakṣyabhimato’rthaḥ ||
siddhānte tu mahāpuruṣatvādityavarṇatvatamasaḥ paratvādiśravaṇādbhagavato mokṣopāyatvamanyasya tvatathātvaṃ ca vedāham
ityādinocyate | tadupapādanāya ca yasmāt param ityādinā paramapuruṣasya samābhyadhikarāhityamucyate | yasmāt paraṃ yasmādanyat kiṃcidapi paraṃ nāsti | kenāpi prakāreṇa puruṣavyatiriktasya paratvaṃ nāstīsyarthaḥ | yasmānnāṇīyo na jyāyo’sti kaścit
iti puruṣavyatiriktasya kasyāpi jyāyastvaṃ sarveśvaratvaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ | tato yaduttarataretyādi | yataḥ puruṣatattvamevottarataraṃ tataḥ tasmāddhetoḥ - yaduttarataraṃ tadarūpamanāmayaṃ - karmakṛtadehatatkṛtaduḥkhavirodhi etadye viduste muktā bhavanti itare - etadvedanaśūnyāstu duḥkhameva prāpnuvantītyarthaḥ | anena ca puruṣavedanasyāmṛtatvahetutvaṃ taditarasyānupāyatvaṃ ca upakrame pratijñātaṃ sahetukamupasaṃhniyate | pratijñāyā hetusākāṅkṣatvāt, tata ityasya hetuparatvamevocitam | puruṣeṇeti tṛtīyāntapadanirdiṣṭasya śābdaprādhānyarahitasya tatpadena parāmarśārhatvaṃ hetutvaṃ copapannam | evaṃ ca tata ityasya hetvarthakatvasvārasyenāvadhyarthakatvābhāvādasmin vākye puruṣāt parasya kasyacidvidhānābhāvena nedaṃ vākyaṃ yasmāt paraṃ nāparamityuktaparaniṣedhasaṅkocakaṃ bhavitumarhatīti ||
tadidamāha - prastutyetyādinā | śvetāśvatarāgame ādipuruṣaṃ kāraṇabhūtaṃ
bhagavantam | amṛtahetuṃ prastutya mokṣopāyatvenopakrame(ti) pratipādya tasya - amṛtasya | mārgāntaram - upāyāntaram | nyakkṛtya - niṣidhya | daśaite rājamātaṅgāstasyaivāmī turaṅgamāḥ itivadvṛttvaryaikadeśalyāpi parāmarśaḥ | etadvaṭanāya - bhagavadupāyatvetarānupāyatvayorupapādanaya parāntarasadbhāve hi tasyāpyupāyatvaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ | niṣpratibhaṭām - asaṃkucitamaprāyikīmiti yāvat | sārveśvarīm - sarveśvaratvam | sarveśvaratvādbhāve ṣyañaḥ ṣitvāt ḍīṣ | udghuṣya - sphuṭaṃ pratipādya | sārveśvarīmityetāvaduktau prāyikatvabuddhiḥ syāditi niṣpratibhaṭāmityuktam | anena ca śrutisthakiṃcitkaścicchabdalabdhārtho’nūdyate | atra pūrvārdhe prathamapādena vedāhametam ityāderarthonūditaḥ | dvitīyapādena tu yasmāt paraṃ nāparam ityāderartha uktaḥ | tatra - śvetāśvetare | tato yaduttaratamiti yā uktiḥ (taduttaraśabdoktam ,) muktidāyi mokṣapradamiti ca yā uktiḥ etadarthikā ya etadviduramṛtāste bhavanti ityuktiśca | eko’pyayamiti śabdaḥ sāmarthyāt svarūpaparo’rthaparaśca bhavati | tato yaduttarataram iti vākyarūpāyā uktirbhavati tat - sā uktiḥ | nigamanamiti vidheyavaśāt klībatvam | proktasya prārambhe uktasya | upakramoktabhagavadupāyatvānyānupāyatvarūpārthasya, hetūktimannigamanam - hetukathanasahitopasaṃhāro bhavati | mokṣopāyatvīyabhagavadasādhāraṇyanirūpitaṃ
yadbhagavadīyanissamābhyadhikatvaniṣṭhaṃ jñāpakatvarūpaṃ hetutvaṃ taduktipūrvako’yaṃ bhagavadupāyatvānyānupāyatvayorūpasaṃhāraḥ kirayata ityarthaḥ | ato’dhikavastuvādasya nātrāvakāśa iti bhāvaḥ | tadidaṃ sūtritam tathānyapratiṣedhāt iti ||
sarvānanaśirogrīvaḥ sarvabhūtaguhāśayaḥ |
sarvavyāpī sa bhagavān tasmāt sarvagataḥ śivaḥ
iti vākyasthaśivaśabdasya bhagavatparatvamupapādayati - (yadityādi)
yacchavetāśvatarāgamo bhagavati spaṣṭaprayogāntaraṃ
tasminnirdiśati ghruvaṃ śivapadaṃ prakrāntamuktiprade |
sarvavyāpini sarvatomukhaśirogrīve sa eva prabhuḥ
sattvasyaiṣa mahān pravartaka iti vyaktaḥ punaḥ pūruṣaḥ || 77 ||
śvetāśvatarāgamaḥ śvetāśvatarasthasarvānanetyādivākyaṃ kartṛ | yat śivapadaṃ nirdiśati tasmāt (sarvagataḥ) śiva ityatratyaṃ yacchivapadaṃ prayuṅkte | yattadorniyasaṃbandhāt tadityadhyāhāraḥ | tat, bhagavati nārāyaṇe | spaṣṭaprayodāntaraṃ śāśvataṃ śivam iti nissaṃśayaṃ prayuktam | anyaḥ prayogaḥ prayogāntaraṃ mayūravyaṃ sakāditvāt samāsaḥ | spaṣṭaṃ prayodāntaraṃ yasyeti vigrahe bahuvrīhiḥ | nārāyaṇānuvākasya bhagavatparatāyā bahupramāṇasiddhatvāt tatratyaṃ śivapadaṃ magavatparamiti spaśṭamiti bhāvaḥ | tathāvidhaṃ śivapadaṃ karma | śvetāśvatarāgamaḥ kartā | prakānta śvāsau muktipradaśce ti vigrahe nimnonnatādivat samāsaḥ | vedāhametam ityādinā prastute mokṣapradāyini ca | sarvavyāpini vyāpya nārāyaṇaḥ sthitaḥ iti bhagavaddharmatayā nārāyaṇānuvākupratipāditaṃ yat sarvavyāpitvaṃ tadvattayā sarvavyāpī ceti pratipādite | sarvatomukhaśirogrīve puruṣasūktoktasarvānanaśirogrīvatvādimattayā prakṛtavākyokte | tasminneva bhagavatyeva | nirdiśati | uktapratyabhijñāpakaiḥ bhagavatpara evāyaṃ śivaśabda ityarthaḥ | dhruvamiti niścaye | sa bhagavān mahān prabhurvai puruṣaḥ sattvasyaiṣa pravartakaḥ
ityuparitanavākyena punarapi vyaktaḥ bhūyo’pi pratipāditaḥ | tatra hi puruṣaśabdasya śravaṇāt, sattvapravartakatvarūpabhagavalliṅgācca, bhagavatparatvaṃ spaṣṭamityatratyaṃ śivapadamapi bhagavatparamiti bhāvaḥ | yo ha khalu vā asya sattvikoṃ’śaḥ so’sau brahmacāriṇo yo’yaṃ viṣṇuḥ iti maitrāyaṇīyaśrutyā sattvapravartakatvaṃ bhagavalliṅgamityavagatam | bhagavadasādhāraṇa iti bhagavāniti śabdoyam ityādināvagatam | ataḥ pūrvottarasvarasyācchivaśabdo bhagavatpara iti bhāvaḥ | yato bhagavānataḥ sarvagatatve’pi śivo maṅgala ityarthaḥ | anyathā sarvavyāpisarvagataśabdayoḥ punaruktyāpattiḥ ||
ākāśādiśabdavadrūḍhyarthasyāyogyatvādapi śivaśabdo bhagavatparaḥ | tadidamāhurbhaṭṭasudarśanapādāḥ - sarvānanaśirogrīvatvaṃ ca puṃsūktārthasmārakam ityādinā | vistareṇa vedārthasaṅgrahe ca bhagavatā (bhāṣyakāreṇā)bhihitam - puruṣasyaiva śabaddhiguṇayogena śivaśabdābhidheyatvam śāśvataṃ śivam ityādinā jñātameva | puruṣa eva śivaśabdābhidheya ityanantarameva vadati | mahān prabhurvai puruṣaḥ sattvasyaiṣa pravartakaḥ ityatraitatpadena śivaśabdo’bhihitaḥ pratyabhijñāpitaḥ iti tattātparyadīpikā | sarvānanaśirogrīvaḥ ityatra
sarveṣāṃ bhagavadānanatvādibādhāt sarvatrānanāni śirāṃsi grīvāśceti vigrahe vyadhikaraṇabahuvrīhirityabhiprāyeṇa sarvatomukhaśirogrīva ityuktam | sarvavyāpī ca bhagavānityuktabhagavatyeva tu śivaśabdanirdeśo’yamityabhiprāyeṇa tasminniridiśatīti bhagavacchabdanirdiṣṭe nirdiśatītyuktam || 77 ||
yadā tamastanna divā na rātrirna sanna cāsacchiva eva kevalaḥ |
tadakṣaraṃ tatsaviturvareṇyaṃ prajñā ca tasmāt prasṛtā purāṇī ||
ityuparitanavākyasthaśivaśabdasya bhagavatparatvamupapādayati - (yadityādi)
yacchvetāśvataraśrutiḥ śivapadaṃ vakti prayuktaṃ harā
vagre jāgrati viśvakāraṇaparaṃ tatraiva yuktaṃ hi tat
sādhāraṇyaviduraviṣṇubhagavannārāyaṇādyāhvayairāhuḥ kāraṇamenamaupaniṣadā bhāgāstadākāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 18 ||
śvetāśvataraśrutiryacchivapadaṃ, agre upariṣṭādvakti prayuṅkte, na sanna cāsacchieva kevalaḥ ityupariṣṭādyacchivapadaṃ nirdiśati | harau prayuktam śāśvataṃ śivam sarvaḥ śarvaḥ śivaḥ, ityādiṣu sarvasaṃhārakartari bhagavati prayuktam | viśvakāraṇaparam - jagatkāraṇaviṣayam | tat śivapadam |
agre ityatrāpi saṃbadhyate | agre - sṛṣṭeḥ prākkāle | jāgrati ānīdavātaṃ svadhayā tadekam ityuktarītyā svainaiva dhāraṇasāmarthyena brahmeśānādivadapralīnatayā tiṣṭhati | tatraiva hariśabdanirdiṣṭe bhagavatyeva | yuktaṃ bodhakatvātmakasaṃbandharūpayogārhaṃ, yadā tama ityādyukta jagacchūnyatve’pi maṅgalarūpatvaṃ vyañjayituṃ tatraiva prayogārhaṃ, kāraṇatvaśaṅkitadoṣavyāvartanāya vā, tatraiva prayogārhamityarthaḥ | asya śivaśabdasya, kāraṇaviṣayatvādbhagavata eva mahopaniṣadādau kāraṇatvavidhānāt, śivaśabdasya cānyatra bhagavati dṛṣṭaprayogatvāt, prasiddhaśivasya ca saṃhāryatvaśravaṇādbhagavataśca tadabhāvāt, māṅgalyarūpatādivyañjanasya cātrāvaśyakatvāddevatāntararūḍhyā vināpi
śivaśabdaprayogabāhulyādayaṃ śivaśabdo bhagavatpara iti bhāvaḥ | nanu śivaśabdaśaktidvayānugrahāya rudrapara evāyaṃ śivaśabda ityatrāha - sādhāraṇyetyādi | hi yasmāt | aupaniṣadā bhāgāḥ mahopaniṣadādyupaniṣadgatapradeśaviśeṣāḥ | tadākāṅkṣiṇaḥ santaḥ - kāraṇatvaśaṅkitadoṣavyāvartanāya,, sarvaśūnyatve’pi maṅgalatvabodhanāya vā sādhāraṇaṃ tacchivapadamākāṅkṣamāṇāḥ santaḥ | sādhāraṇyavidūraiḥ yogena yodarūḍhyā vā vyaktyantaravācitvarahitaiḥ viṣṇustadāsīt sarvavyāpī sa bhagavān eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt ityādivākyagatai rviṣṇubhagavannārāyaṇādyairāhvayairnāmabhiḥ, puruṣādināmnāmādyaśabdena parigrahaḥ | enaṃ śrutisthaśivaśabdaviṣayatayā pūrvārdhe mayopāttamimaṃ bhagavantaṃ kāraṇamāhuḥ - jagatkāraṇaṃ vidadhati | tasmāttacchivapadaṃ tatraiva yuktamityarthaḥ | na tu nārāyaṇādīnāṃ nāmnām anyatra saṃbhavaḥ | anyanāmnāṃ gatirviṣṇureka eva prakīrtitaḥ iti vacanena,
bhagavāniti śabdo’yam ityādinā, nārāyaṇatvena ca sādhāraṇyavidūratvaṃ viṣṇavādināmnāmavagatam | atra ca śivaśabde rūḍhidvayānugrahe nārāyaṇaśabde lakṣaṇā syāt | śivaśabde viśeṣarūḍhiṃ parityajya sāmānyarūḍhimātrāṅgīkāre tu na kutrāpi lakṣaṇeti viśeṣarūḍhirnānugrāhyā | api ca bahirājyanayānna viśeṣarūḍhiḥ śivaśabdasya | na caivaṃ sati puruṣaśabde’pi viśeṣarūḍirna syāditi vācyam | tatsaṃjñakatvapramāṇabalena puruṣaśabde viśeṣarūḍhisvīkārāt | nārāyaṇaśabdasya lakṣaṇāprasaṅgādeva īśādiśabdeṣu yaudikeṣvapi vṛttidvayāgraho nirastaḥ | nārāyaṇaśabdasya bhagavato’nyatra yogāṅgīkāre tu ṇatvābhāvaḥ suprasiddhaḥ | yathākathaṃcit yena kenacidvyutpattiviśeṣeṇa aṇ kyaṅluṭsukṛteṣu ? ityādinā tadupapādane tu manusmṛtyādiprasiddhayogatyāga iti bhāvaḥ | prakārāntareṇa navyotprekṣitaṇatvopapattinirāsaścāsmatkṛtaṇatvatattvaprapañce’nusandheyaḥ | mahācāryairasya vākyasyārtho’nugṛhītaḥ - yadā tamaḥ tamaḥ pare deva ekībhavati iti avibhaktāvasthaṃ tamaḥ na divādi ca, samastakāryavidhuro bhagavāneva - tattadā - akṣaramiti bhagavataḥ svarūpato vikārābhāvo vidhīyate atra ca śivaśabdena bhagavannirdeśaḥ sarvaśūnyatve’pi maṅgalarūpatvaṃ vyañjayitum | astu vā śivaśabdasyaiva vidhāyakatvam | athāpi śivaśabdena bhagavata eva kāraṇatayā vidhiḥ syāt | nārāyaṇakāraṇatvavidhyanusārāt | na ca vaiparītyaṃ śaṅkyam | śivādiśabdānāṃ bhagavadabhidhānatvādrūḍhyantaranimajjanasāmānyataravadrūḍhimattvādbhagavati dṛṣṭaprayogatvācceti || 78||
yacchvetāśvatara ityādiślokadvayoktaṃ vivṛṇoti - ( yadagra ityādi)
yadagre jāgarti śrutiśivapadaṃ brahma paramaṃ
jagaddhetuḥ śvetāśvatarānegamānte sa bhagavān |
prayukto yattasmin yajuṣi śivaśabdaḥ śrutiśirasyatatsādhāraṇyaṃ na samagaṇi nārāyaṇagiraḥ || 79 ||
śvetāśvataranigamānte - śvetāśvataropaniṣadi | agre śrutaśivapadam tasmāt sarvagataḥ śivaḥ śiva eva kevalaḥ ityuparitanakhaṇḍayoḥ śivaśabdābhihitam | jagaddhetuḥ - jagatkāraṇabhūtam | yatparamaṃ brahma jāgarti kiṃ kāraṇaṃ brahma udgītametat paramaṃ nu brahma iti vākyayorupakramagatayoḥ pratīyate | saḥ - śvetāśvatare śivaśabdābhihitaṃ brahma | bhagavān sarvavyāpī sa bhagavān puruṣa iti cintyam tamāhuragryaṃ puruṣam ityupakramopasaṃhāramadhyeṣu puruṣabhagavadādiśabdanirdiṣṭo nārāyaṇa eva | vidheyaprādhānyāt sa iti pulliṅgatā | kuta ityatrāha - prayukta ityādi | yat - yasmāt | yajuṣi śrutiśirasi taittirīyake śivaśabdaḥ, tasmin bhagavati | śāśvataṃ śivamacyutam iti prayuktaḥ tataḥ śvetāśvatarasthaśivaśabdābhihito’pi bhagavānevetyarthaḥ | nanu taittirīyakasya kathaṃ bhagavatparatvamiti śaṅkāyāṃ tiṣṭhantu samudraśāyitvalakṣmīpatitvādiliṅgāni, nārāyaṇaśabdaśrutireva taittirīyakasya bhagavatparatvasādhane paryāptetyāha - atatsādhāraṇyamityādinā | nārāyaṇagiraḥ nārāyaṇaśabdasya | atatsādhāraṇyaṃ (mateṣu) bhagavadvyatirikteṣu brahmarudrādiṣu sādhāraṇyaṃ bhagavadvṛttitve sati vṛttimattvarūpaṃ sāmānyaśabdatvam | na samagaṇi - nābodhi | atra śrutismṛtivyākaraṇairityādiḥ | mahopaniṣadādyaikārthyānnārāyaṇaśabdasya bahulamabhyāsācca taittirīyaṃ bhagavatparamiti, tatratyaśivaśabdābhidheyo bhagavāneveti, tadaikārthyācchvetāśvatarasthaśabdābhidheyo’pi sa evetyarthaḥ | tadidamuktaṃ mahācāryacaraṇaiḥ - kāraṇaviṣayanārāyaṇaśabdasyeyaṃ niruktiḥ, prakaraṇāt | kāraṇagocaranārāyaṇaśabdasyaiva ca tvayā rudraparatvamiṣyate | atastainaiva yogena tatra pravṛtte
ṇatvānupapattiranivāryā | na ca tannirukteścaturmukhaparatvaṃ śaṅkārham | aṇḍotpattyanantarotpannasya mahadāditattvakāraṇatvāsaṃbhavāt | mānavavacanavyākhyānaṃ ca vedāntavijaye draṣṭavyam iti || 79 ||
itham prastutya ityādicatuḥślokyā śvetāśvataropaniṣado bhagavatparatvamupapāditam | atha yaddevairanuyuktaḥ ityādyekādaśaślokyā atharvaśiraso bhagavatparatvamupapādayati - ( yaddhevairityādi)
mū yaddevairanuyukta uttaramuśan rudro vivṛtyātmani
vyājahne mahatīmadhīśvaradhurāmātharvaṇe mūrdhani |
sa prādurbhavadantarātmabhagavadbhūmāphaṇatso’ntarādantaḥ prāviśadityadhītavidhayā tadvāmadevādivat || 80 ||
atraitatprabhṛti ṣaṭślokyupayukto’tharvaśirovākyasandarbhaḥ devā ha vai svargaṃ lokamagaman | te devā rudramapṛcchan | ko bhavāniti | so’bravīdahamekaḥ prathamamāsaṃ vartāmi ca bhaviṣyāmi ca | nānyaḥ kiścinmatto vyatirikta iti | so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat | diśaścāntaraṃ pravāviśat | so’haṃ nityo’nityo brahmāhaṃ prāñcaḥ pratyañco’haṃ dakṣiṇāṃ codañco’hamadhaśca ūrdhvaṃ cāhaṃ diśaśca vidiśaścāhaṃ pumānahamapumāṃstryakṣaścāhaṃ sāvitryahaṃ gāyatryahaṃ triṣṭupjagatyanuṣṭabhaścāhamityādidharmeṇa tarpayāmi svena tejasā ityantaḥ ||
asyāyamarthaḥ - devāḥ svargalokaṃ gatvā, tapasviveṣadharaṃ yuktāvasthaṃ rudraṃ dṛṣṭvā, muniveṣadarśanena nirdhāraṇāśaktyā rudraṃ jñātvāpi tasya svātantryapāratantryajijñāsayā vā kastvamiti pṛṣṭavantaḥ | so’bravīt - devapraśnasyottaraṃ rudro’bravīt | kimasmadādivaddevatāmātramuta paramakāraṇabhūta iti praśnāśayaḥ | so’bravīdityādi - ahameka āsaṃ sṛṣṭikālasthānāmarūpātmakabahutvapratidvandvyekatvarūpakāraṇāvastho’bhavam | prathamamāsaṃ vartāmi ca bhaviṣyāmi ca kālatrayavartināṃ sarveṣāmātmāham | nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatiriktaḥ - madanyaḥ ko’pi na | atra rudreṇa nirdiṣṭo’smacchabdo rudrāntaryāmibhagavatparaḥ | atastadgatameva kāraṇatvādikaṃ rudreṇa nirdiṣṭam | itiśabdasya hetvarthakatvaṃ prakārārthakatvaṃ sāmāptyarthakatvaṃ ca | tatra hetvarthakatve yojanādvayam | samāptyarthakatve prakārārthakatve ca pratyekameka evānvayaḥ | nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatirikta iti madvyatiriktābhāvādahamekaḥ prathamamāsamityeko’nvayaḥ | atra sa buddhisthaḥ paramātmā ānandamayaḥ, antarātprāṇamayāntarānmanomayādantaraṃ vijñānamayaṃ jīvaṃ prāviśat | diśaścāntaraṃ prāviśat digupalakṣitabāhyācetanādantaraṃ jīvaṃ ca prāviśat | svasmin paramātmapraveśasya dṛṣṭāntābhiprāyeṇoktaṃ diśaścāntaraṃ prāviśaditi so’ntarādantaramityāderarthaḥ | madvyatiriktābhāvādahameva kāraṇatvasarvātmabhāvavānityukte, "puruṣa evaṃdaṃ sarvam" iti puruṣasyaiva khalu sārvātmyam; kathamasyetyapekṣāyāṃ prahlādanyāyasphoraṇāya so’ntarādityādivākyam | yadvā so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśaditi paramātmā jīvātmāntaryāmitayā jīvaṃ prāviśaditi hetoḥ, maccharīrakaparamātmano’nyannāstītyarthaḥ | "sarvagatvādanantasya sa evāhamavasthitaḥ"iti dyuktaṃ prahlādena | itthamitiśabdasya hetvarthakatve yojanādvayaṃ darśitam | nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatirikta ityukte sārvātmyavyapadeśaḥ kīdṛśa ityapekṣāyāmāha - itīti | itthamityarthaḥ | vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇetyarthaḥ | vakṣyamāṇaḥ ka ityatrāha - so’ntarādityādi sarvaśarīrakatvaprayuktaṃ sārvātmyamityarthaḥ | itthamitiśabdasya prakārārthakatvaṃ darśitam | astu vāyamitiśabdaḥ samāptyarthakaḥ | so’hamityādyanantaraṃ so’bravīdityadhyāhariṣyate | nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatirikta iti so’bravīdityanvanayaḥ so’ntarādityādi - sa rudraḥ - antarāt manomayāntarādvijñānamayāt antaraṃ ānandamayaṃ paramātmānaṃ prāviśat buddhyā prāviśat | ato rudro vāmādevaprahlādādivadahameva sārvātmyādimānityupadideśetyarthaḥ | atra yojanātraye itiśabdasya svasthāna eva sthitiḥ | so’ntarādantaḥ prāviśaditi nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatirikta iti yojanāyāṃ tvasya sthānāccalanamiti bhedaḥ | samāptyarthakatvenoktayojanāyāṃ ca uttaratra so’bravīdityadhyāhāraḥ | kṛtsnarudravākyasamāptāvitikaraṇaṃ yojyamiti pakṣe tu nādhyāhāraḥ | kiṃ tu sthānāccalanameveti, prāviśaditi svenaiva uktam | paramātmānaṃ sa iti parāmṛśya sa paramātmaivāhaṃ ca nityaścānityaścetyevaṃ sārvātmyaṃ prapañcayati rudraḥ so’hamityādinetyayaṃ siddhāntaḥ |
pūrvapakṣe tu so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśadityetadutpāṭya kṛtsnarudravacanasamāptyanantaraṃ prakṣipya rudro devaḥ dṛśyaṃ sthūlarūpaṃ vihāya sūkṣmarūpaṃ prāviśadityasyārthamāhuḥ | sa tu na samīcīno’rthaḥ
| utpāṭanaprakṣepayoranucitatvādantarādantaramiti kośapratyabhijñāyāḥ, prāviśaditi nāmarūpavyākaraṇaprasiddhānupraveśapratyabhijñāyāśca bādhānaucityācca | tadatretiśabdasya hetvādyarthakatvamuparitanapañcaślokyā vakṣyan, praśnaprativacanarūpaśrutivākyasandarbhasyārthamāha
yaddaivairityādinā |
ātharvaṇe mūrdhani - atharvaśirasi | rudraḥ devairanuyuktaḥ san - ko bhavāniti svātantatryajijñāsayā pṛṣṭaḥ kāntau | kāntiricchā | mahatīm - asaṅkucitām | adhīśvaradhurām - adhīśvaratvabhāram | "ṛkpūrabdhuḥ" ityādinā dhūrśabdāntasyākāraḥ pratyayaḥ | vivṛtya - so’hamityādinā prapañcya | ātmani - svasmin | vyājahne - "ahamekaḥ prathamamāsam" ityādinā "svena tejasā" ityantena sandarbheṇoktavāniti yat | tat karma | saḥ rudraḥ | so’ntarādantaḥ prāviśadityadhītavidhayā - paramātmakartṛkajīvātmakarmakānupraveśarūpaḥ svakartṛkaparamātmakarmakabuddhyavagāhanarūpānupraveśātmakaśca yaḥ "so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat"iti śrutyuktaḥ prakārastena prakāreṇa | prādurbhavadantarātmabhagavadbhūmā san - antarātmā vāsau bhagavāṃśca antarātmabhagavān, "eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā sarvavyāpī ca bhagavān"iti śrutiṣvantarātmatayā prasiddho bhagavān, tasya bhūmā - bahutvam, sarvātmabhāvaḥ, prādurbhāvan - svavacanena devanāṃ buddhau svabuddhau ca prakāśamāno’ntarātmabhagavadbhūmā yasya tathāvidhaḥ san | vāmadevādivat | ādinā prahlādaparigrahaḥ |
aphaṇat - uktavāniti yojanā | yadidam "ahamekaḥ prathamamāsam"ityādikamatharvaśirasi rudravacanaṃ, tattasya vacanam, ""ahaṃ manurabhavam"sarvagatvādanantasya sa evāhamavasthitaḥ"ityādikaṃ vāmadevaprahlādādīnāṃ vacanamiva sarvatra bhagavata ātmatayā anupraveśāt svasya bhagavadātmakatvapratisandhānamūlakamiti bhagavatsārvātmyaparam | na tu rudrasyaiva sārvātmyādiparamityarthaḥ | "so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat" iti ca śrutyarthanirdeśo na śrutinirdeśa iti nāntaśśabdānupapattiḥ || 80 ||
ghddhaḍḍhadhtdṛdvadma "nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatirikta" iti itiśabdasya hetvarthakatvaṃ "so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat" ityasya upari ca svasthāna eva cānvayaṃ ca ślokadvayena vakṣyaṃstasya hetvarthakatvavirodhinīṃ samāptyarthakatāṃ nirākaroti(nānya ityādi)
nānyaḥ kaścinmatta ityugravākyādūrdhvaṃ netistatsamāptiṃ bravīti |
prāgvat paścādugravākyānuvṛtteḥ
so’haṃ nityo’nitya ityādibhaṅgyā || 81 ||
ātharvaṇe mūrdhanīti vartate | atharvaśirasi, prāgvatiti padāt pūrvatra "ahamekaḥ pratamamāsam" ityādirudravākyasyeva | paścātiti śabdāduttaratra | " so’haṃ nityo’nitya ityādibhaṅgyā " - rītyā | ugrasya rudrasya vākyānāmanuvṛtteḥavicchedāddhetoḥ | nānyaḥ kaścicanmatta ityugravākyādūrdhvamitiḥ nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatirikta itīti śrūyamāṇa itiśabdaḥ, kartā tatsamāptiṃ rudravākyasamāptim | na bravītina vakti | "itiḥ hetuprakaraṇaprakārādisamāptiṣu" iti kośena itiśabdasya samāptyarthakatvānuśāsane’pi atra samāptyarthakatvabādhāduttaratrādhyāhāraprasaṅgācca; nāyamitiśabdaḥ samāptyarthakaḥ | "so’bravīt" ityasyottaratrādhyāhārāpekṣayāsyetaśabdasya sthānāccalanamātrasya nyāyyatvācceti bhāvaḥ| taduktaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ"yadyapi itiśabdasya samāptirevārtho’rthāntaraṃ na syāt; tathāpi "dharmikalpanāto varaṃ dharmakalpanā" iti nyāyāt prayuktasyetiśabdasya rudravākyāvasāne’nvayakalpanaṃ nyāyyam; na tvaprayuktapadāntarādhyāhāraḥ"iti || 81 ||
evaṃ samāptyarthakatvaṃ nirasya hetvarthakattvam "so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat"ityasyānantaramanvayaṃ cāha(so’ta ityādi)
so’to hetuṃ vakti netiḥ prakāraṃ
prahlādokto heturantaḥ praveśaḥ |
prahlādoktiḥ sarvagatvādananta
syeti vyaktā veṣṇavākhye purāṇe || 82 ||
ataḥsamāptyarthakatvānupapatteḥ | sa itiḥpūrvokta itiśabdaḥ | hetuṃ
vaktihetvarthakaḥ | prakāraṃ na vaktināpi prakārārthakaḥ | vākyabhedāpatteriti śeṣaḥ| so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśaditi nānyaḥ kaścidityādyanvayaḥ | bhagavataḥ sarvajīveṣvātmatayānupraveśānmaccharīrakaḥ paramātmaiva sārvātmyādimān na tadvyatiriktaḥ kaścidityasyārthaḥ | svaśarīrakaparamātmano’smacchabdena nirdeśe paramātmanaḥ sarvajīveṣvātmatayānupraveśo hetutayānyatrāpyukta ityāhaprahlādokta iti| antaḥ praveśaḥbhagavataḥ sarvajīveṣvātmatayāntaḥ praveśaḥ | hetuḥ prahlādoktaḥasmacchabdena svaśarīrakaparamātmirdeśahetutayā prahlādenāpyukta ityarthaḥ | kā sā prahlādoktirityātrāha - prahlādoktiriti | veṣṇavākhye purāṇe sarvagatvādanantasyeti prahlādoktiḥ" sarvagatvādanantasya sa evāhamavasthitaḥ" ityādikā prahlādoktiḥ | vyaktāsphuṭā | atastadvadatrāpi hetūktiruciteti stānāccalane’pi hetvarthakatvameva yuktam | na vākyabhedādhyāhārāpādakaprakārasamāptyarthakatvamiti bhāvaḥ | viṣṇupurāṇa iti vaktavye veṣṇavākhya iti nirdeśo -
"vedeṣu pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ dharmaśāstreṣu mānavam |
bhārate bhagavadgīti purāṇe vaiṣṇavam param ||"
iti vacanasmaraṇenāsya sakalapurāṇāpekṣayābhyarhitatvavyañjanārthaḥ || 82 ||
hetvarthakatva eva svasthānānvayapakṣamāha(atha vetyādi)
atha veti padena heturatra
vyatireko’kathi nānya ityadhītaḥ |
ahameka itīritānvayasya
pratipattuṃ sukaraḥ prakārapakṣaḥ || 83 ||
atha vā( "so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat" iti "nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatiriktaḥ" iti ) (itiśabdasya)
sthānāccalanenānvayābhāve | atretipadenasvasthānasthitenetiśabdena | nānya ityadhīto vyatirekaḥ" nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatiriktaḥ" iti vākyabodhito rudraśarīrakaparamātmavyatiriktavastunaḥ svaniṣṭhatvenābhāvaḥ | ahameka itīritānvayasya " ahamekaḥ prathamamāsam" ityādinā bodhitasya rudraśarīrakaparamātmani sārvātmyādisadbhāvarūpānvayasya | heturakathi hetutvena bodhitaḥ | svaniṣṭhavastvantarasadbhāve
bhagavataḥ sārvātmyaṃ na na sidhyatīti svaniṣṭhavastvantarābhāvaḥ sārvātmyahetutayā śrutisthetiśabdenokta ityarthaḥ| pūrvayojanāyāmitiśabdasya sthānāccalane’pi śrutyantaraprasiddhānupraveśahetutvabodhanāt svārasyam | asyāṃ tu tadabhāve’pi svasthāna evānvayāt svārasyamiti vivekaḥ | itiśabdasya prakārārthakatvamapi saṃbhavatītyāhapratipa ttuṃ sukaraḥ prakārapakṣa iti | itiśabdasya hetvarthakatve sthānāccalanapūrvaśeṣatvayorāpatteḥ | "iṣṭaṃ hi viduṣāṃ loke samāsavyāsadhāraṇam"iti saṃgrahavistaragarbhatvena vākyabhedasyāpyadoṣatvāt prakārapakṣo’pi yukta eveti bhāvaḥ || 83 ||
itiśabdasya prakārārthakatve svatantravākyatve ca sati "so’ntarādantaram" iti vākyasya tadviraṇatvamityāha(rudrasyetyādi)
rudrasyoktiḥ so’ntarādityato’sau
tasmādanyaḥ ko’pi tasyāntarātmā |
anyeṣāṃ cetyucyate so’ntarādityuktyā raudryā so’pi viṣṇuḥ prasiddhaḥ || 84 ||
ataḥprakārārthakāt svatantravākyabhūtādetasmāditiśabdāt , parā |
so’ntarādityasau uktiḥ rudrasya, na tu devasya, itiśabdasya samāptyarthakatve tu so’ntarādityādervedavākyatvaṃ vakṣyate | | asya ca rudravākyatvaṃ itiśabdasya hetuprakārārthakatvayoraviśiṣṭamiti vyañjanāya hetuprakārārthakatvopapādanāvasāne rudravākyatvoktiriti dhyayam | arthamāhatasmādityādinā | raudryā rudravaktṛ katayā śrutisthayā | so’ntarādityuktyā"so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat"itivākyena | na tasmādanyaḥrudrādanyaḥ | tasya rudrasya | anyeṣāṃ ca jīvātmanām | antarātmā ko’pi vyaktiviśeṣarūpeṇānirdiṣṭatvanibandhanaḥ ko’pīti nirdeśaḥ | ityucyateiti pratipādyate | "so’ntarādantaram" ityenena rudravyatirikto rudrasyānyeṣāṃ jīvānāṃ cāntarātmā pratipādyata ityarthaḥ | antarātmatvamātreṇa pratipādane’pi bhagavata evātra pratipādyatvaṃ sidhyati | antarātmatvasya tatraiva prasiddherityāhaso’pi viṣṇuḥ prasiddha iti | saḥ antarātmā | viṣṇuḥ prasiddhaḥ "sata antaryānī" "eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātm""tavāntarātmā mama ca" ityādiṣu
viṣṇutvena prasiddhaḥ | apiśabdenātharvaśirasyapi "yo vai rudraḥ sa bhagavān yaśca brahmā" ityādinā bhagavacchabdanirdeśena bhagavataḥ sarvāntarātmatvakīrtanaṃ samuccīyate | so’pi viṣṇuḥ prasiddha iti viṣṇuśabdopādānaṃ "viṣṇave śipiviṣṭāya"iti vākyasmāraṇārtham | śipiṣu paśuṣu antaryāmitayā viṣṭaḥ praviṣṭa iti hi tasyārthaḥ | yadvā "pratipattuṃ sukaraḥ prakārapakṣaḥ"ityatraivetiśabdasya saṃgrahatvaṃ so’ntarādityādervistaratvaṃ ca sukarapadenābhipretam | asmiṃstu śloke hetuprakārārthakatvāditiśabdasya so’ntarādityādikaṃ rudravākyameveti vadaṃstasyārthamapyāharudrasyetyādinā | ataḥ - itiśabdasya hetuprakārārthakatvāt | śeṣaṃ pūrvavat | itiśabdasya yojanāntarābhidhāne yojanāntaradūṣaṇaṃ tu yojanānāṃ svārasyaviśeṣasāmyavyañcanārthamiti na doṣaḥ || 84 ||
itiśabdasya rudravākyasamāptyarthakatvapakṣamāha(itītyādi)
ityuktirvā samāptiṃ vadatu haragiraḥ so’ntarādityato’sau
mā bhūdrudroktiritthaṃ sati na vighaṭanā kiñcidasmin matasya |
rudraḥ svātmātmabhūtaṃ harimamaladhiyā saṃpraviṣṭaścikīrṣurmedhāvīva svavaśyaṃ naramaparamiti vyajyate vedavācā || 85 ||
vāśabdaḥ pakṣaparivṛttidyotanārthaḥ | ityuktiḥ itiśabdaḥ, "nānyaḥ kaścinmatto vyatiriktaḥ" ityayamitiśabdaḥ | haragiraḥ"ahamekaḥ prathamamāsam" ityādirudravākyasya | samāptimavasānam | vadatubodhayatu | atisarge loṭ | atisargaḥ
kāmacārānujñā | ataḥitiśabdasya samāptyarthakatvāddhetoḥ | so’ntarādityasau rudroktirmā bhūtaso’ntarādityādivākyaṃ rudravākyam na syāt | asya rudravākyatvābhāvo’pyastu | śrutivākyatvaṃ cāstviti bhāvaḥ | itthaṃ satiitiśabdasya samāptiparatve, so’ntarādityasya śrutivākyayatve ca satyapi | asmanmatasyaśrutismṛtisūtravṛttisvārasyānurodhināmasmākamiṣṭasya "rudro na sarvāntarātmatayātharvaśiraḥpratipādyaḥ; kiṃ tu tadantaryāmī bhagavāneva sarvāntarātmatayā tatpratipādyaḥ"ityevaṃrūpasyārthasya | kiṃcidapi vighaṭanā na - ekadeśasyāpi virodho nāstītyarthaḥ ||
atretiśabdasya samāptyarthakatve so’ntarādityādivākye tacchabdena rudra eva parāmṛśyate
| tataśca tasyaivāntaryāmitvamucyeta ityekā vighaṭanā | antaramityasya bhagavatparatve prāviśadityasyānanvayādantaramiti na bhagavatparamityaparā vighaṭanā | saiṣā dvayyapi nāsti | "so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat"iti vākye tacchabdena rudraparāmarśe’pi na tasya sarvāntarātmatvaṃ vaktumanena śakyam | "eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā" ityādipramāṇagaṇena sarvāntarātmatvasya bhagavalliṅgatāvagamāt , antarādantaramiti cānandavallīpratyabhijñayānandamaya evāntarādantaramiti nirdiśyati iti, tasya "sa yaścāyaṃ puruṣe yaścāsāvāditye"
ityādityamaṇḍalāntarvartitāvagamādādityaṇḍalāntarvartinaśca puṇḍarīkākṣatvaśravaṇāt , antarādantaramiti bhagavānevocyate | "viṣṇurātmā bhagavataḥ""tavāntarātmā mama ca"iti viśiṣyaivaṃ rudrāntaryāmitvaṃ bhagavato’vagatam | ataḥ praviśateḥ samyagavabodhārthakatvāt sa rudro jīvāntaryāmiṇaṃ bhagavantaṃ samyaganusaṃhitavāniti "so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat"ityasyārtha iti na kaścidvirodha iti tadidamāharudra ityādinā |
aparaṃ naraṃ svavaśyaṃ cikīrṣurmedhāvī naro’paraṃ naramiva rudraḥ svātmātmabhūtaṃsvasyānyeṣāmātmanāṃ cātmabhūtam | harimbhagavantam | amaladhiyā samyagjñānena | saṃpraviṣṭaḥ avagāhateti vedavācā vyajyate | "so’ntarādantaraṃ prāviśat"iti śrutivākyenāyamartha ucyata ityarthaḥ ||
aparaṃ naramityuktermedhāvī nara iti labhyate | atra ca -
"yasya yasya hi yo bhāvastena tena hi tannaram |
anupraviśya medhāvī kṣipramātmavaśaṃ nayet ||"
iti cāṇakyavacanamabhipretam | tena tena hi tannaramanupraviśyeti tattadbhāvayuktatayā taṃ jñātvetyarthaḥ | evaṃ catadvacanasthamedhāvipuruṣakartṛkapuruṣāntarakarmakabuddhyavagāhanarūpapraveśasadṛśo yaḥ svetarasarvajīvāntaryāmibhagavatkarmakabuddhyavagāhanarūpapraveśastatkartā rudra iti lābhastadvacana iva śrutivākye’pi praviśatirbuddhyavagāhanārthaka ityuktaṃ bhavati | prakṛtarudrakartṛkabuddhyavagāhane cāpakyavacanasthamedhāvikartṛkāvagāhanasādṛśyaṃ ca praviśativācyatvarūpaṃ vivakṣitam| vede ca śabdārthasaṃbandhaviśeṣāvadhāraṇasya lokatantratvāccāṇakyavacanaṃ dṛṣṭāntatayopāttam | atra ca praviśaterbuddhyavagāhanārthakatvameva dṛṣṭāntopajīvyam | ata upamānasya medhāvino
lokasiddhatvābhāvānmedhāvīvetyuktirayuktā; "devadatta iva yajñadatto gacchati" ityatrevātrāpyarthasādṛśyameva labhyeteti śaṅkāyā nāvakāśaḥ | cāṇakyavacanopādānaṃ ca pradarśanārtham | tanmatapraveśo’smanmatapraveśe’pi
prayoge’pyanupraviśatirbuddhyavagāhanārthako dṛṣṭa ityapi dhyeyam | taduktaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ"anupraveśaśabdasya samyagbodhanārthakatvaṃ lokoktisiddham | tathā cāṇakyaprayogaśca -
"yasya yasya hi yo bhāvastena tena hi tannaram |
anupraviśya medhāvī kṣipramātmavaśaṃ nayet ||"
iti rudraḥ svāntaryāmiṇaṃ sarvapadārthāntaryāmiṇaṃ ca paramātmānaṃ taccharīrakaṃ buddhyānusaṃhitavānityarthaḥ" iti | atra ca sarvāntarātmatvaṃ bhagavata eva śrutyantareṣu prasiddham | atrāpi "yo vai rudraḥ sa bhagavān"iti vakṣyata ityabhiprāyeṇa harimityuktam | "eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmāpahatapāpmā sata ātmāntaryāmyamṛtaḥ"ityādiṣvantarātmatve’pi bhagavato na tadgatadoṣaiḥ saṃsparśa iti bodhanāya, "sarvabhūtāntarātmā sata ātmā" ityuktvā "apahatapāpmāmṛtaḥ" ityuktam | tatsūcanāya cātra svātmātmabhūtaṃ hari mityuktam | "haraṇādeva pāpānāṃ harirityucyate budhaiḥ"iti hariśabdanirukteḥ ||
nanvitiśabdasya uktyarthakadhātusamabhivyāhṛtasya svasamabhivyāhṛtavākyasvarūpatadarthaparatvameva dṛṣṭamiti nāsya
samāptyarthakatvaṃ yuktam | kiṃ cetiśabdasya samāptyarthakatāyām "ityupaniṣat"ityādāviva svabodhyasamāptimadvācakapadāt pūrvanirdeśo yukta iti cedatrāhuḥ | yadyapi pūrvaṃ bodhyavākyanirdeśaḥ, tadanantaramitiśabdaḥ, tadanantaramuktyarthakadhātunirdeśa ityetādṛśakramavatsu -
"sutanu jahihi maunaṃ paśya pādānataṃ māṃ
na khalu tava kadācit kopa evaṃvidho’bhūt |
iti nigadati nāthe"
ityādiṣūktyarthakadhātuśirasketi śabdasya, svasamabhivyāhṛtavākyajanyabodhasamānākārabodhaparatvaṃ, tasya tu bodhasya janakatāsaṃsargeṇoktyarthakadhātuvācyoktikriyāyāṃ prakāratayā bhānaṃ, itiśabdottaroktyarthakadhātośca
svasamabhivyāhṛtetiśabdasamabhivyāhṛtavākyasamānānupūrvikavākyabodhakatvaṃ ca
vyutpattisiddham; tathāpi tādṛśakramasyātrābhāvāt "paśya mṛgo dhāvati"ityādāviva mṛgadhāvanarūpavākyārthasya "so’bravīt" ityuktikriyāṃ prati "ahamekaḥprathamamāsam"ityādivākyārthasya karmatvabodhāditiśabdasya samāptyarthakatvaṃ yuktam | samāptyarthaketiśabdasya svavācyasamāptimadvācakapadottaranirdeśaścākṣarapāṭhādau dṛṣṭaḥ | ataḥ samāptyarthakatvamucitam | evaṃ sati so’ntarādityatra sa itiśabdaḥ svasamānavibhaktiprakṛtikena so|ñabravīdityatra(ityena)(sthena) sa ityanena ekārtho bhavati ! evamupakramasvārasyānurodhena "so’haṃ nityo’nityaḥ"ityādyuparitanavākye "so’bravīt" ityadhyāhāro na duṣyati | so’hamityādivākyasya ca "ahamekaḥ" ityādivākyavivaraṇarūpatvānna kriyāntarāpekṣeti samāptyarthakatvaṃ yuktameveti || 85||
evamatharvaśirasi prathamakhaṇḍastharudravākyānāṃ rudrāntaryāmibhagavatparatvamupapāditam | idānīṃ dvitīyakhaṇḍasthadevatāstutivākyānāṃ rudraśarīrakaparamātmaparatvamupapādyate(śrutvetyādi)
śrutvā tudravacastadīyamahimastotrāya labdhodyamāḥ
sarvaiśvaryamatharvamūÐdhna yadapi vyājahnire’syāmarāḥ |
te’ntaryāmisamādhihetava iha vyaktā hi rudrāktayastadyuktāstadanūktayastu diviṣadvākāstadekāśayāḥ || 86 ||
atrāyaṃ dvitīyakhaṇḍasthavākyasandarbhaḥ"yo vai rudraḥ sa bhagavān yaśca brahma tasmai vai namo namaḥ | yo vai rudraḥ sa bhagavān yaśca maheśvarastasmai ve namo namaḥ"ityādiḥ, "yo vai rudraḥ sa bhagavān yacca satyaṃ tasmai vai namo namaḥ
"ityantaḥ | atra ca - yo rudraḥ sa bhagavāneva, yo brahmā so’pi bhagavāneva, ata ubhayātmakāya bhagavate nama ityādirarthaḥ | yaśca viṣṇurityavatāraviśiṣṭatayā namaskāryatvamucyate | pratiparyāyaṃ rudraviśeṣaṇopādānādrudrāntaryāmiṇaḥ sārvātmyopāsanamiha vidhīyate ||
atra mahācāryacaraṇāḥ"bhagavacchabdasyāpi rudraparatve yo rudro yaścānyastābhyāṃ nama iti vācyaṃ, tasmā ityananvitameva | yo rudro brahmābhūdityarthāṅgīkāre dvitīyayatpadāsāmañjasyam | yo rudro’bhūdyo brahmābhūdityarthāṅgīkāre dharmyantaraṃ svarasato labhyeta | pakṣadvaye’pi sa bhagavāniti tacchabdasya svārasikayacchabdapratinirdeśakatvabhaṅgaḥ | yo vai rudraḥ sa bhagavān tasmai namaḥ (eva ca) (yo vai) brahmā (sa bhagavān) tasmai nama ityarthaḥ | brahmaśabdo rudraparyanta
ityaṅgīkāre’pi ayaṃ doṣaḥ samānaḥ | tasmai nama ityāvṛttiradhikā | yaśca brahmā so’pi bhagavāniti tadbhagavacchabdāvṛttistu na doṣaḥ | cakāreṇa dyotanāt | evaṃ ca yo vai yaśceti vākyadvayena ubhayatādātmyamuktvā tasmai nama iti tṛtīyavākyena tādṛśasya bhagavata eva namaskāraḥ kriyata iti | athāpi yo rudraḥ sa bhagavāniti bhagavacchabdayaugikatvameveṣyata iti cenna; bādhakaṃ vinā svārasikarūḍhiparityāgāyogāt | kiṃ ca yo rudraḥ sa sārvajñyādiguṇakaḥ, yaśca brahmā so’pi sārvajñyādiguṇaka ityuktāvanyatrāpi tathātve’yogyārthatā | rudrastutivirodhaśca | tasmādrūḍhyā bhagavatparatvameva yuktam"iti cānujagṛhaḥ ||
tadidamāhaśrutvetyādinā | atharvamūÐdhnaatharvaśirasi | amarāḥdevāḥ | rudravacaḥ śrutvā"ahamekaḥ"ithyādikaṃ rudravākyaṃ śrutvā | tadīyasya rudravākyapratipāditatvena rudravākyīyasya vastunaḥ, yo mahimā sārvātmyādiprayukta utkarṣaḥ, tasya stotrāya tatpratipādakaśabdasandarmaṃ racayitum | labdhodyamāḥ santaḥ prayatnavantaḥ santaḥ | sarveṣāmaiśvaryaṃ caturmukhādīn sarvān (pratyanīśvaratvamasya) (prati īśvaratvamasya) vyājahnireyo vai rudra iti pratiparyāyaṃ rudraśabdanirardeśena
rudragatatayā vyāhṛtavanta iti yat , te diviṣadvākāstadekāśayā yuktā iti yojanā | hi yasmāt | ihaatharvaśirasi prathamakhaṇḍe | rudroktayo’ntaryāmisamādhihetavo vyaktāḥ "ahamekaḥ prathamamāsam"ithyādayo rudragiro rudrasya svāntaryāmibhagavaddhyānahetukā devānāṃ svaśravaṇena
rudrāntaryāmibhagavaddhyānahetubhūtāśceti vyaktāḥ, sphuṭāḥ | tattasmāt | tadanūktayaste diviṣadvākāḥrudravākyārthībhūtanārāyaṇasarvāntaryāmitvānuvādinyaḥ "vai rudraḥ"ityādayo devoktayaḥ | tadekāśayā yuktāḥtasminnantaryāmiṇi eko mukhya āśayastātparyam eṣāṃ tathoktā yuktāḥ, ucitā ityarthaḥ | vacerbhāve ghañi kutvam ||
ākhyātāntapadena kriyāyāḥ kriyāntarākāṅkṣānutthāpakatācchedakadharmavattvarūpasādhyatvaprakāreṇa bodhanāt , vyājahnira ityatra vyāharaṇakriyāgataliṅgasaṅkhyayoranabhivyakteḥ tadviśeṣaṇaṃ yatpadamautsagrikanapuṃsakaliṅgaikavacanāntatayā nirdiṣṭam | tasyā eva tu kriyāyāḥ siddhabhāvastu yastasyāḥ sa ghañādinibandhana ityuktarītyā ghañantadhātunā liṅgasaṅkhyābhivyaktiyogyasiddhāvasthayā pratipādanāt "kṛdabhihito bhāvo dravyavat prakāśate
dravyavalliṅgasaṅkhyādīn gṛhṇāti"iti liṅgasaṅkhyādibodhakavibhaktiprayogasyāvaśyakatvena "ghañantaṃ puṃsi" iti puṃliṅgatvaṃ, vākyabahutvāt bahuvacanāntatvaṃ ca vākaśabdasya yuktamiti tadviśeṣaṇaṃ tatpadamapi padvadeva puṃliṅgabahuvacanayuktatayā nirdiṣṭamiti dhyeyam | api śabdaśca rudravākyānāṃ tadantaryāmiparatvaṃ samuccinoti | tuśabdaśca pratiparyāyaṃ rudraśabdābhyāsādrudraparatvamiti śaṅkāyāmabhyāsasya rudraśarīrakabhagavadupāsanatātparyakatvenopapternāntaryāmiparatvakṣatiriti parihāraṃ sūcayati | atra - śrutvā rudravacastadīyamahimasto trāyetyādinā "te devā rudraṃ nāpaśyan te vā rudraṃ stuvanti"iti prathamakhaṇḍāntavākyadvayārtha uktaḥ | rudraṃ nāpaśyan - yathāpūrvaṃ svātantryaviśiṣṭaṃ nāpaśyannityarthaḥ | rudraṃ stuvanti rudraśarīrakaṃ bhagavantaṃ stuvantītyarthaḥ | antaryāmisamādhihetava ityanena ca "te devā rudraṃ dhyāyanti"iti tatratyacaramavākyasyārtho’nūditaḥ | rudraṃ dhyāyanti - yathopadeśaṃ bhagavadātmakatayā rudraṃ
dhyāyantītyarthaḥ | tadidamuktaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ"dvitīyakhaṇḍo rudravākyānuvādena stutiparatvādantaryāmiviṣayaḥ"iti, rudravākyārthībhūtanārāyaṇasarvāntaryāmitvānuvādena stutiparatvāditi bhāvaḥ"iti ca | tadbhāvaprakāśikā "tadīyamahimastotrāya"iti | kriyārthetyādinā caturthī || 86 ||
evamatharvaśiraḥprathamadvitīyakhaṇḍayorbhagavatparatvamupapāditam | tṛtīyakhaṇḍastu paramātmavaibhavapratipādakatvāt dvitīyakhaṇaaḍasya tatparatvopapādanenaiva bhagavatparatayopapāditaprāyaḥ | idānīṃ turīyakhaṇḍasya bhagavatparatvapratibandhinīṃ rudraparatāṃ rudreśānādināmaviśeṣaśravaṇasaṃbhāvitāṃ nirācaṣṭe(oṅkāretyādi)
oṅkārapūrvakatayā pratipāditāni
nāmāntarāṇi nanu rudrapurassarāṇi |
ātharvaṇe śirasi tāni tu na prasiddharudreśitṛtvamupapādayituṃ kṣamante || 87 ||
atrāyaṃ tṛtīyakhaṇḍavākyasandarbhaḥ -
"hṛdisthā devatāḥ sarvā hṛdi prāṇāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
hṛdi tvamasi yo nityaṃ tisro mātrāḥ parastu saḥ ||
tasyottarataḥ | śiro dakṣiṇataḥ pādau yaduttarataḥ sa oṅkāraḥ sa praṇavo yaḥ praṇavaḥ sarvavyāpī
yaḥ sarvavyāpī so’nanto yo’nantastattāraṃ yattāraṃ tatsūkṣmaṃ yatsūkṣmaṃ tacchuklaṃ yacchuklaṃ tadvaidyutaṃ tatparaṃ brahmeti sa eko rudraḥ sa īśānaḥ sa bhagavān sa īśvaraḥ sa mahādevaḥ"iti | atra pūrvakhaṇaaḍapratipāditaparamātmavcakasya prakṛtavidyāṅgabhūtasya oṅkārasya nāmadheyāni nirdiṣṭāni | tadāhuḥ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādāḥ"tadvācakasyoṅkārasya nāmadheyāni caturthe iti |
nadāhaoṅkārapūrvakatayotyādinā | ātharvaṇe śirasiatharvaśirasi
| turīyakhaṇḍa iti śeṣaḥ | rudrapurassarāṇi rudrādināmāntarāṇi rudreśāneśvaramahādevātmakanāmaviśeṣāḥ | "yo vai rudraḥ sa bhagavān"iti pūrvapūrvanirdiṣṭanāmno’nyatvābhiprāyeṇāntaraśabdanirdeśaḥ | anyadevatatvāntaram anyadākarāntaramityādāvivāntaraśabdasya viśeṣaparatvānnāmaviśeṣā iti vyākhyātam | oṅkārapūrvakatayā pratipāditāni
nanu - " tisro mātrāḥ parastu saḥ" iti paramātmavācakatayoktoṅkāhaṃ sa sa iti tacchabdainirdiśya tannāmadheyatayā vihitānyevetyarthaḥ | tāni - oṅkāranāmadheyatayoktāni rudradīni nāmāni | prasiddhasya sṛjyatvādinā śrutyādiprasiddhasya, rudrasyeśitṛtvaṃ paratvamupapādayituṃ vaktum , na kṣamantenārhanti | tuśabdena pūrvoktaśaṅkāvyudasyate | oṅkāranāmadheyatayāvagatatvānna prasiddharudraparatvameṣāmityarthaḥ | rudrāntaryāmibhagavatparatvaṃ tu yujyata iti sūcanāya
prasiddhe tyuktam | taduktaṃ mahācāryacaraṇaiḥ"īśānādiśabdāḥ " yaḥ sarvān" ityādinā yogārthāmidhānāttanmukhena bhagavadabhidhāyakāḥ santastadabhidhāyakaṃ praṇavaṃ bodhayanti"iti || 87 ||
pañcamakhaṇḍasya bhagavatparatvamāha( samākhyā ityādi )
amākhyāḥ prakāntapraṇavaviṣayiṇyaḥ pratipadaṃ
niruktā rudrādyā yadapi hi mahādevaśirasaḥ |
śruterātharvaṇyāḥ śirasi tadidaṃ tāravibhavaṃ
vyanaktyoṅkāraḥ syāduparitanavidyāparikaraḥ || 88 ||
ātharvaṇyāḥ śruteḥ śirasiatharvaśirasi | pañcamakhaṇḍa iti śeṣaḥ | prakāntapraṇavaviṣayiṇyaḥ turīyakhaṇḍe paramātmavācakatayoktapraṇavabodhinyaḥ | ekadeśineti jñāpakāt karmadhārayādiniḥ | rudrādyāḥ rudraśabdaprabhṛtayaḥ mahadevaśiraso mahādevaśabdāntāḥ | samākhyāḥ - nāmadheyāni | pratipadaṃ pade pade |
niruktāḥ pratyekaṃ yogārthapradarśanena vyākhyātā iti yat | tat rudrādisamākhyānirvacanaṃ kartṛ | idaṃ tārasya praṇavasya, vibhavaṃ prāśastyam | vyanaktibodhayati | api śabdena rudrādināmadheyavattannirvacanamapi na rudraparatvasādhakamiti sūcyate | praṇavavācyaparamātmavartināṃ rudreśānādiśabdayogārthānāṃ tadāśrayaparamātmavācakapraṇave kīrtanaṃ praṇavaprāśastyabodhanārthamityarthaḥ | (nanvavihitavyāḥ syānaṅgasya praṇavasya kiṃ prāśastyabodhanenetyatrāha) (anupayuktapraṇavapraśaṃsanamayuktamityatrāha) oṅkāra ityādi | oṅkāraḥ praṇavaḥ | uparitanī vidyā - "tadetadupāsitavyam"iti ṣaṣṭhakhaṇḍe vidhāsyamānā rudraśarīrakabhagavadupāsanarūpā vidyā | tasyāḥ parikaraḥ aṅgam | syāt - bhavet ||
uparitanaśabdena sannidhyāmnānarūpapāṭhasādeśya lakṣaṇasthānākhyaviniyojakapramāṇavaśāt praṇavasya rudraśarīrakabhagavadupāsanarūpavidyāviśeṣāṅgatvamavagamyata iti tatstutyarthatvaṃ rudrādisamākhyānirvacanasya yuktamiti sūcyate | arhatvavācinā ca syādityanena tadabhidhāyakasya tadupāsananiṣpādanayogyatārūpavastusāmarthyalakṣaṇaṃ liṅgamucyate | ato liṅgasthānābhyāṃ praṇavasya prakṛtavidyāparikaratvasiddhestatpraśaṃsārthaṃ samākhyānirvacanamiti, nātra rudraparatvāvakāśa iti bhāvaḥ | "atha kasmāducyate oṅkāro yasmāduccāryamāṇa eva sarvaṃ śarīramūrdhvamunnāmayati tasmāducyate oṃkāraḥ"ityādinā oṅkārapraṇavasarvavyāpyanantatārasūkṣmaśuklavaidyutaparabrahmarūpanāmnāṃ nirvacaneṣu kṛteṣvapi rudrādināmanirvacanānāmeva pūrvapakṣāvakāśahetutvātteṣāṃ siddhante saughaṭyopapādanāvaśyakatvadyotanāya "rudrādyā mahādevaśirasaḥ" ityuktam | ata evoṅkārādināmanirvacanānāṃ dṛṣṭāntatvamapi sūcyate || 88 ||
ṣaṣṭhakhaṇḍaṃ sāṅgasapraṇavarudraśarīrakabhagavadupāsanavidhāyakatvena vyācaṣṭe ( panthā ityādi )
panthā bandhasya śānteḥ paśupatibhajanaṃ nanvihātharvamūÐdhna
proktaḥ prācyā taduktyā sphurati haririhopāsanīyastadātmā |
yadvanmadhvādividyāsvitarasuratanuḥsraṣṭṛsṛjyādibhāvaḥ
śrautastvatrāpi tulyaśrutamiha bhasidaṃ prastutopāsti śeṣam || 89 ||
atharvamūÐdhnaatharvaśirasi | paśupatibhajanaṃsthāpayitvā tu rudre rudra ekatvamāhū
rudraṃ śāścataṃ vai purāṇamityādyanusāreṇa siddharudropāsanarūpaḥ | bandhasya śānteḥ panthāḥ pāśavimokṣāyetyuktabandhanivavṛttyupāyaḥ | iha buddhisthaṣaṣṭhakhaṇḍe | proktaḥ "tadetadupāsitavyam"iti vākyena vihitaḥ | nanvi tyavadhāraṇe | nirviheti ca pāṭhaḥ vihintīti vihā vidhātakastadrahito nirvihā niṣpratyūha iti tatrārthaḥ | panthānaṃ bandhaśānteḥ paśupatibhajanaṃ nirvahetyapi nirvahetyapi pāṭhaḥ | nirvaheti ca saṃsthānaṃ kalayākṛtivacchiṣyaṃ prati nirdeśaḥ | rudropāsanamatra, yadyapi vihitamevetyarthaḥ |
tathāpīti śeṣaḥ | tathāpi proktaḥ upāsanīyaḥ tadātmā hariḥ sphuratītyanvayaḥ | iha atharvaśirasi uktarudropāsane, hariḥ bhagavān tadātmā rudrasyāntaryāmī san upāsanīyaḥ upāsyaḥ, viṣaya iti yāvat | prācyā taduktyā prathamakhaṇḍādisthayā "so’ntarādantaram"ityādyuktyā, "yo vai rudraḥsa bhagavān"ityādyuktyā, "eṣo ha devaḥ"ityambhasi vākyasarūpoktyā ca sphurati prakāśate | antaryāmitvena bhagavāneva rudropāsanaviṣaya iti pūrvavākyairavagatamityarthaḥ | idamupalakṣaṇam"yenedaṃ pañcavidhaṃ tamīśānaṃ puruṣaṃ devamīḍham"ityuparitanavākyasyāpi; atra hi śāsanaśabdo adhitiṣṭhatītyuktayogārthaparaḥ san puruṣaviśeṣaṇam; puruṣaśabdastu bhagavadasādhāraṇa ityapi bodhyam | atra ca "tadetadrudracaritam"iti ṣaṣṭhakhaṇḍasyādimaṃ vākyam | hṛdaye sapraṇavaṃ
sārvātmyaviśiṣṭabhagavadanusandhānaṃ rudreṇānuṣṭhitamiti tasyārthaḥ | evaṃ ca madhuvidyānyāyena rudrasya svaśarīrakabhagavadupāsane kartṛtvaṃ saṃbhavatītyāhayadvadityādinā | madhvādividyāsvitarasuratanuryadvat itarasuratanuriva iha tadātmā harirūpāsanīya itya nvayaḥ | madhyādityādipadena prāṇavidyāparigrahaḥ |
"asau vā ādityo devamadhu"ityādinā "māmupāssva"ityādinā ca vihitāsu madhuvidyāprāṇavidyādiṣu | itarasuratanurivaprakṛtavidyāviṣayarudravyatiriktavasvādityendrādidevatāśarīraka iva | vasvādibhirivāsyāmapi vidyāyāṃ rudreṇa rudraśarīrakaḥ paramātmaivopāsya ityarthaḥ | nanu kāraṇatvādiśrutibalādatra svānantryeṇopāsyatvam; nāntaryāmiprakāratayeti cet , indrādīnāmapi "indro māyābhiḥ pururūpa īyate" "māmupāssva""indraṃ nicikyuḥ parame vyoman"iti kāraṇatvādyavagamāt svātantryeṇopāsyatvaṃ syāt | nanu teṣāṃ kāryatvāvagamānna svātantryeṇopāsyatvamiti cet , asyāpi kāryatvāvagamānna svātantryeṇopāsyatvamiti tulyamityāhasraṣṭṛsṛjyādibhāva ityādinā; śrautaḥ śrutyaktaḥ | srṛṣṭasṛjyādibhāvaḥ kāraṇatvakāryatvādikam | ādiśabdena saṃhāryatvādiparigrahaḥ | atrāpi tutyaḥrudre’pi samānamityarthaḥ | tuśabdaśca
bhagavadutpatteravatāratvaśravaṇādvaiṣamyasūcakaḥ | atra ca panthā ityanena "ayaṃ panthā vitatam" iti vākyaṃ sūcyate | "vratametat pāśupatam" iti vākyasya pāśupataśabdasya paśupatirūpaparamātmopāsanāṅgatayā paśupatisaṃbandhamityarthābhiprāyeṇa paśupatibhajanamityuktam | "tasmādupasaṃhartre mahāgrāsāya namo namaḥ"iti vākyābhiprāyeṇa harirityuktam | "madhvādiśabdenāsyā api vidyāyāḥ saṃgraha iti sūcanāya yadvanmadhvādividyāsvityuktam | evamaṅgividhyaṃśo vyākhyātaḥ | aṅgavidhiṃ vyācaṣṭeśrutamityādinā | iha śrutaṃ bhasitaṃ vratametat "pāśupatam" agniriti bhasma, vāyuriti bhasma, jalamiti bhasma, sarvaṃ havā idaṃ bhasma, mana etāni cakṣūṃṣi
bhasma, agnirityādinā bhasma gṛhītvā vimṛjyāṅgāni saṃspṛśettasmādvratametat pāśupataṃ pāśavimokṣāya"iti atharvaśirasi śrutaṃ bhasmagrahaṇam | prastutopāstiśeṣamrudraśarīrakaparamātmopāsanarūpaprakṛtavidyāṅgaṃ bhavati | saṃbandhamanuvartata iti saṃbandhaśabdavat ghañantatve’pi śeṣaśabdasya napuṃsakatvam | atra ca prastutopāstīti prastutapadena prakaraṇākhyaviniyojakapramāṇaṃ sūcitam |
prakaraṇaṃ nāma - sannihitasya phalavato’navagatāṅgatātmapadārthaviṣayetikartavyatātvenāpekṣaṇam | rudraśarīrakabhagavadupāsanasya ca sannihitatvāt "śāntimatyantameti"iti phalavattvācca śrutyāditrayāviṣayatvāt kriyārūpatvācca bhasmagrahaṇasyetikartavyatātvenāpekṣaṇamastīti bhasmagrahaṇaṃ prakaraṇāt rudraśarīrakaparamātmopāsanāṅgamityarthaḥ | iha śrutaṃ bhasitamityanena pāśupataśabdasya pāśupapatantroktaparatā, lakṣaṇāpatteḥ | nāpi taddhitena tadabhidhānam "vṛddhāccha"iti chapratyayāpatteḥ, taddhitadvayakalpanāyāṃ gauravāpatteśca | ataḥ paśupatirūpaparamātmopāsakāṅgatayā paśupatisaṃbandhaṃ pāśupatamiti sudarśanabhaṭṭapādāḥ | tadetadrudracaritamityaṅgyupakramāt pāśupataṃ paśupatinānuṣṭhitamiti mahācāryaḥ | pakṣadvaye’pi "tasyedam" ityṇapratyayaḥ | prakaraṇācca viśeṣavagamaḥ | tyadādīnāṃ tattatprakārakabodhajanakatvādvā viśeṣāvagamaḥ | na ca "sāsya devatā" iti devatārthako’yamaṇpratyaya iti śaṅkyam | tyajyamānahaviruddeśyatvamantrastutyatvātmakadevatātvasya havirmantravṛttipratisaṃbandhitākatvena, bhasmāgrahaṇarūpakarmaviśeṣasya devatāpratisaṃbandhitvābhāvena devatārthakāṇpratyayāsaṃbhavat |
"śāntimatyantameti"ityuktāṅgiphalam "pāśavimokṣāya"ityaṅge praśaṃsārthaṃ kīrtitam | tathā ca pāśupatatantroktavratāṅgakatvābhāvānna prakṛtavidyāyāṃ rudrasya svātantryeṇopāsyātvamiti
bhāvaḥ | idameva vrataṃ kālāgnirudropaniṣadi pratipādyate | ataḥ sāpi bhagavadupāsanāṅgavidhiparatayā bhagavatparaiveti mahācāryāḥ | ato na rudraparatvamasyā iti || 89 ||
nanu bhasmagrahaṇasya
"madyaṃ pītvā gurudārāṃśca gatvā
steyaṃ kṛtvā brahmahatyāṃ ca kṛtvā |
bhasmacchanno bhasmaśayyāśayāno
rudradhyāyī mucyate sarvapāpaiḥ ||"
iti śātātapasmṛtau sarvapāpakṣayahetu tvasmaraṇāt pāpakṣayasya sarvabrahmavidyāpekṣitatvādapekṣālakṣaṇavastusāmarthyaliṅgena prakaraṇaṃ bādhitvā sarvabrahmavidyārthatvamevopagantavyam | ata eva "tametaṃ vedānuvacanena"ityatra daharavidyāyāḥ prakṛtatve’pi, "dharmeṇa pāpamapanudati"iti śrutipratipannasya karmajanyasya pāpakṣayarūpadvārasya sarvavidyāpekṣitatvādyajñādikarmaṇāṃ sarvavidyāsādhāraṇyam | tathā "tasmādevaṃvicchānto dāntaḥ"ityādiśrutau prakṛtavidyāparāmarśakaivaṃvicchabde satyapi, śamadamasādhyacitaikāgryādeḥ sarvavidyāpekṣitatvādeva, sarvavidyāsādhāraṇyamupetaṃ ityāśaṅkya yadi prakaraṇapaṭhitānāmapyapekṣāmātreṇa sarvasādhāraṇyaṃ tarhi darśapūrṇamāsaprakaraṇapaṭhitānāṃ vrīhiprokṣaṇāvaghātādīnāṃ sannipatyāṅgānām "varma vā etadyajñasya kriyate"ityādyarthavādapratipannayāgāpekṣitopakārajanakānāṃ prayājādyaṅgānāmapyupadeśata eva sarvayāgasādhāraṇyaprasaṅgaḥ; "yajñena dānena"ityādisthale tu "kaṣāye karmabhiḥ pakve tato jñānaṃ pravartate"ityādisarvavidyāsādhāraṇyapratipādakaśāsrāntaravaśena sarvavidyāsādhāraṇyasiddhāvapi, anyatra tathātve pramāṇābhāvāt | śamādisthale’pi
śamādyekasādhyacittaikāgryasādhyatvādbrahmopāsanasya, śamādeḥ sarvavidyāsādhāraṇyam | brahmavidyāpekṣitapāpakṣayasya tu bhasmoddhūlanaikasādhyatve pramāṇabhāvādbhasmoddhūlanasya na
sarvabrahmavidyāṅgatvamiti parihārābhiprāyeṇāha - ( prastutyetyādi)
prastutya rudravapuṣaḥ puruṣottamasya
vidyāmatharvaśirasīritamaṅgamasyāḥ |
bhasmaprasaktiritaratra na cāsti yadvat
sautrāmaṇiprakaraṇoktasurāgrahasya || 90 ||
atharvaśarasi, ṣaṣṭhakhaṇḍa iti śeṣaḥ | rudravapuṣaḥ rudraśarīrakasya | puruṣottamasya bhagavataḥ | vidyāmupāsanam | prasatutya "tadetadupāsitavyam"iti pūrvavākyena vidhāya | īritam | "agniḥ" ityādinā, "bhasma gṛhītvā vimṛjyāṅgāni saṃspṛśet" ityādinā vihitam | bhasmāsyāḥ aṅgaṃ rudraśarīrakaparamātmopāsanarūpaprakṛtavidyāṅgaṃ, bhavati | sautrāmaṇiprakaraṇesautrāmaṇyākhyayāgaviśeṣaprakaraṇe, uktasya
vihitasya, surāgrahasya yadva tsurāgrahasyeva | itaratra svaprakaraṇino’nyatra prasaktiḥ nāsti bhasmanaḥ aṅgatvāpattirnāsti tattaprakaraṇagṛhītānāmanyatrāṅgatvaprasaktirnāsti hītyarthaḥ | cakāreṇa tarśapūrṇamāsaprakaraṇagṛhītaprayājādayo dṛṣṭāntatvena samuccīyante | atra rudravapuṣaḥ puruṣottamasyetyanena "tamīśānaṃ puruṣaṃ devamīḍhyam""buddhyā saṃcintya sthāpayitvā tu rudre"ityanayorvākyayorarthaḥ smāritaḥ || 90 ||
evamekādaśabhiḥ ślokairatharvaśiraso bhagavatparatvamupapāditam | athātharvaśikhāyā bhagavatparatvamupapādyate | tatra "straṣṭhṛsṛjyādibhāvaḥ śrautastvatrāpi tulyaḥ"ityatra tuśabdābhipretaṃ bhagavadutpatteravatāratvaṃ vivriyate - ( ātharvaṇyāmityādi)
ātharvaṇyāṃ śikhāyāmagaṇi bhagavato yā janiḥ kāraṇaikadhyeyatvedaṃparāyāṃ jagadupakṛtaye so’yamicchāvatāraḥ |
yadviṣṇoḥ kāraṇaikapravaṇaphaṇitibhiḥ kāraṇatvopadeśaḥ
spaṣṭo dṛṣṭaprayogaḥ suravajanakatayā śambhuśabdo’pi tasmin || 91 ||
atharvaśikhāyāṃ paippalādo’ṅgirāḥ sanatkumāraścātharvāṇamūcuḥ bhagavān
kimādau prayuktā dhyānaṃ dhyāyitavyaṃ kiṃ taddhyānaṃ ko vā dhyātā kaśca dhyeya iti śrūyate | atrādyaḥ praśno dhyānamātraviṣayaḥ ;
dvitīyo dhyānābhimānidevatāpraśnaḥ; tṛtīyo dhyātrabhimānidevatāpraśnaḥ; turīyaḥ saviśeṣaṇadhyeyapraśnaḥ | tatrādyapraśnasyottaram omityetadakṣaram ādau prayuktaṃ dhyānaṃ dhyāyitavyamiti; dvitīyasyoktaraṃ sarvakaraṇāni ca manasi saṃpratiṣṭhāpya dhyānaṃ viṣṇuriti; tṛtīyasyottaraṃ manasi saha karaṇaiḥ saṃpratiṣṭhāpya dhyātā rudra iti turīyasyottaramīśānaṃ pradhyāyitavyaṃ sarvamidaṃ brahma viṣṇurudrendrāste sarve saṃprasūyante | sarvāṇi cendriyāṇi saha bhūtairna kāraṇaṃ kāraṇānāṃ dhyātā dhātā kāraṇaṃ tu dhyeyaḥ sarvaiśvaryasaṃpannaḥ sarveśvaraḥ śaṃbhurākāśamadhye dhyeya iti ||
atra mahācāryāḥ - "caturthapraśnasyottaramāha - "īśānaṃ pradhyāyitavyam"iti | "kaśca dhyeyaḥ"iti praśnasārūpyāt pūrvapraśnottaravadidameva caturthapraśnottaram | na tu dhyāyīteśānamiti | praśnavairūpyāt pūrvapraśnottaravairupyācca | īśāna iti pulliṅganirdeśaḥkṛtaḥ | īśitavyaviśeṣānirdeśāt sarveśānaṃ vastu dhyātavyamityarthaḥ | pradhāneṣu prādhānyamupāsyasya vaktuṃ brahmādīnāṃ prādhānyamāha - "sarvamidaṃ brahma viṣṇurudrendrāḥ"iti idaṃpramāṇasiddhaṃ sarvamapi brahmaviṣṇurudrendrāḥ rājā rāṣṭramitivannirvāhyanirvāhakabhāvanibandhanaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyam | te sarve saṃprasūyanta iti vākyāntaram | evaṃ sati tacchabdasya prayojanalābhaḥ | bhagavataḥ prādhānyaṃ vyañjayitum idamuttaravākye caiṣu bhagavataḥ prasūtyabhāvasya vakṣyamāṇatvāttadvatteṣu tadanyasyāpi kasyacittadāśaṅkā mā bhūditi sarva ityuktam | evaṃ satyeva hyasya prayojanalābhaḥ | na ca sarvamidaṃ brahmaviṣṇurudrendrā ityuktarāśidvayaparāmarśitayā te sarva ityasya prayojanalābhaḥ | cakārābhāvena te sarva iti samudāyaparāmarśasyāpekṣitatvāditi vācyam | napuṃsakaśeṣāpatterindriyādisahapāṭhāttatulyā parāyattā prasūtirbrahmāderiti svarasato’vagamyate | bhagavataḥ prasūtiṃ niṣedhati - "na kāraṇaṃ kāraṇānāṃ dhyātā dhātā"iti kāraṇānāṃ trayāṇāṃ madhye kāraṇaṃ dhyātā dhātā na prasūyata iti vipariṇāmo’nuṣaṅgaḥ | vipariṇāmenānuṣaṅgaḥ parapakṣe’pi samānaḥ | kāraṇaṃ dhyāteti kāraṇāvasthāyā dhyātṛtvena prasiddha ityavagamyate | tathā prasiddhaśca "bhagavāneva nārāyaṇo’kāmayata" "so’nyaṃ kāmaṃ manasā dhyāyīta"iti dhātṛśabdaśca "aṇoraṇīyān" ityatra bhagavatparatayā śaṅkalikhitoparbṛṃhite prasiddhaḥ | sahastrānāmasu ca "anādinidhano dhātā"iti ||
kāraṇamiti śeṣaṣaṣṭhyevāstu | arthāttu nirdhāraṇalābhaḥ | athavā, mā bhūdarthānnirdhāraṇalābhaḥ | viṣṇorapi saṃprasūtireva pratipādyatām | sā ca prasūtiḥ, "ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate | tasya dhīrāḥ parijānanti yonim""na sā karmanimittajā""jananaṃ svacchayaiva ca"ityādipratipannā svadhīnā na tu brahmarudrādivat karmādhīnā | yadvā nātra vipariṇāmaḥ kāryaḥ | nāpi dhātṛśabdasya prasiddhārthatyāgaḥ | dhyātā caturmukho dhyātā rudraśca kāraṇānāṃ kāraṇamityarthaḥ | viṣṇureva kāraṇamiti bhāvaḥ | dhyātā rudra iti prāgabhidhānāt dhātṛdevatā rudro dhyātetyabhidhīyate | yadvā nātra kasyāpyutpattiḥ pratipādyate | saṃprasūyanta iti "ṣūñ preraṇe" iti dhātoḥ karmaṇi lakāre rūpam | na tu "ṣañ prāṇiprasave" ityasya" "ṣūñ prāṇigarbhavimocane" ityasya vā tasya prāṇaviṣayatayā sarvāṇi cendriyāṇi saha bhūtairityatrānuṣaṅgiṇo’pyanvayāyogāt | jāyanta ityadhyāhāraprasaṅgāt | īśānaṃ pradhyāyita vyamiti pūrvavākye asaṃkocalabdhasarveśvaratvasya sākṣādupapādakatayā preraṇārthasyaiva dhātoratra grāhyatvācca | paramakāraṇabhūto dhyātā dhātā tu na kenacit preryata iti na kāramityādivākyasyārthaḥ | "kaśca dhyeyaḥ"ityatra dhyeyaviśeṣaṇasyāpi vivakṣitatvāt dhyātavyasya hṛdayādhikaraṇaṃ vidadhāti | ""kāraṇaṃ tu dhyeyaḥ sarvaiśvaryasaṃpannaḥ sarvaiśvaraḥ śaṃbhurākāśamadhye"iti | sarvaiśvaryasaṃpanna ityatra sarvaṃ ca tadaiśvarye ceti karmadhārayaḥ | na tu sarvaiśvaraśabdāt ṣaṣṭhīsamāsāt ṣyañ | ādivṛddhiprasaṅgāt | sarvaprakāramīśvaramīśānaṃ vivakṣitam | asti ca niyamane prakārabhedaḥ | yayāntaḥ praviśya niyamanaṃ rājāderiva bahiḥ sthitvā niyamanaṃ dhāraṇapravṛttyādiviṣayaṃ ca sadvāramadvārakaṃ ca | idaṃ ca sarvaprakārakamaiśvaryaṃ na katipayaviṣayamityucyate sarveśvara iti | evaṃ ca sarvaiśvaryasaṃpannasarveśvaraśabdayoraikārthyabhramāt sarvaiśvaraśabde tacchabdavācyalakṣaṇāmāśritya paunaruktyaparihāraḥ kenacit kṛto nirastaḥ | vastuvṛttamuktam tathātve’pi siddhānte na doṣaḥ sarvaiśvaraśabdasya devatāntare rūḍhyasiddheḥ | ihaiva kalpanāyāṃ bhagavatyovāstu | atra ca sarvāṇīndriyāṇīti pṛthagindriyagrahaṇamindriyapreraṇarūpam hṛṣīkeśaśabdārthaṃ nārāyaṇatvaliṅgamupasthāpayitum | yadvā "kāraṇaṃ tu dhyeyaḥ"iti caturthapraśnasyottaram; upoddhātarītyā tadupapādanāya "īśānāṃ pradhyāyitavyam"iti sarveśitureva mokṣapradānasāmarthyattasyaivopāsyatvaṃ yuktamiti bhāvaḥ | "kāraṇaṃ tu dhyeyaḥ"ityatra kāraṇapadaṃ
sarvakāraṇaparaṃ trimūrtikāraṇaparaṃ vā | bhagavata eva trimūrtikāraṇatvaṃ ca, lubālopaniṣadi kāryeṣu sthitipratipādakaparyāyeṣu trimūrtiṣu sthitiśravaṇāt, paiṅgirahasye - "yamavaruṇaviṣṇurudrendrāḥ"iti śravaṇācca prasiddham | tasmādatra subālopaniṣadi
prasiddhakāraṇatvānuvādena bhagavata eva dhyātavyatvaṃ vidhīyate | śaṃbhuśabdaścābhidhānakośādiṣu bhagavadabhidhānatayā suprasiddhaḥ | śaṃbhurghātṛharīśeṣvityādinā subālopaniṣattāpanīyādiṣu bhagavati sarvaiśvaratvāvagamāt tatsamānādhikṛtaḥ śambhuśabdo bhagavatpara eva | na tu rudra eva svārasyamiti cet - satyam | tattu trimūrtyantargata eva tatparigrahaścānupapannaḥ | sṛjyatvānapahatapātmatvayoḥ śravaṇāt | parānabhimataśca tatparigrahaḥ | ato bhagavata eva parigraho yuktaḥ | viṣṇoḥ sargaśravaṇe’pi "ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate | tasya dhīrāḥ parijānanti yonim"ityavatāratvāvagamānna sa parādhīna iti vyācaravyuḥ ||
tadidamucyate - ātharvaṇyāmityādinā | kāraṇasya yadekaṃ dhyeyatvaṃ tasminnidaṃparāyāṃ tātparyavatyāṃ, puruṣasūktādiprasiddhakāraṇatvānuvādena dhyātavyatāvidhiparāyāmityarthaḥ | ekaśabdaśca dhyeyatāyāṃ tātparyātirekavyañjanārthaḥ | tadāhurbhaṭṭasudarśanapādāḥ - "asya prakaraṇasya "kaśca dhyeyaḥ" ityupakramya "kāraṇaṃ tu dhyeya"ityanena kāraṇatvamanūdya dhyeyavidhiparatvāvagamāt puruṣasūktādipratipannasyaiva bhagavato dhyeyatvavidhānaparamidaṃ vākyam"iti | ātharvaṇyāṃ śikhāyām - atharvaśikhāyām | bhagavato yā janiragaṇi - nirdauṣakalyāṇaguṇākarasya nārāyaṇasya yajjanma bodhitam, "sarvamidaṃ brahmaviṣṇurudrendrāste sarve saṃprasūyante"ityanena yā bhagavato’pyutpattirbodhitetyarthaḥ | so’yam - " ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate | tasya dhīrāḥ parijānanti yonim""yena sūryastapati tejaseddhaḥ"pitā putreṇa "putṛmānyoniyonau"ityādiprāpakavākyaprasiddho dhyeyavidhiparāyāmasyāmitarasṛjyasajātīyatayāvatīrṇasya upāsakasaulabhyaprajñāpanārthamanūditaścodbhavaḥ | jagadupakṛtaye - "jagatāmupakārāya"
ityuktasādhuparitrāṇādirūpajagadupakāraprayojakaḥ bhagavata icchāvatāraḥ - bhagavataḥ svecchāhetukaḥ prādurbhāva ityarthaḥ | icchāpadena jīvotpattivat karmamūlakatvamasya netyucyate | "saṃbhavāmyātmamāyayā""jananaṃ svecchayaiva ca"ityādikamabhipretya icchāvatāra ityuktam |
vidheyaliṅgānusārāt so’yamiti nirdeśaḥ | dhyeyatvavidhyupayogī cāyaṃ bhagavato janmānuvādaḥ "ajāyamānaḥ"ityādipurovādaprāptāvatārānuvādakaḥ saulabhyajñāpanārtha iti bhāvaḥ | yat - yasmāt ekasmin, pravaṇābhiḥ tātparyavatībhiḥ, phaṇitibhiḥ puruṣasūktasubālopaniṣatpaiṅgirahasyādibhiḥ upaniṣadbhiḥ | viṣṇoḥ kāraṇatvopadeśaḥ - bhagavataḥ kāraṇatvavidhānam | spaṣṭaḥ sphuṭam | vanaṣaṇa saṃbhaktau | saṃbhaktiḥ prārthanam | anena ca tātparyaṃ vivakṣyate | tasmin kāraṇatvena śrutyukte bhagavati śaṃbhuśabdo’pi sukhajanakatayā śaṃ sukhaṃ bhāvayatīti vyutpattisiddhasukhajanakatvarūpayogārthena dṛṣṭaprayogaḥ "śrṛṇu me vacanaṃ śaṃbho pitāmahapitāmaha"ityādyupabṛhaṇeṣu, "viśvākṣaṃ viśvaśaṃbhuvam"ityādiśrutau ca śrutaprayogo bhavati | tasmāt kāraṇaśaṃbhuśabdādinā bhagavantamanūdya tasya dhyeyatvapare’smin bhagavato janmaśravaṇamavatārarūpajanmānuvādakamityarthaḥ |
tadidamuktaṃ bhaṭṭasudarśanapādaiḥ - "na ca śaṃbhuḥ kāraṇamiti vākyavṛttiḥ, kiṃ tu kāraṇaṃ śaṃbhurdhyeya iti | anyathā kaśca dhyeya(tva) ityuttaratvāyogāt | śaṃbhuśabdasyāsādhāraṇye’pi ākāśaprāṇādivat paramātmaparatvaṃ nyāyyam | na cāyamasādhāraṇaḥ "śaṃbhuḥ svayaṃbhūrdruhiṇaḥ"iti nāmapāṭhāt"ityādinā | (khiprasūlibādvasaṃjñāyāmiti prakaraṇe) "mitadravādibhya upasaṃkhyānam"iti kīrtekena śaṃbhuśabdo ḍhutpratyayāntatayā vyutpādito vaiyākāraṇaiḥ | "mitaṃ dravatīti mitadruḥ | śatadruḥ śaṃbhuḥ, antarbhāvitaṇyartho’tra bhavatiḥ"iti | guṇayoganimittako’pyayaṃ śumbhuśabdaḥ śivaśabdavat | tathā ca prayujyate "svayaṃbhūḥ śaṃbhuraṃbhojalocane tvatpayodharaḥ | nakhena kasya dhanyasya candracūḍo bhaviṣyati ||"iti | viśiṣyaiva ca bhagavatyabhidhānakośena sahaprayogo’sya darśitaḥ | aparyavasānavṛttyā bhagavatparatvamapi saṃbhavati ato bhagavatparaivaiyam atharvaśikheti || 91 ||
śaṃbhuśudasyāparyavasānavṛttyā bhagavatparatvamiti pakṣe tvarthaśirasīvātharvaśikhāyāmapi rudraśarīrakaparamātmopāsanameva vidhīyata (ityatrāha) (ityāha) - 
uditamupāsanadvayamatharvaśiraśśikhāyoḥ
sphuṭamapunarbhavāya puravairikalevariṇaḥ |
kamaladṛśaḥ kvacit kvacana kāraṇatattvaparaśrutivihitasya hārdamuṣimadhyajuṣo viduṣaḥ || 92 ||
atharvaśiraśśikhayo - atharvaśirasi, atharvaśikhāyāṃ ca | apunarbhavāya - mokṣāya uditam uktam, vihitamiti yāvat | upāsanadvayam - "tadetadupāsitavyam""śaṃbhurākāśamadhye dhyeyaḥ"ityuktamupāsanadvayam | kvacit kvacana kāraṇatattvaparaśrutivihitasya | kvacit kvacana - kvacit kvacit nārāyaṇānuvākamahopaniṣastubālopaniṣadādiṣu, kāraṇatattvaparābhiḥ upāsanavidhyādyanyaparatāgandhavidhuratayā kāraṇavastupratipādikābhiḥ, kāraṇayāthātmyapratipādikābhirvā, śrutibhiḥ "sa brahma sa śivaḥ sendraḥ"ityādikābhiḥ, vihitasya tattaccharīrakatayā tattadvidyopāsyatayā bodhitasya | viduṣo hārdasuṣimadhyajuṣaḥ - upāsakahṛdayavivaramadhyagatasya | "randhraṃ śvabhraṃ vapā suṣiḥ"iti kośaḥ | "juṣī prītisevanayoḥ" ityasmāt kvip | puravairikalebariṇaḥ - rudraśarīrakasya | manuṣyādyapekṣayā prāśastyavivakṣayā tu rudrasya karmadhārayādiniḥ prayuktaḥ | "na karmādhārayāt"iti niṣedhastu nātra bhavati | bahuvrīhiṇā samānārthakasya saṃbandhamātravācino matvaryoyasyaiva niṣedho na tu praśaṃsādyarthaviśeṣavivakṣādhīnasyeti vaiyākaraṇairuktatvāt | kamaladṛśaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣasyaiti | sphuṭam - rudraśarīraka bhagavadviṣayakamiti spaṣṭam | atra ca kāraṇasyaiva dhyātavyatvavidhānāt, bhagavata eva ca kāraṇatayā śrutyantarabodhitatvāt, prasiddharudrasya ca sṛdyatvādiśravaṇāt, avatāraśruteścātatparatvāt, bhagavataśca sarvavidyopāsyatvenānanyaparanārāyaṇānuvākasiddhatvāt, "padmakośapratīkāśaṃ hṛdayam"ityādināvagatasya ca hṛdayamadhyopāsyatvasyākṣākāśamadhye dhyeya iti pratyabhijñānāt, "sa yaścāyaṃ puruṣe | yaścāsāvāditye | sa ekaḥ"ityādinā puruṣāntarvartinaścādityamaṇḍalāntarvartinaikyāvagamāt, tasya ca puṇḍarīkākṣatvaśravaṇāt bhagavānevātra rudraśarīrakatayopāsya iti sphuṭaṃ pratīyata ityarthaḥ ||
atrātharvaśirograhaṇaṃ dṛṣṭāntārtham; taduktopāsanasya rudrāntaryāmibhagavadviṣayakatvasya pratipāditatvāt | tacca "dhyatā rudraḥ"iti rudrasyaiva dhyātṛtve’pi madhuvidyānyāyena taccharīrakasya paramātmano dhyeyatvaṃ na virudhyata iti sphoraṇāya | atra ca rudraśarīrakaparamātmana eva vedyatve’pi sarvaiśvaryādiguṇabhedādvidyābheda ityabhiprāyeṇa upāsanadvayamityuktam | "etāmadhītya dvijo garbhāvāsādvamukto vimucyate
"iti vākyasiddhārthābhiprāyeṇāpunarbhavāyetyuktam ||
"viṣṇurātmā bhagavato bhavasyāmitatejasaḥ |
tasmāddhanurjyāsaṃsparśaṃ sa viṣehe maheśvaraḥ ||"
iti vacanasmāraṇena rudrasya bhagavadantaryāmikatvaṃ bahupramāṇasiddhamiti vyañjanāya puravairikalebariṇa ityuktam | "ākāśamadhye dhyeyaḥ" iti puruṣāntaryāmitvāvagamāt tasya cādityamaṇḍalāntarvartinā aikyāvagamāt tasya ca puṇḍarīkākṣatvaṃ śrutamiti bhagavānevātra dhyeyatayā sidhyatītyabhiprāyeṇa kamaladṛśa ityuktam | hṛdayamadhyagatatvamananyaparanārāyaṇānuvākena bhagavanniṣṭhatayāvagatamiti tasyātra "ākāśamadhye dhyeyaḥ"ityanenoktatvāt bhagavata evātra dhyeyatvaṃ sidhyatītyabhiprāyeṇa viduṣo hārdasuṣimadhyajuṣa ityuktam || 92 ||
"tato yaduttarataram""tasmin yadantaḥ"iti śrutiliṅgābhyāṃ devatāntarasya bhagavadapekṣayotkarṣapratipādanaṃ tayoratatparatvopapādanena nirastam "yatsaṃjñāmūrtikartuḥ""prastutyāmṛtahetum"ityādiślokadvaye | tatprasaṅgāccātharvaśiraśśikhayoratatparatvopapādanena bhagavatparatvamupapāditam "yaddaivaiḥ"ityādibhiḥ | idānīṃ samākhyayā tadāśaṅkya śrutyādibalavanmānabādhitā samākhyā nārthaniścayakarīti samākhyāvalambinaṃ pūrvapakṣaṃ nirasyati tribhiḥ ślokaiḥ ||
ayaṃ hi sa pūrvapakṣaḥ; sopapadanirupapadapuruṣaśabdavācyāttathāvidhe(śvara)śabdavācyasya
prakarṣaḥ | puruṣaśabdo hi jīvasādhāraṇo bhagavati prayujyate | mahattvaviśeṣitaśca tasmin prayujyate | īśvaramaheśvaraśabdau ca rudre prayujyate | puruṣapadaṃ ca nikarṣayuktajīvasādhāraṇaṃ bhagavati prayujyamānaṃ tadvadeva bhagavatyapi nikarṣamāpādayati | īśvarapadaṃ tu sarvajīvavyāvṛttaṃ puruṣatvādutkṛṣṭamevaiśvaryaṃ bodhayati | mahacchabdaviśeṣitaṃ tu tasya mahattvaṃ bodhayati | tathā ca
puruṣāmātrādbhagavato rudrasya maheśvarasyotkarṣaḥ samucita iti | tamimaṃ pūrvapakṣamāha - (uktamityādi)
uktaṃ kenacidīśvaraḥ puruṣa ityuktī śivaśrīśayorvartete bahuśo mahatparamavāgyukte vimukte api |
ityutkarṣanikarṣagocarapadavyākhyātayoretayorutkarṣo giriśasya vaktumucitaḥ puṃsaḥ pradhānādiva || 93 ||
mahacca paramaṃ ceti mahatparame; śabdasvarūpāpekṣayā klībayoritaretarayogaḥ | mahatparame ca te vācau ca mahatparamavācau; śabdasvarūpaparatāyām bandhuni bahuvrīhau ityatreva nityanapuṃsakaliṅgatvādeva strīliṅgaviśeṣaṇatve’pi na strītvam | itarathā hi sahatī parameti syāt | samānādhikaraṇottarapadaparatayā bhāve napuṃsakabhāvāpravṛtteḥ | tābhyāṃ yukte tatsamabhivyāhṛte, viyukte api, buddhisthatvānmahatparamaśabdābhyāṃ asamabhivyāhṛte kevale iti yāvat | īśvaraḥ puruṣa ityuktī īśvarapuruṣaśabdau īśvaraḥ maheśvaraḥ parameśvaraḥ, puruṣo mahāpuruṣaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ iti rupeṇa bahuśa - pracuraṃ, vartanakriyāpekṣayā śaspratyayaḥ | śivaśrīśayorvartete - śivaviṣṇavoḥ prayujyete | śaktyā tadvācakau bhavata ityarthaḥ iti hetauḥ utkarṣanikarṣigocarapadaiḥ krameṇa īśvaramaheśvaraparameśvaraśabdarūpotkarṣabodhakapadaiḥ, puruṣamahāpuruṣaparamapuruṣaśabdarūpanikarṣabodhakapadaiśca, vyākhyātayo rviśeṣeṇa pracuramākhyātayoḥ vyavahṛtayoḥ | viśabdena tayoḥ krameṇa puruṣeśvarādipadaiḥ kvācitkavyavahāre’pi na doṣa iti sūcyate | etayoḥ - śivaśrīśayormadhye | nirdhāraṇe sappamī| anvādeśasyāvivakṣitatvādenādeśavirahaḥ | pradhānādina acetanabhūtāyāḥ prakṛteriva tatsahapaṭhitāt puṃsaḥ puruṣāt , pradhānapuruṣāviti pradhānasahapaṭhitapuruṣaśabdavācyāt
bhagavata utkarṣo giriśasya rudrasya, vaktumucitaḥ arhaḥ | girau ḍaśchandasi iti giriśaśabdasya chandoviṣayatve’pi giriśamupacacāra pratyahaṃ sā sukeśī iti prayuktatvāt matvarthīyaśapratyayāntatvamityuktam | atreśvarādipadānāmuktarṣagocaratvamutkarṣabodhakatvamutkarṣavyañjakatvaṃ ca | puruṣādipadānāṃ nikarṣagocaratvaṃ tu nikarṣavyañjakatvarūpam | īśvaratvaṃ hi utkarṣarūpamutkarṣāvaṃha ca | puṃstvaṃ tu na nikarṣarūpaṃ, kiṃ tvanīśvaravṛttitayā nikarṣasamānādhikaraṇam | tathā ca tadvācakapadavācyatāyāstadgatotkarṣāpakarṣasajātīyotkarṣāpakarṣagamakatvājjīvavācakapuruṣapadavācyasya jīvāntaravadīśvarāvadhikanikarṣasiddhiḥ | mahāsacivaśabdasya sacivāntarāpekṣayā mahattvabodhakatve’pi rājahārājaśabdoktarājāpekṣayā yathāpakarṣaḥ; tathā mahāpuruṣaśabdavācyasya puruṣāntarāpekṣayotkarṣe’pīśvarāpekṣayā nikarṣa ityarthaḥ | iti kenaciduktam - nāmagrahaṇānarheṇa kenāpyuktamityarthaḥ || 93 ||
atrāyaṃ siddhāntaḥ - śrutiliṅgadiṣu mitho virodhe pūrvapūrvasya kalpyapramāṇapratibandhakatvarūpamuttarottarabādhakatvam | virodhaścaikasya śeṣasya śeṣidvaye pramāṇadvayasattve | yathā aindrimantrasya śrutyā gārhapatyāṅgatve liṅgācca aindrāṅgatve | yathā vā ekasmin śeṣiṇi śeṣadvayaviniyojakapramāṇadvaye | yathā gārhāpatye śrutyā aindrīmantro liṅgādāgneyaḥ evamuttaratrāpi virodho draṣṭavyaḥ | atra ca sarvatra uttarottarasya pūrvapūrvakalpakatvena pramāṇyādbhinnaviṣayeṇa pūrvapūrveṇa klṛptena śeṣaśeṣiṇornirākāṅkṣatvāpādanena kalpanāpratibandharūpasuttarottarabādhakatvam | evaṃ ca liṅgasamākhyābhyāṃ bhakṣamantrāṃśasya grahaṇāṅgatvabhakṣaṇāṅgatvayoḥ prasaktau liṅgena samākhyābādhanādgrahaṇāṅgatvaṃ, saṃbandhamātraparāyāḥ samākhyāyāstu grahaṇādidvārā paraṃparayāpyupapattiḥ | kramasamākhyayorvirodhe pauroḍāśikasamākhyāte kāṇḍe samāmnātasya śundhadhvamiti mantrādeḥ sānnādhānāt sānnāyyāṅgatvam ||
tathā caikasmin rudrarūpe śeṣiṇi īścaratvarūpadharmadvayātmakaśeṣadvayabodhikayoḥ kāryatvakarmavaśyatvādipratipādakavākyajāteśvarasamākhyayorvirodhādvākyena samākhyābādhaḥ | yathā parikrayādau yajamānakartṛkatvabodhakena yajeta svargakāmaḥ iti vākyenādhvaryukartṛkatvabodhikādhvaryavasamākhyāyāḥ yathā ca gārhapatya eva
aindrīsaṃbandhabodhakaśrutyā āgneyyāṅgatvabodhakaliṅgasya | tathā hi - īśvaraśabdastāvat rudre yogarūḍhaḥ | tatra ca stheśabhāsapisakasovaracu iti sūtroktavaracpratyayānteśadhātubodhitasya īśanaśīlarūpayogārthasya rūḍhyupasthitarudre
tādātmyenānvayādīśanaśīlarudrabodho jāyate | tatra yogartheśanaśīle rūḍhyartharudratādāmyaṃ, rūḍhyartharudre yogartheśanaśīlatādātmyaṃ vā na vidhātuṃ śakyam | tathā vyutpattyabhāvāt | tathā ca samākhyāyāḥ siddhavannirdeśānyathānupapattyā yo rudraḥ sa īśanaśīlaḥ ityādivākyakalpanā yāvadrudre īśvaratvaṃ vidhīyate, tāvadeva klṛptena kāryatvādibodhakavākyenānīśvaratvabodhakena vākyakalpanāpratibandhāt samākhyābādhaḥ hutvātmānaṃdevadevaḥ tadā darśitapanthānau ityādivākyāvagatabhagavadāyattāpekṣakeśvaratvaparatayā ca samākhyāpyupapannā | ato vākyena samākhyābādhānna rudrasyotkarṣasiddhiḥ | kiṃ ca vyutkrameṇotkarṣāpakarṣagocaraviśvarūpanārāyaṇabhikṣurudrādisamākhyāntaraiḥ pratirodhānna samākhyayā vivakṣitotkarṣāpakarṣasiddhiḥ || kiṃ ca,
"nāmāni tava govinda yāni loke mahānti ca |
tānyeva mama nāmāni nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||""
ityanena bhagavadgateśvarādisamākhyānāṃ rudre saṅketakaraṇāvagamānna tayā vivakṣitārthasiddhiḥ | yathā cāyamarthaḥ tadvacanasya tathā pārāśaryavijaye mahācaryairupapāditamanusandheyam | kiṃ ca khadyotapuṇyajanādisamākhyānāmalpayogārtha paratvaviparyayaparatvayordarśanenātrāpi tathāsandehānna samākhyayeṣṭasiddhiḥ | evaṃ tadvācakapadavācyatvāttadvannikarṣādisiddhirityatra tadvācaketyādihetuśarīre yadi yogārthaikyaṃ vivakṣitaṃ tadā hetoḥ svarūpāsiddhiḥ | pūrvataṃ vāhamihāsam ityādiśrutyā yadi vā pūrvamevāsamiheti puruṣaṃ viduḥ yadi vā bahudānādvaiviṣṇuḥ puruṣa ucyate iti pādmopabṛṃhitayā bhagavati puruṣaśabdasya yogārthāntarāvagamāt | yadi ca pravṛttinimittaikyaṃ vivakṣitaṃ tadā tvātmatvena jīvavācakapadavācyatvadīśvarasyāniṣṭaprasaṅgaḥ | yadi pravṛttinimittaikyaṃ vivakṣitaṃ tadā tvātmatvena jīvavācakapadavācyatvadīśvarasyāniṣṭaprasaṅgaḥ | yadi pravṛttinimittabhedo vivakṣitaḥ tathāpi sthāṇuḥ pumān mahādeve śaṅkāvatrī sthire triṣu ityabhidhānakośāt rudrasya ūrdhvatāviśiṣṭastambhādivadapakarṣaprasaṅga | bhagavataśca sarvetkṛṣṭatvapratipādakapramāṇagaṇaiḥ kālātyayāpadeśo hetoḥ | tulyanyāyatayendramahendrasamākhyābhyām idi paramaiśvarye
iti
paṭhitadhātuniṣpannābhyāmaiśvaryapāramyatanmahattvapratipādakābhyāmindrasya pāramyasiddhiḥ | śrutyādivirodhe samākhyādaurbalyaṃ tu rudre’pi samānam | avatārādiśrutyā ca bhagavati sakalaśrutisāmarasyamupapāditam iti ||
tamimaṃ siddhāntamāha dvābhyām
mū naitat sādhu samākhyayā hi balavadvākyādibhagrārthayā notkarṣo giriśasya vaktumucitaḥ kenāpi devendravat |
indraḥ syāt parameśvarastvitarathā tattatsamākhyāvaśādākhyā hi prabalapramāṇavihatā tasmin mahāvṛkṣavat || 94 ||
etat - samākhyayotkarṣāpakarṣapratipādanam | na sādhu - na samyak | kuta ityatrāha - samākhyayā hītyādi | hi - yasmāt | balavadvākyādibhiḥ - klṛptatvarūpaprabalyayuktervākyaliṅgādibhiḥ (stadupastabdheḥ samākhyānnaraiśca) bhagnārthayā ucchinnapūrvapramāṇānumānayā | samākhyayā - īśvarādisamākhyayā | tattatsamākhyāvaśāt devendravat - indramahendrasamākhyayā devendrasyeva | giriśasya - rudrasya | utkarṣaḥ - pāramyam | kenāpi vaktuṃ nocitaḥ | tasmānnaitat sādhvityarthaḥ |
artho’numānam vācyavācakasaṃbandhastayorarthāt pratīyate ityatra tathā darśanāt | pāramyarūpaprayojanaṃ vā bhagnaṃ yasyā iti bhagnārthayetyasyāryaḥ | giriśasyetyupameye ṣaṣṭhīdarśanāt devendravaditi ṣaṣṭhyantādvitiḥ | upamānopameyayornaikā samākhyāstītyupamāne tattatsamākhyāvaśādityākṛṣyate | mahādevaśabdavācyasya devatāntarāpekṣayotkarṣe’pi devendraśabdoktaśaśīpatyapekṣayā nikarṣāpattiriti sūcanāya devendriva dityuktam | īśvaratve hi puruṣapratiyogikatvaṃ kaṇṭhoktam | indratve tu devapratiyogikatvaṃ kaṇṭhoktam | evaṃ satyapi śrutyādibhiḥ samākhyābādhāt yadīndrasya nikarṣaḥ tarhi rudrasya nikarṣaḥ kaimutyenetyarthaḥ | (uttamevapibdayati indraḥ syādityādinā) indraḥ syādityādinā | itarathā samākhyāmātrādutkarṣāpakarṣasiddhau | tattatsamākhyāvaśāt - indramahendradevenadrarūpasamākhyādhīnatayā | indraḥ parameśvaraḥ syāt - sarvotkṛṣṭaḥ syāt | na cedamiṣṭaṃ parasyeti bhāvaḥ | parihārasāmyābhiprāyeṇa indrapratibandyuktiḥ | tadāha - 
ākhyetyādinā tasmin - rudre | ākhyā - īśvarādisamākhyā | mahāvṛkṣavat mahāvṛkṣavaditi tantreṇa nirdeśaḥ |
saptamīprathamāntaprakṛtikavatipratyayāntaryormahāvṛkṣasadṛśe tasmin mahāvṛkṣasadṛśī samākhyetyādyanvayaḥ | sākṣāt paraṃparānādareṇa prabalapramāṇavihatatvaṃ dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikayorbodhyam | vastuvailakṣaṇye prāmāṇike tu sati sahapaṭanamakiñcitkaraṃ tejastimiravaditi phalapuṣpacchāyādidaurbhāgyaviṣayakapratyakṣeṇa yathā mahāvṛkṣe mahāvṛkṣaśabdoktapūjyatvarūpamahattvabādhaḥ tathānīśvaratvabodhakavākyādirūpaprabalapramāṇabādhāt samākhyayā notkarṣasiddhiḥ ityarthaḥ || 94 ||
tadvācakapadavācyatvarūpahetuṃ kālātyayāpadeśaviruddhatvābhyāṃ dūṣayati ||
kiṃ jīvaḥ puraṣoktigocaraḥ iti śrīśo’pi tadgocaro
jīvaḥ syādgiriśo dhruvo bhavati kiṃ tau sthāṇusaṃjñāviti |
takṣatvāya ghaṭeta kiṃ mukhabhuvāmācāryasaṃśabdanaṃ
maṇḍūkatvamupaiti kiṃ haripadasparśena kaṇṭhīravaḥ || 95 ||
