jīvaḥ puruṣoktigocara iti - jīvaḥ puruśaśabdavācya iti hetoḥ | tadgocaraḥ - tadvācyaḥ śrīśo’pi jīvaḥ syāt kim | kiṃśabdaḥ kṣepārthakaḥ | śrīśo na jīvo bhavedityarthaḥ | "śrīyaśca śrīrbhavedagryā"ityādibhiḥ sarvotkṛṣṭāyāḥ śriyo’pyutkarṣo bhagavatyavagata iti, nikarṣavyañjakaheturbādhita iti bhāvaḥ | "hrīśca te lakṣmīśca patnyau""dyāvāpṛthivī janayan deva ekaḥ""iyaṃ hi prakṛtiḥ sūkṣmā""tatra nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān mayā bhikṣāṃ prayācitaḥ"ityādyavagataṃ bhagavataḥ paramakāraṇatvaṃ paramodāratvaṃ ca pratyabhijñāpayatā śrīpadena samākhyārūpeṇa, "yadi vā pūrvamevāsamiheti puruṣaṃ viduḥ""yadi vā bahudānādvai viṣṇuḥ puruṣa ucyate"ityuktapuruṣapadayogārthasmāraṇāt pūrvoktasvarūpāsiddhirapi sūcitā | tau - stambharudrau śaṃkurudrau vā | sthāṇusaṃjñāviti - "sthāṇuḥ pumān mahādeve śaṅkāvastrī sthire triṣu"iti kośena sthāṇuśabdāvācyatayāvagatāvati hetoḥ | giriśaḥ - rudraḥ | dhruvo bhavati kiṃ, śaṅkustambho vā na bhavati kiletyarthaḥ | "dhruvaḥ śaṅkau vaṭe kīle yoge tārāntare pumān | tarkaniścitayoḥ klībe nityaniścalayostriṣu ||"iti kośo’trābhipretaḥ | tathā ca
tadvācakapadavācyatvarūpahetuḥ samīhitarudrotkarṣaviparītatannikarṣasādhakatvādviruddha iti bhāvaḥ | sukhabhuvām - brāhmaṇānām | ācāryasaṃśabdanam - ācāryapadena vyavahāraḥ | mukhabhuvāṃ takṣatvāya - grāmacaṇḍālamadhyaparigaṇitatakṣakatvamānetum | ghaṭeta kim - arhati kim ? na ghaṭata ityarthaḥ | takṣatvāyeti "kriyārthopapada" ityādinā caturthī | kaṇṭhīravaḥ - siṃhaḥ, haripadavācyatvarūpa haripada (sparśena) (saṃbandhena) maṇḍūkatvam upaiti kiṃ nopaitītyarthaḥ | karaṭikaraṭavikaṭataṭavipāṭanapaṭutarakaduravatayā prasiddhasya siṃhasya, kūpamaṇḍūka iti bhekānāṃ ? prathamodāharaṇasiṃhāsanamūrdhābhiṣiktabhekabhāvo na tadvācakapadavācyatvamātreṇāyātītyarthaḥ | atra "yamānilendracandrārkaviṣṇusiṃhāṃśuvājiṣu | śukāhikapibhekeṣu harirnā kapile triṣu"iti ko’śo’bhisaṃhitaḥ || 95 ||
samākhyābhañjakavākyānāṃ tadupaṣṭambhakavākyāntarairanyaparatvaśaṅkāyāḥ, ayogyārthakatvena saṃpratipannākāśaprāṇādiśabdānāmiva kāryatvakarmavaśyatvādibhirayogyārthakatvenāvadhṛtaśivādiśabdaviśeṣāṇāmapi guṇayogavaśenāparyavasānavṛttyā vā nyaparanārāyaṇānuvākapratipāditā (nanyāṃtāsiddhā) vatārādiyuktabhagavatparatvaṃ nyāyyamiti na vākyāntaraiḥ samākhyopaṣṭabha iti parihāramabhiprayannāha - ( prāṇotyādi)
prāṇākāśamukhaiḥ padairupaniṣadbhāgeṣu joghuṣyate
kiṃ na brahma paraṃ paratvagaṇanā jāgarti kiṃ tāvatā |
prāṇādyeṣu śivādiśabdagāditaṃ taccetparatvaṃ kutasvattatkāraṇavākyasiddhabhagavatsṛjyeṣu rudrādiṣu || 97 ||
upaniṣadbhāgeṣu - chāndogyādipradeśaviśeṣeṣu | paraṃ brahma - trividhakāraṇatvatadupayuktaguṇajātādibhiḥ
sarvaprakāraprakṛṣṭaṃ, svarūpeṇa guṇaiścānavadhikātiśayabṛhattvayuktaṃ vastu | prāṇākāśamukhaiḥ padaiḥ - kāryattvādinā nirjñātapaṃcavṛttiprāṇabhūtākāśādiprasiddhapraṇākāśādiśabdaiḥ | na joghuṣyate kim - punaḥ
punaḥ spaṣṭaṃ na pratipādyate kim, pratipādyata evetyarthaḥ ||
atra ca yogavṛtthiraparyavasānavṛttirvā vivakṣitā | mukhapadenāvyākṛtājādipadaparigrahaḥ | paunaḥpunyarūpeṇa kriyāsamabhihāreṇa "prāṇādeva sarvāṇi" "prāṇo’smi" "ākāśādeva" "ākāśo ha vai" "yadeṣa ākāśaḥ" "daharo’sminnantara ākāśaḥ" ityādiprāṇādipadāvṛttirabhipretā | anena cābhyāsarūpaṃ tātparyaliṅgamapyupapattilakṣaṇatātparyaliṅgapratyanīkānupapattyātmakāyogyārthakatvena pratihataṃ kileti vyajyate | na kimityanenāyamarthaḥ saṃpratipanna iti vyajyate ||
tāvatā vedānteṣu prāṇākāśādipadānāṃ kāraṇaprakaraṇeṣu śravaṇamātreṇārthasāmarthyamapyanapekṣya, prāṇādyeṣu - kāryattvādinā prasiddhapañcavṛttiprāṇabhūtākāśādiṣu | paratvagaṇanā - pāramyaśaṅkhā | jāgarti kim - unmiṣati kim ? vastusāmarthyavyatirekaniścayena paratvaśaṅkāyā evānunmeṣa eva kiletyarthaḥ | tat paraṃ brahma | upaniṣadbhāgeṣu - śvetāśvatarādiṣu | anuṣaktasyopaniṣadbhāgoṣvityasya yogyatayā viśeṣaparatvam | śivādiśabdagāditaṃ cet - guṇayogenāparyavasānavṛttyā vā śivarudraśaṃbhvādiśabdairbodhitaṃ yadi | kāraṇatvasya bhagavaddharmatāyā bahabapaniṣatsiddhatvācchivādiśabdānāṃ bhagavati prayogādguṇayogasya sṛṣṭalayādiprakaraṇeṣvapekṣitatvācchivādiśarīrakatvabodhanasya tathopāsanārthamāvaśyakatvācca kāraṇaprakaraṇasthaśivādiśabdānāṃ bhagavatparatve nyāyye prasiddharudrāderayogyatāpi tatsahakāriṇītyāha - tattatkāraṇetyādinā | tattatkāraṇavākyasiddhaṃ - ananyaparamahopaniṣannārāyaṇopaniṣanmahānārāyaṇopaniṣatsubālopaniṣat pauṅgirahasyādibodhitaṃ, bhagavatsṛjyatvaṃ yeṣāṃ tathokteṣu | rudrādiṣu, ādiśabdena indrādiparigrahaḥ | dṛṣṭāntārthaśca tatparigrahaḥ | paratvaṃ - pāramyam | kutaḥ kasmāt, na kasmādapi hetoḥ sidhyatītyarthaḥ | ato neśvarādisamākhyāyāstairupaṣṭambha iti bhāvaḥ | mahāprakaraṇasya nigamanamapyayaṃ ślokaḥ | bhagavatsṛjyeti bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ | tad brahma śivādiśabdagaditaṃ cet rudrādiṣu paratvaṃ kuta ityanvayaḥ | parabrahmabhūtabhagavata eva śivādiśabdavācyatvāt, rudrādīnāṃ ca tatsṛjyatvānna
rudrādeḥ pāramyamityarthaḥ | evaṃ samākhyāyā vākyāntarairupaṣṭambhāsiddheḥ sā durbaleti na tayeṣṭasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ || 96 ||
vedāntavākyārthanirṇaye hi bhagavatā bādarāyaṇonopāyadvayaṃ pravartitaṃ brahmasūtrāṇi upabṛṃhaṇāni ca | tatra adhītaśākhārthanirṇāyakāni brahmasūtrāṇi, anadhītaśākhārthanirṇāyakāni tūpabṛṃhaṇāni | tadāhurbhaṭṭasudarśanapādāḥ - ""adhītaśākhārthasya saṃśayaviparyayayādinirāsako nyāyaḥ; anadhītaśākhārthaikakaṇṭhyenādhītaśākhārthajñāpanārthamupabṛṃhaṇamityarthaḥ"iti | tatra caitāvatā ślokagaṇena "vaidharmyācca na svapnādivat""etena sarve vyākhyātāḥ""kāmādītaratra tatra cāyatanādibhyaḥ""tathānya pratiṣedhāt"iti sūtrānusāreṇa paridṛśyamānavedāntavākyānāmarthanirṇayaḥ kṛtaḥ | idānīmanadhītopaniṣatsūktaviparyasaṃdehanirākaraṇāya tāsvapyayamevārthaḥ pratipādyate, na tvetadviruddha iti jñāpanāya nyāyayopabṛṃhaṇayorupayogabhedaṃ darśayannupabṛṃhaṇānāṃ bhagavatpāramyasvārasyamāha - 
mū - yadupaniṣadbhirapṛ (pra)thi parihraḍhimā paramaḥ
kamaladṛśo viśuddhanayalabdhapariṣkṛtibhiḥ |
itaragiro’nuroddhumidameva ghaṭanta iti
sphuṭamupabṛṃhayanti munayo’pi manupramukhāḥ || 97 ||
viśuddhanayaiḥ - samanvayādhyāyādyukaiḥ pratitarkaparāhatyabhāvādinā nirdoṣairnyāyaiḥ, labdhā - niṣpannā, pariṣkṛtiḥ - anyonyavirodhaparihāreṇa samyagarthāvadhāraṇasāmarthyarūpaguṇādhānātmikā yāsāṃ tāstathoktāstābhiḥ |
pariṣkrṛtirbhūṣaṇaṃ, tacceha uktarūpameva | "saṃparibhyāṃ karotau bhūṣaṇe"iti suḍāgamaḥ | upaniṣadbhiḥ - atrādhīyamānābhirmahopaniṣadādibhiḥ | paramaḥ - sarvotkṛṣṭatvādutkṛṣṭāntaraśūnyaḥ | kamaladṛśaḥ - puṇḍarīkākṣasya | paribraḍhimā - paribṛḍhatvaṃ, brahmatvamityarthaḥ | apṛ(pra)thi - prakhyāpitaḥ | pratha prakhyāne | ghaṭāditvena mitvāt "ciṇṇamūloḥ"iti dīrghavikalpena
hasvaḥ karmaṇi ciṇi talopaśca ||
atra paramaḥ paribraḍhimetyanena "brahma paribṛḍhaṃ sarvataḥ""tadeva brahma paramam"ityādivākyadvayaṃ sūcitam | paribṛḍhaśabdāt bhāva imanici "ra ṛto halādeḥ" iti rabhāvaḥ | kamaladṛśa ityanena "brahmavidāpnoti param" ṭhabrahma puccham""brahmaṇo vidvān"iti brahmatvena pratipāditasya puruṣāntarāvasthitasya ānandamayasyādityamaṇḍalāntarvartitvāvagamāt tasya ca puṇḍarīkākṣatvāvagamāt bhagavata eva parabrahmatvaṃ nyāyyamiti sūcyate |
iti yadidameva - samanvayādilakṣaṇoktanyāyānugṛhītābhiradhīyamānābhirupaniṣadbhirbhagavato brahmatvaprakhyāpanaṃ, karma | itaragiraḥ - anadhītaśākhāḥ, katrryaḥ | anuroddhaṃ - anusartum | ghaṭante - arhanti | iti - imamartham | manupramukhā munayo’pi, sphuṭamupabṛṃhayanti - upabṛṃhaṇena spaṣṭaṃ upapādayanti | atra copabṛṃhaṇaṃ svayogamahimasākṣātkṛtasakalavedārthapratipādakagranthapraṇayanam ||
manupramukhā iti pramukhaśabdena parāśarapāraśaryaprācetasaśukaśaunakādīnāṃ saṃgrahaḥ | manvityanena kapilādīnāṃ vyudāsaḥ | tatkṛtopabṛṃhaṇasya śrutyananuguṇatvāt | apiśabdena pañcarātrapraṇeturbhagavataḥ samuccayaḥ | sphuṭamityanena - 
"trayaste kāraṇātmano jātāḥ sākṣānmaheśvarāt
brahmā sarge haristrāṇe rudraḥ saṃharaṇe punaḥ ||
tathāpyanyonyamātsaryādanyonyātiśayārthinaḥ |
tapasā toṣayitvavā taṃ pitaraṃ parameśvaram ||
brahmanārāyaṇau rudraḥ purā kalpāntare’sṛjat |
kalpāntare punarbrahmā rudraviṣṇū jaganmayaḥ ||
viṣṇuśca bhagavāṃstadvadbrahmāṇamasṛjat punaḥ |
nārāyaṇaṃ punarbrahmā brahmāṇaṃ ca punarbhavaḥ ||
evaṃ kalpeṣu kalpeṣu brahmaviṣṇumaheśvaraḥ |
parasparasmājjāyante parasparajayaiṣiṇaḥ ||
tattatkalpāntavṛttāntamadhikṛtya maharṣibhiḥ |
prabhāvaḥ kathyate teṣāṃ parasparasamudbhavāt ||
kvacidbrahmā kvacidrudraḥ kvacidviṣṇuḥ praśasyate |
nānena teṣāmādhikyaṃ nyūnatvaṃ vā kathaṃcana ||
tattatkalpāntavṛttāntamadhikṛtya maharṣibhiḥ |
tāni tāni praṇītāni vidvāṃstatra na muhyati ||"
ityādīnāmapracaravadavasthānāṃ nityavadāmnānaviruddhānāṃ prakṣepaśaṅkāspadānāṃ vyudāsaḥ || 97 ||
evaṃ samanvayāvirodhalakṣaṇayoravyākhyeyāṃśā vyākhyātāḥ | adya sādhanalakṣaṇe "adhyayanamātravataḥ"iti sūtravivakṣitamadhyayanavidherakṣararāśigrahaṇaparyavasitatvamucyate | tatra "svādhyāyo’dhyetavyaḥ"ityasyādhyayanagṛhītasvādhyāyenārthajñānaṃ bhāvayedityartha iti guravaḥ | arthajñānaphalakaguṇakarmādhyayananiyamavidhiparatvamasyeti
kecit | arthajñānaphalatāyā aśravaṇaṃ pakṣatrayasādhāraṇo doṣaḥ | arthajñānajanakatāyāḥ pratyakṣasiddhatvena vidhānāsaṃbhavaḥ, "svādhyāyo’dhyetavyaḥ"iti pratīyamānaguṇakarmatvavirodhaśca prathamadvitīyasādhāraṇaḥ | ato noktārthatrayaṃ saṃbhavati | kiṃ tu svādhyāyādhyayanena svādhyāyāvāptiṃ bhāvayediti tasyārthaḥ |
samānapadopāttasvādhyāyaniṣṭhatvāt, adhyayanabhāvanāṃ pratyarthajñānāpekṣayā sannihitatvāt, humādivyāpakatvāttatra phalatayā klṛptatvācca svādhyāyāvāptireva svamātroccāraṇānukūladṛḍhatarasaṃskārapracayādhāyakākṣarāvadhāraṇātmikā bhāvyā | yathā tuṣavimokasya dṛṣṭatvādyāgārthapuroḍāśopayogācca vrīhīṇāṃ pūrvamanupayuktatayā pratipattikarmaṇo bhinnatvādavahananasya tatsaṃskṛtavrīhīṇāṃ vituṣāṇāṃ bhāvyupayogākāṅkṣiṇāṃ puroḍāśamukhena kratūpayoga iṣyate; evamarthajñānasya brahmayajñajapādeśca dṛṣṭaphalatvācca pūrvamanupayuktasvādhyāyādhyayanasya pratipattikarmaṇo bhinnatvāt gṛhītasya svādhyāyasya uttaropayogārthakāṅkṣiṇo’rthajñānajapamukhena dṛṣṭopayoga iṣyate | asya ca niyamavidhitvāllikhitapāṭhādikaṃ vyāvartyate | evaṃ cānadhītasvādhyāyajanyārthajñānādikaṃ na kratūpayogīti sidhyati | rāddhānte’dhyayanavidhivākyārthaniṣkarṣaḥ ||
atra keciccodayanti - arthajñānasya vidhyasparśe svādhyāyasyāvivakṣitārthatvaprasaṅga | arthābodhamuddiśya śabdoccāraṇena hi loke vākyānāṃ tātparyamavagamyatannarvāhārthaṃ mukhyāsaṃbhave lakṣaṇādikaṃ parikalpyate, na tūccāramamātreṇa | ata eva "gauraśvaḥ puruṣo hastī" iti padānāmuccāraṇamātreṇa lakṣaṇā na kalpyate | yadā viśiṣṭārthabodhamuddiśya teṣāmuccāraṇamiti tātparyamavagamyate, tadaiva tannirvāhārthamayaṃ gaurbalīvardaḥ, aśvo vegavān, puruṣo niyataceṣṭaḥ, hastī mahābaka iti gavādipadānāṃ lakṣaṇādi kalpyate | evaṃ ca vedavākyānāmarthāvabodhamuddiyośyoccāraṇābhāve tātparyāsiddhyā tannirvāhārthaṃ lakṣaṇādikalpanocchedaprasaṅgādavaśyaṃ vede’pyarthāvabodhamuddiśyoccāraṇasya vaktavyatvāt vede ca loka iva tamuddiśya rāgaprayuktoccāraṇābhāvādvidhiprayuktameva tadāśrayaṇīyamityadhyayanavidhisparśo’rthajñānasyāvasyakaḥ | ato’dhyayanavidhernākṣarāvadhāraṇārthakatvaṃ yuktamiti ||
atrāyaṃ parihāraḥlokavyutpattisiddhasya śabdānāmarthaparatvasyautsargikasya kvacitkvacillaukikavākye vaktṛdoṣāpavāde’pyapauriṣeye tadabhāvānnirapavādasya svata eva samarthasyārthajñānoddeśyakaśabdoccāraṇasya vidhinirapekṣatvānmauninā likhitvā datte pareṇāpi
mauninānusaṃhite ślokādāvarthāvabodhamuddaśya śabdoccāraṇasya adarśanena cādhyayanavidherarthajñānasparśānavakāśaḥ | anyatra viniyogaśca na svābhāvikaṃ śabdasyārthaparatvaṃ vihanti, japavidhivat | "yājayitnā pratigṛhya vānaśnan trisvādhyāyaṃ vedamadhīyīta trirātraṃ vā sāvitrīm""vedameva japennityam" ityādividhānaṃ hi na vedasyārthaparatvaṃ nivārayatīti | tadidamāha - 
aidamperyamapauruṣeyavacasāmartheṣu naipargikaṃ
sākṣādakṣarasiddhiparyavasitaḥ svādhyāyavidhyāśayaḥ |
vyāhantīti vadanti ye japavidhinyāyena saṃtuṣyatāmāyuṣmadbhirananyalabhyaviṣaye śabdaḥ samujjṛmbhate ||
adhyayanavidherasamīcīnārthavādināṃ pūrvopadarśitāstrayaḥ | asya ca vadantītyanenānvayaḥ | sākṣādakṣarasiddhiparyavasitaḥmukhyāyāmāsannāyaṃ pratyakṣāyāṃ svavākyopāttāyāṃ ca, akṣarasiddhau - svamātroccāraṇaprayojakasmṛtiviśeṣahetusaṃskārātirekaḍanakākṣarāvadhāraṇe, paryavasitaḥ tadadhikasparśaviraheṇa tatraivāpavargi | svādhyāyavidhyāśayaḥ"svādhyāyo’dhyetavyaḥ" ityadhyayanavidhāyakavākyābhiprāyaḥ | adhyayanavidherakṣarāvadhāraṇabhāvyakatātātparyamityarthaḥ | naisargikaṃsvābhāvikam | apauruṣeyavacasāṃvedavākyānām | arthaiṣu - yāgopāsanasvargamokṣādirūpasvābhidheyeṣu | aidamparyaṃidaṃparatvaṃ, tātparyamiti yāvat | vyāhantiniruṇaddhi | vaidānāmarthabodhakatvagrahaṃ pratibadhnātīti ye vadantītyanvayaḥ | atra yattadornityasaṃbandhāttairityadhyāhāraḥ | ayuṣmadbhiḥ - taiḥjapavidhinyāyenavedasya jape viniyoge’pi svataḥprāptārthavattvakṣatyabhāvadaraśanena | santuṣyatām adhyayanavidhinākṣaragrahaṇe viniyoge’pi na svataḥ prāptarthivattvakṣatirityāśvāsaḥ prāptavya ityarthaḥ | śabdaḥśāstrarūpaḥ | anyalabhyaviṣayemānāntarāprāptārthe | samujjṛmbhateniraṅkuśaṃ vyāpriyate ||
anyatra viniyago na naisargikamarthaparatvaṃ vihantīti bodhanāya samityuktam |
atrāpauruṣeyapadenāpavādavaktṛdoṣaviraho’bhipretaḥ | naisargikapadena vidhinirapekṣatvaṃ sūcyate | evaṃ parihāradvayaṃ sūcitam | japavidhinyāyaśca tṛtīyaparihāraḥ | sākṣātpadena ca sākṣātpratyakṣamukhyayoriti tantreṇārthadvayaṃ vivakṣitam | anena cānāsannānupāttārthajñāvyudāsaḥ | paryavasitaśabdenākṣaragrahaṇasyāsannatvāt svavākyopātatvācca lāghavena bhāvyatvaṃ, na tu vyavahitasyāśābdasyāpyarthajñānasya bhāvyatvaṃ gauravāpatteriti sūcitam || 98 ||
arthajñānasya vidhyasparśe svādhyāyasyāvivakṣaitārthatvaprasaṅga iti vadan vādī vikalpyate - "svādhyāyo’yetavyaḥ" ityadhyayanavidhirarthaparaḥ, uta na | ādye’arthāvabodhamuddiśyādhyayanavidhiṃ vinaiva yathā "svādhyāyo’dhyetavyaḥ" iti vākyasya svataḥprāptamarthavattvaṃ vākyāntarāṇāmapi taṃ vinaiva svataḥprāptamarthavattvamanivāryam | dvitīye tu atharvābodhamuddiśyādhyayanakartavyatāyā ivākṣarāvadhāramuddiśyādhyayanakartavyatāyā apyasiddhyā niṣpratibhaṭaṃ vedānāmarthavattvamiti | tadidamāha - ( ya ityādiḥ ) - 
yaḥ svārtheṣvanidaṃparāḥ śrutigiraḥ svādhyāyavidhyāśayādācaṣṭe sa vikalpamarhati vidhiḥ sārthaḥ kimāsthīyate |
yadvānarthaka ityanena vacasā tulyatvamāsāṃ girāmādye syāditaratra tatpratibhaṭaḥ kiṃ syādakasmādvidhiḥ || 99 ||
yaḥ svādhyāyavidhyāśayāt - akṣarāvadhāraṇabhāvyakādhyayanasaṃskārabhāvanātātparyakatvamadhyayanavidherasmaduktamanūdya | śrutigiraḥ - vedavākyāni | svārtheṣvanidaṃparāḥ - svataḥprāpteṣvabhidheyeṣu tātparyaśūnyā bhaveyuriti | ācaṣṭe - bravīti | adhyayanavidherakṣaragrahaṇaparyavasitatve vedasyāvivakṣitārthatvaprasaṅga iti yo vadatītyarthaḥ | āśayāditi lyabablope pañcamī | saḥ - vādī | vidhiḥ - adhyayanavidhiḥ | sārthaḥ - sābhidheyatvena | asthīyate kiṃ - tvayā svīkriyate kim ? yadvā anarthakaḥ - anabhidheyakatayā āsthīyata iti vikalpaṃ - dūṣayiṣyamāṇakoṭidvayābhyupagamapraśnam | arhati - mate, sa evaṃ praṣṭavya ityarthaḥ | tatrādyakoṭiṃ
dūṣayati - anenetyādinā | ādye - svādhyāyādhyayanavidheḥ sārthakatvaśirasi | āsāṃ girāṃ - adhyayanavidhivākyavyatiriktavedavākyānām | anena vacasā tulyatvaṃ - arthāvabodhamuddiśyādhyayanavidhyabhāve’pi svataḥprāptenārthavattvenārthabodhakena "svādhyāyo’dhyetavyaḥ" iti vākyena vidhyabhāve’pyarthabodhakatvaṃ dharmatastaulyam | atra bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | "tulyārthaiḥ"ityādinā vacaseti tṛtīyā | dvitīyaṃ dūṣayati - itaratretyādinā | itaratra svādhyāyādhyayanavidherarthaśūnyatve | vidhiḥ - adhyayanavidhiḥ | akasmāt - niṣkāraṇam | tatpratibhaṭaḥ syāt kiṃ - vedasyārthaparatvapratikṣepako bhavet kim ? na bhavedityarthaḥ | adhyayanavidherarthaśūnyatve hi
akṣapāvadhāraṇoddeśenādhyayanakartavyatvāsiddhyā vedānāmarthaparatvaṃ niṣpratyanīkamiti bhāvaḥ ||
idamevābhipretya mahācāryacaraṇairanugṛhītam - "na cārthajñānasya vidhyasparśe svādhyāyasyāvivakṣitārthatvaprasaṅgaḥ svādhyāyādhyayanavidhivadeva upapatteḥ"iti | nanvadhyayanavidhivākyasyākṣaragrahaṇoddeśenādhyayanavidhāyakavākyāntarābhāvena svataḥprāptamarthavattvamanapoditam | tena tu vākyāntarāṇāmakṣaragrahaṇoddeśenādhyayanavidhānādarthaparatvamapodyeteti cenna; japavidhinyāyasyoktatvāt | taduktaṃ meghanādārisūricaraṇaiḥ "ato japavidherapi svārthapratītisiddhyarthaṃ svādhyayanasyārthaparatāpekṣitaiva | na ca japārthaparatvayorvastuvirodho nirarāthakoccāraṇarūpatvājjapasyeti vācyam, anuṣṭhānakāle vivakṣābhāvamātrādapi tadupapatteḥ"iti || 99 ||
adhyayanasya saṃskārakarmatvaṃ, tadvidherniyamaparatvaṃ, adhyāpanasyācāryarāgataḥ prāptatvaṃ (svavidhi) prayuktatvaṃ, upanayanasya māṇavakadvārādhyayanaśeṣatvaṃ, adhyayanavidherakṣaragrahaṇaparyavasitatve’pi vedasya svataḥprāptatvādarthavattāyā rāgata eva vicārapravṛttiḥ, samāvṛttasyāpi rāgataḥ śravaṇaṃ, gṛhasthadharmāṇāṃ ca hitaiṣivacanādanuṣṭhānaṃ, tasmin pramāṇamūlakatvaniścayārthaṃ praṣṭṝṇāṃ svayamupadeśārthaṃ ca śravaṇamiti siddhāntaparipāṭī ||
atrāhuḥ - "
rāgato’pi samāvṛttasya śravaṇaṃ nopapadyate |
gṛhasthakarmānuṣṭhānānavaruddhaśravaṇāvasarālābhāt gurvarthamudakāharaṇakāṣṭhasaṃpādanādibhiranuṣṭhānoparodhācceti | atrāyaṃ parihāraḥ - guroḥ svadharmānuṣṭhānānuparodhena "pratigrahādinā dhanamārjayet" iti śāstrānuṣṭhānavidhiriti śravaṇāvasaraḥ sulabhaḥ | adhyayanavidhiprayuktasyodakāharaṇādestannivṛttyā nivṛttatvānnānuṣṭhānoparodhaśca | udakāharaṇādyabhāve’pi vittaravyātyādiprayojanāntaraspṛhayā śiṣyasnehena ca guroḥ śrāvayitṛtvamupapadyate"iti | tadidamāha dvābhyāṃ ślokābhyām | tatra codyamanuvadan parihāraṃ pratijānīte prathamena - (yadityādi)
yacchroturgṛhamedhino gurugṛhe kāṣṭhodakādyāhṛtivyāsaktasya na saṃbhavatyavasarastattatsavakarmakrame |
syāccedgurvanuvartanādyanuguṇaḥ kutrāvakāśo bhave - 
dityuktaṃ katibhiścidatra kuśalairitthaṃ samādhīyate || 100 ||
katibhiścit - pūrvepadarśitaistribhirvādibhiḥ | idaṃ coktamityatrānveti | gurugṛhe kāṣṭhodakādyāhṛtivyāsaktasya - gṛhagṛhe gurvarthamudakāharaṇendhanasaṃpādanādibhiranyaparasya | gṛhamedhinaḥ - gṛhasthasya | "mudhamedhāsaṃgamanayoḥ" "meghṛsaṅgame ca" iti dhātupāṭhaḥ | gṛhairdāraiḥsaṃgacchata iti gṛhameghī tasya | kṛtodvāhasyetyarthaḥ | śrotuḥ - śravaṇādhikāriṇaḥ | teṣāṃ teṣāṃ svīyānāṃ karmaṇām agnihotrapañcamahāyajñādīnāṃ, krame anupūrvyeṇāvicchedenānuṣṭhāne | avasaraḥ - kālaḥ | anuṣṭhānakāla ityarthaḥ | na saṃbhavati syāccet svadharmānuṣṭhānaṃ saṃbhavati cet | gurvanuvartanādeḥ kāṣṭhodakādyāharaṇapraṇipātādeḥ, anuguṇaḥ anukūlaḥ avakāśaḥ, kutra bhavet kathaṃ bhavet, na bhavedityarthaḥ | tathā ca gṛhasthasya svadharmānuṣṭhānoparodhaprasaṅgāttadabhāve guruśuśrūṣoparodhaprasaṅgācca na vicārapravṛttyupapattiriti codyagranthārthaḥ | iti yaduktam | atra - asmin codye | kuśalaiḥ - sūtrakārahṛdayānusāribhirbhagavadbhāṣyakārādibhirvedārthanirvahaṇanipuṇairasmādācāryaiḥ | itthaṃ - vakṣyamāṇadiśā | samādhīyate - pariharaḥ kriyate | samādadhāteravivakṣitakarmatvādbhāve lakāraḥ || 100 ||
itthamiti (abhipretaṃ) (parābhipretaṃ) samādhānaprakāramāha - yadvedetyādi)
yadvedākṣararāśisiddhyuparataḥ svādhyāyavidhyāśayastattanmūlakaśiṣyakṛtyaviraterlabdhāvakāśāḥ kriyāḥ |
śrotuḥ śrāvayituryathetyavasaraḥ śrotuśca sidhyettathā
snehena spṛhayā ca vittayaśasoḥ śiṣyaṃ guruḥ śrāvayet || 101 ||
yat yasmāddhetoḥ | svādhyāyavidhyāśayaḥ - adhyayanavidhivākyatātparyam | vedaḥ cāsau akṣararāśiśceti vigrahe karmadhārayaḥ, tasya siddhau avadhāraṇe, uparataḥ - arthajñānāsparśena tatraiva paryavasitaḥ | atra bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | uparata ityanena vyākhyātrupapādanena vināpi svayameva tathā paryavasita iti sūcanādarthāntarābhidhānaṃ śrutipīḍanameveti labhyate | taduktaṃ mahācāryacaraṇaiḥ - "svatātparyamāvedayantīṃ śrutimanādṛtyābhīṣṭāsvarthabodhanāya śrutipīḍane tacchatidrohādanarthaparamparātaśca na kadācidapi muktiḥ"iti | tat - tasmāddhetoḥ | idaṃ ca viratāvanveti | śrotuḥ - śravaṇādhikāriṇaḥ gṛhamedhina ityanuṣajyate | kriyāḥ - pañca mahāyajñādayaḥ | tanmūlakānāṃ adhyayananimittakānāṃ, śiṣyakṛtyānāṃ - udakāharaṇādyavaśyakartavyānāṃ karmaṇāṃ, virateḥ - nimittabhūtādhyayananivṛttiprayuktanivṛtterhetoḥ | labdhāvakāśāḥ - saṃbhavadanuṣṭhānakālā iti hetoḥ | itiradamatrākṛṣyate | sa ca hetvarthaḥ | idaṃ ca dārṣṭāntike’nveti | yathā śrāvayituḥ avasaraḥ sidhyet tathā śrotuḥ kriyā adhyayananivṛttiprayuktodakāharaṇādiśiṣyakṛtyanivṛttyā labdhāvakāśā iti hetoḥ | śroturapyavasaraḥ, sidhyarha ityarthaḥ ||
adhyayanavidhiprayuktānāmudakāharaṇādīnāṃ adhyayanāya niyamenopādeyatvābhiprāyeṇa mūlapadanirdeśaḥ | bhavati hi phalasyāpi saṅkalpadvārā svopāyahetutvam | atra śrāvayituryathetyādinā svadharmānuṣṭhānānāvaruddhaśravaṇakālaḥ, tanmūlaketyādinā gurvanuvartanena tadanuparodhaścoktau | atra caikaṃ śroturiti padaṃ kriyā ityanenānveti | dvitīyaṃ tvavasara
ityaneneti na punaruktiḥ | atra cādhyāyanavidherakṣararāśigrahaṇaparyavasitatva eva gṛhasthasya śravaṇāvasaraḥ saṃbhavati, na tvarthajñānaparyantatve, udakāharaṇāderyāvadadhyayanavidhisiddhārthānuṣṭhānasamāptyanuvṛttergṛhasthasya pañcamahāyajñādisvadharmānuṣṭhānoparodhagurvanuvarthanoparodhayoranyatarasyāvarjanīyatvāt | na ceṣṭāpattiḥ | yudhiṣṭhirādīnāṃ gārhasthyaniṣṭhānāmapi bhīṣmādibhyaḥ śravaṇasya bhāratādiṣu pratipāditatvāt | atastvaduktadoṣastadaiva bhavatīti vedākṣararāśisiddhyuparata ityanena dhvanyate ||
nanvanuvartanābhāve guruḥ kathaṃ śrāvayedityatrāha - snehenetyādi | guruḥ, snehena - śiṣyaprītyā | ( vittayaśasoḥ spṛhayā) | śiṣyadattadhanecchayā, tadabhāve vidyādānaprayuktaparamodāratvaprathākāmanayā vā | etena adhyāpanasya vaidhatvaṃ nirastam | śiṣyaṃ śrāvayet - | atra vaidrthamiti śeṣaḥ |
atra snehena śvetaketuviṣaye, vittecchayā jānaśrutiviṣaye, yaśaskāmanayā maitreyaviṣaye cāruṇiraikvaparāśarāṇāṃ śrāvaṇaṃ śrutyupabṛṃhaṇasiddhaṃ drṣṭavyam | yadyapi parāśarasya snehena maitreyaṃ prati śrāvaṇam; tathāpi "māmanyenākṛtaśramaṃ kathayiṣyanti"ityanena yaśaso’pi hetutvaṃ dhvanyate | atra ca vitaraṇamūlakaprathāyā yaśastvaprasiddhervidyāvitaraṇaṃ yaśohetiriti vittayaśasorityuktam | "kumārya iva kāntasya trasyanti spṛhayanti ca"ityatreva śeṣatvavivakṣayā vittayaśasoriti ṣaṣṭhī | prakarṣavivakṣāyāṃ paratvāt karmasaṃjñayā kṛdyogalakṣaṇā vā ṣaṣṭhī | "samaṃ brāhmaṇo dānam"itivat saṃpradānasyādhikaraṇatvavikṣayā saptamī vā | "satkārakīrtidhanalabdhiṣu nirvyapekṣaḥ praśnociteṣu parayā kṛpayā prasīda"ityādikamapyatrānusandheyam || 101 ||
evaṃ samanvayāvirodhasādhanalakṣaṇeṣu vyākhyeyāṃśāḥ prāyaśo vyākhyātāḥ | idānīṃ phalalakṣaṇe’rcirādipādaṃ vyācaṣṭe - (arcirityādi)
arcirahassitapakṣānudagayanābdamarudarkendūn |
api vaidyutavaruṇendraprajāpatīnātivāhikānāhuḥ || 102 ||
arciḥ - arcirabhimānidevatā | ahaḥ - dinābhimānidevatā | sitapakṣaḥ - śuklapakṣābimānidevatā | arciścetyādivigrahe itaretarayogadvandvaḥ | tān | udagayana - 
uttarāyaṇābhimānidevatā | śabdaḥ - saṃvatsarābhimānidevatā | marut - vāyudevatā | arkaḥ - ādityaḥ | induḥ - candramāḥ | tān | atrāpītaretarayogadvandvaḥ | vaidyutaḥ - vidyudabhimānidevatā | varuṇaḥ prasiddhaḥ, indraḥ prasiddhaḥ, prajāpatiḥ - dhāt, tān | atrāpi pūrvavat dvandvaḥ | uktān dvādaśa |
viduṣaḥ - muktasya | ātivāhikān - ativahane prāpaṇe paramapuruṣeṇa niyuktān | āhuḥ - vadanti | sūtrakārā iti śeṣaḥ ||
atra arcirityārabhya prajāpatīn ithyantena kramasya kramiṇāṃ ca vivakṣitatvāt, arcirādinā tatprathiteḥ vāyumabdādaviśeṣaviśeṣābhyām taṭito’dhivaruṇaḥ saṃbandhāt iti sūtraiḥ pūrvatantroktaśrutyarthapāṭhapravṛttimukhyakāṇḍakrameṣu śrutyarthapāṭharūpāṇāṃ saṃbhavatāṃ kramāṇāṃ chāndogyakauṣītakībṛhadāraṇyakādivākyokteṣvarciḥprabhṛtiṣu padārtheṣu nirṇītatayā pratyabhijñānena mārgaikyasyāpi sādhitatvāt | vāyudevalokaśabdayoścachāgapaśunyāyena ekārthavṛttitvanirṇayena jyotirmayakṛtsnavaimānikadevatāgaṇasya vāyupāśadhṛtatvādvāyoreva devalokaśabdārthatayā vāyulokaśabdasya ca vāyuścāsau loka iti karmadhārayajyāyastvena devāvāsabhūtavāyuviṣayatvāt, arcirādireka eva darśitakramaviśiṣṭo mārga ityayamartho’bhisaṃhitaḥ | ātivāhikānityanena ca neme mārgaci(hni)(nvā)bhūtā na vā bhogabhūmayaḥ, kiṃ tu paramapuruṣeṇa muktānāmativāhe niyuktā devatāviśeṣā iti ātivāhikāstalliṅgāt ityadhikaraṇanirṇītārtho’bhipretaḥ | atra ca vaidyutasyānavasyaiva gamayitṛtvaṃ, varuṇādīnāṃ tvanugrāhakatayātivahanānvaya ityayamartho’pyanusandheyaḥ | ida ca vaidyutenaiva tatastacchateḥ iti sūtreṇa nirṇītam | ativāhikaśabdaśca tatraniyukta iti sūtravihitaniyuktārthakaṭhakpratyayāntaḥ | asmin śloke udgītirnāmāryābhedaḥ - 
paripūrṇaphalanirūpaṇaparaṃ śārīrakāntamalakṣaṇāntimapādāntimādhikaraṇam
(vyācikhyāsuḥ vyākhyeyaṃ nirdiśati - antimādhikaraṇa iti | asya vyākhyeyaṃ vyākhyāyata iti śeṣaḥ |) jagadvyāpārādhikaraṇamantimādhikaraṇam |
anāvṛttiśśabdādityasyādhikaraṇāntaratve tadevāntimādhikaraṇam | tatra hi tadarthavicāraparaṃparāyā muktānāmasvātantryasyānandavaikalyāpādakatvānāpādakatvayoḥ krameṇa pūrvottarapakṣayorniviṣṭatayā rāddhāntaniviṣṭamasvātantryasyāndavaikalyānāpādakatvaṃ sahetukamupapādyata ityarthaḥ | asvatantrasyāvṛttisaṃbhavayorapi krameṇa tanniviṣṭatayā tatrāsvātantryamamamapi niviṣṭamiti tasya kimasvātantryarūpatvādduḥkhāvahatvamutātmābhimānānuguṇatvādityasyāpi niviṣṭatvenānāvṛttisūtrasyādhikaraṇāntaratve’pi rāddhāntābhimatamasvātantryasyānandavaikalyānāpādakatvamupapādyata ityarthaḥ - (śeṣatvamityādi)
śeṣatvaṃ nanu duḥkhamīkṣitamato mukteṣu yuktaṃ na
tanmaivaṃ mādhavadāsyamaupaniṣadaṃ jāgarti bhūyaḥ priyatam |
dharmigrāhakamānādhikkṛtamatastadduḥkhatāsādhanaṃ
yadvā tadvyatiriktadāsyamasukhaṃ sādhyeta siddhaṃ hi tat ||
nanviti śaṃkāyām | śeṣatvaṃ - asvātantryam | yasya parātiśaya eva paramaprayojanaṃ sa śeṣa paraśśeṣī iti bhaṭṭasudarśanapādānugṛhītaniṣkarṣarītyā mātrayāpi svaprayojanavirahitaparaprayojanahetutvarūpaṃ śeṣatvamityarthaḥ | duḥkhamīkṣitaṃ - cetayamānasya pratikūlamiti lokadṛṣṭam | tat - śeṣatvam | ataḥ - duḥkharūpatvāddhetoḥ | mukteṣu - sarvaduḥkhavinirmukteṣu muktātmasu | na yuktaṃ - anupapannam | nanu anupapannamiti śaṃkyata ityarthaḥ ||
śeṣatvaṃ duḥkharūpaṃ śeṣatvāt saṃpratipannavadityanumānamihābhipretam | sarvaṃ paravaśaṃ duḥkham sevā śvavṛtti(rvyā)(rā)khyātā tasmātmāṃ parivarjayet iti smṛtiśca vivakṣitā | atra ca priyaviṣayaśeṣatvasya sukharūpatvadarśanāddhetoranaikāntyam | śeṣatve duḥkharūpatāyāḥ karmamūlakadehātmābhimānapruyaktatvāt | sopādhikatvaṃ ca bhogyaguṇavadanyanirūpitaśeṣatvaṃ duḥkharūpatvaniyatatve’pi ubhayaliṅgakabhagavaccheṣatve tadapravṛttiḥ | bhagavaccheṣatve sukharūpatvaṃ
śāstrasiddhamiti kālātyayāpadeśaśca | anyaśeṣatvasya duḥkharūpatvasādhane siddhasādhanam | śeṣatvasāmānyasya duḥkharūpatvasādhane’pyaṃśato bādhitatvam | aṃśataśca siddhasādhanatvam |
sarvaṃ paravaśamityādisāmānyavacanasya bhagavaddāsyasukhatvabodhaka vacanaśataiḥ padāhavanīyānyāyena saṃkocaḥ pramāṇikaḥ | priyaviṣayaśeṣatvāṃśe pratyakṣavirodhāccāvaśyakaḥ saṃkocaḥ | mātāpitṛśeṣatve sukharūpaṃ dṛṣṭamiti sarveṣāmeva lokānāṃ pitā mātā
ca mādhavaḥ | ityuktabhagavaccheṣatvaṃ sukharūpamiti yuktam | śāstravihitaṃ ca mātāpitṛśeṣatvam ato nānupapattiḥ ||
ātmābhimānānuguṇā puruṣārthavyavasthitiḥ |
yatastato na śeṣatve susādhā duḥkharūpatā ||
tṛṇāderhi gavādibhogyatve tṛṇatvādikaṃ na niyāmakam | vyāghrādīn pratyapi tadāpatteḥ | nāpi tṛṇādibhogyatābuddhau svāśrayāvacchinnatvaghaṭitasāmānādhikaraṇyena gotvādikaṃ kāraṇamiti vaktuṃ yuktam | gavādergotvāpratisandhānadaśāyāmapi tṛṇādermogyatvāpateḥ | prasiddhaṃ hi bharatajanakādīnāṃ manuṣyatvavatāmapi tadibhimānavirahamātreṇa manuṣyatvaniyatasukhādyananvayaḥ | dṛṣṭaśca janmāntare brāhmaṇasya jātissarasya śukraviśeṣasya caṇḍālagṛhe jalapānādyabhāvavādaḥ | atastattadātmābhimānānurūpaiva puruṣārthavyavasthā - 
taduktam
annaṃ bhojyaṃ manuṣyāṇāmamṛtaṃ tu divaukasām |
śvapaśū viṭtṛṇāhārau santo dāsyaikajīvanāḥ || iti |
atra ca asti brahmeti cedveta santamenaṃ tato viduḥ ityuktarītyā prāptaśeṣinirūpitasvaniṣṭhaśeṣatvādhyavasāyavantaḥ santaḥ | ata eva manuṣyāṇāmityādau manuṣyatvādyabhim (ni)navatāmiti labdham | idaṃ ca vedārthasaṅgrahataddīpikayorvyaktamupapāditam | śrutaprakāśikāyāṃ ca - sarveṣāṃ hi svātmābhimānānuguṇā bhogyavyavasthā tadviparyavyavasthā ca | tathā puruṣapaśvādiṣu dṛśyate | tasmādātmana avirbhūtayathāvasthitasvarūpasya śeṣatvameva svābhāvikaṃ rūpamaparokṣyataḥ svaśeṣiparamapuruṣaparicaraṇameva priyatamaṃ syāt | tadviparyayeṇeśitṛtvamatyantāniṣṭameva |
ityuktam | na ca svabhogasya mātrayāpyanuddeśyatve sarvadā svena bhagavato yatkiṃcitprayojanasiddherna mumukṣā syāditi vācyam; līlārasāditaḥ
kaiṃkaryādhīnabhagavadānandasyātiśayitatvāt | na caivamapi yena kenācidānandasiddherna niyamataḥ svamokṣecchā syāditi vācyam; svopādanaparamaprayojanaṃ bhagavataḥ siddhyedityevarūpecchāyā avirodhāt | sarvo hi cetanaḥ svārthameva pravartata iti punaḥ prakṛṣṭapāsnehānviteṣu vyabhicārīti śrutaprakāśikāyāṃ sphuṭam ||
tadidaṃ sarvamabhisaṃdhāyāha - maivaṃ mādhavadāsyamiti | maivaṃ - pūrvoktadūṣaṇaṃ netyarthaḥ | kuta ityatrāha - mādhavadāsyamityādi | mādhavadāsyaṃ - śriyaḥpatiparicaraṇam | dāsaśabdāt karmaṇi ṣyañpratyayaḥ | aupaniṣadaṃ sat - upaniṣatpratipādyaṃ sat | bhūyaḥ priyaṃ - niratiśayapriyam | jāgarti - pratyakṣadṛṣṭamapi bhavati | ataḥ - pūrvoktāddhetoḥ | tasya - paramapuruṣaparicaraṇasya, duḥkhatāsādhanam - duḥkharūpatānumānam | dharmigrāhakamānābhyāṃ - duḥkharupatārūpasādhyadharmaṃ prati pakṣatayā dharmibhūtaparicaraṇasvarūpagrāhakābhyāṃ śāstrapratyakṣābhyāṃ, dhikkṛtaṃ - bādhitaviṣayam | atra bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | yadveti - pakṣāntare | tavdyatiriktadāsyaṃ - paramapuruṣaparicaraṇādanyadanāptaparicaraṇam | asukhaṃ sādhyeta - duḥkharūpamanumīyate cet | tat - paramapuruṣaparicaraṇādanyasya duḥkharūpatvam | siddhameva hi - pramāṇāntarapramitameva khalvityaryaḥ ||
atra kālātyayāpadeśaḥ siddhasādhanatvaṃ ca kaṇṭhoktam | upādhyanukte svābhāvikatvāvagamakopaniṣannirdeśacca duḥkharūpatvaprarayojakopādhikatvarūpopādhinivṛtiḥ pakṣe darśitā | māghaveti sarvaprakāramaṅgalarūpatvāvagamāt bhogyaguṇavadanyanirūpitatvanivṛtiśca darśitā | jāgartityanena sāketanagaravāstavyayāyajūkajanaguhamārutisugrīvavibhīṣaṇavidurākrūramālākāraprabhṛtīnāṃ bhagavaddāsyaṃ sukharūpaṃ dṛṣṭamiti hetoranaikāntyam | teṣāṃ hi - ebiśchāyāṃ vidhāsyāmaḥsevaiśchatrairvājapeyikaiḥ
ityādibhirbhagavaddāsyaṃ sukharūpamavagatam | bhūyaśśabdena ca niratiśayatvavācinā svalpapriyakāmināparicaraṇādervyāvartyatvapratītestatrāpyanaikāntyaṃ sūcitam | mādhavadāsyamaupaniṣadamiti bhagavaddāsyavidhāyakaviśeṣavacanaiḥ sarvaṃ paravaśam ityādisāmānyavacanasaṅkoco nyāyya iti dhvanitam | sarveṣāmeva lokānāṃ pitā mātā ca
sādhavaḥ ityuktabhagavadviṣayaparicaraṇaṃ mātāpitṛparicaraṇameveti tasya priyatvaṃ śāstravihitatvaṃ cāstīti sūcanāya mādhavetyuktam | ato dāsyaṃ sukharūpameveti na muktasyānandavaikalyamiti bhāvaḥ | pūrvam tasyānantasukhāptaye ca yatate lakṣmīsahāyaḥ svayaṃ, tatkāruṇyapurassaro guṇagaṇastasyāyamujjṛmbhate ityuktam | atra
mādhavadāyatvopayogikāruṇyādiguṇagaṇodbodhakatvalakṣaṇamupāyaviśeṣaṇatvameva lakṣmyā iti lakṣmīsahāyapadena sūcitam | bhagavaddāsyagatasyatātiśayaprayojakabhāvena prāpyavardhakatvena ca saha prāpyāntargatatvaṃ lakṣmyā iti mādhavapadena sūcitam | anena ca idameva śārīrakacaturlakṣaṇyabhipretamti sūcitam | ata eva ca brahmaṇi śrīnivāse iti śāstrārthasaṅgrāhake nirdiṣṭaṃ bhagavatā | pucchādiṣu dhātvartha ityeva siddham ityatrevātrāpi, siddhaṃ hi taditi siddhaśabdo granthāntamaṅgalārthaḥ ||
svakṛtagranthasya śraddheyatamatvasiddhyarthaṃ svasya bhagavadbhāṣyakārasaṃpradāyābhijātatvaṃ tatprasāditaśrībhāṣyapravacanabhadrapīṭhādhyakṣatvaṃ ca vadan svasya prayojanāntaraspṛhāmantareṇa kevaladīnajanānujighukṣātiśayena tattvasāragranthakaraṇamāhavaradetyādi
varadāhvayamaṇḍano manīṣī
yatibṛndārakabhāgineyapautraḥ |
nigamāntapayodhikarṇadhāro
vidadhe viścahitāya tattvasāram ||
yatibṛndārakasyayatirājasya, bhagavadbhāṣyakārasya, bṛndārakanāgakuñcaraiḥ pūjyamānam ityanena samāsaḥ | bhāgineyaḥbhaginīsuto vatsābhijano varadaguruḥ, tasya pautraḥputrasya putraḥ |
bhagavato bhāṣyakṛto dve sodarye | tatraikasyāḥ suto vādhūladāśarathiḥ | yasya svalvajahadbhāgineyatvaṃ prasiddham | taduktam -
itarasulabā kimeṣā bhāṣā vādhūladāśarathirekaḥ |
na parityaktasatyajatābandhūniti sadasi lakṣmaṇāryakṛtā? || iti |
anyasyāstu suto vātsyavaradaguruḥ | taduktam - 
bhūmikamalābhidhāne puṇye rāmānujāryasodarye |
yābhyāmubāvabhūtāṃ putrau dāśarathivaradanāmānau || iti|
tasyāyaṃ pautraḥ | anena nāsyābrahmavit kule bhavati nāsyāvarapuruṣāḥ kṣīyante ityādiśrutyuktarītyā brahmavidagresarabhagavadbhāṣyakāradehasaṃbandhanibandhanajñānaprakarṣo’bisaṃhitaḥ ||
nigamānta eva payodhiḥvedānta eva dugdhanidhiḥ, bhagavadatyantābhimatatvavipulatvabhogyacatvādibhiḥ sādṛśyādrūpaṇaṃ, tasya karṇadhāraḥ nāvikaḥ | atra ca śrībhāṣyasyācchidrāṃ pārayiṣṇumityuktarītyā svapratipādyavadeva nausādṛśyaṃ vivakṣitam | atastatpravacanabhadrapīṭhaniyuktasya svasya karṇadhāratvamuktam | karṇadhārastu nāvikaḥ iti kośaḥ | varadāhvayo maṇḍanam - alaṃkāro yasya sa tathoktaḥ | bhāṣyāmṛtapradānādyaḥ saṃjīvayati māmapi ityuktarītyā varadābhidhānamukhyābhidheyabhagavanniṣṭhaparamodāratvasadṛśaparamogāratvādiguṇayogavato’sya dadabhidhānābhidheyatvaṃ vyudastam | manīṣā - dhīmatāmagresaraḥ | viśveṣām - mṛdumadhyatīvraprajñānāmadikāriṇāṃ, hitāya - apekṣitasaṃgrahavistarānubhayarūpatayā nirdoṣapuruṣārthasādhanārtham | śiṣyahitāyeti ca paṭhyate | arthaḥ spaṣṭaḥ | viśvebhyo hitaṃ viśvahitamiti caturthī vā | "caturthī tadartha" ityādinā samāsaḥ | tattvasāraṃ - śārīrakapratipādyārthajāteṣvabhyarhitārthajātapratipādakatāprayuktatadvācakapadavācyatākaṃ granthaviśeṣam, vidadhe - cakre | śiṣyahitasya svahitatvābhisaṃdhinā kriyāphalasya, kartṛgāmitvaparaṃ ātmanepadaṃ prayuktam | bhāṣyāmṛtaniṣevaṇasukhaparavaśena svenāpi granthakaraṇamapratisaṃhitamiti sūcanāya parokṣe liḍhuktaḥ || 104 ||
śrībhāṣyamerukaṭakodaralabdhamūrtiṃ
saudarśanoktidṛḍhapañjarapālyamānam |
saccaṇḍamārutarayaṃ vijayaikahetuṃ
sāraudhamāśayatu vo maṇisāriṇīyam ||
nārasiṃhaguroḥ putro vahadāryakṛpādhanaḥ |
vīrarāghavadāso’haṃ tattvasāraṃ vyavīvaram ||
iti śrīvāghūlakulatilakaśrīnarasiṃhaguruvaratanayena taccaraṇaparicaraṇaparāyaṇena śrīmadvādhūlaveṅkaṭāryasaṃbhavaśrīvaradagurunirvyājadivyakuṇāsamadhigatanigamāntayugalahṛdayena vīrarāghavadāsena viracitā tattvasāravyākhyā ratnasāriṇīsamākhyā sampūrṇā ||
